Text:

<1>
<1.1>
prathamaḥ paricchedaḥ
māyākalpitamātṛtāmukhamṛṣādvaitaprapañcāśrayaḥ
satyajñānasukhātmakaḥ śrutiśikhotthākhaṇḍadhīgocaraḥ|
mithyābandhavidhūnanena paramānandaikatānātmakaṃ
mokṣaṃ prāpta iva svayaṃ vijayate viṣṇurvikalpojjhitaḥ||1||
śrīrāmaviśveśvaramādhavānāmaikyena sākṣātkṛtamādhavānām|
sparśena nirdhūtatamorajobhyaḥ pādotthitebhyo'stu namo rajobhyaḥ||2||
bahubhirvihitā budhaiḥ parārthaṃ vijayante'mitavistṛtā nibandhāḥ|
mama tu śrama eṣa nūnamātmambharitāṃ bhāvayituṃ bhaviṣyatīha||3||
śraddhādhanena muninā madhusūdanena saṅgṛhya śāstranicayaṃ racitātiyatnāt|
bodhāya vādivijayāya ca satvarāṇāmadvaitasiddhiriyamastu mude budhānām||4||
vipratipattivicāraḥ
tatrādvaitasiddherdvaitamithyātvasiddhipūrvakatvād dvaitamithyātvameva prathamamupapādanīyam| upapādanaṃ ca svapakṣasādhanaparapakṣanirākaraṇābhyāṃ bhavatīti tadubhayaṃ vādajalpavitaṇḍānāmanyatamāṃ kathāmāśritya sampādanīyam| tatra ca vipratipattijanyasaṃśayasya vicārāṅgatvānmadhyasthenādau vipratipattiḥ pradarśanīyā| yadyapi vipratipattijanyasaṃśayasya na pakṣatāsampādakatayopayogaḥ, siṣādhayiṣāvirahasahakṛtasādhakamānābhāvarūpāyāstasyāḥ saṃśayāghaṭitatvāt| anyathā śrutyā''tmaniścayavato'numitsayā tadanumānaṃ na syāt| vādyādīnāṃ niścayavattvena saṃśayāsambhavā dāhāryasaṃśayasyātiprasañjakatvācca| nāpi vipratipatteḥ svarūpata eva pakṣapratipakṣaparigraha phalakatayopayogaḥ| `tvayedaṃ sādhanīyam' `anenedaṃ dūṣaṇīyam' ityādimadhyasthavākyādeva tallābhena vipratipattivaiyarthyāt| tathāpi vipratiprattijanyasaṃśayasyānumityanaṅgatve'pi vyudasanīyatayā vicārāṅgatvamastyeva| tādṛśasaṃśayaṃ prati vipratipatteḥ kvacinniścayādinā pratibandhādajanakatve'pi svarūpayogyatvāt| vādyādīnāṃ ca niścayavattve niyamābhāvāt| `niścitau hi vādaṃ kurutaḥ' ityābhimānikaniścayābhiprāyam| parapakṣamālambyāpyahaṅkāriṇo viparītaniścayavato jalpādau pravṛttidarśanāt| tasmāt samayabandhādivat svakartavyanirvāhāya madhyasthena vipratipattiḥ pradarśanīyaiva|
tatra mithyātve vipratipattiḥ -- brahmapramātiriktābādhyatve sati sattvena pratītyarhaṃ cidbhinnaṃ pratipannopādhau traikālikaniṣedhapratiyogi na vā ? pāramārthikatvākāreṇokta niṣedhapratiyogi na veti| atra ca pakṣatāvacchedakasāmānādhikaraṇyena sādhyasiddheruddeśyatvāt pakṣaikadeśe sādhyasiddhāvapi siddhasādhanateti mate śuktirūpye siddhasādhanavāraṇāya brahmajñānetarābādhyatvaṃ pakṣaviśeṣaṇam| yadi punaḥ pakṣatāvacchedakāvacchedenaiva sādhyasiddhiruddeśyā tadaikadeśe sādhyasiddhāvapi siddhasādhanābhāvāt tadvārakaṃ viśeṣaṇamanupādeyam| itaraviśeṣaṇadvayaṃ tu tucche brahmaṇi ca bādhavāraṇāyādaraṇīyameva| pratyekaṃ vā vipratipattiḥ -- viyanmithyā na vā ? pṛthivī mithyā na veti| evaṃ viyadādeḥ pratyekaṃ pakṣatve'pi na ghaṭādau sandigdhānaikāntikatā| pakṣasamatvād ghaṭādeḥ| tathā hi -- pakṣe sādhyābhāvasandehasyānuguṇatvāt pakṣabhinna eva tasya dūṣaṇatvaṃ vācyam| ata evoktaṃ `sādhyābhāvaniścayavati hetusandehe eva sandigdhānaikāntikate' ti| pakṣatvaṃ tu sādhyasandehavattvam| sādhyagocarasādhakamānābhāvavattvaṃ vā| etacca ghaṭādisādhāraṇam| ata eva tatrāpi sandigdhānaikāntikatvaṃ na doṣaḥ| pakṣasamatvoktistu pratijñāviṣayatvābhāvamātreṇa| na ca tarhi pratijñāviṣayatvameva pakṣatvam| svārthānumāne tadabhāvāt|
evaṃ vipratipattau prācāṃ prayogāḥ -- vimataṃ mithyā dṛśyatvāt, jaḍatvāt, paricchinnatvāt, śuktirūpyavaditi| nāvayaveṣvāgrahaḥ| atra svaniyāmakaniyatayā vipratipattyā laghubhūtayā pakṣatāvacchedo na viruddhaḥ| samayabandhādinā vyavadhānāt| tasya anumānakālāsattve'pi upalakṣaṇatayā pakṣatāvacchedakatvam|
yadvā vipratipattiviṣayatāvacchedakameva pakṣatāvacchedakam| prācāṃ prayogeṣvapi vimatamiti padaṃ vipratipattiviṣayatāvacchedakāvacchinnābhiprāyeṇetyadoṣaḥ|
nanu kimidaṃ mithyātvaṃ sādhyate ? na tāvat `mithyāśabdo'nirvacanīyatāvacanaḥ' iti pañcapādikāvacanāt sadasattvānadhikaraṇatvarūpamanirvācyatvam| taddhi kim asattvaviśiṣṭa sattvābhāvaḥ ? uta sattvātyantābhāvāsattvātyantābhāvarūpaṃ dharmadvayam ? āhosvit sattvātya ntābhāvavattve sati asattvātyantābhāvarūpaṃ viśiṣṭam ? nādyaḥ| sattvaḥ mātrādhāre jagatyasattvaviśiṣṭasattvānabhyupagamāt| viśiṣṭābhāvasādhane siddhasādhanāt| na dvitīyaḥ| sattvāsattvayorekābhāve aparasattvāvaśyakatvena vyāghātāt| nirdharmakabrahmavatsattvarāhitye'pi sadrūpatvena amithyātvopapattyā arthāntarācca| śuktirūpye abādhyatvarūpasattvavyatirekasya sattvena bādhyatvarūpāsattvasya vyatirekāsiddhyā sādhyavaikalyācca| ata eva na tṛtīyaḥ| pūrvavad vyāghātāt, arthāntarātsādhyavaikalyācceti cet, maivam| sattvātyantābhāvāsattvātyantābhāvarūpa dharmadvayavivakṣāyāṃ doṣābhāvāt| na ca vyāhatiḥ| sā hi sattvāsattvayoḥ parasparaviraharūpatayā vā ? parasparavirahavyāpakatayā vā ? parasparavirahavyāpyatayā vā ? tatra nādyaḥ| tadanaṅgīkārāt| tathā hyatra trikālābādhyatvarūpasattvavyatireko nāsattvam| kiṃ tu kvacidapyupādhau sattvena pratīyamānatvānadhikaraṇatvam| tadvyatirekaśca sādhyatvena vivakṣitaḥ| tathā ca trikālābādhyavilakṣaṇatve sati kvacidapyupādhau sattvena pratīyamānatvarūpaṃ sādhyaṃ paryavasitam| evaṃ ca sati na śuktirūpye sādhyavaikalyamapi| bādhyatvarūpāsattvavyatirekasya sādhyāpraveśāt| nāpi vyāghātaḥ, parasparaviraharūpatvābhāvāt| ata eva na dvitīyo'pi| sattvābhāvavati śuktirūpye vivakṣitāsattvavyatirekasya vidyamānatvena vyabhicārāt| nāpi tṛtīyaḥ| tasya vyāghātāprayojakatvāt| gotvāśvatvayoḥ parasparavirahavyāpyatve'pi tadabhāvayoruṣṭrādāvekatra sahopalambhād| yacca nirdharmakasya brahmaṇaḥ sattvarāhitye'pi sadrūpavatprapañcasya sadrūpatvenāmithyātvopapattyā arthāntaramuktam tanna| ekenaiva sarvānugatena sarvatra satpratītyupapattau brahmavat pratyekaṃ prapañcasya satsvabhāvatākalpane mānābhāvāt| anugatavyavahārābhāvaprasaṅgācca| satpratiyogikāsatpratiyogikabhedadvayaṃ vā sādhyam| tathā cobhayātmakatve'nyatarātmakatve vā tādṛgbhedāsambhavena tābhyāmarthāntarānavakāśaḥ|
na ca asattvavyatirekāṃśasyāsadbhedasya ca prapañce siddhatvenāṃśataḥ siddhasādhanamiti vācyam| `guṇādikaṃ guṇyādinā bhinnābhinnaṃ samānādhikṛtatvāt' iti bhedābhedavādiprayoge tārkikādyaṅgīkṛtasya bhinnatvasya siddhāvapi uddeśyapratītyasiddheryathā na siddhasādhanam tathā prakṛte'pi militapratīteruddeśyatvānna siddhasādhanam| yathā tattvābhede ghaṭaḥ kumbha iti sāmānādhikaraṇyapratīteradarśanena militasiddhiruddeśyā tathā prakṛte'pi sattvarahite tucche dṛśyatvādarśanena militasya tatprayojakatayā militasiddhiruddeśyeti samānam|
ata eva sattvātyantābhāvavattve satyasattvātyantābhāvarūpaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ sādhyamityapi sādhu| na ca militasya viśiṣṭasya vā sādhyatve tasya kutrāpyaprasiddhyā aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam| pratyekaṃ prasiddhyā militasya viśiṣṭasya vā sādhane śaśaśrṛṅgayoḥ pratyekaṃ prasiddhyā śaśīyaśrṛṅgasādhanamapi syāditi vācyam| tathāvidhaprasiddheḥ śuktirūpya evoktatvāt| na ca nirdharmakatvād brahmaṇaḥ sattvāsattvarūpadharmadvayaśūnyatvena tatrātivyāptiḥ| sadrūpatvena brahmaṇaḥ tadatyantābhāvānadhikaraṇatvāt| nirdharmakatvenaivābhāvarūpadharmānadhikaraṇatvācceti dik| iti sadasadvilakṣaṇatvarūpamithyātvavicāraḥ||1||
pratipannopādhau traikālikaniṣedhapratiyogitvaṃ vā mithyātvam| nanu pratipannopādhau traikālikaniṣedhasya tāttvikatve advaitahāniḥ, pratibhāsikatve siddhasādhanam, vyāvahārikatve'pi tasya bādhyatvena tāttvikasattvāvirodhitayā arthāntaram, advaitaśruteratattvāvedakatvaṃ ca, tatpratiyogino'prātibhāsikasya prapañcasya pāramārthikatvaṃ ca syāditi cenna| prapañcaniṣedhādhikaraṇībhūtabrahmābhinnatvānniṣedhasya tāttvikatve'pi nādvaitahānikaratvam| na ca tātvikābhāvapratiyoginaḥ prapañcasya tāttvikatvāpattiḥ| tāttvikābhāvapratiyogini śuktirajatādau kalpite vyabhicārāt| atāttvika eva vā niṣedho'yam| atāttvikatve'pi na prātibhāsikaḥ, kiṃ tu vyāvahārikaḥ| na ca tarhi niṣedhasya bādhyatvena tāttvikasattvāvirodhitvādarthāntaramiti vācyam| svāpnārthasya svāpyaniṣedhena bādhadarśanāt| niṣedhasya bādhyatvaṃ pāramārthikasattvāvirodhitve na tantram| kiṃ tu niṣedhyāpekṣayā nyūnasattākatvam| prakṛte ca tulyasattākatvāt kathaṃ na virodhitvam ? na ca niṣedhasya niṣedhe pratiyogisattvāpattiriti vācyam| tatra hi niṣedhasya niṣedhe pratiyogisattvamāyati yatra niṣedhasya niṣedhabuddhyā pratiyogisattvaṃ vyavasthāpyate| na niṣedhamātraṃ niṣidhyate| yathā rajate nedaṃ rajatamiti jñānānantaram idaṃ nārajatamiti jñānena rajataṃ vyavasthāpyate| yatra tu pratiyoginiṣedhayorubhayorapi niṣedhastatra na pratiyogisattvam| yathā dhvaṃsasamaye prāgabhāvapratiyoginorubhayorniṣedhaḥ| evaṃca prakṛte'pi niṣedhabādhakena pratiyoginaḥ prapañcasya tanniṣedhasya ca bādhanānna niṣedhasya bādhyatve'pi prapañcasya tāttvikatvam| ubhayorapi niṣedhyatāvacchedakasya dṛśyatvādestulyatvāt| na cātāttvikaniṣedhabodhakatve śruteraprāmāṇyāpattiḥ| brahmabhinnaṃ prapañcaniṣedhādikam atātvikamityatātvikatvena bodhayantyāḥ śruteraprāmāṇyāsambhavāt|
nanu tanniṣedhapratiyogitvaṃ kiṃ svarūpeṇa, utāsadvilakṣaṇasvarūpānupamardena pāramārthikatvākāreṇa vā| nādyaḥ, śrutyādisiddhotpattikasyārthakriyāsamarthasyāvidyopādānakasya tattvajñānanāśyasya ca viyadāde rūpyādeśca dhīkālavidyamānena asadvilakṣaṇasvarūpeṇa traikālikaniṣedhāyogāt| nāpi dvitīyaḥ; abādhyatvarūpapāramārthikatvasya bādhyatvarūpa mithyātvanirūpyatvena anyonyāśrayāt| pāramārthikatvasyāpi svarūpeṇa niṣedhe prathamapakṣoktadoṣāpattiḥ, atastasyāpi pāramārthikatvākāreṇa niṣedhe anavasthā syād iti cenmaivam| svarūpeṇaiva traikālikaniṣedhapratiyogitvasya prapañce śuktirūpye cāṅgīkārāt| tathā hi -- śuktau rajatabhramanantaram adhiṣṭhānatattvasākṣātkāre rūpyaṃ nāsti nāsīnna bhaviṣyatīti svarūpeṇeva, `neha nāne ' ti śrutyā ca prapañcasya svarūpeṇaiva niṣedhapratīteḥ| na ca tatra laukikaparamārtharajatameva svarūpeṇa niṣedhapratiyogīti vācyam| bhramabādhayorvaiyadhikaraṇyāpatteḥ, aprasaktapratiṣedhāpatteśca| na ca tarhyutpattyādyasambhavaḥ| na hyaniṣiddhasvarūpatvamutpattyādimattve tantram| parairaniṣedhyarūpatvenāṅgīkṛtasya viyadāderutpattyādya naṅgīkārāt| kintu vastusvabhāvādikamanyadeva kiñcit prayojakaṃ vaktavyam| tasya mayāpi kalpitasya svīkārāt|
na ca traikālikaniṣedhaṃ prati svarūpeṇāpaṇasthaṃ rūpyaṃ pāramārthikatvākāreṇa prātibhāsikaṃ vā pratiyogīti matahāniḥ syāditi vācyam| asyācāryavacasaḥ pāramārthika laukikarajatatādātmyena pratītaṃ prātibhāsikameva rajataṃ pratiyogītyarthaḥ| tacca svarūpeṇa pāramārthikatvena vetyanāsthāyāṃ vāśabdaḥ| etāvaduktiśca purovartitādātmyenaiva rajataṃ pratīyata iti matanirāsārthaṃ, laukikaparamārtharajatatādātmyenāpi pratīyata iti pratipādayituṃ ca| taduktaṃ tattvapradīpikāyām -- `tasmāllaukikaparamārtharajatameva nedaṃ rajatamiti niṣedhapratiyogīti pūrvācāryāṇāṃ vācoyuktirapi purovartini rajatārthinaḥ pravṛttidarśanāt laukika paramārtharajatatvenāparokṣatayā pratītasya kālatraye'pi laukikaparamārtharajatamidaṃ na bhavatīti niṣedhapratiyogitāmaṅgīkṛtya netavye ' ti| ayamāśayaḥ -- ekavibhaktyantapadopasthāpite dharmiṇi, pratiyogini ca naño'nyonyābhāvabodhakatvaniyamasya vyutpattibalasiddhatvād `ghaṭaḥ paṭo na bhavatīti' ti vākyavad `idaṃ rajataṃ na bhavatī' ti vākyasya anyo'nyābhāvabodhakatve sthite abhilāpajanyapratītitulyatvādabhilapyamānapratīteḥ `nedaṃ rajata' miti vākyābhilāpya pratīteranyonyābhāvaviṣayatvameva| tathā ca idaṃśabdanirdiṣṭe purovartiprātītikarajate rajataśabdanirdiṣṭa vyāvahārikarajatānyonyābhāvapratīterārthikaṃ mithyātvam| `nātra rajata' miti vākyābhilapyā tu pratītiratyantābhāvaviṣayā| bhinnavibhaktyantapadopasthāpitayoreva dharmipratiyoginornañaḥ saṃsargābhāvabodhakatvaniyamāt| sā ca purovartipratītarajatasyaiva vyāvahārikamatyantābhāvaṃ viṣayīkarotīti kaṇṭhoktameva mithyātvam| ato nāpasiddhānto nānyathākhyātyāpattirna vā granthavirodha ityanavadyam|
nanvevam atyantāsattvāpātaḥ, pratipannopādhau traikālikaniṣedhapratiyogitvaṃ hyanyatrāsattvena sampratipannasya ghaṭādeḥ sarvatra traikālikaniṣedhapratiyogitvaṃ paryavasitam| anyathā teṣāmanyatra sattvāpātāt| na hi teṣāmanyatra sattā sambhavatīti tvadukteśca| tathā ca kathamasadvailakṣaṇyam, na hi śaśaśrṛṅgāderito'nyadasattvam| na ca nirupākhyatvameva tadasattvam; nirupākhyapadenaiva khyāyamānatvāt| nāpyapratīyamānatvamasattvam;asato'pratītau asadvailakṣaṇyajñānasya satpratītinirāsasyāsatpadaprayogasya cāyogāt| na cāparokṣatayā apratīyamānatvaṃ tat, nityātīndriyeṣvativyāpteḥ iti cenmaivam; sarvatra traikālikaniṣedhapratiyogitvaṃ yadyapi tucchānirvācyayoḥ sādhāraṇam| tathāpi kvacidapyupādhau sattvena pratītyanarhatvamatyantāsattvam| tacca śuktirūpye prapañce ca bādhāt pūrvaṃ nāstyeveti na tucchatvāpattiḥ| na ca bādhāt pūrvaṃ śuktirūpyaṃ prapañco vā sattvena na pratīyate| etadeva sadarthakenopādhipadena sūcitam| śūnyavādibhiḥ sadadhiṣṭhānakabhramānaṅgīkāreṇa kvacidapyupādhau sattvena pratītyanarhatvarūpāsadvailakṣaṇyasya (kvacidapyupādhau sattvena pratītyarhatvarūpasya) śuktirūpye prapañce cānaṅgīkārāt|
nanvevaṃ sati yāvatsadadhikaraṇakātyantābhāvapratiyogitvaṃ paryavasitam| tathā ca kevalānvayyatyantābhāvapratiyogiṣu gaganādiṣu tārkikāṇāṃ siddhasādhanam| yadadhikaraṇaṃ yatsat tanniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvaṃ tasya mithyātvamiti vivakṣayām, adhikaraṇa padenāvṛttinirākaraṇe'pi saṃyogasambandhena samavāyasambandhena vā yad ghaṭādhikaraṇaṃ samavāyasambandhena saṃyogasambandhena vā ghaṭasya tanniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitayā sarveṣu vṛttimatsu duruddharaṃ siddhasādhanam| yena sambandhena yadyasyādhikaraṇaṃ tena sambandhena tanniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvamiti vivakṣāyām avyāpyavṛttiṣu saṃyogādiṣu siddhasādhanam iti cenna| yena rūpeṇa yadadhikaraṇatayā yat pratipannaṃ tena rūpeṇa tanniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvasya pratipannapadena sūcitatbāt| tacca rūpaṃ sambandhaviśeṣo'vacchedakaviśeṣaśca| na hi sambandhaviśeṣamantareṇa bhūtale ghaṭādhikaraṇatā pratīyate, avacchedakaviśeṣamantareṇa vā vṛkṣe kapisaṃyogādhikaraṇatā| tathā ca yena sambandhaviśeṣeṇa yena cāvacchedakaviśeṣeṇa yadadhikaraṇatāpratītiryatra bhavitumarhati, tenaiva sambandhaviśeṣeṇa tenaiva cāvacchedakaviśeṣeṇa tadadhikaraṇakātyantābhāvapratiyogitvaṃ tasya mithyātvamiti paryavasite kva siddhasādhanam ?
yadi punaḥ dhvaṃsaprāgabhāvapratiyogitvamivātyantābhāvapratiyogitvamākāśādau na syāt| sādhakamānābhāvasya tulyatvād| ihākāśo nāstīti pratyakṣapratītyasambhavād| anumāne cānukūlatarkābhāvāt| sāmānyato dṛṣṭamātreṇa dhvaṃsaprāgabhāvapratiyogitvasyāpi siddhiprasaṅgāt| tadvyatirekeṇa kasyacit kāryasyānupapatterabhāvācca| evaṃ saṃyogasambandhena ghaṭavati bhūtale samavāyasambandhena ghaṭābhāvasattve mānābhāvāllāghavena ghaṭātyantābhāvatvenaiva ghaṭasāmānādhikaraṇya virodhitvakalpanāt sambandhaviśeṣapraveśe ca gauravād ghaṭasamavāyādyabhāvamātraviṣayakatayā pratīterupapatteḥ, ādhārādheyabhāvasya pratyakṣasiddhatvena ghaṭasyāvṛttitvaśaṅkānudayād, uktayukteśca na ghaṭāderatyantābhāvasāmānādhikaraṇyam| evaṃ saṃyogatadabhāvayornaikādhikaraṇyam| agre vṛkṣaḥ kapisaṃyogī mūle ne 'ti pratīteragramūlayoreva saṃyogatadabhāvavattayopapatteḥ, tadā sanmātraniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvameva mithyātvaṃ mantavyam|
na caivaṃ sati bhāvābhāvayoravirodhāttajjñānayorbādhyabādhakabhāvo na syāditi vācyam| bhinnasattākayoravirodhe'pi samasattākayorvirodhāt| yatra bhūtale yasya ghaṭasyātyantābhāvo vyāvahārikaḥ tatra sa ghaṭo na vyāvahārika iti niyamāt| na caivaṃ sati `śuktiriyaṃ na rajata' miti jñānaviṣayībhūtābhāvasya vyāvahārikatvena purovartipratītarajatasya vyāvahārikatvāpahāre'pi prātītikasattvānapahārād bādhottarakāle'pi `idaṃ rajata' miti pratītiḥ syāditi vācyam| tatra `iyaṃ śuktiḥ' rityaparokṣapramayā prātītikarajatopādānājñānanivṛttau prātītikasattvasyāpyapahārāt| śuktyajñānasya prātītikarajatopādānatvena tadasattve prātītikarajatāsattvasyāvaśyakatvāt| ata eva yatra parokṣayā'dhiṣṭhānapramayā na bhramopādānājñānanivṛttiḥ,tatra vyāvahārikatvāpahāre'pi prātītikatvānapahārāt `tikto guḍaḥ' ityādipratītiranuvartata eva| ekamakhaṇḍabrahmasākṣātkārātpūrvaṃ parokṣabodhena prapañcasya vyāvahārikatvāpahāre'pi pratītiranuvartata eva| adhiṣṭhānājñānanivṛttau tu nānuvartiṣyate|
etena upādhiśabdenādhikaraṇamātravivakṣāyāmarthāntaram| vāyvadhikaraṇakātyantā bhāvapratiyogitve'pi rūpasyāmithyātvād| adhiṣṭhānavivakṣāyāṃ tu bhramopādānājñāna viṣayasyādhiṣṭhānatvenānyonyāśrayatvam| jñānasya bhramatve viṣayasya mithyātvam, viṣayasya mithyātve ca jñānasya bhramatvamiti parāstam; uktarītyā adhikaraṇavivakṣāyāṃ doṣābhāvāt| na ca `sa evādhastād' iti śrutyā pratipanno deśakālādyupādhau paramārthato brahmaṇo'bhāvāttatrātivyāptiriti vācyam| nirdharmake tasminnabhāvapratiyogitvarūpadharmābhāvāt| na caivaṃ -- satyatvamapi tatra na syāt, tathā ca `satyaṃ jñānamananta' mityādiśrutivyākopa iti vācyam| adhikaraṇātiriktābhāvānabhyupagamenoktamithyātvābhāvarūpasatyatvasya brahma svarūpatvāvirodhāt| etena svaprakāśatvādyapi vyākhyātam| paraprakāśyatvābhāvo hi svaprakāśatvam, kālaparicchedābhāvo nityatvam, deśaparīcchedābhāvo vibhutvam, vastuparicchedābhāvaḥ pūrṇatvamityādi| tathā ca bhāvarūpadharmānāśrayatve'pi brahmaṇaḥ sarvadharmābhāvarūpatayā na kā'pyanupapattiriti sarvamavadātam|
iti sadasadvilakṣaṇatvarūpadvitīyamithyātvavicāraḥ||
jñānanivartyatvaṃ vā mithyātvam| nanu -- uttarajñānanivartye pūrvajñāne ativyāptiḥ, mudgarapātādinivartye ca ghaṭādāvavyāptiḥ, jñānatvena jñānanivartyatvavivakṣāyāmapyayaṃ doṣaḥ, adhiṣṭhānasākṣātkāratvena nivartye śuktirajatādau ca jñānatvena jñānanivartyatvābhāvāt sādhyavikalatā| jñānatvavyāpyadharmeṇa jñānanivartyatvavikṣāyāṃ jñānatvavyāpyena smṛtitvena jñānanivartye saṃskāre ativyāptiriti cenna, jñānaprayuktāvasthitisāmānyavirahapratiyogitvaṃ hi jñānanivartyatvam| avasthitiśca dvedhā svarūpeṇa kāraṇātmanā ca; satkāryavādābhyupagamāt| tathā ca mudgarapātena ghaṭasya svarūpeṇāvasthitivirahe'pi kāraṇātmanāvasthitivirahābhāvād, brahmajñānaprayukta eva sa iti nātītaghaṭādāvavyāptiḥ| ata evottarajñānanivartye pūrvajñāne na siddhasādhanam; na vā viyadādau brahmajñānanāśyatve'pi tadvadeva mithyātvāsiddhyā'rthāntaram; uttarajñānena līnasya pūrvajñānasya svakāraṇātmanāvasthānādavasthitisāmānyavirahānupapatteḥ| śaśaviṣāṇādāvavasthitisāmānyavirahe'pi tasya jñānaprayuktatvābhāvānnātivyāptiḥ| śuktirajatā deścāparokṣapratītyanyathānupapattyā pratibhāsakāle avasthityaṅgīkārānna bādhakajñānaṃ vinā tadviraha iti na sādhyavikalatā| ata evoktaṃ vivaraṇācāryaiḥ -- ajñānasya svakāryeṇa pravilīnena vartamānena vā saha jñānena nivṛttirbādha'iti| vārtikakṛdbhiścoktam -- 
`tattvamasyādivākyotthasamyagdhījanmamātrataḥ|
avidyā saha kāryeṇa nāsīdasti bhaviṣyati|| iti|
`saha kāryeṇa nāsī' diti līnena kāryeṇa saha nivṛttyabhiprāyam, `saha kāryeṇa na bhaviṣyatī' ti tu bhāvikāryanivṛttyabhiprāyamityanyadetat| rūpyopādānamajñānaṃ svakāryeṇa vartamānena līnena vā sahādhiṣṭhānasākṣātkārānnivartate| tattadbhramopādānānām ajñānānāṃ bhedābhyupagamāditi na dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyam, mudgarapātāntaraṃ ghaṭo nāstīti pratītivadadhiṣṭhānajñānānantaraṃ śuktyajñānaṃ tadgatarūpyaṃ ca nāstīti pratīteḥ sarvasaṃmatatvāt| jñānatvavyāpyadharmeṇa jñānanivartyatvamityapi sādhu| uttarajñānasya pūrvajñānanivartakatvaṃ ca na jñānatvavyāpyadharmeṇa ? kiṃtvicchādisādhāraṇenodīcyātmaviśeṣaguṇatvena udīcyatvena veti na siddhasādhanādi| nāpīcchādyanivartye smṛtitvena jñānanivartye saṃskāre ativyāptiḥ, smṛtitvena smṛteḥ saṃskāranivartakatve mānābhāvāt| smṛtau hi jātāyāṃ saṃskāro dṛḍho bhavatītyanubhavasiddham teṣāṃ dṛḍhatvaṃ ca samānaviṣayakasaṃskārānekatvamityadoṣaḥ| vastutastu sākṣātkāratvena jñānanivartyatvaṃ vivakṣitam; ato na pūrvektadoṣaḥ| nāpi niścayatvena jñānatvavyāpyadharmiṇa jñānanivartye saṃśaye ativyāptiriti sarvamavadātam|
iti tṛtīyamithyātvavicāraḥ
svāśrayaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvaṃ vā mithyātvam| tacca svātyantābhāvādhikaraṇa eva pratīyamānatvam, ataḥ pūrvavailakṣaṇyam| dūṣaṇaparihāraśca pūrvavat| na ca saṃyogini samavāyini vā deśe tadatyantābhāvāsambhavaḥ, sambhave tūpādānatvādyanupapattiriti vācyam, kāle sahasaṃbhavavad deśe'pi sahasambhavāvirodhāt, prāgabhāvasattvenopādānatvāvirodhācca| na ca atyantābhāvādhikaraṇe prāgabhāvasyāpyanupapattiriti vācyam| kāle vyabhicārāt| na ca kāle prāgabhāvatyantābhāvayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyam idānīṃ ghaṭātyantābhāvaḥ, idānīṃ ghaṭaprāgabhāva iti pratītibalādaṅgīkṛtam, deśe tu tadubhayasāmānādhikaraṇye na kiñcidapi pramāṇamiti vācyam, mityātvānumiteḥ śrutyādeśca pramāṇatvāt| viṣamasattāka bhāvābhavayoravirodhaḥ pūrvamupapāditaḥ| na ca asatyativyāptiḥ, svātyantābhāvādhikaraṇa eva sattvena pratīyamānatvasya vivakṣitatvāt| na ca `taddhaika āhurasadevedamagra āsī' diti śrutyā asataḥ sattvapratītestatrātivyāptirduṣparihareti vācyam, `sadevedamagra āsī' dityasyārthasyābhāva eva nañā pratipādyate, na tvasataḥ sattvam, virodhātuḥ ato nātivyāptiḥ| sarvaṃ cānyat pūrvoktamevānusandheyamityuparamyate||
iti caturthamithyātvavicāraḥ||
sadviviktatvaṃ vā mithyātvam| sattvaṃ ca pramāṇasiddhatvam| pramāṇatvaṃ ca doṣāsahakṛtajñānakaraṇatvam, tena svapnādivatpramāṇasiddhabhinnatvena mithyātvaṃ sidhyati| pramāṇasiddhatvaṃ cābādhyatvavyāpyamityanyat| atrāpyasati nirdharmake brahmaṇi cātivyāptivāraṇāya sattvena pratīyamānatvaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ deyam| tayoḥ sattvaprakārakapratītiviṣayatvābhāvāt| ata eva sadviviktatvamityatra sattvaṃ sattājātyadhikaraṇatvaṃ vā ? abādhyatvaṃ vā ? brahmarūpatvaṃ vā ? ādye ghaṭādāvāvidyakajātestvayābhyupagamenāsambhavaḥ, dvitīye bādhyatvarūpamithyātva paryavasānam, tṛtīye siddhasādhanamiti nirastam, anabhyupagamādeva| sadasadvilakṣaṇatvapakṣokta yuktayaścātrānusandheyāḥ| avaśiṣṭaṃ ca dṛṣṭāntasiddhau vakṣyāmaḥ||
ityadvaitasiddhau pañcamamithyātvaniruktiḥ||
nanu -- uktamithyātvasya mithyātve prapañcasatyatvāpātaḥ, ekasmin dharmiṇi prasaktayoḥ virudvadharmayorekamithyātve aparasatyatvaniyamāt, mithyātvasatyatve ca tadvadeva prapañcasatyatvāpatteḥ| ubhayathāpyadvaitavyāghāta iti cenna; mithyātvamithyātve'pi prapañcasatyatvānupapatteḥ| tatra hi viruddhayordharmayorekamithyātve aparasatyatvam‌, yatra mithyātvāvacchedakamubhayavṛtti na bhavet; yathā parasparaviraharūpayo rajatatvatadabhāvayoḥ śuktau, yathā vā parasparavirahavyāpakayo rajatabhinnatvarajatvayoḥ tatraiva; tatra niṣedhyatāvacchedakabhedaniyamāt| prakṛte tu niṣedhyatāvacchedakamekameva dṛśyatvādi| yathā gotvāśvatvayorekasmin gaje niṣedhe gajatvātyantābhāvavyāpyatvaṃ niṣedhyatācchedakamubhayostulyamiti naikataraniṣedhe anyatarasattvam, tadvat| yathā ca satyatvamithyātvayorna parasparaviraharūpatvam, na vā parasparavirahavyāpakatvam, tathopapāditamadhastāt| parasparaviraharūpatve'pi viṣamasattākayoravirodhāt, vyāvahārika mithyātvena vyāvahārikasatyatvāpahāre'pi kālpanikasatyatvānapahārāt, tārkikamatasiddha saṃyogatadabhāvavat satyatvamithyātvayoḥ samuccayābhyupagamācca| ekasya sādhakena aparasya bādhyatvaṃ viṣamasattākatve prayojakam,yathā śuktirūpyatadabhāvayoḥ| ekabādhakabādhyatvaṃ ca samasattākatve prayojakam, yathā śuktirūpyaśuktibhinnatvayoḥ asti ca prapañcatanmithyātva yorekabrahmajñānabādhyatvam| ataḥ samasattākatvānmithyātvabādhakena prapañcasyāpi bādhānnādvaitakṣati riti kṛtamadhikena|
iti mithyātvasāmānyopapattiḥ|
nanu -- mithyātve sādhye hetūkṛtaṃ yad dṛśyatvaṃ tadapyupapādanīyam| tathā hi -- kimidaṃ dṛśyatvam ? vṛttivyāpyatvaṃ vā ? phalavyāpyatvaṃ vā ? sādhāraṇaṃ vā ? kadācit kathañcit cidviṣayatvaṃ vā ? svavyavahāre svātiriktasaṃvidantarāpekṣāniyatirvā ?asvaprakāśatvaṃ vā ? nādyaḥ, ātmano vedāntajanyavṛttivyāpyatvena tatra vyabhicārāt| ata eva na tṛtīyo'pi| nāpi dvitīyaḥ nityātīndriye śuktirūpyādau ca tadabhāvena bhāgāsiddhi sādhanavaikalyayoḥ prasaṅgāt| nāpi caturthaḥ, brahma pūrvaṃ na jñātamidānīṃ vedāntena jñātamityanubhavena ātmani vyabhicārāt| nāpi pañcamaḥ, brahmaṇyapyadvitīyatvādiviśiṣṭavyavahāre saṃvidantarāpekṣāniyatidarśanena vyabhicārāt| nāpi ṣaṣṭhaḥ, sa hi avedyatve satyaparokṣavyavahārayogyatvābhāvarūpaḥ| tathā ca śuktirūpyāderapi aparokṣavyavahārayogyatvena sādhanavaikalyād -- iti cenmaivam, phalavyāpyatvavyatiriktasya sarvasyāpi pakṣasya kṣodakṣamatvāt| na ca vṛttivyāpyatvapakṣe brahmaṇi vyabhicāraḥ, anyathā brahmaparāṇāṃ vedāntānāṃ vaiyarthyaprasaṅgāditi vācyam, śuddhaṃ hi brahma na dṛśyam, `yattadadreśyam -- iti śruteḥ, kintūpahitameva, tacca mithyaiva, na hi vṛttidaśāyāṃ anupahitaṃ tadbhavati| na ca `sarvapratyayavedye vā brahmarūpe vyavasthite' iti svavacanavirodha iti vācyam, tasyāpyupahitaparatvāt|
na ca -- evaṃ sati śuddhasiddhirna syāditi -- vācyam, svata eva tasya prakāśatvena siddhatvāt| nanu -- ajñāte dharmiṇi kasyacit dharmasya vidhātuṃ niṣeddhuṃ vā aśakyatvena śuddhe dṛśyatvaṃ niṣedhatā śuddhasya jñeyatvamavaśyaṃ svīkaraṇīyam| na ca svaprakāśatvena svataḥ siddhe śuddhe śrutyā dṛśyatvaniṣedha iti vācyam| śuddhaṃ svaprakāśamiti śabdajanyaviśiṣṭavṛttau śuddhāprakāśe tasya svaprakāśatvāsiddheḥ iti -- cet, na| vṛttikāle vṛttirūpeṇa dharmeṇa śuddhatvāsambhavāt śuddhasya vṛttiviṣayatvaṃ na sambhavati, ataḥ `śuddhaṃ svaprakāśam -- iti vākyasya lakṣaṇayā aśuddhatvamasvaprakāśatvavyāpakamityarthaḥ| tathā ca aśuddhatvavyāvṛttyā śuddhe svaprakāśatā paryavasyati, yathā bhedaniṣedhena abhinnatvam| na ca śuddhapadena abhidhayā lakṣaṇayā vā śuddhāprakāśe tatprayogavaiyarthyamiti vācyam, paryavasitārthamādāya sārthakatvopapatteḥ| evaṃ ca `śuddhaṃ na dṛśyaṃ na mithyā' ityasyāpyaśuddhatvaṃ dṛśyatvamithyātvayorvyāpakamityetatparatvena śuddhe dṛśyatvamithyātvayorvyatirekaḥ paryavasyati| etena sphuraṇamātrameva mithyātve tantram, lāghavād; ataḥ `svataḥ sphuradapi brahma mithyaive' ti śūnyavādimatamapāstam; svataḥ sphuraṇarūpatāyāḥ śuktirūpyādāvabhāvāt, sphuraṇaviṣayatvasya brahmaṇyasiddheḥ|
nanu -- viśiṣṭajñāne viśeṣyasyāpi bhāne śrutyā viśiṣṭasya dṛśyatvenaiva viśeṣyasyāpi dṛśyatvād vyabhicāraḥ, na ca `viṣṇave śipiviṣṭāye' tyādau viśiṣṭasya devatātvavad viśiṣṭasya viṣayatvam, agnīṣomayormilitayordevatātvavadvā militasya viṣayatvam, ato na viśeṣye viṣayatvamiti vācyam, tadvadeva viśeṣaṇasyāpyaviṣayatve bhāgāsiddhiprasaṅgād iti cenna, viśeṣyatāpannasya viṣayatve'pi kṣatyabhāvāt, tasya mithyātvābhyupagamāt| ata eva upahitaviṣayatve'pyupadheyaviṣayatvamakṣatameva iti apāstam, upahitātmanā tasyāpi mithyātvābhyupagamāt, jñānāntaraviṣayatvena viśeṣaṇe bhāgāsiddhyabhāvācca| nanu vedāntajanyākhaṇḍavṛtterupahitaviṣayatve tadānīmupādhyantarābhāvena tasyā evopadhāyakatvāt svaviṣayatvāpattiḥ| na ceṣṭāpattiḥ| śābdabodhe śabdānupasthitābhānaniyamena vṛtteḥ śabdānupasthitāyā bhānānupapatteḥ| yathākathañcidupapattau vā na tato'jñānatatkāryayornivṛttiḥ syād| ajñānatatkāryāviṣayakajñānasyaiva tadubhayanivartakatvād| anyathā `ahamajñaḥ ayaṃ ghaṭa' ityādijñānānāmapyupahitaviṣayakatvena ajñānanivartakatvaprasaṅga iti cenna| vṛtteḥ śābdavṛttāvanavabhāsamānāyā evopadhāyakatvābhyupagamāt| taduktaṃ kalpatarukṛdbhiḥ `śuddhaṃ brahmeti viṣayīkurvāṇā vṛttiḥ svasvetaropādhinivṛttiheturudayate, svasyā api upādhitvāviśeṣāt| evaṃ ca nānupahitasya viṣayatā| vṛttyuparāgo'tra sattayopayujyate;na tu bhāsyatayā viṣayakoṭipraveśene'ayamabhiprāyaḥ -- yathā ajñānopahitasya sākṣitve'pi nājñānaṃ sākṣikoṭau praviśati, jaḍatvāt| kiṃ tu sākṣyakoṭāveva| evaṃ vṛttyupahitasya viṣayatve'pi na vṛttiviṣayakoṭau praviśati; svasyāḥ svaviṣayatvānupapatteḥ| kiṃ tu svayamaviṣayo'pi caitanyasya viṣayatāṃ sampādayatīti na kāpyanupapattiḥ| etena jñānājñānayorekaviṣayatvaṃ vyākhyātam; ajñānamapi hi svopadhānadaśāyāmeva brahma viṣayī karoti; svānupadhānadaśāyāṃ svasyaivābhāvāt| tathā ca jñānājñānayorubhayorapyupādhyaviṣayakatve satyupahitaviṣayakatvāt samānaviṣayatvamastyeva| etena upādhiviṣayajñānānāmajñānānivartakatvaṃ vyākhyātam; ajñānasyo pādhyaviṣayatvena samānaviṣayatvābhāvāt, samānaviṣayatvenaiva tayornivartyanivartakabhāvāt|
vastutastu -- śabdājanyavṛttiviṣayatvameva dṛśyatvam, anyathā śaśaviṣāṇaṃ tucchamityādiśabdajanyavṛttiviṣaye tucche vyabhicārasya duruddhatvāt| evaṃ ca sati śuddhasya vedāntajanyavṛttiviṣayatve'pi na tatra vyabhicāraḥ, tucchaśuddhayoḥ śabdājanyavṛttiviṣaya tvānabhyupagamāt| yadvā -- saprakārakavṛttiviṣayatvameva dṛśyatvam, prakāraśca sopākhyaḥ kaściddharmaḥ, tena niṣprakārakajñānaviṣayībhūte śuddhe nirupākhyadharmaprakārakajñānaviṣayībhūte tucche ca na vyabhicāraḥ| abhāvatvasyāpi sopākhyatvādabhāvatvaprakārakajñānaviṣayībhūte abhāve na bhāgāsiddhiḥ| upākhyā ca astīti dhīviṣayatvādityanyat| etena vṛttivyāpyaphalavyāpyayoḥ sādhāraṇaṃ vyavahāraprayojakaviṣayatvarūpaṃ dṛśyatvamapi hetuḥ,brahmaṇi tucche ca vyabhicāraparihāropāyasyoktatvāt| yadvā dṛśyatvaṃ cidviṣayatvam, tacca yathā kathañcit citsambandhitvarūpaṃ hetuḥ, tacca na caitanye abhede bhedanāntarīyakasya sambandhasyābhāvād, ato na vyabhicāraḥ| tucche ca vyabhicāraḥ pariharaṇīyaḥ| yadvā -- svavyavahāre svātiriktasaṃvidapekṣāniyatirūpaṃ dṛśyatvaṃ hetuḥ; saṃvicchabdena viṣayābhivyaktaṃ vā vṛttyābhivyaktaṃ vā śuddhaṃ vā caitanyamātramabhipretam, tathā ca ghaṭādau nityātīndriye sākṣibhāsye ca sarvo'pi vyavahāraḥ svātiriktasaṃvitsāpekṣa iti nāsiddhiḥ| vyavahāraśca sphuraṇābhi vadanādisādhāraṇaḥ| tatra brahmaṇaḥ sphuraṇarūpe vyavahāre nityasiddhe svātiriktasaṃvidapekṣā nāstīti niyatipadena vyabhicāravāraṇam| svagocarayāvadvyavahāre svātiriktasaṃvidapekṣāyāṃ paryavasānāt| ata evāsvaprakāśatvarūpaṃ dṛśyatvamapi hetuḥ; svaprakāśatvaṃ hi svāparokṣatve svātiriktānapekṣatvam, `yatsākṣādaparokṣād brahme' ti śruteḥ| tathā cānyānadhīnāparokṣatvaṃ paryavasitam; tannirūpitabhedavattvaṃ hetuḥ| tacca nityaparokṣe anyādhīnāparokṣe ca ghaṭādāvastīti nāsiddhiḥ| na ca -- brahmaṇo'pi brahmapratiyogikakālpanikabhedavattvāttatra vyabhicāraḥ, akalpita bhedasya kvāpyasiddhatvāditi vācyam, tadbhedasyānyānadhīnāparokṣatvarūpadharmānirūpitatvāt| jīvatveśvaratvādirūpasya anyasyaiva dharmasya tannirūpakatvāt| evaṃ cāvedyatve satyaparokṣavyavahārayogyatvābhāvarūpaṃ dṛśyatvamapi hetuḥ| na ca -- phalavyāpyatvābhāvaviśiṣṭaṃ yadaparokṣavyavahārayogyatvam, tasya brahmaṇīvāvidyāntaḥ karaṇādau śuktirūpyādau ca sattvena asiddhisādhanavaikalye iti vācyam; ajñānanivartakavṛttiviṣayatvayogyatvasyāparokṣa vyavahārayogyatvapadena vivakṣitatvāt, tasya cāvidyādau śuktirūpyādau cāsattvāt nāsiddhisādhanavaikalye| yathā ca ghaṭādeḥ phalavyāpyatvam, tathāgre vakṣyāmaḥ| avidyānivṛtteḥ pañcamaprakāratvapakṣe tatra vyabhicāravāraṇāya ajñānakālavṛttitvaṃ hetuviśeṣaṇaṃ deyam| te naiva tucchepi na vyabhicāraḥ| evameva sarveṣu hetuṣu vyabhicāraparihārāya yatanīyam| sadviviktatvamātre tu sādhye tucche pañcamaprakārāvidyānivṛttau ca na vyabhicāragandho'pīti sarvamavadātam|| iti advaitasiddhau dṛśyatvahetūpapattiḥ||
jaḍatvamapi hetuḥ| nanu -- kimidaṃ jaḍatvam ? ajñātṛtvaṃ vā ? ajñānatvaṃ vā ? anātmatvaṃ vā ? nādyaḥ; tvanmate pakṣanikṣiptasyaivāhamarthasya jñātṛtvāttatrāsiddheḥ, śuddhātmano'jñātṛtvena tatra vyabhicārācca| nāpi dvitīyaḥ,vṛttyuparaktacaitanyasyaiva jñānatvena kevalāyā vṛtteḥ kevalasya caitanyasya cājñānatvena vṛttāvasiddhiparihāre'pi caitanye vyabhicāratādavasthyāt| nāpi tṛtīyaḥ, ātmatvasyaiva nirūpayitumaśakyatvāt| taddhi na jātiviśeṣaḥ, tvayātmana ekātvābhyupagamād, viśiṣṭaśatmanāṃ bhede'pi teṣāṃ pakṣakukṣinikṣiptatvāt| nāpyānandarūpatvam, vaiṣayikānande tadvyatirekasya hetorasiddheḥ, tasyāpyātmatve ajñānapakṣoktadoṣaḥ prasañjanīya iti -- cet, maivam dvitīyatṛtīyapakṣayoḥ doṣābhāvāt| tathā hi `ajñānatvaṃ jaḍatvami' ti pakṣe nātmani vyabhicāraḥ, arthopalakṣitaprakāśasyaiva jñānatvena mokṣadaśāyāmapi tadanapāyāt| na ca abhāve sapratiyogitkavavadicchājñānādiṣvapi saviṣayakatvasya svābhāvikatvādicchāyāmiva jñāne'pi tasya samānasattākatvamiti vācyam; jñānasya hi saviṣayatvaṃ viṣayasambandhaḥ, sa ca na tāttvikaḥ, kintvādhyāsikaḥ, vakṣyamāṇarītyā tāttvikasambandhasya nirūpayitumaśakyatvāt, ato na tasya svābhāvikatvam; na hi śuktau rūpyaṃ svābhāvikam| evaṃ ca jñānopādhikasyaiva saviṣayatvasya icchādiṣvabhyupagamāt natarāṃ tatra tasya svābhāvikatvam| na caivaṃ jñānavad viṣayasambandhaṃ vināpi kadācidicchāyāḥ sattvāpattiriti -- vācyam; saviṣayatvaprayojakopādhyapekṣayā adhikasattākatvasya tatra prayojakatvād, icchāyāśca tatsamānasattākatvāt| na ca tvayā mokṣāvasthāyāmātmano nirviṣayatvāṅgīkārād ānandāprakāśe tadapumarthatvaṃ syāditi vācyam; tadā hyānanda eva prakāśo na tvānandasya prakāśatvam, arthopalakṣitaprakāśatvaṃ vā tadāstyeveti na jñānatvahānirityuktam| nanu -- tathāpi jñāturabhāvāt tadā tanna jñānam; na hi bhoktṛhīnā bhujikriyā bhavati| na ca -- anāditvena kriyārūpatvābhāvād anapekṣatvamiti vācyam;anādeḥ prāgabhāvasya pratiyogini, jātervyaktau, jīvabrahma vibhāgasya dharmipratiyoginoḥ,ajñānasya cāśrayaviṣayayoḥ , brahmasattāyāśca kartaryapekṣādarśanāt, anyathā `asti brahme' tyādau kartari lakāro na syāt| evaṃ cātītādijñānasya īśvarajñānasya ca utpattyarthamarthānapekṣatve'pi tannirūpyatvadarśanena jñānasya jñātṛjñeyanirūpyatvaṃ svabhāvaḥ,anyathā `idamahaṃ jānāmī' tyanubhavo na syāt, `jñāturarthaprakāśasya jñānatvā' diti vivaraṇavirodhaśca syād -- iti cennaḥ jātervyaktinirūpya tve'pi kadācittadasambandhavadupapatteḥ, sambandhaprayojakopādhyapekṣayā adhikasattākatvāt| ata eva jñānasya sajñeyatvaṃ sajñātṛtvaṃ ca na svābhāvikam| tathā hi -- sajñeyatvaṃ tāvat jñeyajanyatvaṃ vā ? jñeyavyāpyatvaṃ vā ? nādyaḥ, parokṣajñāne cābhāvāt| nāpi dvitīyaḥ, `yadā jñānaṃ tadā artha' iti kālikavyāptau pūrvavad vyabhicārād, daiśikavyāptistu dūranirastaiva, jñātaritadabhāvāt| na ca yadā `aparokṣajñānaṃ tadārtha' iti kālikavyāptau nāsti vyabhicāraḥ, ātmā ca `yad sākṣād aparokṣād brahme' ti śruteraparokṣajñānarūpa iti so'pyarthavyāpta iti -- vācyam; īśvarajñāne yogijñāne ca vyabhicārāt| `yadaindriyakaṃ jñānaṃ tadārtha' iti tuvyāptiḥ sarvasaṃmatā| na cātmarūpe jñāne aindriyakatvam astīti na tayā virodhaḥ| nanu -- `yadā aparokṣaṃ jñānaṃ tadārtha' iti vyāptyanabhyupagame `idaṃ rajata' mityaparokṣajñānānyathānupapattyā anirvacanīya rajatasiddhirnasyāt, arthaṃ vināpyaparokṣatvopapatteḥ -- iti cenna, `idaṃ rajatamahaṃ jānāmī' tyanusandhīyamānaṃ yat jñānaviṣayatvaṃ tasyāśrayāntarānupapattyā anirvacanīyarajata siddhervakṣyamāṇatvāt| ata eva parokṣabhrame'pi anirvacanīyārthasiddhiḥ| janyāparokṣatvena vā arthavyāpyatā, ārṣajñānasyāparokṣatvānabhyupagamāt| tathā ca nānirvacanīyarajata siddhyunupapattiḥ| evaṃ sajñātṛkatvamapi kiṃ jñātṛjanyatvam ? jñātṛvyāpyatvam ? jñātṛsamavetatvaṃ vā ? ādye īśvarajñāne vyabhicāraḥ, jñānanityatvasya sādhayiṣyamāṇatvācca| dvitīye'pi aprayojakatā| na tṛtīyaḥ, jñānajanyatvavat jñānasamavetatvasyāpi sambhavāt, jñānasya guṇatvakriyātvayoranabhyupagamena dravyāśrayatvānumānāyogāt, kadācit jñātṛjñeyasambandhenaiva anubhavasya vivaraṇavākyasya ca upapatteḥ| `asti brahme ' ti ca lakāro na brahmasattāṃ prati brahmaṇaḥ kartṛtvamāha, nityatvena tadasambhavāt, kintu sādhutvārtha iti draṣṭavyam| nanu -- pramābhakramabhinnaṃ na jñānam, na cātmasvarūpaṃ jñānaṃ pramā, tadviṣayasyāvidyā destāttvikatvāpātāt| na ca apramā,doṣajanyatvāpād -- iti cenna; tārkikasiddheśvarajñānavad ghaṭādinirvikalpakavacca svabhāvata ubhayavailakṣaṇyenāpyupapatteḥ, tatrāpi īśvarajñānasya pramātve guṇajanyatvasya, bhramatve doṣajanyatvasya cāpatteḥ, niṣprakārake ca nirvikalpake tadvati tatprakārakatvasya tadabhāvavati tatprakārakatvasya cānupapatteḥ, janyasavikalpa katvena bhramapramānyataratvaniyame cāsmākaṃ kṣatyabhāvād,vilakṣaṇavṛttidvayoparāgeṇa ca svabhāvato bhramapramāvilakṣaṇasyāpyātmajñānasya tadubhayarūpeṇa vyavahāropapatteḥ| na ca -- jñānapadapācya bhinnatvavivakṣāyām upādherapi jñānapadavācyatvāttatrāsiddhiḥ, jñānapadalakṣyabhinnatvavivakṣāyāṃ tu ghaṭāderapi jñānapadalakṣyatvāttatrāpyasiddhiriti -- vācyam; jñānapadajanyapratītiviśeṣyabhinnatvaṃ vivakṣāyāmuktadoṣābhāvāt| evamānandabhinnatvarūpamanātmatvaṃ mupapādyam, vaiṣayikānandasyāpi brahmarūpatvāt, tadupādhimātrasyaivotpattivināśapratiyogitvāt| na ca -- jñānabhinnatvasyā nandabhinnatvasya ca kālpanikasya brahmaṇi sattvāt, tatra vyabhicāra iti -- vācyam; dharmisamānasattākatadbhedasya hetutvāt| anaupādhikatvena vā bhedo viśeṣaṇīyaḥ, tucche pañcamaprakārāvidyānivṛttau ca vyabhicāraparihāraḥ pūrvavat| evam -- asvaprakāśatvaṃ vā jaḍatvam, tacca pūrvamevopapāditamiti śivam|| ityadvaitasiddhau jaḍatvahetūpapattiḥ||
paricchinnatvamapi hetuḥ| tacca deśataḥ kālato vastutaśceti trividham| tatra deśataḥ piracchinnatvam -- atyantābhāvapratiyogitvam| kālataḥ paricchinnatvam -- dhvaṃsapratiyogitvam| vastutaḥ paricchinnatvam -- anyo'nyābhāvapratiyogitvam| nanu samavāyasambandhenātyantābhāvapratiyogitvam ātmani vyabhicāri| tasyāpyākāśādivat kvāpyasamavetatvāt| saṃyogasambandhenātyantābhāvapratiyogitvamākāśādāvasiddham| tasya yāvanmūrtasaṃyogitvaniyamāt| amūrtaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvābhiprāye tu ātmani vyabhicārastadavasthaḥ| sarvasambandhitvābhāva vivakṣāyāmapi sarvasambandhaśūnye paramātmani vyabhicāraḥ| ajñāne sarvasambandhinyasiddhiśca| dhvaṃsapratiyogitvamapi ākāśādāvasiddham| teṣāṃ parairnityatvābhyupagamāt| anyonyā bhāvapratiyogitvaṃ cātmani vyabhicāri| tasya jaḍaniṣṭhānyonyābhāvapratiyogitvād, anyathā jaḍatvāpatteḥ iti cenna| atyantābhāve anyonyābhāve ca pratiyogisamasattākatvaviśeṣaṇena ātmani vyabhicāraparihārāt| ajñānākāśādau ca svasamānasattākātyantā bhāvānyonyā bhāvapratiyogitvasattvena asiddhyabhāvāt| avidyākāśādervyāvahārikasya pāramārthikābhāvapakṣe `svānyūnasattāke'ti viśeṣaṇaṃ deyam| ata eva prātibhāsikaśuktirūpyādervyā vahārikābhāvapratiyogitve'pi na sādhanavaikalyam| niruktamithyātvaprakārāṇāmevaṃrūpatvābhāvāt na sādhyāviśiṣṭatā| dhvaṃsapratiyogitvaṃ cākāśādau nāsiddham| `tasmādvā etasmādātmana ākāśaḥ sambhūtaḥ' iti śrutisiddhajanyatvenānumitatvāt| `ākāśavatsarvagataśca nitya'ityatra cātmanidarśanatvaṃ svasamānakālīnasarvagatatvena, ābhūtasamplavāvasthāyitvena ceti draṣṭavyam| `ato'nyadārta'mitiśrutyā anātmamātrasyaiva vināśitvapratipādanāt|
ata eva ghaṭādayaḥ svānugatapratibhāse vastuni kalpitāḥ, vibhaktatvād, yathā sarpamālādikaṃ svānugatapratibhāse rajjvā idamaṃśe vibhajyate, `evaṃ brahmaṇyanugacchati ghaṭādikaṃ vibhajyate, san ghaṭaḥ, san paṭa'iti ānandabodhoktamapi sādhu| vibhaktaśabdena svasamānasattākabhedapratiyogitvarūpavastuparicchedasya vivakṣitatvāt na brahmatucchayorvyabhicāraḥ| na ca -- `khaṇḍo gaurmuṇḍo gau' rityevamādisvānugatapratibhāse gotvādau vyaktīnāmakalpitvād vyabhicāra iti -- vācyam| satsāmānyātiriktagotvādisāmānyānabhyupagamāt, gotvādyabhyupagame'pi gotvādivyañjakatāvacchedakasāmānyānabhyupagamāt| vyaktiviśeṣāṇāmevānanugatānāṃ sāsnādimattvādyupādhyanugatānāṃ vā tadvyañjakatvavad vyaktiviśeṣaviśiṣṭatvena satsāmānyasyaiva tattadvyavahārajanakatvopapatteḥ| ata eva -- `ghaṭādikaṃ, sadrūpe kalpitam, pratyekaṃ tadanuviddhatvena pratīyamānatvāt pratyekaṃ candrānuviddhajalataraṅgacandravat iti brahmasiddhikāroktamapi sādhu| nanu -- sadarthasya brahmaṇaḥ rūpādihīnasyā'saṃsāramajñānāvṛtasya śabdaikagamyasya kathaṃ ghaṭaḥ sannityādibuddhiviṣayatā syāt ?tathā ca `ghaṭo'nitya' ityanena ghaṭagatānityateva `ghaṭaḥ sa' nnityanenāpi ghaṭagatameva sattvaṃ gṛhyate| na ca -- svarūpeṇāpratyakṣasya rāhoścandrāvacchedeneva brahmaṇo'pi ghaṭādyavacchedenaiva pratyakṣateti -- vācyam| śabdādyavacchinnasyāpi gaganādeḥ śrāvaṇatvādyāpātāt| rāhostu dūradoṣeṇājñātasya nīlasya yogyasya śuklabhāsvaracandra sambandhāccākṣuṣatā uktā -- iti cenna| yataḥ sadātmanā na brahmaṇo mūlājñānenāvṛtatvam| kintu ghaṭādyavacchinnaśaktyajñānenaiva| tathā ca cakṣurādijanyavṛttyā tadāvaraṇabhaṅge sati `sanghaṭa' ityatra brahmaṇaḥ sphuraṇe bādhakābhāvāt| na ca -- rūpādihīnatayā cākṣuṣatvādyanupapattiḥ bādhiketi -- vācyam| pratiniyatendriyagrāhyeṣveva rūpādyapekṣāniyamāt| sarvendriyagrāhyaṃ tu sadrūpaṃ brahma| nāto rūpādihīnatve'pi cākṣuṣatvādyanupapattiḥ| sattāyāḥ parairapi sarvendriyagrāhyatvābhyupagamācca| taduktaṃ vārtikakṛdbhiḥ -- 
`ato'nubhava evaiko viṣayo'jñātalakṣaṇaḥ|
akṣādīnāṃ svataḥ siddho yatra teṣāṃ pramāṇatā||' iti|
kālasya ca rūpādihīnasya mīmāṃsakādibhiḥ sarvendriyagrāhyatvābhyupagamāt| naca -- śabdāvacchinnasyākāśasyāpi śrāvaṇatvaṃ syāditi -- vācyam; svabhāvato yogyasya hi kenacinnimittena pratiruddhayogyatākasyāvacchedakādinā yogyatā sampādyate, yathā dūradoṣeṇa pratiruddhayogyatākasya rāhoścandrasambandhena | evaṃ cāvaraṇena pratiruddhayogyatākaṃ brahma ghaṭādyavacchedena yogyaṃ bhavati, nabhastu svabhāvāyogyameva, na pratiruddhayogyatākam, yena śabdāvacchedena yogyaṃ bhavet| yadvā -- dravyagrahe cakṣuṣo rūpāpekṣā, na tvanyagrahe| brahma tu na dravyam, `asthūlamanaṇvahrasvamadīrgha ' miti śrutyā caturvidhaparimāṇaniṣedhena dravyatva pratiṣedhāt| ato nānupapattiḥ| astu vā dravyam| tathāpyadhyastadravyatvavati guṇādau rūpānapekṣacākṣuṣatvadarśanena dharmyanyūnasattākadravyatvavatyeva cakṣū rūpamapekṣate| brahmaṇi ca dravyatvaṃ dharmyapekṣayā nyūnasattākamaveti na tadgrahe rūpādyapekṣā| kalpitatvaṃ ca svābhāvavati pratīyamānatvaṃ vā, svarūpajñānanivartyatvaṃ vetyanyadetat| tasmāt paricchinnatvamapi bhavati heturiti siddham| iti paricchinnatvahetūpapattiḥ|
citsukhācāryaistu -- `ayaṃ paṭaḥ, etattantuniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogī, aṃśitvād, itarāṃ śivad ityuktam| tatra tantupadamupādānaparam| tena svopādānaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvalakṣaṇamityātvasiddhiḥ| na ca -- kāryasya kāraṇābhedena tadanāśritatvāt siddhasādhanam, anāśritatvenānyāśritatvena vā upapattyā arthāntaraṃ ca iti vācyam| abhede kāryakāraṇabhāvavyāhatyā kathañcidapi bhedasyāvaśyābhyupeyatvāt| na ca `tadananyatvamārambhaṇa śabdādibhya' ityadhikaraṇavirodhaḥ| upādānavyatirekeṇopādeyaṃ nāstītyasyaiva tadarthatvāt| bādhattanmātrāśritatvena pakṣaviśeṣaṇādvā nārthāntaram| na ca prakṛte'pi bādhaḥ| tasyoddhariṣyamāṇatvāt| na cātyantābhāvasya prāmāṇikatvāprāmāṇikatvavikalpāvakāśaḥ | tasya prāgeva nirastatvāt| na ca -- kasyacit paṭasya saṃyogavṛttyaitattantuṣu sattvena tatra vyabhicāra iti vācyam| tatsamavetasya tanniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvamaṅgīkurvataḥ tatsaṃyoginastanniṣṭhā tyantābhāvapratiyogitvāṅgīkāreṇa pakṣasamatvāt| na cāvyāpyavṛttitve nārthāntaram| paṭatadabhāvayorekādhikaraṇavṛttau virodhasya jagati dattajalāñjalitvaprasaṅgāt, saṃyogatadabhāvayorapyekādhikaraṇavṛttitvānabhyupagamāt| abhyupagame vā etattantutvāvacchinnavṛtti tvamatyantābhāvasya viśeṣaṇaṃ deyam, evametatkālīnatvamapi| tena kālāntarīyābhāvamādāya nārthāntaram| na ceha tantuṣu paṭa iti pratyakṣa bādhaḥ| tasya bhramasādhāraṇatayā candraprādeśikatvapratyakṣavadaprāmāṇyaśaṅkāskanditatvenābādhakatvāt| bādhoddhāre ca vistareṇai tadvakṣyāmaḥ| na ca -- anyāsamavetasyāṃśitvametattantu samavetatvaṃ vinā na yuktamiti viruddho heturiti -- vācyam| etattantuniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitve'pyetattantusamavetasya sattvenāṃśitvasya sādhyenāvirodhāt| etanniṣṭhātyantābhāvāpratiyogitvaṃ hi etatsamavetatve prayojakaṃ na bhavati, paramate kevalānvayidharmamātrasya etatsamavetatvāpatteḥ| kintvetanniṣṭhaprāgabhāvapratiyogitvādikam| taccaitanniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitve'pi na viruddhamityupapāditamadhastāt| etatsamavetatvaṃ caitadupādānakatvam, na tu nityasambandhaśālitvam, tasyānabhyupagamāt| nanu -- ayaṃ paṭa etattantuniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogī na, etattantvārabdhatvād, vyatirekeṇa paṭāntaravaditi pratirodhaḥ| na cāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam| etanniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvaṃ kiñcinniṣṭhātyantā bhāvapratiyogi, saṃsargābhāvapratiyogitvavyāpyatvāt, prāgabhāvapratiyogitvavaditi sāmānyatasta tprasiddheḥ| na ca -- ākāśātyantābhāvasya ghaṭādau saṃsargābhāvapratiyogitva vyāpyatvagrahāt tasya ca kevalānvayitvena kiñcinniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvābhāvāt yatra vyabhicāra iti vācyam| saṃsargābhāvapratiyogitvānadhikaraṇe kevalānvayini dharme sattvenākāśātyantābhāvasya saṃsargābhāvapratiyogitvāvyāpyatvena vyabhicārābhāvād iti cenna, yatraitattantuniṣṭhātyantā bhāvapratiyogitvam, tatraitattantvārabdhatvābhāva iti vyatirekavyāptāvetanniṣṭhaprāgabhāvāpratiyogitvasyopādhitvena pratirodhasya hīnabalatvād, etattantvārabdhatvābhāvavyāpakasyaitattantu niṣṭhaprāgabhāvāpratiyogitvasya pakṣāvṛtteḥ pakṣavṛttitayā sandihyamānaitattantuniṣṭhātyantā bhāvapratiyogitvāvyāpakatvāt| dṛśyatvādyanupapattipratikūlatarkaparāhatervakṣyamāṇatvācca| ata eva etattantvanārabdhatvamapi nopādhiḥ| upādhivyatirekeṇa sādhyavyatireka sādhyamāne sopādhikatvasyoktatvāt| avyāpyavṛttisaṃyogābhyupagame tatra vyabhicārācca| ata eva yatraitattantuniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvam, tatraitattantvanārabdhatvamiti na sādhyavyāpakatāgraho'pi| tatraiva vyabhicārāditi sarvamanavadyam||
evaṃ ca `vimataṃ jñānavyatirekeṇāsat, jñānavyatirekeṇānupalabhyamānatvāt, svapnādivadi' ti vidyāsāgaroktamapi sādhu| jñānavyatirekeṇāsattvamuktamithyātvānyatamatvaṃ cidābhāse sattyevopalabhyamānatvaṃ heturiti na kiñcidanupapannam| evamanyeṣāmapi prayogā yathāyogamupapādanīyā iti śivam| ityaṃśitvahetūpapattiḥ|
nanu -- dṛśyatvādihetavaḥ sopādhikāḥ| tathāhi svabādhakābhimatābādhyadoṣaprayuktabhānatvaṃ svabādhakābādhyabādhakaṃ prati niṣedhyatvena viṣayatvaṃ vā vipakṣād vyāvṛttaṃ samavyāptam| ata eva vyatirekavyāptimadupādhiḥ iti cenna| brahmajñānamātrabādhye dehātmaikye mithyābhūte sādhyāvyāpakatvāt| parvatāvayavavṛttyanyatvādivat sādhanavatpakṣamātravyavartakaviśeṣaṇavattvena pakṣetaratvatulyatvācca| na ca bādhonnītatvāt so'pyupādhiḥ| bādhasyāgre nirasiṣyamāṇatvād| api ca yadvyatirekasya sādhyavyatirekasādhakatvaṃ tasyaiva sādhyavyāpakatvam, itarāṃśe anukūlatarkāprasarāt| tathā ca `kṣityādikaṃ na kartṛjanyam śarīrājanyatvā' dityatra yathā śarīraviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyānna śarīrajanyatvaṃ kartṛjanyatvavyāpakam, evaṃ `viyadādikaṃ na mithyā svabādhakābhimatābādhyadoṣa prayuktabhānatvarahitatvā ' diti sādhyavyatirekasādhane svabādhakābhimatābādhyabhāgasya vaiyarthyāt svabādhakābhimatābādhyadoṣaprayuktabhānatvaṃ na mithyātvavyāpakam| doṣaprayuktabhānatvaṃ tu bhavati sādhyavyāpakam| tacca sādhanavyāpakamapīti nopādhiḥ| dṛśyatvādinaiva mithyātvavattasyāpi sādhanāt| evaṃ dvitīyopādhāvapi `svabādhakābādhyabādhakaṃ pratī' ti viśeṣaṇaṃ vyatirekasādhane vyartham| viśeṣyabhāgastu sādhyasādhanayorvyāpaka iti nopādhiḥ| ata evādhiṣṭhānatvābhimata samasattākadoṣavaddhetu janyajñānaviṣayatvamupādhiḥ| atra ca brahmaṇo'pi bauddhakalpitadoṣavaddhetujanyakṣaṇikatvādi jñānaviṣayatvāt, samavyāptisiddhyarthamadhiṣṭhāna samasattāketi viśeṣaṇam, na tu pakṣamātra vyāvṛttyartham, ato na pakṣetaratulyatetyapāstam| brahmaṇīva brahmaṇi kalpite kṣaṇikatvādāvapi mithyābhūte dharme adhiṣṭhānasamasattākadoṣavaddhetujanyajñānāviṣayatvādupādheḥ sādhyāvyāpteḥ| vyatirekasādhane vyarthaviśeṣaṇatvasyoktatvācca| nāpi śrutitātparyāviṣayatvamupādhiḥ| śrutitātparyaviṣayatvasya brahmamātraniṣṭhatayā tadabhāvasya sādhanavyāpakatvāt| nāpi prātibhāsikatvamupādhiḥ| taddhi brahmajñānetarabādhyatvam| tasya ca dehātmaikye mithyābhūte'pya sattvena sādhyāvyāpteḥ| vyatireke vyarthaviśeṣaṇatvācca| nāpi pratibhāsamātraśarīratvamupādhiḥ| dṛṣṭisṛṣṭipakṣe sādhanavyāpakatvāt, pareṣāmasiddheśceti| iti dṛśyatvādīnāṃ sopādhitvabhaṅgaḥ||
nanu -- vimataṃ prātibhāsikam dṛśyatvāt, brahma mithyā vyavahāraviṣayatvād asadvilakṣaṇatvādvā śuktirūpyavadityādyābhāsasāmyam -- iti cenna| jagato vyāvahārikasattva bādhe vyavahārānupapattiḥ, brahmaṇo mithyātve śūnyavādāpattiśceti pratikūlatarkaparāghātena tayorasādhakatvāt| prakṛte ca pratikūlatarkasya nirasiṣyamāṇatvāt| kiñca pratibhāsikatvaṃ brahmajñānetarabādhyatvam ? pratibhāsamātraśarīratvaṃ vā ? ādye sādhye dehātmaikye vyabhicāraḥ, aprayojakatvaṃ ca| dvitīye dṛṣṭisṛṣṭimatena siddhasādhanam| evaṃ brahmaṇi mithyātve sādhye sopādhike siddhasādhanam| anupādhike vyavahāraviṣayatvarūpo heturasiddhaḥ| vedāntajanyavṛtti viṣayatvābhyupagame'pyaprayojakaḥ| evamasadvilakṣaṇatvamapi brahmaṇyasiddhameva| kvacidapyupādhau sattvena pratītyanarhatvaṃ hyasattvam, sadvilakṣaṇatvaṃ ca kvacidapyupādhau satvena pratītyarhatvarūpam, tacca śuddhe brahmaṇi nāstyeva| na ca -- bādhyatvamasattvam, tadvilakṣaṇatvaṃ cābādhyatvam, tacca brahmaṇyastyeveti -- vācyam, abādhyatvena bādhyatvalakṣaṇāmith‌yātvasādhane virodhāt, śuktirūpyadṛṣṭāntasya sādhanavikalatvācca, śūnyavādasyāgre nirākariṣyamāṇatvācca| tasmānna dṛśyatvādīnāmābhāsasāmyamiti siddham| iti ābhāsasāmyabhaṅgaḥ|
nanu `san ghaṭa' ityādyadhyakṣabādhitaviṣayā dṛśyatvādaya iti cenna; cakṣurādyadhyakṣayogyamithyātvavirodhisattvānirukteḥ| tathā hi -- na tāvat pramāviṣayatvaṃ, tadyogyatvaṃ, bhramāviṣayatvaṃ vā tadṛksattvam| cakṣurādyagamyabhramapramāghaṭitatvena cakṣurādyayogyatvād, vakṣyamāṇadūṣaṇagaṇagrāsācca| tathā hi -- nādyaḥ, asati pramāṇāpravṛtteḥ pramāviṣayatvātprāk sattvasya vaktavyatvena tasya tadanyatvāt| sattvanirūpaṇaṃ vinā sadarthaviṣayakatvarūpapramātvasya nirūpaṇe cānyonyāśrayāt| mithyābhūtasyāpi śuktirajatasaṃsargasya vyavasāyadvārā sākṣācca niṣedhyatvādinā pramāviṣayatvābhyupagamācca| nāpi dvitīyaḥ, yogyatāyā anirūpaṇāt| na tṛtīyaḥ, asiddheḥ, sarvasyaiva kṣaṇikatvādinā bhramaviṣayatvābhyupagamāt| ata eva nāsattvāprakārakapramāviṣayatvamapi, anyonyāśrayācca| nāpi sattvaprakārakapramāviṣayatvam, ātmāśrayāt| nāpyasattvaprakārakabhramāviṣayatvaṃ sattvam, anyonyāśrayāt| nāpi pratipannopādhau traikālikasattvaniṣedhavirahaḥ, ātmāśrayāt| nāpi sattā jātirarthakriyākāritvamasadvailakṣaṇyaṃ vā, eteṣāṃ mithyātvāvirodhitvena tatpratyakṣeṇa mithyātvānumāne bādhābhāvāt| nāpi vedāntyabhimatamithyātvābhāvaḥ sattvam, tucche'tivyāpteḥ| nāpyasadvilakṣaṇatve satyanāropitatvam, anāropitatvaṃ hi āropāviṣayatvam, taccāsambhavi| sarvasyāpi kṣaṇikatvādinā āropaviṣayatvāt| nāpiastitvaprakārakapramāṃ prati kadācit sākṣādviṣayatvaṃ kālasambandhitvaṃ vā sattvam| astitvaṃ ca vartamānatvam| na tu sattvamato nātmāśrayaḥ| atītādirapi kadācid vartata eveti nāvyāptiḥ| āropitaṃ ca kālatrayāsambandhitvena bādhena bodhitamiti na dvitīyalakṣaṇe'tivyāptiriti| vācyam -- pramātvasya sattvaghaṭitatvena cakṣurādyayogyatvena ca pūrvoktadoṣād, vartamānatvaprakārakapramāviṣayatve'pi mithyātvāvirodhācca| dvitīyamapi na mithyātvavirodhi| śuktirūpyasyāpi pratibhāsakālasambandhitvāt| bādhena tāttvikakālatraya sambandhaniṣedhe'pyatāttvikakāla sambandhasyāniṣedhāt| nāpi tāttvikakālasambandhitvaṃ tat| tāttvikatvasyādyāpyanirūpaṇāt| nirūpaṇe vā śaiṣavaiyarthyāt| nanu -- bhavanmate yat sattvaṃ brahmaṇi, tadeveha mama| uktaṃ hi -- `yādṛśaṃ brahmaṇaḥ sattvaṃ tādṛśaṃ syājjagatyapi| tatra syāttadanirvācyaṃ cedihāpi tathāstu naḥ|' iti| na ca -- tatrāparicchinnatvaṃ sattvam, tacca na jagatīti -- vācyam| tucchasyāparicchinnatve'pi sattvānabhyupagamānnāparicchinnatvaṃ sattvam| kiṃ tvanyadeva| tacca brahmaṇīva bhramādhiṣṭhānatvācchuktikāderapi bhaviṣyatīti -- cet, nūnaṃ vivāhasamaye kanyāyāḥ pitrā nijagotraṃ pṛṣṭasya yadeva bhavatāṃ gotraṃ tadeva mamāpi gotramiti vadato varasya bhrātā bhavān, yato jāmātṛśvaśurayorekagotratve vivāhānupapattivajjagadbrahmaṇorekasattve jagato'sattvameva syāt| tathā hi -- svaprakāśādvitīyacaitanyarūpatvameva brahmaṇaḥ sattvam| tadeva cejjaḍasyāpi jagatastadā rajatatva virodhiśuktisattayā rajatasyeva jaḍatvavirodhisvaprakāśasattayā jagataḥ svarūpato mithyātvopapatteḥ| caitanyasyaivāvacchinnānavacchinnājñānaviṣayatvena sarvabhramādhiṣṭhānatvābhyupagamānna bhramādhiṣṭhānatvena śuktyādeḥ sattvasiddhiḥ| nanvevamapi sarvadeśīyatraikālikaniṣedhapratiyogitvamasattvaṃ tucchanirvacanīyasādhāraṇam| tadabhāvaḥ sattvam| tacca brahmaṇīva jagatyapīti brūmaḥ| na ca saṃyoge'vyāptiḥ, tasyāvyāpyavṛttitvābhyupagamāt| tadabhyupagame ca vyāpyavṛttitvenābhāvo viśeṣaṇīyaḥ| nāpi viyatyavyāptiḥ, tadatyantābhāvasya kevalānvayitvānaṅgīkāreṇa lakṣaṇasya vidyamānatvādeva| na hi kasmiṃścid deśe kāle vā tasyābhāvaḥ, nityavibhutvabhaṅgaprasaṅgāt| ākāśātyantābhāvasya kevalānvayitvābhyupagame ca vṛttimatpratiyogikatvenābhāvo viśeṣaṇīyaḥ -- iti cenna| cakṣurādyayogyānekapādartha ghaṭitatvenaitādṛśasattvasya grahaṇe cakṣurāderasāmarthyāt| na hi sarvadeśīyatraikālika vṛttimatpratiyogikavyāpyavṛttiniṣedhapratiyogitvaṃ kasyāpi pratyakṣam| yena tadabhāvaḥ pratyakṣo bhavet| vṛttimatpratiyogikatvavyāpyavṛttitvaparityāge'pi sarvadeśīyatvatraikālikatvayorayogyatvāt|
nanu -- svadeśakālavṛttiniṣedhapratiyogitvābhāve gṛhyamāṇe kālatrayamadhye vartamānakālasya sarvadeśamadhye prakṛtadeśasyāpi praveśena tatra niṣedhapratiyogitvābhāvasya gṛhītatvāttatsaṃvalitaṃ kālatrayavṛttisarvadeśīyaniṣedhapratiyogitvarūpaṃ mithyātvaṃ nānumānena grahītuṃ śakyate -- iti cenna| svadeśakālavṛttisakalaniṣedhapratiyogitvasya cakṣurād‌yayogyatvena tadabhāvasya sutarāṃ tadayogyatvāt| svadeśakālavṛttiyatkiñcinniṣedhāpratiyogitvasya mithyātvāvirodhitvāt| svapratiyogikātyantābhāvāsāmānadhikaraṇyasya ca svapratiyogikātyantābhāvāprasiddhyā kevalānvayini, sambandhabhedena ghaṭādau cāsiddheḥ| svātyantābhāvayāvadadhikaraṇāvṛttitvaṃ vā, svātyantābhāvayatkiñcidadhikaraṇāvṛttitvaṃ veti vikalpena pūrvoktadoṣācca| tasmāttatprakārāntarasya nirūpayitumaśakyatvānmithyātvāvirodhitvācca svasamānādhikaraṇayāvadatyantābhāvapratiyogitvā bhāvarūpameva sattvamupeyam| tacca na cakṣurādiyogyamityuktam| nanu yasminkasmiṃścit svadeśakālavṛttiniṣedhe etad‌deśaitatkālavṛttiniṣedhatvaṃ jñātvā tena pratyāsattibhūtenopasthāpitānāṃ svadeśakālavṛttisakalaniṣedhānāṃ pratiyogitvasyābhāvo ghaṭe grāhyaḥ| tataḥ sārvadikasarvadeśīyaniṣedhapratiyogitvasya grahaṇaṃ ghaṭe durghaṭamiti -- cenna| evaṃ sāmānyalakṣaṇayā sarvaniṣedheṣūpasthiteṣvapi tatpratiyogitvābhāvasya cakṣurādinā grahītumaśakyatvāt| yogyapratiyogika eva hi saṃsargābhāvo yogyaḥ| na cāśeṣaniṣedhānāṃ pratiyogitva matīndriyasādhāraṇaṃ cakṣurādiyogyam|
vastutastu -- sāmānyaṃ nendriyapratyāsattiḥ, mānābhāvāt| na ca -- mahānasīyadhūmendriyasaṃyogena tatraiva vyāptigrahe parvatīyadhūmādanumitirnasyāt, sāmānyasya ca dhūmatvādeḥ pratyāsattitve tasyāpi pratyāsannatvāttatra vyāptigrahe tato'numitiriti -- vācyam| parvatīyadhūmendriyasannikarṣadaśāyāṃ dhūmatvena prakāreṇa gṛhītasmṛtavyāptestatra vaiśiṣṭyagrahasambhavāt| `surabhi candanam' itivad viśeṣyendriyasannikarṣaviśeṣaṇajñānāsaṃsargāgraharūpāyā viśiṣṭhajñānasāmagryāḥ pūrṇatvāt| vyaptismṛtiprakāreṇa vā pakṣadharmatājñānasya hetutā| mahānasīya eva dhūmo dhūmatvena vyāptismṛtiviṣayo bhavati| dhūmatvena parvatīyadhūmajñānaṃ cāpi jātam, tacca sāmānyalakṣaṇāṃ vinaiva| tāvataivānumitisiddheḥ| na ca -- sāmānyapratyāsattiṃ vinā dhūmo vahnivyabhicārī na veti anubhūyamānaḥ saṃśayo na syāt, prasiddhe dhūme vahnisambandhāvagamād aprasiddhasya cājñānāditi -- vācyam| prasiddhadhūma eva tattaddhūmatvādinā vyāptiniścaye'pi dhūmatvena tatsaṃśayopapatteḥ| tathā coktaṃ maṇikṛtā -- `ghaṭatvenetarabhedaniścaye'pi pṛthivītvādinā tatra saṃśayasiṣādhayiṣe bhavata eveti| niścite'pyarthe prāmāṇyasaṃśayāhitasaṃśayavad dhūmatvaṃ vahnivyabhicārivṛtti na veti saṃśayādapi tādṛśasaṃśayopapatteśca| etena vāyū rūpavānnaveti saṃśayo'pi vyākhyātaḥ|
nanu -- siddhe necchā, kintu asiddhe| sā ca svasāmānaviṣayakajñānajanyā| tacca jñānaṃ na sāmānyapratyāsattiṃ vinā| na ca -- siddhagocarasukhatvaprakārakajñānādevājñāte sukhe bhavatīcchā| samānaprakārakatvamātrasya niyāmakatvāditi vācyam| rajatatvena prakāreṇa rajate anubhūyamāne ghaṭādau rajatatvaprakārakecchāprasaṅgāt| na ca prakārāśrayatvamapi niyāmakam| rajatabhramācchuktāvicchānudayaprasaṅgāt| tathā ca samānaprakārakatve sati samānaviṣayakatvaṃ tantram| ata evākhyātipakṣe rajatasmaraṇasyaiva śuktau pravartakatvamityapāstamiti cenna| yato rajatabhramācchuktāvicchā nāstyeva kintvanirvacanīye rajata ityanirvacanīyakhyātau vakṣyate| prakārāśrayatvaṃ niyāmakaṃ vadannakhyātivādī paramevaṃ vibhīṣaṇīyaḥ| tathā ca prakārāśrayatvasya niyāmakatvādanyathākhyātipakṣo'pi nirasta eva| na ca tarhi bhramatvaṃ na syād, idaṃ rajatamiti bhramatvābhimatajñānasya vyadhikaraṇaprakāratvānabhyupagamāditi vācyam| bādhita viṣayatvena hi bhramatvaṃ na tu vyadhikaraṇaprakāratvena, tasyāpi viṣayabādhaprayojyatvāditi hi vakṣyate| nanu -- abhāvajñānasya pratiyogijñānajanyatvāt, prauḍhaprakāśayāvattejoviraharūpasya samasaḥ pratyakṣatā na syāt| sāmānyapratyāsattiṃ vinā pratiyogyanupasthiteriti -- cenna| asmanmate tamaso bhāvāntaratvāt| na ca -- tathāpi tadvyañjakatvāttadapekṣeti -- vācyam| svarūpasata eva tādṛktojovirahasya tamovyañjakatvam, na tu jñātasya mānābhāvādityabhyupagamāt| anyeṣāṃ mate tādṛktejovirahajñānasyāpekṣitatve'pi pratiyogitāvacchedakaprakārakajñānādeva tatsambhavena tadarthaṃ sakalapratiyogijñānajanikāyāḥ sāmānyapratyāsatteranupayogāt| na ca gotvābhāvajñānaṃ gotvatvaprakārakajñānajanyam| tacca gavetarāvṛttitve sati sakalagovṛttitvarūpaṃ sāmānyapratyāsattimantareṇa na śakyamavagantumiti sāmpratam| yatkiñcidgovyaktereva gotvatvarūpatvāt| etena prāgabhāvapratītirapi vyākhyātā| kiñca anāgatajñānasyāpekṣitve anumānādeva tadbhaviṣyati| tathā ca nyāyakusumāñjalau -- 
"śaṅkā cedanumā'styeva na cecchaṅkā kutastarām|
vyāghātāvadhirāśaṅkā tarkaḥ śaṅkāvadhirmataḥ||"
ityanta śaṅkopapādakamanāgatajñāna manumānādevetyuktam| anumānaṃ ca -- vartamānapākaḥ pākaprvakālīnaḥ pākatvādatīta pākavadityādi| na ca caramapāke vyabhicāraḥ| sādhyasiddhayupajīvakasya vyabhicārajñānasyādoṣatvāt| anyathā siddhyasiddhivyāghātāt| kisa śabdādapi sakaladhūmapākādigocarajñānasebhavaḥ| na ca śaṅkādipūrvaṃ śabdasyopasthitiniyamābhāva iti vācyam| kadācideva śabdādanubhūtasya tadānīṃ pramṛṣṭatattākasmṛtisambhavāt| nanu anumiterviśeṣaṇajñānajanyatvena sāmānyapratyāsattisiddhiḥ| na cānumānāntarādviśeṣaṇa jñānamanavasthānāditi cenna| viśeṣaṇatāvacchedakaprakārakajñānādeva sādhyaviśeṣaṇakapakṣa viśeṣyakānumitisambhavāt| etena -- `surabhi candanam' ityādiviśiṣṭajñānāya kalpitā jñānalakṣaṇā pratyāsattirapi nirastā| candanatvena surabhitvānumānopapatteḥ| anyathā sādhyaviśiṣṭapakṣapratyakṣopapatteranumānamātrocchedaprasaṅgāt| na ca -- abhāvasādhyakakevala vyatirekiṇi sādhyaprasiddheranaṅgatvāttatra klṛptāyā anumitisāmagryāḥ pratyakṣasāmagrīto balavattvamiti vācyam| arthāpattivādibhirasmābhistadanabhyupagamāt| parvatavṛttidhūmo vahnivyāpya iti parāmarśāt sādhyaviśeṣyakapakṣaviśeṣaṇakānumityabhyugame tu naiva kāpyanupapattiḥ| anumiteḥ pakṣaviśeṣyatvaniyame mānābhāvāt|
kiṃ ca dhūmatvādisāmānyaṃ na svarūpataḥ pratyāsattiḥ| dhūlīpaṭale dhūmabhramānantaraṃ dhūmatvena sakaladhūmaniṣṭhavahnivyāptigrahānudayaprasaṅgāt| tatra svarūpato dhūmatvābhāvāt| na ceṣṭāpattiḥ| taduttarakālamanumityanudayāpatteḥ| tathā ca dhūmatvajñānaṃ pratyāsattiriti vācyam| tacca dhūmendriyasannikarṣadaśāyāṃ dhūmajñānāt prāṅnāstyeva| nirvikalpake mānābhāvāt| viśiṣṭajñānatvena viśeṣaṇajñānatvena ca kāryakāraṇabhāvānabhyupagamāt| avaśyaklṛptakāryakāraṇa bhāvaviśeṣeṇaiva sarvavyavahāropapatteḥ| na ca dhūmatvena sannikṛṣṭadhūmavyaktijñānāntaraṃ tatsamānākāramasannikṛṣṭadhūmagocaraṃ jñānāntaramutpadyata ityatra mānamasti| dhūmatvena purovartinaṃ dhūmaṃ sākṣātkaromi na vyavahitamityanubhavācca| anyathā jagatīgatasakaladhūmavyaktīrahaṃ sākṣātkaromītyanuvyavasīyeta | na caivamanubhavamātraśaraṇairabhyupeyate| kiṃ ca sāmānyapratyā sattyaṅgīkāre yat prameyam| tadabhidheyam, yatprameyavat tadabhidheyavadityādivyāptiparicchede sārvajñyāpattiḥ| na ceṣṭaiva sā| parajñānaviṣayo ghaṭo na vetyādisaṃśayānupapatteḥ| na ca ghaṭatvaprakārakaghaṭaviṣayakaniścayo ghaṭasaṃśayavirodhī| prameyamiti niścayastu ghaṭaviṣayo'pi na ghaṭatvaprakāraka iti vācyam| bhāsamānavaiśiṣṭyapratiyogina eva prakāratvāt| ghaṭatvasyāpi prameyamiti jñāne bhāsamānavaiśiṣṭyapratiyogitvāt| ghaṭatvaprakārakaniścayasya ghaṭatvajñānajanyatva viśeṣaṇādadoṣa iti cet, na| viśeṣaṇajñānatvenaiva tasya janakatā vācyā| tasyāḥ prāgeva nirāsāt| svarūpasambandhaviśeṣābhyupagame cānirvacanīyavādāpatteḥ ityādidūṣaṇāni bahutaramūhanīyāni| tasmāt sāmānyapratyāsattyā niṣedhamātrapratiyogitvopasthitau tadabhāvagrahād bādha ityanupapannameva| iti sāmānyapratyāsattibhaṅgena laukikālaukikapratyakṣabādhoddhāraḥ|
atha sākṣibādhoddhāraḥ| nanu pratyakṣasya vartamānamātragrāhitve śuktirūpyādeḥ pratipannopādhau traikālikaniṣedhapratiyogitvarūpaṃ mithyātvaṃ kathaṃ pratyakṣaṃ syāt ? atha tatra rajatatvavirodhiśuktitve sākṣātkṛte tadanyathānupapattyā ca rajatatvābhāve niścite mithyaiva rajatamabhāditi tādṛṅniṣedhapratyayaḥ svasambandhasarvāvabhāsakena sākṣiṇaivopapannaḥ, tarhi sākṣāt svaviṣayasya gaganāderbhāvikālaniṣedhāpratiyogitvaṃ sakalakālagrāhiṇā sākṣiṇā gṛhyatāmiti cenna| sākṣiṇo vidyamānasarvāvabhāsakatvenāvidyamānabhāvibādhābhāvabhāsakatvānupapatteḥ| sākṣijñānasya bhramapramāsādhāraṇatvena pramāṇābādhakatvācca| nanu jñānaprāmāṇyaṃ gṛhṇan sākṣī ghaṭādigatamabādhyatvaṃ gṛhṇātyeva| na hi viṣayābādhamanantarbhāvya prāmāṇyagrahaṇaṃ nāma iti cenna| vyavahārakālābādhyatvamātreṇa pravṛttāvapi saṃvādopapatteḥ| tadrūpagataprāmāṇyasya sākṣiṇā grahaṇe'pi virodhābhāvāt| na hi ghaṭādijñānasya saṃvādipravṛttijanakatāvacchedakaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ trikālābādhyaviṣayakatvam| kintu śuktirūpyādijñānavyāvṛttaṃ vyavahārakālābādhyaviṣayaka sakalajñānavṛttivyavahārakālābādhyaviṣayakatvameva| tacca na bhāvikālabādhavirodhītyuktam| bhāvikālabādhatadabhāvau ca na mānaṃ vinā sākṣimā grahītuṃ śakyau| tasya vidyamānamātragrāhitvāditi coktam| nanu tarhi dehātmaikyajñānamuṣṇaṃ jalamityādi jñānaṃ ca pramā syāt| vyavahāradaśāyāṃ viṣayābādhād iti cenna| ābrahmajñānamabādhitatvena teṣāmapi ghaṭādijñānasamānayogakṣematvāt|
nanu kālāntarasthamapi yad bādhakaṃ tadapi kiṃ yatkālāvacchedena anena svārtho gṛhītastatkālāvacchedenaiva tanniṣedhati, utānyakālāvacchedena ? ādye kathamasya prāmāṇyam ? antye tu anityatvādikameva iti cenna| abādhyatvarūpaprāmāṇyasya prapañcajñāne mayā'naṅgīkārāt| yatkālāvacchedenaivānena svārtho gṛhītastatkālāvacchedenaiva tanniṣedhābhyupagamāt| tacca prāmāṇyaṃ mayā'bhyupeyate yad vyavahāradaśāyāṃ viparītapramārūpabādhakasyānutpannatvādastyeva| na ca yad bhavatāṃ ghaṭādibuddheḥ prātibhāsikabuddhito vailakṣaṇyaṃ viṣayasya vyāvahārikasattvasādhakaṃ tadeveha mama viṣayasya pāramārthikasattvasādhakamastviti vācyam| prātibhāsikabuddhivailakṣaṇyaṃ hi ghaṭādibuddheḥ saprakārakajñānābādhyaviṣayatvādirūpam| tanna pāramārthikasattvaṃ ghaṭādeḥ sādhayituṃ śaktam| dehātmaikyajñāne brahmajñānāvyavahitabhrame ca vyabhicārāt| nanu -- `ghaṭassan', `rūpyaṃ mithyā' iti pratītyoraviśeṣe kathaṃ `ghaṭo mithyā', rūpyamithyātvaṃ na mithyā' iti viśeṣaḥ ? na ca tadapi mithyaiva, rūpyatāttvikattvāpatteḥ iti cenna| mithyātvamithyātve'pi yathā na rūpyasya tāttvikatvaṃ tatropapatteruktatvāt| na ca pāramārthikasattvasya pratyakṣāgocaratve tanniṣedhaśrutīnāṃ aprasaktapratiṣedhakatā syāditi vācyam| tāsāṃ cakṣurādiprasaktataniṣedhaparatvāt| pāramārthikatvena dvaitaniṣedhaparatve'pi nāprasaktaniṣedhakatvam| parokṣaprasakteḥ sambhavāt| `nāntarikṣe'gniścetavyaḥ ' ityādivadaprasaktapratiṣedhasyāpyupapatteśca| na ca atāttvikaprapañce yadi tātvikatvamapyadhyakṣeṇa na gṛhyate kathaṃ tarhi tasyātattvāvedakatvam ? na hi tadeva tattvenāvedayadatāttvikaṃ nāma, dṛśyate ca sārvalaukikaḥ prapañce pāramārthikatvānubhava iti vācyam| na hyasmākaṃ tattvāvedakatvaṃ tadvati tatprakārakatvam, tadbhinnatvamatattvāvedakatvam| kintvabādhitaviṣayakatvaṃ tattvāvedakatvam, bādhitaviṣayatvaṃ cātattvāvedakatvam| abādhitaviṣayatvaṃ tu śraute brahmajñāna eva, na tadbhinnajñāne| tātparyavadvedatvenaiva tattvāvabodhakatvāt| tathā ca prapañcapratyakṣasya tāttvikatvāgocaratve'pyatattvāvedakatvaṃ saṅgacchate| sārvalaukikī pāramārthikatvaprasiddhistu jalagatapipāsopaśamanasāmarthyaprasiddhivat parokṣatayā'pyupapannā nāparokṣatvaparyavasāyinī|
tasmādadhyakṣayogyasya sattvasyehāniruktitaḥ|
nādhyakṣabādho mithyātvaliṅgasyātropapadyate||
na laukikaṃ na sāmānyajanyaṃ sākṣyātmakaṃ na ca ||1||
pratyakṣaṃ bādhate liṅgaṃ mithyātvasyānumāpakam||
iti pratyakṣayogyasattvāniruktyā pratyakṣabādhoddhāraḥ ||2||
`kiñcedaṃ rūpyaṃ' ityatra idamitivat `san ghaṭaḥ' ityatrāpi sadityadhiṣṭhānabhūtaṃ brahmaiva bhāsate| na ca cākṣuṣādijñāne rūpādihīnasya brahmaṇaḥ kathaṃ sphuraṇamiti vācyam| rūpādihīnasyāpi kālādinyāyena sphuraṇasya prāgevopapāditatvāt| nanvevaṃ `nīlo ghaṭaḥ' `mithyā rūpyam' asannṛśrṛṅgam 'ityādāvapi `nīlaḥ' ityādiradhiṣṭhānānuvedha iti syāt| na ca nailyaṃ ghaṭādiṣvasti, sattvaṃ tu neti vācyam| asyāropitatvasiddhyuttara kālīnatvenānyonyāśrayād, anyathā `satyaṃ jñānaṃ' ityatrāpi satyamityadhiṣṭhānānuvedha eva syāt iti cenna| sannityasya `ghaṭaḥ'ityanena sāmānādhikaraṇyasya bādhitvāt| tathā hi -- sattājātisphuraṇanibandhanaṃ vā, svarūpasattvanibandhanaṃ vā, kālatrayābādhyatvanibandhanaṃ vā sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ syāt| na cābhāvādisādhāraṇasatpratītau sattājātisphuraṇaṃ sambhavati| abhāvādiṣu tvayāpi tadanaṅgīkārāt| na ca kvacitsākṣātsambandhena kvacit paramparāsambandhena saditi pratītyupapattiḥ| vijātīyasambandhena sāmānākārapratītyanupapatteḥ| anyathā sambandhebheda eva na sidhyet| na ca svarūpasattvenābhāvādau tatpratītiḥ, ananugamāt| ananugatenāpi anugatapratītau jātimātrocchedaprasaṅgāt| ata eva na sarvatrāpi svarūpasattvenaiva sad‌vyavahāraḥ| ekenaiva sarvānugatena sarvatra satpratītyupapattau bahūnāṃ taddhetutvakalpane mānābhāvāt| nāpi kālatrayābādhyatvanibandhanaṃ tat| tasya cakṣurādyagamyatvasyoktatvāt| `sadidaṃ rajatam' ityādibhrame abhāvācca| tasmādekaṃ sarvādhiṣṭhānameva saditi sarvatrānubhūyata iti yuktam| nīlādestu ghaṭādisāmānādhikaraṇye kimapi nāsti bādhakam| na vā nīlāderadhiṣṭhānatvaṃ sambhavati| prāgasattvāt| nīlapītādiprātisvikānantādhiṣṭhānakalpane gauravāt| adhiṣṭheyena tulyayogakṣematvācca| adhiṣṭheyaviṣamasattākameva hyadhiṣṭhānaṃ bhavati| `mithyā rūpyam asannṛśrṛṅgam' ityādau mithyātvāsattvayoradhiṣṭhānatvaśaṅkāpi nāsti| śūnyavādāpatteḥ| tatra cānupapattiruktā, vakṣyate ca| yattu `satyaṃ jñānamanantam'ityatrāpi tathā syād iti| tanna| yato na tatra sattāsambandhena sattvam, kintu svarūpeṇaivetyuktadoṣānavakāśāt| na caivaṃ ghaṭādāvapi svarūpeṇaiva tathātvam| pūrvameva nirākṛtatvāt| iti sanghaṭa iti pratyakṣe'dhiṣṭhānānuvedhanirūpaṇam|
kiṃ ca niścitaprāmāṇyameva pratyakṣamitarabādhakaṃ bhavet, na cātra prāmāṇyaṃ niścitam; āgamavirodhād, anumānavirodhād, bhāvibādhābhāvānirṇayācca| nanu pratyakṣameva prabalamanumānāgamabādhakam| nānumānāgamau| pratyakṣāprāmaṇye tadvirodhābhāvenānumānāgamayoḥ prāmāṇyam, tayoḥ prāmāṇye ca tadvirodhāt pratyakṣāprāmāṇyamityanyonyāśrayāt| na hi pratyakṣasya prāmāṇye'pyevamanyonyāśrayaḥ| tasyānapekṣatvād iti cenna| candratārakādiparimāṇapratyakṣe anumānāgamavirodhena tasyāprāmāṇyadarśanāt, tenāpi svaprāmāṇyasiddhyarthamitarāvirodhasyāvaśyamapekṣaṇīyatvāt| tathā cānyo'nyāśrayatulyatvāt parasparavirodhena prāmāṇyasandehe satyanāptāpraṇītatvādinā pramājanakatvavyāptervedaprāmāṇyaniścaye jāte tena svatassambhāvitadoṣasya pratyakṣasya bādhāt asmanmate kvānyo'nyāśrayaḥ ? anyathā dehātmaikyapratyakṣabuddhyā bādhād dehabhinnatvamapyātmano nāgamānumānābhyāṃ siddhyet|
nanu pratyakṣamanumānādyapekṣayā jātyaiva prabalam| kathamanyathā auṣṇyapratyakṣeeṇa vahniśaityānumitipratibandhaḥ ? na ca tatropajīvyatvanibandhanaṃ pratyakṣasya bādhakatvam| dharmyādeścakṣurādinaiva siddhestvaco'nupajīvyatvāt| kiñca pratyakṣasya prābalyamanumānādya gṛhītarekhoparekhādigrāhakatvādanumānādyanivartitadiṅmohādinivartakatvācca iti cenna| tvācapratyakṣasyāpyupajīvyatvenaiva śaityānumitipratibandhakatvasambhavāt| cakṣurādinā dharmyādi grahe'pi tvacaṃ vinā sādhyaprasiddherabhāvāt| tathā ca na jātyā prābalye mānamasti|
tadagṛhītagrāhitvamapi na prābalye prayojakam| pratyakṣāgṛhītadharmādigrāhakatvena parokṣapramāṇasyaiva prābalyāpatteḥ| nāpyanumānādyanivartitadiṅmohādinivartakatvena prābalyam| etāvatā hi vaidharmyamātraṃ siddham| na ca tāvatetarapramāṇāpekṣayā prābalyaṃ bhavati| anyathā tvācapratyakṣānivartitavaṃśoragabhramanivartakatvāccakṣuṣo'pi tvagapekṣayā prābalyaṃ syāt| tataśca citranimnonnatajñānasya cākṣuṣasya tadvirodhitvācajñānād bādho na syāt| pratyutāgamasyaiva sarvataḥ prābalyaṃ smāryate -- `prābalyamāgamasyaiva jātyā teṣu triṣu smṛtam' iti| na ca tadvaidikārthaviṣayamiti vācyam| advaitasyāpi vaidikārthatvāt| `kva ca pratyakṣataḥ' prāptamanumāgamabādhitam iti tu parīkṣitaprāmāṇ‌yapratyakṣaviṣayam| nanu -- pratyakṣasyāsañjāta virodhitvādupakramamanyāyenaiva prābalyam| uktaṃ hi -- `asañjātavirodhitvādarthavādo yathāśrutaḥ| āstheyastadviruddhasya vidhyuddeśasya lakṣaṇā' iti cenna| yata ekavākyasthaparasparasāpekṣapadatvena ubhayoḥ sāmye satyupakramasthavedapadānurodhenopasaṃhārasthargādipadānāṃ mantramātravācināṃ kṛtsnavedaparatve nirṇīte'pi na prakṛte tannyāyaḥ sambhavati| ubhayoḥ sāmyābhāvād| gṛhītapramāṇabhāvaśrutyapekṣayā bhramavilakṣaṇatvenāniścitasya pratyakṣasya nyūnabalatvāt| anyathā `idaṃ rajataṃ' iti bhramo'pi `iyaṃ śuktiḥ' iti āptopadeśāpekṣayā prabalaḥ syāt| etena liṅgāt śruteriva śīghragāmitvāt pratyakṣasya prābalyam| taduktam -- `pratyakṣe cānumāne ca yathā loke balābalam| śrīghramantharagāmitvāttathaiva śrutiliṅgayoḥ' ityapāstam; parīkṣitasya mantharagāmino'pi prābalyāt|
na ca `yadāhavanīye juhoti' ityasmāt `pade juhoti' ityasya viśeṣaviṣayatvena prābalyavad ghaṭaviṣayasattvāgrāhiṇaḥ pratyakṣasya sāmānyato dvaitaniṣedhakaśrutyapekṣayā prābalyamiti vācyam| sāmānyaviśeṣanyāyasya niścitapramāṇabhāvobhayaviṣayatvāt| anyathā `ayaṃ gauraśvaḥ' ityāderapi gauraśvo na bhavatītyāditaḥ prābalyaṃ bhavet|
na ca yathā `yatkiñcitprācīnamagnīṣomīyāttenopāṃśu caranti' ityatratyasya yatkiñcicchabdasya yatkiñcitprakṛtavācitvena sāmānyāviṣayatve'pi dīkṣaṇīyāvyatirikte sāvakāśatvād `yāvatyā vācā kāmayīta tāvatyā dīkṣaṇīyāyāmanubrūyāt ' ityanena niravakāśena saṅkocastathā pratyakṣeṇa niravakāśena vṛttyantareṇānekārthatvena vā viṣayāntaraparatvena sāvakāśāyāḥ śruteḥ saṅkocaḥ kiṃ na syāditi vācyam| tātparyaliṅgairupakramādibhirdvaitaniṣedhaparatve avadhṛte advaitaśruterapi niravakāśatvāt| pratyakṣasyāpi vyāvahārikadvaitaviṣayatayā sāvakāśatvād| viruddhayośca dvayoḥ `ahaṃ manuṣyaḥ' ityādipratyakṣa `ākāśavatsarvagataśca nityaḥ 'ityādiśrutyoriva tāttvikaprāmāṇyānupapattyā kasyacid vyāvahārikaṃ kasyacittātvikaṃ prāmāṇyamabhyupeyam| atyantāprāmāṇyasyānyāyyatvāt| tatrādvaitaśrutervyāvahārikaprāmāṇyasambhave dvaitagrāhipratyakṣā destāttvikaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ bhavet| tadasambhave tu balādevādvaitaśrutestāttvikaṃ prāmāṇyamiti pratyakṣādervyāvahārikaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ paryavasyatīti kṛtabuddhayo vidāṅkurvantu|
nanu pañcadaśarātre prathame'hanyagniṣṭunnāmake nāmātideśena ekāhāgniṣṭuddharmabhūtā subrahmaṇyāgneyī prāptā| tasyā alpaviṣayāyāścaturdaśāhassu codakena prāptayā aindryā subrahmaṇyayā bahuviṣayayā yathā bādhaḥ, bahubādhasyānyāyyatvāt tathā dvaitagrāhipratyakṣatadupajīvyanumānakarmakāṇḍa saguṇopāsanāvākyādirūpa bahupramāṇābādhāyādvaitavākyasya pratītārthabādhaḥ kiṃ na syāt ? taduktam -- `bahupramāṇavirodhe caikasyāprāmāṇyaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ śuktirajatādijñāne ' iti cenna| dṛṣṭānte bahuviṣayayā bādho'tra bahubhiriti vaiṣamyāt| dehātmaikye pratyakṣānumānaśabdābhāsādisattve'pi dehātmabhedabodhakasyānanya paratvenaprābalyavadatrāpi ananyaparatvenādvaitaśruteḥ prābalyād, vidyāvidyābhedena vidvadavidvatpuruṣabhedena ca virodhābhāvāditi| || pratyakṣasya jātyupakrama nyāyādibhiḥ prābalyanirākaraṇam||
nanu -- uktanyāyaiḥ pratyakṣasya jātyā prābalyābhāve'pi upajīvyatvena prābalyam| upajīvyatvaṃ cānumānāgamāpekṣitāśeṣārthagrāhakatayā| sā ca kvacit sākṣāt kvacitparamparayā dṛṣṭaṃ cāpekṣitaikadeśagrāhiṇāmapyupajīvyatvam, tadviruddhagrahaṇe tena bādhaśca| yathā ghaṭavibhutvānumāne pakṣagrāhiṇā akṣṇā, naraśiraśśucitvānumāne sādhyagrāhakeṇāgamena, manovaibhavānumāne jñānāsamavāyyādhāratvahetugrāhakeṇānumānena| kimu vaktavyamapekṣitāśeṣagrāhiṇā svaviruddhagrāhakasya bādhaḥ ? cakṣurādeśca śabdatajjanyajñānaprāmāṇyādyagrāhitve'pi tad‌grāhiśrotrasākṣyādisajātīya tvādupajīvyatvam| dṛṣṭaṃ ca naraśiraḥ kapālāśucitvabodhakāgamasya tacchucitvānumānopa jīvyaśucitvāgamasajātīyatvena tadanumānāt prābalyam| na cendriyamapi svajñānārtha manumānamupajīvatīti sama evopajīvyopajīvakabhāvaḥ| ajñātakaraṇatayā jñānajananārtha manumānānapekṣaṇāt| anumānāgamādinā tu jñānajananārthameva tadapekṣaṇāditi viśeṣād iti cenna| upajīvyāvirodhāt| tathā hi -- yatsvarūpamupajīvyate tanna bādhyate| bādhyate ca tāttvikatvākāraḥ| sa ca nopajīvyate| kāraṇatve tasyāpraveśāt| taduktam -- 
`pūrvasambandhaniyame hetutve tulya eva nau|
hetutatvabahirbhūtasattvāsattvakathā vṛthā||' iti
kiñcāpekṣitagrāhitvamātreṇa cedupajīvyatā, tayā ca bādhakatvam, tadā'pekṣitapratiyogigrāhakatvena `idaṃ rajatam' iti bhramasya bādhopajīvyatvāt kathaṃ `nedaṃ rajatam' iti bādhabuddhistadviruddhodiyāt ? atha niṣedhyārthasamarpakatayā pratiyogijñānatvena tasyopajīvyatve'pi tatprāmāṇyaṃ nopajīvyam| na hi pratiyogipramātvenābhāvajñānajanakatā, gauravāt| pratiyogibhramādapyabhāvajñānadarśanācca| kintu tajjñānatvenaiva, lāghavāt| atastadviruddhaviṣayakaṃ jñānamudiyādeveti brūṣe, tulyamidaṃ prakṛte'pi, pakṣajñānatvādinā kāraṇatā, na tu tatpramātvādināpīti| atha yatprāmāṇyaṃ svarūpasiddhyarthamapavādanirāsārthaṃ ca yatprāmāṇyamupajīvati tattasyopajīvyam, yathā smṛteranubhavaḥ| na ca rajatabhramastathā iti cet, tarhi vyāptidhiyo'pi nānumityupajīvyatvaṃ syāt| liṅgābhāsādapi vahnimati vahnipramādarśanāt| nanu yena vinā yasyotthānaṃ nāsti tattasyopajīvyamityeva vaktavyam| tathā ca rajatabhramasyopajīvyatvamastyeva, na tu prābalyam| na hyupajīvyatvamātreṇa prābalyam| kintu parīkṣitatayā| parīkṣā ca sajātīyavijātīyasaṃvādavisaṃvādābhāvau| na ca tau rajatabhrame staḥ| prakṛte cākṣasya parīkṣitatvena prābalyam| asti hi `san ghaṭaḥ' iti viśeṣadarśanajanyajñānāntaraṃ ghaṭārthakriyāpratyakṣe klṛptadūrādidoṣābhāvaśca| evameva jīveśābhedaśrutau niṣedhyārpakabhedaśrutiḥ sākṣipratyakṣaṃ cādoṣatvāt parīkṣitamiti| tadapi na bādhyam| evameva ca doṣābhāvādijñānarūpaparīkṣāyāmapi anāśvāse vede pauruṣeyatvābhāvajñāne, tvaduktānumāne ca yogyānupalabdhyādinā hetvābhāsādirāhityajñāne, brahmamīmāṃsāyāṃ pratyadhikaraṇaṃ siddhāntyabhipretārrtha, upakramādyānugupyajñāne cānāśvāsaḥ syāditi pramāṇatadābhāsavyavasthā na syāt iti cenna| parīkṣā hi pravṛttisaṃvādavisaṃvādābhāvadoṣābhāvādirūpā| tayā ca svasamānadeśakālīnaviṣayābādhyatvaṃ prāmāṇyasya vyavasthāpyate, dhūmena svasamānadeśa kālīnavahniriva| tathā ca vyavahāradaśāmātrābādhyatvaṃ dehātmaikyasādhāraṇaṃ parīkṣitapramāṇe vyavasthitamiti kathamatyantābādhyatvābhāvagrāhakāgamānumānayoḥ pravṛttirna syāt ? tasmādviśvāsa -- pramāṇatadābhāsavyavasthā jīveśabhedādikaṃ ca vyāvahārikamityupapannameva sarvaṃ jaganmithyeti|
nanu -- pratyakṣāprāmāṇye tatsiddhasya vyāptyaderbādhenānumeyāderanumityādiprāmāṇyasya ca bādhaḥ| anumeyādervyāptyādinā anumitiprāmāṇyādinā ca samānayogakṣematvāt| anyathā prātibhāsikavyāptyādimatā bāṣpādhyastadhūmena tātviko vyāvahāriko vāgnirvyāva hārikavyāptyādimatā dhūmena tātviko'gnirvyāvahārikeṇābādhena viruddhadharmādhikaraṇatvena ca viśvasya jīveśabhedasya ca tātvikaṃ sattvaṃ sidhyet -- iti cenna| etāvatā hi vyāptyādisamānasattākamanumeyaṃ siddhyatvityāpatteḥ phalito'rthaḥ| sa cāyamanumeyādervyāptyādinā samasattākatvaniyamo'pyasti| vyabhicāriṇāpi liṅgena sādhyavati pakṣe anumitipramādarśanāt| dhvanidharmahnasvatvadīrghatvādiviśiṣṭatvena mithyābhūtairapi nityairvibhubhirvarṇaiḥ satyā śābdapramitiḥ kriyata iti mīmāṃsakairabhyupagamāt| gandhaprāgabhāvāvacchinne ghaṭe tātvikavyāptyādimatāpi pṛthivītvenātātvikagandhānumitidarśanāt| pratibimbena na bimbānumitidarśanāt| na ca tatrāpi bimbarahitāvṛttitvarūpā vyāptistātvikyeveti vācyam| evaṃ satyavṛttigaganāderapi vyāpyatāpatteḥ| na ca tatra bimbapūrvakatvamevānumīyate, bimbavyatirekaprayukta vyatirekapratiyogitvarūpeṇāprātibhāsikena hetuneti vācyam| prayuktatvaṃ hi na tajjanakatvajanyatvādirūpam| vyatirekayoḥ parasparaṃ tadabhāvāt| kintu vyāpyavyāpakabhāvaḥ| tathā ca bimbavyatirekavyāpakavyatirekapratiyogitvaṃ hetuḥ| sa cākāśādau vyabhicāryeva| tasmāttatra pratibimbenaiva bimbānumānam| anumeyasya liṅgavyāptyādisamāna sattākatvaniyamasyāpāstatvāt| etena śabde'pi yogyatāsamānasattākena śabdārthena bhavitavyam, yogyatāvākyārthayoḥ samānasattākatvaniyamāditi kathaṃ vedāntavākyārtho yogyatābādhe'pyabādhitaḥ syaditi parastam| vedāntavākye akhaṇḍārtharūpavākyārthābādharūpāyā yogyatāyā apyabādhācca| na ca tathāpi vedāntatajjñānatatprāmāṇyamithyātve kathaṃ tātvikādvaitasiddhiriti vācyam| śabdatajjñānatātvikatvaṃ hi na viṣayatātvikatve tantram| idaṃ rajatamityanāptavākyasya tajjanyabhramasya ca tvanmate tātvikatve'pi tadviṣayasyātātvikatvāt| na ca jñānaprāmāṇyasya mithyātve viṣayasyāpi mithyātvaṃ śuktirūpyajñāne dṛṣṭamiti prakṛte'pi jñānaprāmāṇ‌yamithyātve viṣayasyāpi mithyātvaṃ syāditi vācyam| prāmāṇyamithyātvaṃ hi na viṣayamithyātve prayojakam| bhramapramābāhirbhūte nirvikalpake viṣayabādhābhāvāt| kintu tadabhāvavati tatprakārakatvādirūpamaprāmāṇyameva tathā| tacca prakṛte nāstyeva| na ca arthābādharūpaprāmāṇyasya mithyātvādarthasyāpi mithyātvaṃ syāditi vācyam; abādhitārthaviṣayatvaṃ hi yat prāmāmyaṃ tasya mithyātvam prakṛte nārthabādhāt| tadbādhakapramāṇāsambhavāt| tasya sarvabādhāvadhitvāt| kintu tadviṣayatvarūpasambandhabādhāttathā| tathā cābādhitārthaviṣayatvarūpapramāṇyamithyātve'pi nārtho mithyā| viśiṣṭasyaikāṃśamithyātve'pyaparāṃśasatyatvāt, yathā daṇḍabādha nibandhanadaṇḍipuruṣabādhe'pi puruṣo na bādhita eveti| || iti advaitasiddhau pratyakṣasyopajīvyatvabhaṅgaḥ||
kiñca vipakṣabādhakasacivamanumānamapi pratyakṣabādhakam| nanu -- evamapi `audumbarīṃ spṛṣṭvā udgāyet', `aindryā gārhapatyamupatiṣṭhate', `śaramayaṃ barhirbhavati' iti śrutitrayagrāhi pratyakṣaṃ yathākramaṃ `audumbarī sarvā veṣṭayitavyā' iti smṛtirūpeṇa sarvaveṣṭanaśrutyanumānena, `kadācana starīrasi nendra saścasi dāśuṣe' iti mantrasāmarthyalakṣaṇenendraśeṣatvaśrutyanumānena codanāliṅgarūpeṇa kuśaśrutyanumānena ca bādhyeteti sarvamīmāṃsonmūlinā syāditi -- cenna| vaiṣamyāt| tathā hi -- kimidamāpādyate, śrutitrayagrāhipratyakṣamanumānairbādhyeteti vā, pratyakṣaviṣayībhūtaśrutitrayamiti vā ? nādyaḥ, virodhābhāvena tadbādhyabādhakabhāvasya śāstrārthatvābhāvāt, asmābhigaranabhyupagamācca| anuktopālambhamātratve niranuyojyānu yogāpatteḥ| ataeva na dvitīyaḥ| pratyakṣaviṣayībhūtaśrutitrayasya liṅgabādhakatvapare'pi śāstre pratyakṣasya liṅgabādhyatve virodhābhāvāt| nahi śabdapratyakṣayoraikyamasti| śabdasyaca sarvapramāṇāpekṣayā balavattvamavocāma| tasmānmauḍhyamātrametanmīmāṃsāvirodhodbhāvanam|
nanu pratyakṣasya liṅgabādhyatve bahnyauṣṇyapratyakṣaṃ śaityānumānasya ātmasthāyitvapratyabhijñānaṃ ca kṣaṇikatvānumānasya bādhakaṃ na syāt, pratyutānumānameva tayorbādhakaṃ syāt ithi cenna| arthakriyāsaṃvādena śrutyanugraheṇa ca tatra pratyakṣayoḥ prābalyenānumānabādhakatvāt| aparīkṣitapratyakṣaṃ hi parīkṣitānumānāpekṣayā durbalaṃ, `nīlaṃ nabhaḥ' iti pratyakṣamiva nabho nīrūpatvānumānāpekṣayā| ato na sāmānyato dṛṣṭamātreṇa sarvasaṅkarāpattiḥ| nanvevaṃ paśutvena śrṛṅgānumānamapi syāt| lāghavāt paśutvameva śrṛṅgitve tantraṃ, na tu tadviśeṣagotvādikam; ananugatatvena gauravādityetattarkasadhrīcīnatvena pratyakṣāpekṣayā prābalyāt| anukūlatarkasācivyameva hi anumāne balam| evaṃ ca yena kenacit sāmān‌yadharmeṇa sarvatra yatkiñcidanumeyam, lāghavatarkasācivyasya sattvāt| tāvataiva ca pratyakṣabādhakatvāditi vyāvahārikyapi vyavasthā na syāt| na hyatra pratyakṣabādhādanyo doṣo'sti iti cenna| ayogyaśrṛṅgādisādhane pratyakṣabādhasyāsambhavena tatra vyāptigrāhakatarkeṣvābhāsatvasya tvayā'pi vaktavyatvena vyavasthāyā ubhayasamādheyatvāt| na hi tarkābhāsasadhrīcīnamanumānaṃ pramāṇamiti kenāpyupeyate| ata upapannaṃ sattarkasacivamanumānaṃ pratyakṣasya bādhakamiti| iti pratyakṣasyānumānabādhyatvasiddhiḥ|
kiṃ ca parīkṣitapramāṇabhāvaśabdabādhyamapi pratyakṣam| nanu -- pratyakṣaṃ yadi śabdabādhyaṃ syāttadā jaimininā `tasmāddhūma evāgnerdivā dadṛśe nārciḥ' ityādyarthavādasya `aditirdyauḥ' ityādimantrasya ca dṛṣṭavirodhenāprāmāṇye prāpte `guṇavādastu' `guṇādavipratiṣedhaḥ syāt' ityādinā gauṇārthatā nocyeta| `tatsiddhijātisārūpyapraśaṃsābhūmaliṅgasamavāyād' -- iti tatsiddhipeṭikāyāṃ `yajamānaḥ prastara' ityādegauṇārthatā ca nocyeta| tvayā'pi pratyakṣāvirodhāya tattvampadayorlakṣaṇā nocyetā| śrutivirodhe pratyakṣasyaivāprāmāṇyasambhavāt| na ca -- tātparyaliṅgānāmupakramādīnāmatra sattvānnādvaitaśrutīnāmamukhyārthatvamiti vācyam| `yajamānaḥ prastaraḥ' ityādāvapyapūrvatvādyekaikaliṅgasya tātparyagrāhakasya vidyamānatvāt| ekaikaliṅgasya tātparyanirṇāyakatve liṅgāntaramanuvādakameva| tvanmate pratyakṣasiddhe bhede śrutiriva| kiṃ bāhulyena iti cenna| vākyaśeṣapramāṇāntarasaṃvādārthakriyādirūpaparīkṣāparīkṣitasya pratyakṣasya prābalyena vyavahāradaśāyāmeva etadviruddhārthagrāhiṇo `dhūma evāgnerdivā dadṛśe', `aditirdyauḥ' `jayamānaḥ prastara' ityādestadvirodhenāmukhyārthatve'pyadvaitāgamasya parīkṣitapramāṇavirodhābhāvena mukhyārthatvopapatteḥ| pratyakṣāderhi parīkṣayā vyāvahārikaprāmāṇyamātraṃ siddham| tacca nādvaitāgamena bādhyate| bādhyate tu tātvikaṃ prāmāṇyam| tattu parīkṣayā na siddhameva| ato na varodhaḥ| `dhūma evāgneḥ' ityādestu mukhyārthatve pratyakṣādervyāvahārikaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ vyāhanyeta| ato virodhāttatrāmukhyārthatvamiti vivekaḥ|
yattu -- pratyakṣāvirodhāya tattvampadayorlakṣaṇā nāśrīyeteti tanna| ṣaḍvidhaliṅgairgatisāmānyena cākhaṇḍa evāvadhāryamāṇasya tātparyasyānupapatterjīveśagatasarvajñatvakiñcijjñatvādīnā maikyānvayānupapatteśca| tātparyaviṣayībhūtākhaṇḍapratītinirvāhāya lakṣaṇāṅgīkaraṇasyaivocitatvāt| tātparyaviṣayībhūtānvayanirvāhāya lakṣaṇāśrayaṇasya sarvatra darśanāt| na ca evaṃ sati amukhyārthatvaṃ syāditi vācyam| taddhi pratīyamānārthaparityāgenārthāntaraparatvaṃ vā ? aśakyārthatvaṃ vā ? nādyaḥ| sāmānādhikaraṇyena pratīyamānasyaikyasyātyāgāt| nāntyaḥ, jahadajahallakṣaṇāśrayaṇena śakyaikadeśaparityāge'pi `so'yaṃ devadattaḥ' ityādivākya iva śakyaikadeśasyānvayābhyupagamāt| viśeṣaṇabādhena viśeṣyamātrānvayasyaivātra lakṣaṇāśabdena vyapadeśāt| tathā coktaṃ vācaspatimiśraiḥ -- `prastarādivākyamanyaśeṣatvādamukhyārtham, advaita vākyaṃ tvananyaśeṣatvānmukhyārthameva| uktaṃ hi śābarabhāṣye -- `na vidhau paraḥ śabdārtha' iti| yathā cāpūrvatvādyekaikatātparyaliṅgena `yajamānaḥ prastaraḥ' ityādyarthavādavākyānāṃ na svārthaparatvaṃ tathā vakṣyāmaḥ| nanu anyaśeṣatvānanyaśeṣatve nāmukhyārthatvamukhyārthatvayoḥ prayojake| kiṃ tu mānāntaravirodhāvirodhau| anyaśeṣe'pi mānāntarāvirodhe `iyaṃ gauḥ krayyā bahukṣīrā' ityādau loke, `so'rodīt' ityādau ca vede prastarādivākyavadamukhyavṛtteranāśrayaṇād| ananyaśeṣe'pi `somena yajeta' ityādau vaiyadhikaraṇyenānvaye viruddhatrikadvayāpattyā sāmānādhikaraṇyenānvaye pratyakṣāvirodhāya ca somavatā yāgeneti matvarthalakṣaṇāyā āśrayaṇāt| evaṃ vicāravidhāyake `athāto brahmajijñāsā' iti sūtre `tadvijijñāsasva' iti
