śrutau ca mānāntaravirodhena vidhyanvayāya jijñāsāśabdena vicāralakṣaṇāyāḥ `sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma' ityādau cāmukhyārthatāyāḥ svīkṛtatvāt| sarvasyāpi vākyasyāvācye brahmaṇi lakṣaṇāyā eveṣṭatvenāmukhyārthatvaniṣedhāyogācca| anvayānupapattestātparyānupapattervā lakṣaṇābījasya vidhyavidhisādhāraṇatvācca| śābaraṃ tu vacanamarthavādamukhyātvāya vidhau na lakṣaṇetyevamparam| tasmānna pratyakṣaṃ śabdabādhyam iti cenna| bhāvānavabodhāt| tātparyaviṣayībhūtārthabodhakatvaṃ hi mukhyārthatvam| na śakyārthamātrabodhakatvam| anyārthatātparyakatvāccamukhyārthatvam| na lākṣaṇikatvamātram| tathā cādvaitāgamasya svatātparyaviṣayībhūtārthabodhakatvanirvāhāya lakṣaṇāśrayaṇe'pi mukhyārthatvamupapannamityavocāma| evaṃ ca `somena yajeta' ityādi viśiṣṭavidherviśeṣaṇe tātparyābhāvānmatvarthalakṣaṇāyāmapi svārthāparityāgācca nāmukhyārthatvam| jijñāsāpade tu jñādhātuneṣyamāṇajñānalakṣaṇāṅgīkārānaṅgīkāramatabhede'pi satpratyayasya vicāre jahallakṣaṇābhyupagamasyobhayatra tulyatvāt, śakyārthaparityāge'pi vidhitātparyanirvāhāt nāmukhyārthatvam| nahi vākyārthapratītyanyathānupapattyā padamātre lakṣaṇāyāmapi vākyasyāmukhyārthatvam| pratītasyārthasyānanyaśeṣatvena mukhyatvāt| yatra punaḥ pratīta eva vākyārtho'nyaśeṣatvena kalpyate, tatra vākyasyāmukhyārthatvameva| anyaddhi padatātparyamanyacca vākyatātparyam| `saundhavamānaya' `gaṅgāyāṃ vasanti' ityādau vākyatātparyaikye'pi padatātparyabhedāt| `viṣaṃ bhuṅkṣva' ityādau padatātparyābhede'pi vākyatātparyabhedāt| ata eva `iyaṃ gauḥ krayyā bahukṣīrā' ityādivākyārthasyāvaśyaṃ kretavyeti vidhiśeṣatvena tatprāśastyalakṣakatvāt, `so'rodhīt' ityādi vākyārthasya ca `barhiṣi rajataṃ na deyaṃ hiraṇyaṃ dakṣiṇā ' iti vidhiśeṣatvena rajatanindādvārā tatprāśastyalakṣakatvāt| `sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma tajjalān' iti vākyārthasya `śāntaupāsīta' iti śamavidhiśeṣatvenātyanāyāsasiddhatvarūpatatprāśastyalakṣakatvādamukhyatvameva| ataeva mānāntara virodha eva lakṣaṇetyapāstam| `iyaṃ gauḥ krayyā bahukṣīrā' ityādinā prāśastyalakṣaṇāyāṃ vyabhicārāt| kiṃ tu paramatātparyaviṣayībhūtārthapratītinirvāhāyaiva sarvārthavādeṣu lakṣaṇā| etāvāṃstu viśeṣaḥ -- vidhiprāśastye lakṣaṇātaḥ prāgarthavādavākyārthajñānam| tasyapramāṇāntaravirodhe bādha eva, yathā `prajāpatirātmano vapāmudakhidat' ityādau| ataeva tatra guṇavādamātram| pramāṇān‌taraprāptau tvanuvādamātram `agnirhimasya bheṣajaṃ' ityādau| ataeva tadubhayatrābādhitājñātajñāpakatvarūpaprāmāṇyaśarīrānirvāhādaprāmāṇyam| yatra punaḥ pramāṇāntaraprāptivirodhau na stastatra prāmāṇyaśarīranirvāhād bhūtārthavādatvam‌ -- yathā `indro vṛtrāya vajramudayacchat' ityādau ayameva devatādhikaraṇanyāyaḥ| nanu tarhi `ādityo yūpaḥ' ityādau vākyārthapratītyarthameva lakṣaṇāṅgīkārādamukhyārthatvaṃ na syāt| na syādyadyādityasadṛśo yūpa iti vākyārthaparyavasānaṃ syāt| kiṃ tu guṇavṛttyā pratītasyāpi vākyārthasya yūpe paśuṃ badhnātīti vidhiśeṣatvena tatprāśastyalakṣakatvamastyeva, tenaivāmukhyatvam, na tvādityapadagauṇatayeti tatsiddhipeṭikāyāṃ sarvodāharaṇeṣvapyavāntaravākyārthapratītaye guṇavṛttiprakārāḥ pradarśitā iti draṣṭavyam| karmaprāśastyalakṣaṇā tu sarvārthavādasādhāraṇī tatrāstyeveti nāmukhyārthatvānupapattiḥ| ata upapannaṃ prastarādivākyavaiṣamyam advaitavākyasya| yaccoktam arthavādamukhyārthatvāya vidhau na lakṣaṇetyevamparaṃ śabarasvāmivacanamiti, tanna| aśvapratigraheṣṭau `pratigṛhṇīyāt' iti vidhau pratigrāhayediti vyavadhāraṇakalpanayā arthavādānu sāreṇa prayojakavyāpāralakṣaṇāyā aṅgīkāraṇāt| tasmādvidhau tātparyavati vākye pratīyamānavākyārthātirikto'nyaḥ śeṣī nāstītyevamparameva tadvacanam| ataḥ siddhamadvaitāgamasya lākṣaṇikatve'pi mukhyārthatvāt pratyakṣabādhakatvamiti śivam|| iti pratyakṣasyāgamabādhyatvam||
kiṃ cāpacchedanyāyenāpyāgamasya prābalyam| yathā hi `paurvāparye pūrvadaurbalyaṃ prakṛtivat' ityadhikaraṇe udgātrapacchedanimittakādakṣiṇayāgena pareṇa pratihartrapacchedanimittaka sarvasvadakṣiṇayāgasya pūrvasiddhanimittasya bādha iti sthitaṃ, tathehāpi udīcyāgamena pūrvasya pratyakṣasya bādhaḥ| nanu pratihartrapacchedanimittakasarvasvadakṣiṇayāgasya pratihartṛmātrāpacchede, yugapadapacchede, krameṇāpacchede'pi pratihartrapacchedasya paścāttve cāvakāśa iti yuktaḥ udgātrapacchedanimittakādakṣiṇayāgena bādhaḥ| anyathā `yadi pratihartā apacchidyeta sarvavedasaṃ dadyāt' iti śāstramaprāmāṇaṃ syāt| ata eva `vipratiṣedhādvikalpaḥ syāt' ityadhikaraṇe dvayoryugapadapacchede vikalpa uktaḥ| kiṃ ca `yadyudgātā jaghanyaḥ syātpunaryajñe sarvavedasaṃ dadyādyathetarasmin' ityadhikaraṇe udgātrapacchedasya pratihartrapacchedātparatve udgātrapacchedanimittaṃ pūrvaṃ prayogaṃ dakṣiṇāhīnaṃ sampādya kartavyajyotiṣṭomasya dvitīyaprayoge `taddadyādyatpūrvasmin dāsyan syāt' iti śrutyuktā yā dakṣiṇā sā pūrvabhāvipratihartrapaccheda nimittakapūrvaprayogasthasarvasvaditsāyā abādhena sarvasvarūpaiva, na tu yā jyotiṣṭome nityā dvādaśaśatarūpā| tasmānna pratihartrapacchedasya sarvathā bādhaḥ| kiṃ tu prayogāntare nikṣepa ityuktam| uktaṃ hi ṭipṭīkāyām -- `tasya prayogāntare nikṣepaḥ' iti| api ca kramikanimittadvayena krameṇādakṣiṇasarvasvadakṣiṇayoḥ prayogayoḥ sambhavena virodha eva nāsti, yathā badaraphale kramikanimittavattoḥ śyāmaraktarūpayoḥ| uktaṃ hyapacchedādhikaraṇe -- `naimittakaśāstrasya hyayamarthaḥ -- nimittopajananāt prāganyathākartavyo'pi kraturnimitte satyevaṃ kartavyaḥ' iti| tasmādapacchedanyāyaḥ sāvakāśaviṣayaḥ| advaitāgamena pratyakṣabādhe tu na pratyakṣapramāṇyasyāvakāśo'sti iti cenna| udgātrapacchedābhāve yugapadubhayāpacchede pratihartrapacchedasya udgātrapacchede paścāttve ca jyotiṣṭomadvitīyaprayoge pratihartrapacchedanimittaka sarvasvadakṣiṇayāgapratipādakaśāstrasya sāvakāśatvavadvyāvahārikaprāmāṇye pratyakṣasyāpi sāvakāśatvāt| tatraikaprayoge virodhavadatrāpi tāttvikatvāṃśe virodhāt| ata eva saguṇasaprapañcaśrutyornirguṇaniṣprapañcaśrutibhyāmapacchedanyāyena bādha iti suṣṭhūktam| taduktamānandabodhācāryauḥ -- `tatparatvātparatvācca nirdoṣatvācca vaidikam| pūrvasya bādhakaṃ nāyaṃ sarpa ityādivākyavat||' iti|
nanu mānāntaravirodhe śrutestatparatvamasiddham| paratvaṃ tu pramānantarabhrame vyabhicāri| dṛśyate ca `na ktvā seṭ' iti paraṃ prati `mṛḍamṛdagudhakuśakliśavadavasaḥ ktvā' iti pūrvamapi bādhakam| nirdoṣatvaṃ tvarthāntaraprāmāṇyenānyathāsiddham| taduktam -- `tatparatvamasiddhatvātparatvaṃ vyabhicārataḥ| nirdoṣatā'nyathāsiddheḥ prābalyaṃ naiva sādhayet|| iti cenna| pratyakṣādervyāvahārikaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ, śrutestu tāttvikamiti virodhābhāvena tatparatvasiddheḥ| paraśabdena ca mānāntarābādhitaparatvaṃ vivakṣitam| tena pramānantarabhrame na vyabhicāraḥ| tasya taduttarabhāvimānabādhyatvāt| `na ktvā seṭ' ityasya tu pāṭhataḥ paratve'pi svabhāvasiddhakittvasyānenāpākaraṇaṃ vinā punastatpratiprasavārtha `mṛḍamṛda' ityāderapravṛttestadapekṣayā arthataḥ pūrvatvameva| apavādāpavāde utsargasyaiva sthiratvāt| nirdoṣatvamapi nānyathāsiddham| tātparyaviṣaya eva prāmāṇyasyābhyupeyatvād ityabodhamātravijṛmbhitamapaccheda nyāyavaiṣamyābhidhānamiti| || ityapacchedanyāyavaiṣamyabhaṅgaḥ||'
nanu yadi pratyakṣabādhitamapyanumānaṃ sādhayettadā vahnyanauṣṇyamapi sādhayet| tathā ca kālātyayāpadiṣṭakathā sarvatrocchidyeta| na ca auṣṇyapratiyogikābhāve sādhye pakṣa eva pratiyogiprasiddhirita tatra bādhaḥ sāvakāśaḥ| prakṛte tu sattvaṃ vyāvahārikaṃ pratyakṣasiddham| tadaviruddhaṃ ca mithyātvam, tasya pāramārthikasattvavirodhitvāt| ato na vyāvahārikasattvagrāhakeṇādhyakṣeṇa bādhyata iti vācyam| vahniviśeṣe auṣṇyābhāvānumāne śaityānumāne vā tadabhāvāt| pakṣātiriktasya pratiyogiprasiddhisthalasya tatra sattvāt| naca yatra pratyakṣaṃ prabalaṃ tatra bādhavyavasthā, na cātra tatheti na bādha iti vācyam| prakṛte'pyauṣṇyapratyakṣasamakakṣasya prābalyaprayojakasya vidyamānatvāt| anauṣṇyānu mitermithyātvānumiteśca samānayogakṣematvāt| na ca mithyātvavādināṃ pratipannopādhāvauṣṇyaniṣedha grāhyanumānena mithyātvānumiteḥ samatvamiṣṭameveti vācyam| auṣṇyānauṣṇyayorbhāvābhāvarūpatayā tadanumitisāmye'pi śaityānumitisāmyasyānabhyupagamāt| śaityasyauṣṇyābhāvarūpatvābhāvāt| tasmād bādhasya doṣatā vā tyājyā, auṣṇyapratyakṣā -- yajamānatvapratyakṣādeḥ sattvapratyakṣāpekṣayā viśeṣo vā vaktavyaḥ| na ca auṣṇyapratyakṣaṃ parīkṣitobhayavādisiddhapramāṇyam| sattvapratyakṣaṃ tu na tatheti viśeṣa iti vācyam| sattvapratyakṣe'pi prāmāṇyāsammatau hetvabhāvāt| parīkṣāyāstulyatvād iti cenmaivam| viruddhārthagrāhitvena viśeṣāt| pratyakṣasiddhāya jamānatvauṣṇyādivacchabdaliṅgagrāhyayajamānatvānauṣṇyādyapi vyāvahārikamiti samatvātpratyakṣeṇa bādhyate| prakṛte tu sattvaṃ vyāvahārikaṃ pratyakṣasiddham| tadviruddhaṃ ca na mithyātvam| tasya pāramārthikasatvavirodhitvāt | ato na tad vyāvahārikasattvagrāhakeṇādhyakṣeṇa bādhyate|
nanu evaṃ vadatastava ko'bhiprāyaḥ ? kiṃ tāttvikaviṣayatvād bādhakataiva mithyātvānumānāderna bādhyatā ? uta sattvamithyātvagrāhiṇorvyāvahārikattvikaviṣayayoḥ parasparaviruddhaviṣayatvābhāvāt na bādhyabādhakabhāvaḥ ? antye'pi kimadhyakṣasiddhavyāvahārikasattvamagṛhītvaiva tadasiddhasya tāttvikasattvasyaivābhāvaṃ gṛhṇātyanumānādi ? uta pratyakṣaviṣayīkṛtasyaiva tāttvikamabhāvam ? nāntyaḥ| pratyakṣaviṣayābhāvagrāhiṇi tadabādhakatvoktyayogāt| na dvitīyaḥ| pratyakṣāgṛhītapratiṣedhakatvenāprasaktapratiṣedhāpatteḥ, pratyakṣaviṣayasya tāttvikatvāpatteśca| na prathamaḥ| upajīvyapratyakṣavirodhenānumityādiviṣayasya tātvikatvāsiddheḥ| iti cenna| prathame dvitīye ca pakṣe anupapattyabhāvāt| tathā hi -- prathame pakṣe na tāttvikatvāsiddheḥ, yasmāt `idaṃ rajataṃ' ityanena `nedaṃ rajataṃ 'ityasya bādhādarśanāt parīkṣitameva bādhakamabhyupeyam| parīkṣā ca pravṛttisaṃvādādirūpā vyavahāradaśāyāmabādhyatvaṃ vinā'nupapannā taddaśā bādhagrāhīṇaṃ bādhate, nādvaitaśrutyanumānādikamityuktameva| dvitīye'pi pakṣe nāprasaktapratiṣedhaḥ, parokṣaprasakteḥ sambhavāt| yattu, kecidātmani tāttvikasattvaprasiddhyā prasaktimupapādayanti, tanna| na hi pratiyogijñānamātraṃ prasaktiḥ,kiṃ tarhi niṣedhādhikaraṇakapratiyogijñānam| na cātmā niṣedhādhikaraṇam| tasmātparokṣaprasaktireva darśanīyā| athavā, mā bhūt prasaktiḥ| abhāvapratyakṣe hi saṃsargaropatvena sopayujyate| śabdānumānayostu tasyāḥ kvopayogaḥ| na cāprasaktau niṣedhavaiyarthyam| anarthanivṛttirūpasya prayojanasya vidyamānatvāt| na ca pratyakṣaviṣayatāttvikatvāpattiḥ, tadviṣayādhikaraṇasyaiva pāramārthikatvavyatirekasya bodhanāt| tathā ca na kā'pyanupapattiḥ| taduktaṃ khaṇḍanakṛdbhiḥ -- 
`pāramārthikamadvaitaṃ praviśya śaraṇaṃ śrutiḥ|
virodhādupajīvyena na bibheti kadācana||' iti|
nanu evamapyanauṣṇyaṃ tāttvikamiti tadanumitirapi na bādhyeta vyāvahārikauṣṇyagrāhiṇādhyakṣeṇa| evaṃ `ca ādityo yūpa' ityādāvapi
`tāttvikādityatāṃ yūpasyāśritya śaraṇaṃ śrutiḥ|
virodhāduvajīvyena na bibheti kadācana||'
ityādyapi syāt iti cenna| anauṣṇyaṃ tāttvikaṃ syāditi ko'rthaḥ ? yadi tattvata auṣṇyaṃ nāstītyarthaḥ, tadā advaite paryavasānādiṣṭāpattiḥ| yadi vyavahārato'pi nāstīti, tadā vyavahārāvisaṃvedādirūpaparīkṣitatvaviśiṣṭamauṣṇyapratyakṣaṃ bādhakamiti nānauṣṇyasya tāttvikatvasiddhiḥ| etena śaityānumānaṃ vyākhyātam| evamādityayūpabhedasya tattvato vyavahārato vā niṣedhe yojyam| śruteranyaśeṣatayā ādityayūpābhedaparatvābhāvena parīkṣitapratyakṣavirodhena gauṇārthatayā stāvakatvopapatteśca| ata eva `tāttvikādityatāṃ yūpasya' ityādinā advaitaśruteḥ `ādityo yūpaḥ' ityādiśrutisāmyāpādanam apāstam| na ca anumitisiddhamithyātvagrāhakatve satyadvaitaśrutiranuvādikā syāt, yathā'gnirhimasya bheṣajam' ityādiśrutiḥ pramāṇāntaragṛhītahimanivāraṇaśaktyanuvādiketi vācyam, svasvacamatkārānusāriṇo'numānasya sakalasādhāraṇyābhāvena tasya śrutyanuvādakatvāprayojakatvāt| taduktaṃ -- `tarkāpratiṣṭhānāt' ityatra vācaspatimiśraiḥ -- 
`yatnenānumito'pyarthaḥ kuśalairanumātṛbhiḥ|
abhiyuktatarairanyairanyathaivopapādyate||'iti|
dṛṣṭāntīkṛtaśrutau tu himanivṛttikāraṇatāyā vahnau sarvasādhāraṇapratyakṣārthāpattibhyā mavaseyatvādvaiṣamyam| tasmānmithyātvānumānasya na vahniśaityānumitisāmyam| iti mithyātvānumānasya śaityānumitisāmyabhaṅgaḥ||
kiṃ ca parīkṣitatvenaiva prābalyaṃ, nopajīvyatvādinā| anumānaśabdabādhyatvasya pratyakṣe'pi darśanāt| tathā hi -- idaṃ rajatamiti pratyakṣasyānumānāptavacanābhyāṃ, nabhonailyapratyakṣasya nīrūpatvagrāhakānumānena, `gauro'haṃ' ityasya `ahamihaivāsmi sadane jānānaḥ' ityasya candraprādeśikatvapratyakṣasya cānumānāgamābhyāṃ `pītaḥ śaṅkhastikto guḍaḥ' ityādeścānumānāptavacanābhyāṃ bādho dṛśyate| nanu sākṣātkāribhrame sākṣātkāriviśeṣadarśanameva virodhītyabhyupeyam; anyathā parokṣapramāyā aparokṣabhramanivartakatvopapattau vedāntavākyānāmaparokṣa jñānajanakatvavyutpādanaprayāse vyarthaḥ syād iti cenna| `nāyaṃ sarpaḥ' ityādivākyādinā savilāsājñānanivṛttyabhāve'pi bhramagatāpramāṇatvajñāpanena bhramapramāṇatvabuddhestadviṣayasatyatābuddheśca nivartanāt| tāvatā ca bhramanivartakatvavyapadeśāt| bhrame prāmāṇyavibhramasya tadviṣaye satyatāvibhramasya ca parokṣatvenāparokṣabādhānapekṣatvāt| na hi duṣṭakaraṇājanyatvamabādhitaviṣayatvaṃ vā prāmāṇyaṃ kasyacit pratyakṣam| na vā sarvadeśasarvakālasarvapuruṣābādhyatvarūpaṃ viṣayasatyatvam| atastayoḥ parokṣapramābādhyatvamucitameva| tayośca bādhitayoḥ rajatādibhramaḥ svarūpeṇa sannapi svakāryākṣamatvādasanniveti bādhita ityucyata ityanavadyam|
nanu -- `idaṃ rajatam' ityatra sayuktikaṃ pratyakṣaṃ bādhakaṃ, na yuktimātram| `gauro'ham' ityatrāpi `mama śarīram' iti balavat pratyakṣameva bādhakam| `ahamihaivāsmi sadane jānāna' iti tu pramāṇameva, jīvasyāṇutvād iti cenna| rajatābhedaśarīrābhedapratyakṣayorjāgratoḥ yuktyā pratibandhākṣamatve tadviṣayapratyakṣotpatte ravonavakāśāt| na ca tatra paramparāsambandhena kardamalipte vastre `nīlaṃ vastram' itivad `gauro'ham' iti gauṇam; kardamavastrayoriva śarīrātmanorbhedānadhyavasāyena dṛṣṭāntadārṣṭāntikayorvaiṣamyāt| tathā cātraikyādhyāsa evocitaḥ| evaṃ ca `uṣṇaṃ jalam' ityatrāpi yadi kardamavastrayoriva toyatejasorbhedagrahaḥ, tadā gauṇataiva| yadica śarīrātmavad bhedānadhyavasāyastadā'dhyāsa eva| tathā ca yuktibādhyameveti tadapyudāharaṇam| yattvahamihaiveti pramāṇamityuktam, tanna| ātmanaḥ `ākāśavat sarvagataśca' iti sarvagatatvena ihaiveti vyavacchedasyāpramāṇikatvāt| na ca jīvo'ṇuḥ, yugapadeva pādaśiro'vacchedena sukhaduḥkhānubhavāt| na hyoko'ṇurekadā vyavahitadeśadvayāvacchinno bhavati| naca yugapatpratītirbhramaḥ, utsargasiddhaprāmāṇyaparityāge bījābhāvāt| vistareṇa caitadagre vakṣyāmaḥ|
nanu -- nabhonailyapratyakṣasya nīrūpatvagrāhakānumānena na bādhaḥ, liṅgābhāvāt| na ca paramamahattvadravyānārambhakatbāderliṅgatvam, tvanmate asiddheḥ| niḥsparśatvaṃ tu tamasi vyabhicāri| pṛthivyāditrayetarabhūtatvādi cāprayojakam| tathā ca nīrūpatvagrāhakasākṣipratyakṣameva tadbādhakaṃ vācyam, na ca rūpagrahaṇāsamarthasya sākṣiṇaḥ kathaṃ nīrūpatvagrāhakatvamiti vācyam| piśācāgrāhakasyāpi cakṣuṣastadabhāvagrāhakatvavadupapatteḥ, pareṇāpi sākṣiṇo'pi rūpavattamogrāhakatvābhyupagamācca acākṣuṣe'pi nabhasi vāyāviva cakṣuṣaiva rūpābhāvagrahaṇasambhavena cākṣuṣapratyakṣabādhād iti cenna| `nīlaṃ nabhaḥ' it pratyakṣe jāgrati rūpābhāvagrahaṇasya cakṣuṣā sākṣiṇā cāsambhavāt| tathā ca balavatī yuktireva tadbādhikā| na ca liṅgābhāvaḥ, cakṣuranvayavyatirekānuvidhāyirūpāviśeṣitapratītiviṣayatvādrūpavaditi liṅgasambhavāt| na cāprayojakatvam, nabho yadi sarūpaṃ syāttadā cakṣuranvayavyatirekānu vidhāyipratītau rūpāsambandhitayā viṣayo na syāditi tarkopapatteḥ| na ceṣṭāpattiḥ, savidhe rūpāsambandhitayā nabhasaḥ siddheḥ sarvajanasammatatvāt| nabhasaḥ sākṣivedyatāyāmapi cakṣuranvayavyatirekānuvidhānamavarjanīyameva| anyathā'ndhasyāpi tadgrahaṇaṃ syāt| na ca pañcīkaraṇādrūpavadārabdhatvena nabhaso nīrūpatvaṃ bādhitamiti vācyam| trivṛtkaraṇapakṣe'sya dūṣaṇasyānavakāśāt| pañcīkaraṇapakṣe'pi apañcīkaraṇadaśāyāṃ yasmin bhūte yo guṇaḥ sa pañcīkaraṇādvyavahārayogyo bhavatītyetāvanmātrābhyupagamānnākāśe rūpārambhaprasaṅgaḥ| na ca `nāyaṃ sarpaḥ' ityukto'pi, `kimevaṃ vadasi param ? api punaḥ parāmṛśya paśyasi ?' iti prativacanadarśanānna śabdamātraṃ rajjusarpādibhramanivarttakam, kiṃ tu pratyakṣameveti vācyam| prativacanasthale bhramapramādādiśaṅkākrāntatvena `nāyaṃ sarpaḥ' ityāderdurbalatayā na bhramanivartakatvam| yatra tu tādṛkśaṅkānākrāntatvaṃ tatra bhramanivartakataiva| ata eva tādṛkśaṅkānākrāntapitrādivacasi nedṛkprativacanaṃ, kintu siddhavatpravṛttyādikameva| jvālaikyapratyakṣamapyevameva yuktibādhyam| na ca nirvāpitāropitasthale spaṣṭatarabhedapratyakṣabādhitaṃ tadityantatrāpi dīrgheyaṃ na hrasveti bhedapratyakṣameva tadbādhakamiti vācyam| nirvāpitāropitātiriktasthale tāvadayaṃ vicāraḥ| tatra ca dīrgheyaṃ na hrasveti bhedapratyakṣaṃ vaktumaśakyam| yaiva hrasvā saivedānīṃ dīrgheti hrasvatvadīrghatvābhyāmupasthitayorabhedasya sākṣātkriyamāṇatvāt| tathā ca jvālāpratyabhijñā yuktibādhyaiva| sarvadā pittadūṣitanetrasya `ṣītaḥ śaṅkhaḥ' it pratyakṣe candraprādeśatvapratyakṣe ca parokṣātiriktasya bādhakasya śaṅkitumapyaśakyatvād yuktyādibādhyataiva vaktavyā|
nanu sarvatraivātra prakārāntareṇāsātkalpe pratyakṣe mānāntarapravṛttiḥ| tathā hi -- dvividhaṃ jñānam| dvikoṭikamekakoṭikaṃ ca| antyamapi dvividhaṃ aprāmāṇyaśaṅkākalaṅkitaṃ tadakalaṅkitaṃ ca| tatrādyau sarvapramāṇāvakāśadau| arthāparicchedakatvādaprāmāṇya śaṅkākalaṅkitatvācca| aprāmāṇyadhīkalaṅkitatvaṃ ca dvedhā bhavati| duṣṭakaraṇakatva niścayādarthābhāvaniścayācca| tathā śailāgrasthitaviṭapināṃ prādeśikatvapratītirdūradoṣanibandhanā dṛṣṭeti dūratasthasya candramasaḥ prādeśikatvapratyayo doṣanibandhana eveti nirṇīyate| evamākāśe samīpe nīrupatvaniścayād dūre rūpavattvadhīrdūradoṣajanyeti prāgeva niściyate| `pītaḥ śaṅkhaḥ' ityādi pratyakṣaṃ tu prāthamikaparīkṣitapratyakṣeṇa `śaṅkho na pītaḥ' ityarthābhāva niścayādaprāmāṇyajñānāskanditamevotpadyate| evaṃ savitṛsuṣirādipratyakṣamapi | tathā ca candrādiprādeśikatvapratyakṣaṃ dūrādidoṣaniścayāt, `pītaḥ saṅkhaḥ' ityadipratyakṣaṃ prāthamikārthābhāvaniścayādeva bādhitamiti paścādanumānāgamādiprasara iti na tābhyāṃ tadvādhaḥ| yena hi yasya bhramatvaṃ jñāyateḥ, tattasya bādhakamityucyate| na ca candraprādeśikatvādipratyakṣasyāgamādinā bhramatvaṃ jñāyate| bhramatvajñānottarakālameva tatpravṛtteḥ| apramāṇyajñānākalaṅkitaṃ tu svārthaparicchedakaṃ niḥśaṅkapravṛttijananayogyam| yathā `vahniruṣṇa eva',`prastaro yajamānabhinna eva', `ghaṭaḥ sanneva ityādi tannānyasyāvakāśadaṃ nānyena bādhyam| na hyatra prāgeva dūrādidoṣadhīrvā arthābhāvaniścayo vā koṭyantarālambitvaṃ vā'sti| kiṃ ca kvacit pratyakṣaṃ pratyakṣāntaragauravādyuktibādhyaṃ bhavatu, kvacicca liṅgādikaṃ śrutigauravāt śrutyanusāriprakaraṇādibādhyaṃ bhavatu, rājāmātya iva rājagauraveṇa rājabhṛtyabādhyaḥ, tathāpi na yuktimātrasya prakaraṇamātrasya vā pratyakṣaliṅgādibādhakatvam| pratyakṣādyanusāritvasya sarvatrābhāvāt| na hi pradhānabhūtācamanādipadārthaviṣayayā `ācāmedupavīti dakṣiṇācāraḥ' ityādismṛtyā padārthadharmabhūtakramādiviṣayā `vedaṃ kṛtvā vediṃ karoti' iti śrutirvedakaraṇānantaraṃ kṣutanimittakācamanopanipāte bādhyata ityanyatrāpi tathā bhavitavyāmiti cet, maivam|yato yuktirevaiṣā| yad yaddūrasthālpaparimāṇajñānaṃ,tat taddūradoṣanibandhanamapramā, śailāgrasthaviṭapyalpaparimāṇajñānavat idamapi tatheti| tathā caivaṃrūpayā yuktyaiva candraprādeśikatvādipratyakṣasya bādhaṃ vadan yuktyā na pratyakṣasya bādha iti vacanenājaiṣīḥ paraṃ mandabuddhe ! mandākṣam, na tu param| evaṃ `pītaḥ śaṅkhaḥ' iti pratyakṣe'pi prācīnārthābhāvapratyakṣaṃ na bādhakam, tasyedānīmabhāvāt| na ca tatsmṛtirbādhikā, tasyā anubhavād durbalatvāt| kevalaṃ yuktyutpādana eva sopayujyate| tena yuktyāgamābhyāmevodāhṛtasthaleṣu bādhaḥ|
yattu kvacidyuktyāderbādhakatvadarśanamātreṇa sarvatra na bādhakatvaṃ vaktuṃ śakyam, yuktyādibādhakatāyā anusriyamāṇapratyakṣagauravanibandhanatvāt ityuktam| tadanuktopālambhanam| na hi mayā kvacid darśanamātreṇa yuktarbādhakatā sarvatrocyate, api tu candraprādeśikatvaśaṅkhapītatvapratyakṣādau yāvadāgamāderbādhakatāprayojakaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tāvatsattvena| na ca tatrānusriyamāṇaṃ pratyakṣamasti, yadgauraveṇa bādhakatāyāmanyathāsiddhiṃ brūyāḥ| tasmāccandraprādeśikatvapratyakṣasya prapañcasattvapratyakṣasya ca tulyavadeva bādhyatā| yuktyāgamayośca tulyavadeva bādhakateti| na hi candrapradeśikatvapratyakṣe'pi prāgeva duṣṭakaraṇakatvaniścayaḥ| naikaṭyasyāpi kvacid doṣatvena sarvatra parimāṇajñānāviśvāsaprasaṅgāt| kintvāgamādinā bādhānantarameva| tadvat prakṛte'pi mithyātvasiddhyanantaramevāvidyārūpadoṣaniścayaḥ| tathā ca sarvātmanā sāmyam| yattu dṛṣṭasya vastuno balavaddṛṣṭiṃ vinā anyadbādhakaṃ nāstītyuktaṃ tad durbalaśabdaliṅgādiviṣayam| yadapyuktaṃ vivaraṇe, `yatrāvicārapurassarameva pratyakṣāvabhāsamapyanumānādinā bādhitamucchinnavyavahāraṃ bhavati| tatra tathā bhavatu| yatra punarvicāra padavīmupārūḍhayorjñānayorbalābalacintayā bādhaniścayastatra nānumānādinā pratyakṣasya mithyātvasiddhiḥ' iti tadapi gṛhītaprāmāṇyakaśabdatadupajīvyanumānātiriktayuktaviviṣayam| ekatra prāmāṇyaniścaye balābalacintāyā evānavakāśāt|| || iti pratyakṣasya liṅgādyabādhyatve bādhakam||
evaṃ ca `bhāvibādhaniścayācca' iti yaduktaṃ tadapyupapannatarameva| prakārāntareṇābādhitasya candraprādeśikatvapratyakṣasya yathā āgamena bādhaḥ tathā prakārāntareṇābādhitasya `san ghaṭaḥ' ityādipratyakṣasya mithyātvabodhakāgamena bādha iti nirṇayāt| evaṃ ca bhāvibādhaśaṅkāmādāya yatparairdūṣaṇamuktaṃ tadanuktopālambhatayā apāstam| vastutastu bādhaśaṅkāmādāyāpi pratyakṣasya bādhakatoddhāraḥ samīcīna eva| pratyakṣaśabdayorbalābalavicārāt prāk kimayaṃ śabda upacaritārthaḥ, āhosvit pratyakṣamapramāṇamiti śaṅkāyāmubhayorabādhakatvaprāptau tātparyaliṅgaiḥ śrūyamāṇārtharatayā niścitasyāgamasyopacaritārthatvaśaṅkāvyudāsena labdhāvakāśatva sambhavāt| na ca śabdaliṅgayoḥ pratyakṣābādhakatayā pratyakṣāntarasyāpramāṇatayā śaṅkyamānatvenābādhakatayā ca bādhakasāmānyābhāve niścite bādhaśaṅkā na yukteti vācyam| śabdaliṅgayoḥ pratyakṣabādhakatvasya vyavasthāpitatvāt pratyekaṃ viśeṣābhāvaniścaye'pi viśeṣāṇāmiyattānavadhāraṇadaśāyāṃ saṃśayasambhavāt| pratyakṣasyāpramāṇatayā śaṅkyamānatvena śaṅkāvirahopapādanasyāsambhavaduktikatvācca|
athaivaṃ jāgradādijñānasyāpramātve svapnadṛṣṭasya śuktirūpyādeśca bādhāsiddhau kathaṃ dṛṣṭāntasiddhiḥ syāditi cenna, āropyasattādhikasattākaviṣayatvenā'pekṣikapramāṇatvenānyūna sattākaviṣayatvena vā bādhakatvāt| ata eva yaduktaṃ bauddhaṃ prati bhaṭṭavārtike -- 
`pratiyogini dṛṣṭe ca jāgradvodhe mṛṣā bhavet|
svapnādidṛṣṭirasmākaṃ tava bhedo'pi kiṅkṛtaḥ||'
iti tatsaṅgacchate| nanu bhramakālīnāparokṣabuddhyaviṣayaviśeṣaviṣayaiva dhīrbādhikā dṛṣṭā| na ca viśvabādhikā dhīstatheti cenna| adhiṣṭhānatattvajñānatvenaiva bhramanivartakatvāt| viśvanivartakabrahmajñānasya tathātvāt| na ca saprakārikaiva dhīrbhramanivartikā, iyaṃ tu niṣprakārikā kathaṃ tatheti vācyam| nivartakatāyāṃ saprakārakatvasya gauravādapraveśāt| nanu āvaśyakaḥ saprakārakatvaniyamaḥ| vyāvṛttākārajñānatvenaiva bhramanivartakatvāt| anyathā anuvṛttākārajñānādapi tannivṛttyāpatteriti cet| satyam| vyāvṛttākāratvena jñānasya bhramanivartakatā, na tu tatra viśeṣaprakārakatvaniyamaḥ| tathā hi -- vyāvṛttākāratā hi dvedhā bhavati| viśeṣaṇād upalakṣaṇācca| tatrā'dye saprakārakatvaniyamaḥ| dvitīpe'pi dharmāntarasya yadupalakṣaṇaṃ tasmād vyāvṛttākāratve saprakārakataiva| yadi tu svarūpopalakṣaṇād vyāvṛttākārātā tadā niṣprakārakataiva| upalakṣaṇasya tatrāpraveśāt| svasya ca svasminnaprakāratvāt| na ca prameyatvādivat svasyaiva svasmin prakāratvamiti vācyam| tvayāpi kevalānvayinyevāgatyā tathāṅgīkārāt| na tu sarvatra| ata ākāraprakārayorabhedād brahmākārataiva brahmabuddhestatprakārateti cet| na viśiṣṭabuddherviśeṣyākāratve'pi tadaprakārakatvāt, ākāraprakārayorbhedāt| ākāraśca vṛttiniṣṭhaḥ kaściddharmo'sādhāraṇavyavahāraheturiti vakṣyate| tasmādyathā''kāśapadācchabdā śrayatvopalakṣitadharmisvarūpamātraṃ jñāyate tadvadatrāpi dvitīyābhāvādyupalakṣitabrahmasvarūpajñānaṃ vyāvṛttākāraṃ dvaitanivartakamaparokṣam| yathā ca śabdāttādṛgjñānasambhavastathā vakṣyate| na ca bādhakadhiyāṃ bhramataddhetvajñānadoṣādhyastadraṣṭrādīnāmabādhakatvaṃ dṛṣṭamiti kathaṃ brahmajñānasya tadbādhakatvaṃ ghaṭatāmiti vācyam| yatra hi svapne draṣṭāraṃ duṣṭakaraṇavantaṃ kalpayitvā tasya bhramaṃ kalpayati tatra jāgarajñānena sarveṣāṃ nivṛttidarśanāt| jāgraddaśāyāmapi yadā manuṣyapratikṛtau caitanyaṃ kalpayitvā tatsamīpavartinyanādarśa evādarśatvaṃ kalpayitvā svapratibimbamayaṃ paśyatīti kalpayati tadā nāyaṃ -- cetano na cāyamādarśa iti pramayā sarvanivṛttidarśanācca neyamadṛṣṭacarī kalpanā| tathā ceyaṃ śuktirityādyadhiṣṭhānajñānaṃ rajjvāṃ sarpabhramamiva draṣṭrādyadhyāsaṃ mā nivīvṛtat tatkasya hetoḥ? tadadhiṣṭhānasākṣātkartavābhāvāt| brahmajñānaṃ tvākāśādiprapañcabhramamiva draṣṭurdoṣādibhramamapi nivartayedeva tatkasya hetoḥ ? aśeṣabhramādhiṣṭhānatattvasākṣātkāratvāt| evaṃ ca bādhabuddhitvaṃ na doṣādyabādhakatve prayojakam, api tu tadbhramādhiṣṭhānatattvasākṣātkārabhinnatvamiti draṣṭavyam| nanu kalpitatvāduktadṛṣṭānte tad bādhyatām| iha tu kathamiti cet ? hanta ! brahmavyatiriktasya sarvasya kalpitatvamaṅgīkurvatāmasmākamidamaniṣṭaṃ mahadāpāditaṃ devānāmpriyeṇa|
nanu sākṣipratyakṣaṃ na bādhyam, doṣājanyatvāt| pratyuta śrutijanitādvaitajñānameva bādhyam, tātparyabhramarūpadoṣajanyatvāditi cet, na| caitanyasya svarūpato doṣājanyatve'pi tadavacchedikāyā avidyāvṛtterdoṣajanyatvāt| tatpratiphalitacaitanyasyaiva sākṣipadārthatvāt| advaitatātparyagrahasya ca pratyakṣādyavirodhena pramārūpatayā doṣatvābhāvāt na tajjanyamadvaitajñānaṃ bādhyam| bhramajanyatvasya viṣayabādhāprayojakatvācca| na ca bādhakatulyamānatākadvaita śrutisaṃvādidvaitapratyakṣaṃ kathaṃ bādhyamiti vācyam| dvaitasya pratyakṣādilaukikamānasiddhatvena tadbodhakaśruteranuvādakatayā phalavadajñātasvārthatātparyakādvaitaśrutisāmyābhāvāt| nanu bādhakadhībodhyaṃ na bādhyam| bhedaśca bādhakadhībodhyaḥ| tayā svaviṣayasya bhinnatvenaiva grahāt| neda rajatamitivad abhinnatayodāsīnatayā vā grahaṇe bādhakatvāyogāditi cet, na| bādhakadhiyo bhedaviṣayatvānabhyupagamāt| iyaṃ śuktirityevabādhabuddhyudayāt| tasyāstu nedaṃ rajatamiti bhedabuddhiḥ phalam| vyāvṛttākārataiva bādhadhiya āvaśyakī| sā ca svarūpopalakṣaṇabalānniṣprakārakabrahmajñāne'pi astīti na bādhakadhībodhyatvaṃ bhedasya| nanu svapnavilakṣaṇaphalaparyantaparīkṣāyāmapi cecchaṅkā syāt tadā advaitaśrutitatpratyakṣatatprāmāṇya śaṅkāyāmadvaitaśrutirapi na siddhyet| bādhe'pi bādhaśaṅkāyāmabādhitabādhaprasiddhirapi na syād, bādhitabādhaśaṅkāyāścābādhyatvāvirodhitvāt| bhāvibādhe'pi bādhaśaṅkāpātena svakriyāvyāghātaśca syāt| śaṅkāpratyakṣe'pi śaṅkāyāṃ śaṅkāpi na siddhyet| evaṃ sarvatra śaṅkāprasarāt sarvaviplavāpattiriti cet, maivaṃ maṃsthāḥ| yataḥ samatvena pramāṇāntare upasthita eva niścite'pi sattvādau śaṅkā bhavatīti brūmaḥ, na tu niścitamātre śaṅkā bhavatīti| tathā ca yaduktaṃ bauddhaṃ prati bhaṭṭavārtike -- 
`duṣṭajñānagṛhītārthapratiṣedho'pi yujyate|
gahītamātrabādhe tu svapakṣo'pi na siddhyati||'
iti, tadapi na virudhyate| gṛhītamātrabādhasya tacchaṅkāyāścānukteḥ|
nanu sattvādipratyakṣe klṛptadūrādidoṣābhāvaniścaye kathaṃ śaṅkodayaḥ ? na ca klṛptānāmabhāvaniścaye'pyaklṛptasya śaṅkā syāt| śabde klṛptavaktṛnibandhanadoṣasya nityatvena vede abhāve'pi doṣāntaraśaṅkāyāḥ suvacatvāt| naca svāpnapratyakṣe tadā dūrādyabhāvaniścaye'pyapramāṇyadarśanena tadvadatrāpi śaṅketi vācyam| śūnyameva tattvamiti svāpnavede'pi tadā bhrāntyādidoṣābhāvaniściye'pyaprāmāṇyadarśanasya vede'pi samānatvāt| svapnavaiṣamyānubhavastūbhayatrāpi samāna iti cet‌na| sattvapratyakṣādvaitāgamayoḥ klṛptadoṣābhāvaniścayasya samānatvena prāmāṇyaśaṅkāyāmapratibandhakatvāt| na hi satpratipakṣe ubhayatra doṣābhāvaniścayaḥ kimatra tattvamiti jijñāsāṃ pratibadhnāti| viruddhaviśeṣādarśanakālikasyaiva viśeṣadarśanasya śaṅkāpratibandhakatvāt| avacchedakavṛttya nityatvenaca sākṣipratyakṣasya doṣajanyatvokteḥ| ata eva yaduktaṃ tārkikaiḥ -- `tadeva hyāśaṅkyate' yasminnāśaṅkyamāne svakriyāvyāghātādayo doṣā na bhavantīti| uktaṃ ca bhaṭṭavārtike bauddhaṃ prati -- 
`ihaṃ janmani keṣāñcinna tāvadupapadyate
yogyavasthāgatānāṃ tu na vidmaḥ kiṃ bhaviṣyati||'
iti| tathā ca prāmāṇyasyotpattau jñaptau ca svatastvādiha cotpattisvatastvāpavādasya doṣasya jñaptisvatastvāpavādasya bādhasya cādarśanāt, nirmūlaśaṅkāyāśca svakriyāvirodhenānutthānābhyupagamāt susthaṃ pratyakṣasya prāmāṇyamiti -- tadapi nirastam| āgamādipramāṇamūlakaśaṅkāyā eva svīkārāt| rūpyādiniṣedhasya tu `nedaṃ rajata' mityāderadvaita śrutyanuguṇatvena nāprāmāṇyaśaṅkāskandanam| ate na vṛddhimicchato mūlahānyāpattiḥ| nāpi `san ghaṭaḥ' ityādeḥ `nedaṃ rajataṃ' ityanena samānayogakṣematā; advaitaśruti virodhābhyāṃ viśeṣāt| ata eva -- sauṣuptikānandānubhavasyāpyaprāmāṇye kathamātmana ānandarūpatā tāttvikī ? ānandaśruteranubhūtātāttvikānandānuvādakatvopapatteriti apāstam| ānandasya brahmarūpatvenādvaitaśrutivirodhābhāvena tadaprāmāṇyaprayojakābhāvāt| ata eva nānanda śruteraprāmāṇyam| taduktaṃ khaṇḍane -- 
`atyantāsatyapi jñānamarthe śabdaḥ karoti hi|
abādhāttu pramāmatra svataḥ prāmāṇyaniścalām||' iti|
uktaṃ ca sureśvaravārtike -- 
`ato'vabodhakatvena duṣṭakāraṇavarjanāt|
abādhācca pramāṇatvaṃ vastunyakṣādivat śruteḥ||' iti|
atra cākṣādivaditi nidarśanaṃ vyāvahārikaprāmāṇyamātreṇeti draṣṭavyam| evaṃ ca tāttvikaprāmāṇyābhāve'pi pratyakṣādīnāṃ vyāvahārikaprāmāṇyābhyupagamāt na svakriyāvyāghātaḥ|
na vā,
`pratyakṣamanumānaṃ ca śāstraṃ ca vividhāgamam|
trayaṃ suviditaṃ kāryaṃ dharmaśuddhimabhīpsatā||'
ityādi smṛtivirodhaḥ| tasmātsiddhaṃ bādhaniścayena tacchaṅkayā vā pratyakṣāderadvaita gamānumānādyavirodhitvam| iti bhāvibādhopapattyā pratyakṣabādhoddhāraḥ||
syādetat -- adhyakṣasya bhinnaviṣayatvādinā bādhākṣamatve'pi anumānameva bādhakaṃ syāt| tathā hi -- brahmapramānyena, vedāntatātparyapramitijanyajñānānyena vā, mokṣahetujñānānyena vā abādhyatve satyasattvānadhikaraṇatve sati brahmānyad, vimataṃ vā sat paramārthasadvā, prātibhāsikatvānadhikaraṇatve satyasadvilakṣaṇatvād, brahmavad, vyatirekeṇa śaśaśrṛṅgavadveti cenna| tvanmate prātibhāsikasyāpyasattvena vyarthaviśeṣaṇatayā vyāpyatvāsiddheḥ| asmānmatamāśritya hetūkaraṇe ca dehātmaikye brahmajñānetarābādhye vyabhicārāt| na hi prātibhāsikatvaṃ brahmajñānetarabādhyatvādanyat| tvayāhi prātibhāsikasya śuktirūpyāderapakṣatvāya satyantamādyaṃ viśeṣaṇatrayaṃ vikalpena pakṣe prakṣiptam| tatra brahma vṛttivyāpyamiti matenādyam| tadanabhyupagame tu śābdapramāṃ prati tātparyapramā heturiti matena dvitīyam| anyonyāśrayatvāt na sā heturiti matena tṛtīyam| tathā ca prātibhāsikasyāsattvānadhikaraṇatvamaṅgīkṛtameva|
anyathā tucchavārakāsattvānadhikaraṇatvaviśeṣaṇenaiva tadvyāvṛttāvetāvatprayāsavaiyarthyāpatteḥ| evaṃ ca dehātmaikyasyāpi pakṣatve bādha eva| bādhe ca sati pakṣaviśeṣaṇasya pakṣatvasyāsiddhyā''śrayāsiddhirapi| ata eva svabādhakābhimatā'bādhyadoṣajanyajñānāviṣayatve satīti vā, svabādhakābhimatābādhyabādhāviṣayatve satīti vā, svasamānādhikaraṇakarmaprāgabhāva samānakālīnajñānāvādhyatve satīti vā veśeṣaṇaprakṣepe'pi na nistāraḥ| dehātmaikye pūrvoktadoṣāvyāvṛttereva|
yattu -- prathame sādhye vyāvahārikasattvamādāya siddhasādhanam| dvitīyasādhye tu vādinaḥ paramārthatvaviśeṣaṇaṃ vyartham| vyāvartyāprasiddheḥ iti| tanna| vyāvahārikasattvaṃ sattvena vyavahāramātramiti matena prathamaprayogāt| anugataṃ pṛthagvyāvahārikaṃ sattvamiti tu mate dvitīyaḥ prayogaḥ| na ca viśeṣaṇaṃ vyartham| parārthānumāne paraṃ prati siddhasādhanoddhārasya tatprayojanatvāt| īśvarānumāne janyakṛtyajanyānītyatra mīmāṃsakaṃ prati janyatvasyeva viśvaparamārthatvavādinaṃ prati paramārthatvasya prameyatvādivaduparañjakatvena viśeṣaṇatvopapatteśca| tasmāt pūrvokta eva doṣaḥ| hetau ca vyarthaviśeṣaṇatvadoṣaḥ| yadyapi matadvaye'pi aprāmāṇikasyāpi niṣedhapratiyogitvābhyupagamādāropitatvenobhayasaṃmatatvarūpasya vā, pratibhāsamātraśarīratvarūpasya vā, pratibhāsikatvasya prasiddhirasti| anyathā siddhānte'pi mithyātvānumāne prātibhāsikānyasyaiva pakṣīkartavyatvāddoṣasāmyaṃ syāt| tathāpi hetau prātibhāsikatvaviśeṣaṇaṃ vyartham| anadhikaraṇatve satyasattvānadhikaraṇatvamātrasyaiva paramārthasattvasādhakatvopapatteḥ| śuddhameva hi brahma dṛṣṭāntatvenābhyupeyam| dharmavato dṛṣṭāntatve sādhyavaikalyāpatteḥ| sādhyantu bādhābhāvarūpatvādadhikaraṇasvarūpameva na dharmaḥ| dharmyatiriktābhāvānabhyupagamasyoktatvāt| tathā ca cakṣustaijasatvānumāne rūpādiṣu madhya ityasyāsiddhivārakasyāpi vyāptigrahaupayikatvena vyabhicāravārakaviśeṣaṇatulyatayā yadyapi sārthakatvam| vyabhicāravārakasyāpi sārthakatve vyāptigrahaupayikatvamātrasya tantratvāt| tathāpi `kṣityādikaṃ na kartṛjanyaṃ śarīrājanyatvāt' ityatra śarīrasyeva vyāptigrahānupayogitvena prātibhāsikatvasya vaiyarthyameva| ākāśādāvajanyatvakartṛjanyatvābhāvayoriva nirdharmake brahmaṇyanadhikaraṇatvaparamārthasattvayorvyāptigrahopapatteḥ| tathā caikāmasiddhiṃ pariharato dvitīyāsiddhyāpattiḥ| svarūpāsiddhiparihārārthaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ prakṣipato vyāpyatvāsiddhirityarthaḥ| vyāptāvanupayogasya darśitatvāt| kiṃ ca vyāvahārikasattvamātreṇaivopapatteḥ, uktahetora prayojakatvam| paramārthasattve bādhānupapattilakṣaṇapratikūlatarkaparāghātācca|
nanu brahmaṇyasatpratibhāsikavyāvṛttirūpaṃ hetuṃ prati vyāvartakatayā prayojakatvena paramārthasattvaṃ klṛptam| apṛthivīvyāvṛttiṃ prati tadvivītvasyevārasadvyāvṛttiṃ prati tadviruddhasattvasyaiva prayojakatvāt| jñānatvānandatvādikaṃ tu na tatprayojakam| sākṣādasattvāvirodhitvāt| prapañce tadabhāvācca| tathā ca brahmaviśvasādhāraṇaṃ paramārthasattvameva tatprayojakam| na ca viśvamithyātvāt paramārthasattvamapi na viśvasādhāraṇaṃ, jñānatvānandatvādivaditi vācyam, anyo'nyāśrayāpatteḥ iti ced, ayuktametat| na hi prātibhāsikāsatoreka vyāvṛttirubhayī vā samavyāptā, yenaikaprayojakaprayojya bhavet| kintu prātibhāsikavyāvṛttiprayojakaṃ brahmaviśvāsatsādhāraṇameva vaktavyam| asatyapi prātibhāsikatvābhāvāt| evamasadvyāvṛttāvapi prayojakaṃ brahmaviśvaprātibhāsikasādhāraṇameva vaktavyam| prātibhāsike'pyasattvābhāvāt| tathā ca tatprayojakadvayasamāveśādeva brahmaṇyubhayavyāvṛttyupapattau nīlatvaghaṭatvarūpāvacchedakadvaya samāveśopapannanīlaghaṭatvavannātiriktaprayojakakalpanāyāmasti kiñcinmānamiti kṛtabuddhayaḥ eva vidāṅkurvantu| nityatvaṃ copādhiḥ| tucchapratibhāsikayornityatbavyatireke sādhyavyatirekadarśanāt|
ata evāniṣedhyatvena pramāṃ prati sākṣādviṣayatvādityapi na hetuḥ| kiṃ ca pramātvaṃ tadvati tatprakārakatvaṃ tattvāvedakatvaṃ vā ? ādye dṛṣṭān‌tasya sādhanavaikalyam| na hi paramārthasataḥ śuddhasya brahmaṇaḥ saprakārakajñānaviṣayatvam| na ca dharmavato dṛṣṭāntatetyuktam| tasya pakṣakukṣinikṣiptatvena niścitasādhyavattvābhāvāt| dvitīye tattvāvedakatvasyābādhita viṣayatvarūpatvena sādhyāviśeṣaparyavasānāddhetugrahe siddhasādhanam| hetvagrahe tu svarūpāsiddhiḥ| yattu -- pramāviṣayatvamātreṇaiva paramārthatvopapattau viśeṣaṇe vyarthe iti| tanna| purovartinaṃ rajatatayā jānāmītyādyanuvyavasāyarūpapramāviṣaye prātibhāsike vyabhicāravārakatvāt sākṣātpadasya| tatraiva ca mithyātvapramiteḥ sākṣādviṣaye vyabhicāravārakatvād aniṣedhyatvenetyasya| na hyanuvyavasāyamithyātvaprame na bhavataḥ|
nāpyaniṣedhyatveneśvaraṃ prati sākṣādaparokṣatvaṃ hetuḥ| satyatvasiddhiṃ vinā aniṣedhyatvenetyaṃśasyāsiddheḥ| tathā cānyonyāśrayaḥ| na ceśvarajñānaviṣayasya prapañcasya mithyātve tasya bhrāntatvaprasaṅgaḥ| mithyābhūtasya mithyātvenaiva grahaṇādaindrajālikavad bhrāntatvāyogāt| anyathā saviṣayakabhramajñātṛtvena bhrāntatvasya durvāratvāpatteḥ| atha niṣedhyatvena jñāne tatpālanārthamīśvarasya pravṛttirna syāt na, aindrajālikapravṛttivadīśvarapravṛtterapi tathāvidhatvāt|
nāpi saprakārakabādhyārthakriyākāritvaṃ hetuḥ| saprakārakajāgradvodhābādhya svapnajalāvagāhanapriyāsaṅgamādiviśeṣitāpramāṇībhūtajñānasyārthakriyākāritvadarśanena tadviṣaye tatra vyabhicārāt| atha tatra jñānameva sukhādijanakaṃ, taccābādhyameveti matam| tadasat| jñānamātrasya hi tādṛksukhājanakatvena kiñcidviśeṣitasyaiva tathātvaṃ vācyam| jñāne ca viśeṣo nārthātiriktaḥ| taduktam -- `arthenaiva viśeṣo hi nirākāratayā dhiyām -- ' iti| arthenetyartha evetyarthaḥ| tathā ca mithyābhūtaviśeṣitasya janakatvābhyupagame mithyābhūtasyāpi janakatvādvyabhicāra eva| tathā coktaṃ śāstradīpikāyāṃ bauddhaṃ prati -- `atha sukhajñānamevārthakriyā taccavyabhicāryeva| na hi kvacidapyasati sukhe sukhajñānamastītyaśaṅkya satyametanna tu tena pūrvajñānaprāmāṇyādhyavasānaṃ yuktam| apramāṇenāpi priyāsaṅgamavijñānena svapnāvasthāyāṃ sukhadarśanād' iti| nanu viṣayaviśeṣopalakṣitasyaiva jñānasya sukhajanakatvamastu| tat kuto viṣayasya janakatvamiti cenna| svarūpāṇāmananugatatayā jñānatvādeścātiprasaktatayā anugatānatiprasaktopalakṣyatāvacchedakābhāvādupalakṣaṇatvāsambhavāt| nanu viśeṣaṇatvamapyasambhavi| anāgatajñānajanye tatkālāvidyamānasya viṣayasya pūrvabhāvirūpajanakatvāsambhavād iti cenna| svavyāpārajanye vyāpāriṇo'sato janakatvavat svajñānajanye'pyasato janakatvasambhavāt| atītānāgatāvasthasyāsattvadharmāśrayatvenaivābhyupagamāt| anyathā dhvaṃsaprāgabhāvapratiyogitva tajjñānaviṣayatvādīnāmanāśrayatvāpatteḥ| pramāṇabalāt kāraṇatvābhyupagamasyātrāpi tulyatvāt| kiñca svarūpābādhyasya viṣayābādhyatvadarśanena viṣayabādhe svarūpabādhasyāvaśyakatayā svapnādijñānaṃ sadevetyasya vaktumaśakyatvāt| anāditvasya viṣamavyāptasyopādhitvācca| na ca arthakriyākāritvaṃ prati paramārthatvasya brahmaṇi prayojakatvenāvadhāraṇādakāraṇakakāryotpatti rūpavipakṣabādhakatarkeṇa hetoḥ sādhyavyāpakatayā tadavyāpakatayopādheḥ sādhyāvyāpakatvamiti vācyam prātibhāsikarajjusarpādau bhayakampādikāryakāritvadarśanena prātibhāsikasādhāraṇasya tucchavyāvṛttasya pratītikālasattvasyaivārthakriyākāritvaṃ prati prayojakatvāt| prātibhāsikasyārthakriyākāritvānabhyupagame saprakārābādhyeti hetuviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyāpatteḥ| kasminnapi deśe kasminnapi kāle kenāpi puruṣeṇābādhyatvaṃ hi paramārthasattvam| tadapekṣayā pratītikālasattvasya laghutvācca| kiñca śuddhasyārthakriyākāritvābhāvāt sādhanavikalatvam| upahitasya pakṣanikṣepāt sādhyavikalatvam|
āropitamithyatvakatvādityapi na hetuḥ| āropitatvaṃ prātibhāsikatvaṃ cet, prapañce hetorasiddhiḥ| tatsiddheḥ pāramārthikasiddhyuttarakālīnatvāt| vyāvahārikatvaṃ cet, śuktirūpyādau vyabhicāraḥ| ubhayasādhāraṇye'pyayameva doṣaḥ|
kalpakarahitatvādityapi na hetuḥ| asati vyabhicārāt| yathāśrutasyāsuddheśca| nanu nāsiddhiḥ| śuddhaṃ hi caitanyaṃ na kalpakam, aduṣṭatvāt| nopahitam| kalpitvādevānyathānavasthānāt| tathā ca yāvadviśeṣābhāve kalpakasāmānyābhāvasiddhiḥ iti cenna| śuddhasyāpyanādyavidyopadhānavaśena kalpakatvopapatteḥ| kalpakatvaṃ hi kalpanāṃ pratyāśrayatvaṃ, viṣayatvaṃ, bhāsakatvaṃ vā| tacca, sarvaṃ kalpanāsamasattākatvena śuddhatvāvyāghātakam| taduktaṃ saṅkṣepaśārīrake -- 
`āśrayatvaviṣayatvabhāginī nirvibhāgacitireva kevalā|
pūrvasiddhatamaso hi paścimo nāśrayo bhavati nāpi gocaraḥ||'
iti| astu vopahitasya kalpakatvam| na cānavasthā| avidyādhyāsasyādhyāsāntarānapekṣatvāt| svaparasādhāraṇasarvanirvāhakatvopapatteḥ| akalpitasya kalpakatvādarśanācca| kalpitaprati bimbaviśiṣṭādarśāderādarśāntare pratibimbakalpakatvadarśanācca| bimbasya dvitīyādarśa saṃmukhatvābhāvena tatra kalpakatvāyogāt| anyathā atiprasaṅgāt| vistareṇa caitadagre vakṣyāmaḥ| tadevaṃ nirākṛtāḥ paramārthasattve sādhye ṣaḍamī hetavaḥ| evamanye'pi nirākāryāḥ| atha vimataṃ, na sadvilakṣaṇam, asadvilakṣaṇatvādātmavaditi anumānāntaraṃ bhaviṣyatīti matam| tanna| prātibhāsike śuktirūṣyādau vyabhicārāt| na ca tatrāsadvilakṣaṇatvahetureva nāstīti vācyam| asadvilakṣaṇatvābhāve hi aparokṣatayā pratītireva na syāt| nanu tarhyasadvilakṣaṇatve tadviruddhasadvilakṣaṇatvāyogaḥ,tathā ca sādhyasyāpi vidyamānatvānna vyabhicāra iti cenna| sattve sarvajanasiddhabādhavirodhāt| gajādau govailakṣaṇye'pi tadviruddhāśvavailakṣaṇyayogavat sadvailakṣaṇye'pyasadvailakṣaṇyayogopapatteḥ prathamamithyātva niruktāvuktatvāt|
nanu -- vimataṃ,na caitanyājñānakāryaṃ, na tatkāryadhīviṣayaḥ, na tatkāryasattvavat, na tajjñānabādhyasattvavadvā, tasminnaparokṣe'pyaniṣedhyatvena sākṣādbhāsamānatvād, yadevaṃ tadevam, yathā ghaṭe aparokṣe'pyaniṣedhyatvena sākṣādbhāsamānaḥ paṭo na ghaṭājñānakāryādiḥ| vipakṣe ca tadāparokṣye tadajñānavyāhatireva bādhikā| na cāsiddhiḥ, adhiṣṭhānatayā sukhādisākṣitvena cedānīmapi caitanyāparokṣyād -- iti cenna| sāmānyākāreṇāparokṣye'pi śuktyādau rajatāderaniṣedhyatvena sākṣādbhāsamānatayā tanna vyabhicārāt| atha vyāvṛttākāreṇa yasmin bhāsamāne yadaniṣedhyatvena sākṣād bhāsate, na tattadajñānakāryādīti vyāptiriti manyase, tarhyasiddhiḥ| nahi caitanyamidānīṃ bhramanivartakatvābhimatavyāvṛttākārāparokṣapratītiviṣayaḥ| tathā satyadhiṣṭhānameva na syāt| yadā tu vedāntavākyajanyavṛttau vyāvṛttākāratayā aparokṣaṃ tadā aniṣedhyatvena prapañce āparokṣyaśaṅkā'pi nāsti| ataḥ pramāṇajanyāsādhāraṇākārabhānasyaivājñānavirodhitvānnāparokṣatāmātreṇājñānaparāhatiprasaṅgaḥ| yattvajñānapadena jñānābhāvoktau siddhasādhanam; anirvacanīyajñānoktau ca tasya khapuṣpāyamāṇatvena pratiyogyaprasiddhiriti tattuccham| asapratiyogikābhāvaṃ svīkurvataḥ parābhyupagamamātreṇaiva pratiyogiprasiddhisambhavāt|
nanu -- vimataṃ, nātmanyadhyastam; ātmasākṣātkāravatpravṛttiviṣayatvāt, yadevaṃ tadevaṃ, yathā ghaṭasākṣātkāravatpravṛttiviṣayo ghaṭo na tatrādhyastaḥ| na cāsiddhiḥ, īśajīvanmuktayorātmasākṣātkāravatorapi jagadrakṣaṇabhikṣāṭanādau pravṛtteḥ, śaṅkhe adhyastamapi pītatvaṃ na śaṅkhaśvetatvasākṣātkāravatpravṛttiviṣaya iti na tatra vyabhicāra -- iti cenna| pratibimbe vyabhicārāt| sahi mukhaikyasākṣātkāravatpravṛttiviṣayo mukhe'dhyastaḥ| tadvyatirekeṇopalabhyamānatvasyopādhitvācca|
evaṃ ca -- vimataṃ, neśvaramāyākalpitaṃ, taṃ pratyaparokṣatvāt, yadevaṃ tadevaṃ, yathā caitraṃ pratyaparokṣo ghaṭo na caitramāyākalpitaḥ| vimataṃ, na jīvakalpitaṃ, tasmin suṣupte'pyavasthitatvāt, ātmavat| na cāsiddhiḥ, pratyabhijñānād| adṛṣṭāderabhāve punarutthānāyogācca -- ityapi nirastam| ādye aindrajālikaṃ pratyaparokṣe tanmāyākalpite vyabhicārāt| māyāvidyayorabhedena dehātmaikyabhrame vyabhicārācca| dvitīye tvasaddheḥ| na ca pratyabhijñayā prapañcasya sthāyitvasiddhernāsiddhiḥ| suṣuptikālasthāyitvāsādhakatvasya pratyabhijñāyā dṛṣṭisṛṣṭisamarthane vakṣyamāṇatvāt, adṛṣṭādeḥ kāraṇātmanā'vasthitatvena punarutthānasambhavācca||
mithyātvaṃ ātmānyasarvavṛtti na, mithyāmātravṛttitvāt, śuktirūpyatvavad ityapi na| mithyātvanyūnavṛttitvasyopadhitvāt| mithyātvaṃ ca sadasadvilakṣaṇatvaṃ, sadvilakṣaṇatvamātraṃ vā| ādye siddhasādhanam| tasyātmānyasarvamadhyapatitāsadvṛttitvābhāvāt| dvitīye tu hetau mithyāpadasya sadasadvailakṣaṇyaparatve svarūpāsiddhiḥ| sadvailakṣaṇyarūpe pakṣe tucchasādhāraṇe sadasadvilakṣaṇetarāvṛttitvarūpahetvabhāvāt| tasyāpi sadvailakṣaṇyamātraparatve sandigdhānaikāntikatā| sādhyābhāvavatyātmabhede hetusandehāt| aprayojakatvādikaṃ ca pūrvoktaṃ dūṣaṇamanuvartata eva|
ātmā, paramārthasadanyaḥ, paramārthatvādanātmavat| na ca kalpitātma pratiyogikabhedenārthāntaram| kalpitamithyātvena mithyātvānumāne'pi siddhasādhanāpatterityapi na| vyāvahārikapadārthamādāya siddhasādhane atiprasaṅgābhāvāt| anānandatvasyopādhitvācca|
atha ātmā, yāvatsvarūpamanuvartamānānātmavān, yāvatsvarūpamanuvartamāna bhāvarūpānātmavān vā, svajñānābādhyānātmavān, svajñānābādhyabhāvarūpānātmavānvā, padārthatvād, bhāvatvādvā ghaṭādivad iti| atra pañcamaprakārāvidyānivṛttyabhyupagamapakṣe siddhasādhanaparihārāya sādhyayorbhāvarūpapadamanātmaviśeṣaṇamityapi mandam| `yāvatsvarūpaṃ' ityasya yatkiñcitsvarūpaparatve siddhasādhanād| ātmasvarūpaparatve sādhyāprasiddheḥ| na hi yāvadātmasvarūpamanuvartamāno'nātmā prasiddho'sti| tathā satyanumānavaiyarthyāt| atha -- svarūpapadasya samabhivyāhṛtaparatvād vyāptigrahadaśāyāṃ dṛṣṭāntasvarūpaṃ pakṣadharmatāgrahadaśāyāṃ cātmasvarūpameva prāpyata iti na sādhyāprasiddhirna vā siddhasādhanamiti -- cenna| śabdasvabhāvopanyāsasyānumāne anupayogāt| svajñānābādhyetyatra svaśabde'pi tulyo'yaṃ doṣaḥ |
atha eva -- vimatā, bandhanivṛttiḥ, svapratiyogiviṣayaviṣayakajñānābādhyānātma samakālīnā, uktajñānābādhyabhāvarūpānātmasamānakālīnā vā, bandhanivṛttitvāt; nigaḍabandhanivṛttivadityapi -- nirastam; pakṣadṛṣṭāntayorbandhapadārthasyaikasyābhāvena svarūpāsiddhi sādhanavaikalyānyatarāpātāt| svapade coktaḥ sādhyāprasiddhidoṣaḥ| hetau ca bandhetiviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyād vyāpyatvāsiddhiḥ| aprayojakatvaṃ ca kasyāścinnivṛtteranātmasamāna kālīnatvadarśanaṃ nivṛttimātrasya tathātvasādhaneḥ, saṃsārakālīnāyā duḥkhanivṛtteḥ samānādhikaraṇaduḥkhaprāgabhāvakālīnatvadarśanamiva duḥkhanivṛttimātrasya tathātvasādhane|
nanvevaṃ sāmānyānumāneṣu nirākṛteṣu viśeṣyānumānaṃ bhaviṣyati -- 
ātmadhīḥ, na svaviṣayaviṣayakadhībādhyā, dhītvāt śuktidhīvat -- ityapi bālabhāṣitam; svavirodhyaviṣayaka(pratyayaviṣayaka)tvasyopādhitvāt| andho'yaṃ rūpajñānavānityandhasya rūpaviṣatayā kalpitaṃ yad jñānaṃ tasya rūpaṃ nāndhagamyamiti svaviṣayaviṣayakapratyayabādhyatvadarśanena vyabhicārāt| kalpitatvāttatra tadbādhane prakṛte'pi vṛtteḥ kalpitatvaṃ samam| dhīpadena caitanyamātravivakṣāyāṃ tu siddhasādhanameva|
ātmādhiṣṭhānakabhramahetuḥ, na svakāryabhramādhiṣṭhānajñānabādhyaḥ, bhramahetutvād, yadevaṃ tadevam, yathā suktyadhiṣṭhānakabhramahetukācādī' tyapi ca sādhu| vyāvṛttākārādhiṣṭhānajñānānavadhitvasya svakāryabhramādhiṣṭhānānāropitatvasya vā upādhitvād| dūrādidoṣādupalādau yatra cākacakyakalpanā tena cākacikyadoṣeṇa śuktāviva rajatakalpanā, tatrādhiṣṭhānajñānena cākacikyarūpyayorubhayorapi bādhadarśanena vyabhicārācca|
brahmānyadanādi, paramārthasat, anāditvāt, brahmavadityapi na bhadram| tatra dhvaṃsāpratiyogitvasyopādhitvāt|
brahma, deśakālasambandhaṃ vinā nāvatiṣṭhate, padārthatvād, ghaṭavadityapi na| kālasambandhaṃ vinā nāvatiṣṭhata ityasya yadā brahma tadāvaśyaṃ kālasambandha ityevaṃrūpā vyāptirarthaḥ| tathā ca siddhasādhanam| na hi yasmin kāle brahma tasmin kāle brahmaṇaḥ kālasambandho nāsti| evaṃ yatrātmā tatra kālasambandha iti daiśikavyāptāvapi siddhasādhanam| na hi deśakālāsambandhaḥ kadāpyasti| paramamuktau tu na deśo na kāla iti susthiraṃ siddhasādhanam|
brahmānyadvedaikagamyaṃ dharmādi, paramārthasat, śrutitātparyaviṣayatvād, brahmavādityapi na sādhu| paramārthikatvena śrutitātparyaviṣayatvasyopādhitvāt|
sākṣivedyaṃ sukhādi, paramārthasat, aniṣedhyatvena doṣājanyajñānaṃ prati sākṣādviṣayatvād, ātmavadityapi na; śuktirūpyādiṣu vyabhicārāt| teṣāṃ doṣajanyavṛttiviṣayatve'pi doṣājanyasākṣiviṣayatvāt| śuddhasya vṛttiviṣayatvānabhyupagame dṛṣṭāntasya sādhanavikalatvācca| doṣajanyajñānāviṣayatvavivakṣāyāṃ cā'siddho hetuḥ| sākṣyavacchedikāyā avidyāvṛtterdoṣajanyatvāt| asadgocaraśābdajñānātmakavikalpasya doṣājanyatvenāsati vyabhicārācca| ātmano vṛttiviṣayatvābhyupagame doṣajanyadehātmaikya bhramaviṣayatvāt sādhanavikalo dṛṣṭāntaḥ| tadanabhyupagame tu aviṣayatvamātrasyaiva paramārthasattvasādhakatvopapattau doṣajanyajñāneti viśeṣaṇavaiyarthyādvyāpyatvāsiddhiḥ| tāvanmātraṃ ca pakṣe svarūpāsiddhamityanyatra vistaraḥ|
vimataṃ, paramārthasat, svaviṣayajñānātpūrvabhāvitvād, ātmavadityapi na| dṛṣṭasṛṣṭipakṣe asiddheḥ| viṣamavyāptasyānāditvasyopādhitvācca|
anyonyābhāvātiriktaitadghaṭasamānādhikaraṇaitadghaṭapratiyogikābhāvatvaṃ, etadghaṭa samānakālīnavṛtti, anyonyābhāvātiriktaitadghaṭasamānādhikaraṇaitadghaṭapratiyogikābhāvamātra vṛttitvāt, etadghaṭaprāgabhāvatvavad, vyadhikaraṇadharmāvacchinnābhāvapakṣe vyadhikaraṇadharmā navacchinnetyapi viśeṣaṇīyam| atra ca svasamānādhikaraṇaḥ svasamānakālīnoyo'tyantā bhāvastadapratiyogitvalakṣaṇasattvasiddhirityapi na sādhu| sādhanāvacchinnasādhyavyāpakasyaitadghaṭaprati yogikajanyajanakānyataramātravṛttitvasyopādhitvāt| na ca -- pakṣībhūtadharmasyātyantā bhāvavṛttitvasandehe sādhanāvyāpakatvasandeha iti -- vācyam, vipakṣavādhakatarkānavatāradaśāyāṃ sandigdhopādherapi dūṣaṇatvasambhavāt| ghaṭātyantābhāvatve ca vyābhicārāt| saṃyogasambandhena ghaṭavatyapi bhūtale samavāya sambandhena ghaṭātyantābhāvasattvāt sādhyābhāvavati tatra hetorvṛtterityalamativistareṇa| ityadvaitasiddhau viśvasatyatvānumānabhaṅgaḥ|
ata mithyātve viśeṣānumānam
mithyātve ca viśeṣato'numānāni -- brahmajñānetarābādhyabrahmānyāsattvānadhikaraṇatvaṃ pāramārthikasattvādhikaraṇāvṛtti, brahmāvṛttitvāt, śuktirūpyatvavat, paramārthasadbhedavacca| vimataṃ mithyā, brahmānyatvāt, śuktirūpyavat| paramārthasattvaṃ, svasamānādhikaraṇānyonyābhāvapratiyogyavṛtti, saditarāvṛttitvād, brahmatvavat| brahmatvamekatvaṃ vā sattvavyāpakam, sattvasamānādhikaraṇatvād, asadvailakṣaṇyavat| vyāpyavṛttighaṭādiḥ, janyābhāvātiriktasvasamānādhikaraṇābhāvamātrapratiyogī, abhāvaprati yogitvād, abhidheyatvavat| abhidheyatvaṃ hi paramate kevalānvayitvādanyo'nyā bhāvamātrapratiyogi| sa ca samānādhikaraṇa eva| asmānmate tu mithyaiveti, nobhayathāpi sādhyavaikalyam| atyantābhāvaḥ, pratiyogyaśeṣādhikaraṇavṛttimātravṛtti, pratiyogyavacchinnavṛttimātravṛtti vā, nityābhāvamātravṛttitvād, anyonyābhāvatvavat| ghaṭātyantābhāvatvaṃ (sva) pratiyogi janakābhāvasamānādhikaraṇavṛtti, etatkapālasamānakālīnaitadghaṭapratiyogikābhāvavṛttitvāt, prameyatvavat| etatkapālametadghaṭātyantābhāvādhikaraṇamādhāratvātpaṭādivat| brahmatvaṃ na paramārthasanniṣṭhānyo'nyābhāvapratiṃyogitāvacchedakam, brahmavṛttitvādasadvailakṣaṇyavat| paramārthasatpratiyogiko bhedo na paramārthasanniṣṭhaḥ, paramārthasatpratiyogikatvāt, paramārthasattvāvacchinnapratiyogikābhāvavat| bhedatvāvacchinnaṃ sadvilakṣaṇapratiyogyadhi karaṇānyataravad, abhāvatvācchuktirūpyapratiyogikābhāvavat| paramārthasanniṣṭho bhedaḥ, na paramārthasatpratiyogikaḥ paramārthasadadhikaraṇatvāt, śuktirūpyapratiyogikabhedavat| mithyātvaṃ brahmatucchobhayātiriktatvavyāpakam, sakalamithyāvṛttitvāt, mithyātvasamāvanādhi karaṇātyantābhāvāpratiyogitvādvā, dṛśyatvavat| dṛśyatvaṃ paramārthasadavṛtti, abhidheya mātravṛttitvācchuktirūpyatvavat| dṛśyatvaṃ paramārthasadbhinnatvavyāpyam, dṛśyetarāvṛtti dharmatvāt, prātibhāsikatvavat| ubhayasiddhamasadvilakṣaṇaṃ mithyātvāsamānādhi karaṇadharmānadhikaraṇam, ādhāratvācchuktirūpyavat| pratiyogyavacchinno deśaḥ, atyantābhāvāśrayaḥ, ādhāratvātkālavat| ātmatvāvacchinnaṃ paramārthasattvādhi karaṇapratiyogika bhedatvāvacchinnarahitaṃ paramārthasatvāt, paramārthasatvāvacchinnavat| paramārthasati paramārthasadbhedāṅgīkāravādimate'pi sa bhedo na paramārthasattvāvacchinnaniṣṭhaḥ, kintu ghaṭatvādyavacchinnaniṣṭha eva| śuktirūpyaṃ mithyātvena prapañcānna bhidyate, vyavahāraviṣayatvād, brahmavat| sādhyasattvamatra tredhā -- svasyāmithyātvenobhayormithyātvenobhayoramithyātvena vā| tatrāntimapakṣasyāsambhavāt pakṣe sādhyasiddhiparyavasānaṃ madhyamapakṣeṇa, dṛṣṭānte tu prathamapakṣeṇeti vivekaḥ| vimataṃ mithyā, mokṣahetujñānāviṣayatve satyasadanyatvāt, śuktirūpyavat| mokṣahetujñānaviṣayatvaṃ paramārthasattvavyāpakam, paramārthasattvasamānādhikaraṇatvāt, pāramārthikatvena śrutitātparyaviṣayatvavat| etatpaṭātyantābhāvaḥ, etattantuniṣṭhaḥ, etatpaṭānādyabhāvatvāt, etatpaṭānyonyābhāvavat| tantunāśajanyapaṭanāśasya kadāpi tantuvṛttitā nāstīti tatra vyabhicāravāraṇāyānādipadam| yasya paṭasyāśrayavibhāgena nāśastadatyantābhāvasya pakṣatve tvanādipadamanādeyameva| atra caitatpaṭapratiyogikātyantābhāvatvāvacchinnasya pakṣīkaraṇānna sambandhāntareṇātyantābhāvamādāyāṃśataḥ siddhasādhanam, pakṣatāvacchedakāvacchedena sādhyasiddhe ruddeśyatvāt| samavāyasambandhāvacchinno vyadhikaraṇadharmānavacchinnaśca yaḥ etatpaṭātyantābhāvaḥ, sa eva vā pakṣaḥ| tantuśabdena ca paṭopādānakāraṇamuktam| tatra ca prāgabhāvasya sattvānna tena vyabhicāraḥ| kāryakāraṇayorabhedena siddhasādhanādi dūṣaṇāni prāgeva tattvapradīpikānumānopanyāse nirākṛtāni| yadvā samavāyasambandhāvacchinno'yametatpaṭā tyantābhāvaḥ, etattantuniṣṭhaḥ, etatpaṭapratiyogikātyantābhāvatvāt, sambandhāntarāvacchinnaitatpaṭā tyantābhāvavaditi viśiṣyānumānam| avyāpyavṛttitvānadhikaraṇatve satyuktapakṣa tāvacchedakavat, svasamānādhikaraṇātyantābhāvapratiyogi, anātmatvāt, saṃyogavat| na ca viśvātyantābhāve vyabhicāraḥ, tasyādhikaraṇasvarūpatve anātmatvahetorevābhāvāt| atiriktatve tasya mithyātvenātyantābhāvapratiyogitayā sādhyasyaiva sattvāt| na ca -- atyantābhāvasyātyantābhāve tatpratiyogitvalakṣaṇamithyātvāsiddhiriti vācyam; abhāve abhāvapratiyogitvasya bhāvagatābhāvapratiyogitvavirodhitvāt, prāgabhāvasyātyantābhāva pratiyogitve'pi tatpratiyogitvasya ghaṭādau sarvasiddhatvāt| upapāditañcaitanmithyātvamithyātve| atra cāvyāpyavṛttitvānadhikaraṇaśabdenaikadeśāvacchedenāvidyāmānatvaṃ pakṣaviśeṣaṇaṃ vivakṣitam| etena -- svasamānadhikaraṇātyantābhāvapratiyogitvoktau bādhaḥ| avayavavṛttitvānadhikaraṇatvoktau ghaṭādīnāmapakṣatvāpattiriti dūṣaṇadvayamapāstam| anātmatvehetustu jaḍatvahetuvyākhyānenaiva vyākhyātaḥ| ata eva nityadravyānyadavyāpyavṛttitvānadhikaraṇamuktapakṣatāvacchedakavat, kevalānvayyatyantābhāvapratiyogi, padārthatvāt, nityadravyavadityapi sādhu| dṛṣṭāntaścāyaṃ pararītyā, svamate tu śuktirūpyavadityeva| na ca -- svarūpeṇātyantābhāvapratiyogitve atyantāsattvāpātaḥ, tadvailakṣaṇyaprayojakābhāvaditi -- vācyam, utpattinivṛttyoranyatarapratiyogitvena parihārāt| ātmatvāvacchinnadharmiko bhedo na paramārthasatpratiyogikaḥ, ātmāpratiyogikatvāt, śuktirūpyapratiyogikabhedavat| na ca ghaṭapaṭasaṃyoge vyabhicāraḥ, hetumattayā nirṇīte aṅkurādāviva sādhyasandehasyādoṣatvāt| evamanye'pi prayogā yathocitamāracanīyā vipaścidbhiriti dik|
`hetavo'bhīṣṭasiddhyarthaṃ samyañco bahavaśca naḥ|
alpāḥ parasya duṣṭāścetyatra spaṣṭamudīritam||
abhīṣṭasiddhāvanukūlatarkabalābalaṃ cātra parīkṣya yatnāt|
pravakṣyate doṣagaṇaḥ pareṣāṃ na khedanīyaṃ tu mano'dhunaiva|'
ityadvaitasiddhau viśvamithyātve viśeṣato'numānāni||
nanu -- astu śabdabādhaḥ, tathāhi -- `viśvaṃ satyam', `yacciketa satyamittanna mogham', `yāthātathyato'rthān vyadadhācchāśvatībhyaḥ samābhyaḥ' ityādiśrutibhiḥ `asatyamapratiṣṭhaṃ te jagadāhuranīśvara' mityadismṛtibhiḥ `nābhāva upalabdheḥ' `vaidharmyācca na svapnādiva' dityādisūtreśca viśvasya satyatvapratipādanāditi -- cenna| śrutestatparatvābhāvāt| tathā hi -- 
`viśvaṃ satyaṃ maghavānā yuvoridāpaścana praminanti vrataṃ vām|
acchendrābrahmaṇaspatī havirno'nnaṃ yujeva vājinā jigāta' -- 
miti ṛksaṃhitādvitīyāṣṭakavākyasyāyamarthaḥ -- he indrābrahmaṇaspatī ! maghavānā maghavānau ghanavantau maghamiti dhananāma, makhavantāviti vā| viśvaṃ sarvaṃ satyaṃ karma, sadbhūtatvāt, phalasyāvaśyambhāvitvādvā| tādṛśaṃ karma yuvorit yuvayoḥ it itthamavadhāraṇe vā| yuvāmevoddiśya sarvāṇi karmāṇyanuṣṭheyānītyarthaḥ| āpo vyāpanaśīlā devatāḥ, canetyetatpadadvayasamudāyaḥ, aikapadyaṃ tvadhyāpakasampradāyasiddham| vāṃ yuvayorvrataṃ saṅkalpaṃ karma vā na praminanti na hiṃsanti (mīṅhiṃsāyāṃ, kraiyādikaḥ,)kintvanumodanta iti yāvat| no'smākaṃ havirdadhyādikaṃ annaṃ ca puroḍāśādikaṃ ca| accha abhilakṣya| vājinā vegavantāvaśvaviva| yujā yuktau santau| jigātaṃ debayajanamāgacchatam| (jigātirgatikarmā jauhotyādikaḥ) annaṃ ghāsaṃ prati aśvāviveti vā| yadvā -- he indrābrahmaṇaspatī ! viśvaṃ sarvaṃ, satyaṃ satyatvena paridṛśyamānaṃ jagat, yuvorit yuvayoreva, yuvābhyāmeva sṛṣṭam| athavā -- yuvayoreva viśvaṃ sarvaṃ stotraṃ, satyaṃ yathārtham, yadyat guṇajātaṃ stutyā pratipādyate tatsarvaṃ yuvayorvidyamānameva na tvāropitamityarthaḥ| āpo vyāpanaśīlā devatāḥ, abupalakṣitāni pañcamahābhūtāni vā| yuvayorvrataṃ jagadutpādanākhyaṃ karma na hiṃsanti| itthaṃ mahānubhāvau yuvāṃ jigātam| śeṣaṃ pūrvavadvyākhyeyam| tathā ca stutiparatayā nāsya viśvasatyatve tātparyam||
`śākmanā śāko aruṇaḥ suparṇa ā yo mahaḥ śūraḥ sanādanīlaḥ|
yacciketa satyamittanna moghaṃ vasu spārhamuta jetota dātā'
ityasyāpi aṣṭamāṣṭakasthasyendrastutiparatayā na viśvasatyatve tātparpam| tathā hi -- śākmanā śākaiva śākmā tena śākmanā balena| śākaḥ śaktaḥ, svaśaktyaiva sarvaṃ kartuṃ śakta ityarthaḥ| na hīndrasya sahāyāntarāpekṣāsti, indratvādeva| aruṇaḥ aruṇavarṇaḥ kaścit śobhanaparṇaḥ pakṣī ā gacchatītyadhyāhāraḥ, upasargaśruteḥ| yo maho mahān śūraḥ vikrāntaḥ, sanāt purāṇaḥ, anīlaḥ anīḍaḥ nīḍasyākartā| na hīndraḥ agnivat kutracidapi yajñe niketanaṃ karoti| evaṃ suparṇa ityādirūpakeṇendramāha| sa indra idamidānī kartavyamiti yacciketa jānāti, tatsatyamitsatyameva, na moghaṃ na vyartham| spārhaṃ spṛhaṇīyaṃ vasu nivāsārhaṃ dhanaṃ jetā jayati śatrubhyaḥ sakāśāt| uta api,dātā dadāti ca stotṛbhyaḥ| jetā dāteti tṛn| tena `na loke' tyādinā ṣaṣṭhīpratiṣedhaḥ| evamevānyadapi satyatvapratipādakamunneyam| `yāthātathyato'rthān vyadadhā' dityapi vākyaṃ na prapañcasatyatve pramāṇam| tasya pūrvasṛṣṭaprakāreṇa sarjanamarthaḥ, na tu jagatsatyatvaṃ jagatsarjanagatasatyatvaṃ vā| yatra ca stutyādiparatvaṃ nāsti, tatrāpi pratyakṣasiddhānuvādakatayā `agnirhimasya bheṣaja' mityādivākyavanna tatparatvam| na ca -- tvanmate sarvatra brahmasattvasyaiva sphuraṇāttadatiriktasya kālakṣayābādhyatvarūpasya ghaṭādisattvasya pratyakṣeṇāprāpteḥ tadvodhakatvena śruternānuvādakatvamiti vācyam; itarasattvabādhapurassaratvād brahmasattvasphuraṇābhyupagamasya tatraiva satyādipadapravṛttisvīkāreṇa tadatiriktaviśvasatyatvasya śābdabodhāviṣayatvāt tadādāyānuvāda katvāparihārāt| atha -- `pṛthivī itarabhinna, `na hiṃsyātsarvā bhūtānī' tyādau ghaṭādāvekadeśe pratyakṣeṇa, brāhmaṇādāvekadeśe vākyāntareṇa, vidheyasiddhāvapi sarvatrāsiddhatvād yathā nānuvādakatvaṃ, tathā viśvamātrasatyatvasya pratyakṣeṇāprāptatvat nānuvādakagatvamiti -- maivam; dṛṣṭānte hi pṛthivītvaṃ hiṃsātvaṃ ca eko'nugato dharma iti tadavacchedena vidheyasyāprāptatvena tatra nānuvādakatvaṃ yuktam, iha tu viśvatvaṃ nāma naiko dharmo'sti, kintu viśvaśabdaḥ sarvanāmatvāttena tena rūpeṇa ghaṭapaṭādīnāmupasthāpakaḥ| teṣu ca pratyekaṃ sattvaṃ gṛhītameveti kathaṃ nānuvādakatvam ? prakāravailakṣaṇyābhāvāt| na ca ekaśākhāsthavidhivākyaikārthaśākhāntarastha vidhivākyasya puruṣāntaraṃ pratīva yena puṃsā vādivipratipattyādinā ghaṭādisattā pratyakṣeṇa na nirṇītā, taṃ pratyarthavattvena nānuvādakatvamiti -- vācyam, evaṃ satyanuvāda sthalasyaivābhāvaprasaṅgāt| na ca sarvāvivādasthalamevodāharaṇam, sarvāvivādasya niścetumaśakyatvāt| purovādapūrvakatvādanuvādasyātrāyaṃ purovāda ityasyaivābhāvāt na śākhāntarasthavākyasyānuvādakatvaprasaṅgaḥ|
yattu -- bṛhadāraṇyakabhāṣye dehabhinnātmabodhikāyāḥ `astītyevopalabdhavya' ityādiśruteḥ pratyakṣaprāptānuvāditvamāśaṅkya vādivipratipattidarśanādityādinā tatparihṛtam; tathā ca pratyakṣasiddhasattvagrāhakatve'pi vādivipratipatti nirāsārthakatvena nānuvādakatvaṃ prakṛte'pītyuktaṃ tadayuktam| bhāṣyārthānavabodhāt| tathāhi tatra vādivipratipattidarśanena dehavyatiriktatvenātmanaḥ pratyakṣataiva nāsti| anyathā pratyakṣaprāmāṇyavādinaścārvā kādestatra vipratipattirna syādityuktam, na tu vādivipratipattinirāsenāstītyādessārthakatvam, ananuvādakatvaṃ vā| tathā coktaṃ tatraiva -- tasmājjanmāntarasambandhyātmāstitve janmāntareṣṭāniṣṭaprāptiparihāviśeṣopāye ca śāstraṃ pravartata iti| nanu -- cāturmāsyamadhyaparvaṇoḥ `dvayoḥ praṇayantī ' ti vākyasya codakaprāptāgnipraṇayanavyatiriktāgni praṇayanavidhāyakatvāt pratyakṣaprāptavyāvahārikasattva vilakṣaṇatrikālaniṣedhāpratiyogitvarūpasattvapramāpakatvaṃ prakṛte'stviti -- cenna| traikālikasattva niṣedhaśrutivirodhena viśvasatyatvaśrutestraikālikasattvaparatvābhāvāt| na ca -- vaiparītyameva kiṃ nasyāt ? vinigamakābhāvāditivācyam ; tātparyanyathānupapapattigatisāmānyānāmeva vinigamakatvāt| advaitaśrutirhi ṣaḍvidhatātparyaliṅgopetā| tatra trividhaṃ tātparyaliṅgaṃ prāmāṇyaśarīraghaṭakamarthaniṣṭhama jñātatvamabādhitatvaṃ prayojanavattvaṃ ca| trividhaṃ tu śabdaniṣṭhamatiprasaṅgavārakamupakramopa saṃhārayoraikarūpyam abhyāsaḥ, arthavādaśceti| tatra śabdaniṣṭhaliṅgatraye tāvanna vivādaḥ| sarvāsāmevopaniṣadāmeva pravṛttatvāt| mānāntarāsiddhatayā mokṣahetujñānaviṣayatayā ca ajñātatvaṃ saprayojanatvaṃ ca nirvivādameva| abādhitatvamātraṃ sandigdham| taccānyathānupapattyā gatisāmānyena ca nirṇīyate| na hi sarvaprapañcaniṣedharūpamadvaitaṃ vyāvahārikam, yena tatra śrutervyāvahārikaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ syāt ; atastatra tāttvikameva prāmāṇyam, dvaitasatyatvaṃ tu vyāvahārikam| atastatra na śrutestāttvikaṃ prāmāṇyam| parasparaviruddhayordvayostāttvikatvāyogād, vastuni ca vikalpāsambhavāt, tāttvika vyāvahārikaprāmāṇyabhedena ca vyavasthopapatteḥ, atatparatvenāvadhāritasya viśvasatyatvavākyasyai vānyathā vyākhyātumucitatvāt| tathā hi -- caturdhā hi sāmānādhikaraṇyam‌ -- adhyāse `idaṃ rajata' mityādau, bādhāyāṃ `sthāṇuḥ pramāni' tyevamādau, viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvena `nīlamutpalamityādau' abhedena `tattvamasī'tyevamādau| atra ca bādhāyāmadhyāse vā sāmānādhikaraṇyopapatterna satyatvabodhakaśruteḥ ṣaḍvidhatātparyaliṅgopetādvaitaśrutibādhakatvam| nanu ātmana ānandatvabodhikā śrutirapi `sukhaṃ supto'smī' ti sākṣipratyakṣasiddhānandānuvādinā sattvaśrutivadbhavet iti cenna, sākṣiṇa upahitānandaviṣayatvena śruteśca nirupādhikānandaviṣayatvena bhinnaviṣayatvādanuvādatvāyogāt| tadā hi svarūpānando gṛhyate| svarūpaṃ cājñānopahitameva sākṣiviṣayaḥ| nanu -- `tattvamasī' tyādau navakṛtvo'bhyāsavat, pipāsitasya jalagocarapramāṇasamplavavat, aikye ṣaḍvidhatātparyaliṅgavat, bhāvarūpājñāne pratyakṣasiddhe `tamaāsī' dityādiśrutivat, sattvaśrutirdārḍhyārthā -- iti cenna| aśeṣaviśeṣagrāhipratyakṣaprāpte dārḍhyārthamanyānapekṣaṇāt| pipāsitasya śabdaliṅganantaraṃ jale pratyakṣamapekṣitam, na tu pratyakṣānantaraṃ śabdaliṅgeḥ na ca tarhi `tama āsī' dityādeḥ na kiñcidavediṣamiti pratyakṣasiddhājñānadārḍhyārthatvaṃ na syāditi -- vācyam, `tama āsī' dityasya sṛṣṭipūrvakālasambandhitvenājñānagrāhitayā suṣuptikālasambandhitvenājñānagrāhakaṃ pratyakṣamapekṣya bhinnaviṣayatvenaiva prāmāṇyasambhavāt|
nanu -- `ṣaḍviṃśatirasya vaṅkraya' iti mantrasyāśvamedhe codakaprāptasya `catustriṃśadvājino devabandho' riti vaiśeṣikamantreṇāpoditasya ṣaḍviṃśatirityeva brūyāditi vacanavat pratyakṣaprāptajagatsattvasya mithyātvaśrutyāpātato'poditasya pratiprasavārthaṃ sattvaśrutiḥ iti cenna, mithyātvaśruteḥ pratyakṣabādhakatvābhyupagame tasyāḥ balavattvena tadvirodhāt satyatvaśruteranyaparatvād devatādhikaraṇanyāyāsambhavācca pratiprasavārthatvasya vaktumaśakyatvāt|
nanu sattvapratyakṣaprāmāṇye tenaiva mithyātvaśrutyanumānādibādhaḥ, tadaprāmāṇye na tena sattvaśruteranuvādakatvam -- iti cenna, pratyakṣāprāmāṇye'pi tatsiddhabodhakasyānuvādakatvasambhavāt| na hi pramitapramāpakatvamanuvādakatvam,kintu paścādvādakatvamātram| paścātvaṃ ca pramāṇāvadhikamapramāṇāvadhikaṃ ceti na kaścidviśeṣaḥ| na ca śruteḥ sarvasiddhapramāṇabhāvāyāḥ sadarthatvāyānanuvādakatvāya ca pratyakṣāprāptatāttvikasattvaviṣayatvamavaśyaṃ vaktavyam| tathā cāpramāṇena pratyakṣeṇa kathaṃ śruteranuvādakatvamiti -- vācyam; sattvāṃśasya pratyakṣasiddhatve'pi vākyārthasya kriyādisamabhivyāhārasiddhasyāpūrvatvena tadviṣayatayaivānuvādakatvopapattāvadvaita śrutiviruddhatāttvikasattvakalpanāyāstadarthamayogāt| paramārthasadviṣayatā tu sarvaśrutīnāṃ śuddhabrahmatātparyakatvenaiva,avāntaratātparyamādāya vyāvahārikasadviṣayateti karmakāṇḍaprāmāṇyopa pādane vakṣyate| na ca pratyakṣaṃ svaprāmāṇyanirṇanārthaṃ śrutisaṃvādamapekṣata iti na tena śruteranuvādakatvam| anyathā `satyaṃ jñānam' `neha nāne' tyādiśrutirapyanuvādinī syāt, brahmasattvasya lokato bhramādhiṣṭānatvena liṅgena ca mithyātvasya dṛśyatvādyanumānenā vedamūlapravāhānādivijñānavādādinā ca prāpteriti vācyam| yadi hi dṛṣṭe'pyarthe pratyakṣaṃ svaprāmāṇyanirṇayāya śrutisaṃvādamapekṣeta tadā śrutisaṃvādavirahiṇi dṛṣṭe kutrāpi niśśaṅkapravṛttiḥ na syāt| na syāccaiva `magnirhimasya bheṣajamityādyapi anuvādakam| na ceṣṭāpattiḥ, mānāntaragṛhītapramāṇabhāvapratyakṣanirṇīte mānāntarasyānanuvādakatve jagatyanuvādakatvakathoccheda prasaṅgāt| na ca `satyaṃ jñānam' `neha nāne' tyāderapyanuvādakatāpattiḥ| anuvādakatā hi na tāvat pratyakṣeṇa| brahmatvasāmānādhikaraṇye sattvādikaṃ hyanena pratipādanīyam| prāgeva nirākṛtatvāt| nāpi pravāhānādivijñānavādimatena| tasyāpauruṣeyaśrutyavadhika pūrvatvābhāvāt|
na ca -- sattvaśruteḥ sattvapratyakṣānapekṣatvāt na sāpekṣānuvādakatvam| nirapekṣānuvādakatvaṃ tu dhārāvahanavannāprāmāṇyahetuḥ, uktaṃ hi nayaviveke -- `sāpekṣānuvāde hi na pramitiḥ, na tu daivādanuvāde, dhārāvahanavaditi ' iti -- vācyam| yato lāghavādanuvādakatvamevāprāmāṇye prayojakam| na tu sāpekṣānuvādakatvam| anadhigatārthabīdhakatvasya prāmāṇyaghaṭakatvasya tāvataiva gatārthatvāt| na ca tarhi dhārāvahanabuddhāvaprāmāṇyam, tasyaḥ vartamānārthagrāhakatvena tattatkṣaṇaviśiṣṭagrāhakatayā anuvādakatvābhāvāt| kiṃ ca śruteratatparatve prāptatvamātrameva prayojakam| anyathā vaiphalyena svādhyāyavidhigrahaṇānupapatteḥ| api ceyaṃ sattvaśrutirapi sattvapratyakṣasāpekṣatvāt sāpekṣānuvādinyeva| na hi sattvapratyakṣaṃ vinā tanmūlaśaktyādigrahamūlakaśabdapravṛttisambhavaḥ| ata eva "yatra tu pramāṇāntarasaṃvādastatra pramāṇāntarādivārthavādādapi so'rthaḥ prasidhyati, dvayoḥ parasparānapekṣayoḥ pratyakṣānumāna yorivaikārthe pravṛtteḥ, pramātrapekṣayā tvanuvādakatvam| pramātā hyavyutpannaḥ prathamaṃ pratyakṣādibhyo yathā'rthamavagacchati, na tathā''mnāyataḥ, tatra vyutpattyapekṣatvāt" iti vācaspatyuktamapyetamarthaṃ saṃvadati| tenāmnāyasya vyutpatyapekṣatvena pratyakṣasāpekṣatvasyaivokteḥ| na ca vādivipratipattinirāsaprayojanavatvena na niṣprayojanānuvādakatvam| saprayojanānuvādakatvaṃ tu na svārthaparatvavirodhi| vidvadvākye samudāyadvitvāpādanarūpaprayojanavattvenānuvādyasvārthaparatāyā dṛṣṭatvāt| ata eva tatra vākyaikavākyatoktā| anyathā arthavādavat padaikavākyataiva syāditi vācyam| pratyakṣasiddhe vādivipratipattinirāsarūpaprayojanavattvena pramāṇāntarasya saprayojanatayā svārthaparatvoktau `agnirhimasya bheṣaja' mityādyapi tenaiva prayājanena saprayojanaṃ svārthaparaṃ ca syāt| tathā ca na pratyakṣasiddhe vādivipratipattinirāsārthamanyāpekṣā| dṛṣṭānte tu samudāyānuvādena dvitvasampādanasyoddeśyasyānyato labdhumaśakyatayā tena prayojanena svārthaparatvasya vaktuṃ śakyatvāt| etadabhiprāyaṃ ca pūrvoktaṃ nayavivekavākyam| na ca anuvādatve'pi naiṣphalyamātram, na tvaprāmāṇyam, yāthārthyameva prāmāṇyaṃ, na tvanadhigatārthatve sati yāthārthyamiti vācyam| tātparyaviṣaye śabdaḥ pramāṇam `yatparaḥ śabdaḥ sa śabdārtha' ityabhiyuktābhyupagamāt| anyathā svādhyāyavidhigrahaṇānupapatteruktatvācca| na hyanyataḥ siddhe'rthe śāstratātparyam, ato na tatra prāmāṇyam, yadāhurbhaṭṭācāryāḥ `aprāpte śāstramarthava ' diti|
nanu ayamanuvādo na `vāyurvai kṣepiṣṭhā devate' tyādivat stutyarthaḥ| na vā
`dadhnā juhotī' tyādivadanyavidhānārthaḥ| anuvādyatve'pi anyavidhānāya pramāṇānūditasya tāttvikatvaniyamāt| nahi `vrīhīn prokṣatī' tyādāvāropitavrīhyāderdhīḥ, anuvādyasyāsattve hyāśrayāsiddhau dharmadharmisaṃsargarūpānumitivedya ivānuvādyavidheyasaṃsargarūpavākyārtho bādhitaḥ syād iti cenna| asyānuvādasyāprāptānyaprāptyarthatvāt| na ca pramāṇānūditasya tāttvikatvaniyamaḥ| svapnādhyāye, śuktau `nedaṃ rajata' miti vākye na vyabhicārāt| atha tatra jñānaviṣayatayā niṣedhyatayā cānuvāda iti na tāttvikatvam, tarhi prakṛte'pi `neha nāne' ti niṣedhārthatvādasyānuvādasya na tāttvikatvamiti gṛhāṇa| ata eva vākyārthasyāsattvaprasaṅgaḥ, tātparyaviṣayasya sattvāt|
atha `kiṃ cane' tyanenaivānuvādasya kṛtatvāt kimadhikeneti cenna, sāmānyato niṣedhasya hi `kiṃ cane ' tyanena niṣedhyasamarpaṇe'pi viśiṣya niṣedhe viśiṣya niṣedhyasamarpaṇasyopayogāt| atha niṣedhavākyasya na niṣedhyasamarpakavākyāntarāpekṣā, anyathā `na kalañjaṃ bhakṣaye ' dityādāvapi niṣedhyasamarpaṇārthaṃ `kalañjaṃ bhakṣaye' dityādivākyāntarasāpekṣatvaprasaṅgaḥ iti cenna, sarvatrāpekṣāniyamābhāvāt| sati sambhave prakṛte tyāgāyogāt| `atirātre ṣoḍaśinaṃ gṛhṇāti' `nātirātre ṣoḍaśinaṃ gṛhṇātī' tyadau vākyāntaraprāptasya niṣedhadarśanācca| na ca tadvadeva vikalpāpattiḥ, siddhe vastunivikalpāyogāt| ṣoḍaśigrahaṇāgrahaṇavākyayorubhayorapi mānāntarāprāptaviṣayatvena tulyabalatvavadiha sattvaśrutermānāntaraprāptaviṣayatvena niṣedhaśruteścā prāptaviṣayatvena tulyabalatvābhāvācca| ata eva niṣedhavākyaprābalyāttadanurodhenetarannīyate|
atha -- aprāptānyaprārptthatve'pyalaukikasya `āpaśca na pra minantī' ityādipadārthasaṃsargasya vidheyasya sattvānna niṣedhyārthānuvādakatvamiti cenna, tadanyaparatvasya prāgevoktatvāt| nanu `yattanne ' ti niṣedhānuvādaliṅgābhāvānnānuvādaḥ| na| yatkiñcilliṅgābhāvena laiṅgikābhāvasya vaktumaśakyatvāt nanu tarhi `tatsatyami' tyādyapi `na sattannāsaducyata' iti, `asadvā idamagra āsī' diti ca niṣedhāya ` san ghaṭaḥ' `sad ghaṭajñānam' `satsukhasphuraṇa' mityādisiddhabrahmasattvānuvādi syād -- iti cenna| brahmatvasāmānādhikaraṇyena sattvasya pratyakṣādibhyo'prāpteḥ| śūnyavādaprasaṅgena tasya niṣedhāyogācca| `idaṃ sarvaṃ yadayamātme' tyatrānuvādaliṅgasambhavena kalpanācca| evamānandaśruterapi, `aduḥkhamasukhaṃ sama' miti niṣedhāya na pratyakṣaprāptānandānuvāditvam, duḥkhasāhacaryeṇa sukhasyāpi vaiṣayikasyaiva grahaṇena tanniṣedhāya brahmarūpasukhānuvādāyogāt| etacca sarvamuktaṃ vivaraṇe -- "niṣprapañcāsthūlādivākyānusāreṇa `idaṃ sarvaṃ yadayamātme' tyādīni niṣedhyasamarpakatvenaikavākyatāṃ pratipadyante, suṣuptau niṣprapañcatāyāḥ puruṣārthatvadarśanād " iti| atha -- niṣprapañcatā na puruṣārthaḥ| mūrcchāyāṃ tattvādarśanāt, na ca tadā tadajñānamātraṃ na tu tadabhāva iti vācyam, samaṃ suṣuptāvapīti -- cenna| mūrcchāyāṃ svarūpasukhasphuraṇābhāvāt| tathā ca sūtram -- "mugdhe'rdhasampattiḥ pariśeṣād " -- iti| suṣuptimuktikālīnaniṣprapañcatāyāṃ svarūpasukhānubhavena tasyāḥ puruṣārthatvāt| tathā ca śrutiḥ "dvitīyādvai bhayaṃ bhavati" iti| atha `tasmādekākī na ramata' iti śruteḥ saprapañcatāpi puruṣārthaḥ| na| tasyā duḥkhasādhanatvena puruṣārthatvāyogāt| karmakāṇḍavadasyāḥ śruteḥ avivekipuruṣaparatvācca|
nanu `pṛthagātmānaṃ preritāraṃ ca matvā juṣṭastatastenāmṛtatvametī' ti bhedajñānasya mokṣahetutvaśravaṇāt kathaṃ na saprapañcatā puruṣārtha iti cenna| mateḥ pūrvaṃ mamāpi prerakapṛthaktvadṛṣṭeḥ, saguṇabrahmajñānavat prerakatvena brahmajñānasyāpi paramparayopakārakatvāt| `ekadhaivānudraṣṭavya' -- mityādivākyasvārasyādabhedajñānasyaiva sākṣāt mokṣahetutvāt| ata eva prerakatvajñānasya joṣahetutvamuktam| tathottaratrāpi `vedavido viditvā līnā brahmaṇi tatparā ye vimuktāstadātmatattvaṃ prasamīkṣya dehī ekaḥ kṛtārtho bhavate vītaśoka' ityabheda eva śrūyate| ato na bhedajñānasya mokṣahetutvam| etena `neha nāne' ti śrutireva `viśvaṃ satyam' ityabādhyatvarūpabādhaniṣedhāya vijñānavādādiprāptaviśvaniṣedhānuvādinī kiṃ na syāditi nirastam| bhāvābhāvayoḥ parasparaviraharūpatve same'pi bhāvagraho nirapekṣatvāt nābhāvagrahamapekṣate| abhāvagrahastu sapratiyogikatayā bhāvagrahamapekṣate| ato `neti neti' śrutereva sattvaśrutyapekṣā, na tu sattvaśruterneti śrutyapekṣā, anyathā anyon‌yāśrayāpatteḥ|
nanu utsargāpavādanyāyo'stu yathā hi `na hiṃsyāt sarvā bhūtānī' ti śrutiraviśeṣapravṛttāpi hiṃsātvasāmānyasya pratyakṣādiprāptatvānniṣedhyopasthitau nāgnīṣomīyavākyamapi niṣedhyasamarpaṇāyāpekṣate, tathā `netine' tyādiśrutiraviśeṣapravṛttāpi pratyakṣaprāptaghaṭādi sattvarūpaniṣedhyamādāya nirākāṅkṣā satī na pratyakṣāprāptadharmādharmādisatyatvabodhikāṃ `viśvaṃsatya' mityādiśrutimapi niṣedhyasamarpaṇāyāpekṣitumarhati| yatra tu mānāntareṇa niṣedhyasyāprāptistatra niṣedhaśrutiḥ niṣedhyasamarpaṇāya śrutyantaramapekṣata eva| yathā ṣoḍaśigrahaṇāgrahaṇayoḥ mānāntareṇa niṣedhyopasthitāvapi vākyāpekṣaṇe agnīṣomīyahiṃsāyā api niṣedhyatvenādharmatvaṃ syād iti cet, maivam, agnīṣomīyavākyasya niṣedhaviṣayanyūnaviṣayatvenānanyaśeṣatayā svārthatātparyavattavena ca na niṣedhyasamarpaṇadvāreṇa niṣedhavākyaśeṣatā| viśvaṃ satya'mityādestu niṣedhaviṣayasamaviṣayatvena svārthatātparyarahittvena ca niṣedhyasamarpaṇadvāreṇa niṣedhavākyaśeṣatocitaiva| ata eva pratyakṣāprāptadharmādisattvopasthāpanena vākyasāphalyamapi| svārthatātparyarahitatvena ca nāgnīṣomīyavākyatulatyatvamityuktam| ato dṛśyatvādihetordharmādyaṃśe'pi śrutyā na bādhaḥ|
athavā vyāvahārikasattvapareyaṃ viśvasatyatvaśrutiḥ| na ca vyāvahārikasattve sarvāvipratipattestatpratipādanavaiyarthyam| daśāviśeṣe svarganarakādisattvapratipādanena tatprāpti parihārārthaṃ pravṛttinivṛttyoreva tatprayojanatvāt| vyāvahārikatvaṃ ca brahmajñānetarābādhyatvaṃ na tu abādhyatvam, mithyātvabodhakaśrutivirodhāt| na caivaṃ dṛḍhabhrāntijanakatvād atyantāprāmāṇyāpattiḥ, svapnārthapratipādanavadupapatteḥ| etāvāneva viśeṣaḥ -- tat prātibhāsikam, idaṃ tu vyāvahārikamiti| nanu mithyātvaśruterlakṣaṇayā akhaṇḍacinmātraparatvena sattvabodhanād avirodhitvameva| na| akhaṇḍārthabodhasya dvitīyābhāvabuddhidvārakatvena jagatsatyatvavirodhitvāt| na ca prapañcasatyatvaśruteraprāmāṇyaprasaṅgaḥ, atattvāveda katvasyāvāntaratātparyamādāyeṣṭatvāt| paramatātparyeṇa tu tattvāvedakatvaṃ sarvaśrutīnāmapi samam| prātibhāsikavyāvṛttasya vyāvahārikasya tadvati tatprakārakatvādirūpasya nirākartumaśakyatvāt| āsāṃ vyāvahārikaṃ prāmāṇyamavyāhatameva| `asadvā idamagra āsī' dityādiśrutyanurodhenāpi `tatsatya' mityādiśrutirna brahmaṇi vyāvahārikasattvaparā| brahmaṇo vyavahārātītatvāt| tasyāparamārthatvena ca niradhiṣṭhānatayā śūnyavādāpatteḥ| kiñcittattvamagṛhītvā ca bādhānupapatteḥ| ata eva satyatvaśrutivirodhena mithyātvaśrutirevānyaparetyapi na| ṣaḍvidhatātparyaliṅgopetatvena mithyātvaśruterananyaparatayā prabalatvāt| vaidikatātparyaviṣayasya ca tāttvikatvaniyamena tātparyajñāpakānāmapi liṅgānāmarthatathātva eva paryavasānāt| sattvaśrutivākyasthapadānāṃ cānyaparatvānna sattve tātparyaliṅgāśaṅkā|
nanu yadi sattvaśrutiḥ pratyakṣaprāptārthatvānna svārthaparā, tarhi mithyātvaśrutirapi tadviruddhārthatvāt svārthaparā na syāt| tatprāptitadvirodhayostātparyābhāvahetvorubhayatrāpi samatvād iti cenna| pratyakṣāpekṣayā candrādhikaparimāṇabodhakāgamasyeva mithyātvabodhakāgamasyāpi balavatvena pratyakṣaprāptānuvādisattvaśrutyapekṣayāpi balavattvāt (prāptārthāprāptārthayorvirodhe prāptārthasyāprāptavidhyarthatvenānyaśeṣatvaniyamādaprāptārthasyaivānanyaśeṣatvena balavattvāt|) anyatho bhayorapi aprāmāṇyāpatteḥ| taduktaṃ saṅkṣepaśārīrake -- `atatparā tatparavedavākyairvirudhyamānā guṇavāda eveti'| ata evānanyaśeṣamithyātvaśrutivirodhāt na pratyakṣāgṛhītatrikālā bādhyatvarūpasatyatvaparā jagatsatyatvaśrutirityuktam| advaitaśruteśca prābalye niravakāśatvatātparya vattvādikameva prayojakam, na niṣedhavākyatvam| etena niṣedhavākyatvena prābalye kiti taddhite vṛddhividhāyakāt `kiti ca' iti sūtrāt sāmānyato guṇavṛddhiniṣedhakaṃ kṅiti ceti sūtraṃ balavat syād, agnīṣomīyavākyādahiṃsā vākyam, ṣoḍaśino grahaṇavākyādagrahaṇavākyam, `satyaṃ jñānamananta' mityādivākyād‌ `asadvā idamagra āsī' dityādivākyaṃ ca balavat syādityapāstam| sāmānyaviśeṣabhāvādinā sāvakāśatvaniravakāśatvādirūpabalavaiparītyāt| `viśvaṃ satya' mityādestu vyāvahārika sattvaviṣayatayā anyaśeṣatayā ca sāvakāśatvādeḥ prāguktatvāt| tasmānna sattvaśrutivirodhaḥ| nāpi
asatyamapratiṣṭhaṃ te jagardāhuranīśvaram|
etāṃ dṛṣṭimavaṣṭabhya naṣṭātmāno'lpabuddhayaḥ||
ityādismṛtivirodhaḥ, sadviviktatvavādino mama jagatyasadvailakṣaṇyāṅgīkāreṇa tatpratipādakasmṛti virodhābhāvāt|
nanu `nābhāva upalabdhe' `vaidharmyācca na svapnādiva' diti sūtradvayena jagataḥ pāramārthikasattvabodhanena virodhaḥ| na cānena śūnyavādinirāsārthenāsadvailakṣaṇyamātrapratipādanānna virodhaḥ| arthakriyākāritvalakṣaṇasyāsadvailakṣaṇyasya śūnyāvādimate'pi sattvena tanmatanirāsārthatvānupapatteḥ| niṣedhāpratiyogitvarūpasyāsadvailakṣaṇyasya tvayāpyanaṅgīkārāt| asadvailakṣaṇyamātrasya sādhane sūtre svapnavailakṣaṇyoktyayogācca| vyāvahārikasatyatvamātreṇa svapnavailakṣaṇyasya tvayāpyaṅgīkārād| asadvailakṣaṇyamātrasya tanmate'pi satvācca| taduktaṃ bauddhaiḥ -- `dve satye samupāśritya buddhānāṃ dharmadeśanā' iti cenna| sūtrārthānavabodhāt| tathā hi -- sadrūpād brahmaṇo jagatsargaṃ vadataḥ samanvayasya sarvamasadityanumānena virodhasandehe
`sannāsanna sadasanna nacānubhayatattvakam|
vimataṃ tarkapīḍyatvānmarīciṣu yathodakam||'
iti brahmasādhāraṇyāṃ nistattvatāyāṃ prāptāyāṃ sūtreṇa parihāraḥ| sato brahmaṇo nābhāvaḥ na śūnyatvam, upalabdheḥ sattvena prāmāṇāt pratīteḥ| tathā ca kiñcitparamārthasadavaśyaṃ śūnyavādināpi svīkāryam, anyathā bādhasya niravadhikatvaprasaṅgāditi sūtrārthaḥ| sa ca na prapañcamithyātvavirodhī| tathā coktaṃ -- 
`bādhito'pahnavo mānaiḥ vyāvahārikamānatā|
mānānāṃ tāttvikaṃ kiñcid vastu nāśritya durbhaṇā||' iti|
nāpi svapnavaidharmyoktyayogaḥ, tasyāḥ `vimataṃ nistattvaṃ tarkapīḍyatvāt marumarīcikājalava' dityanumāne bādhyatvapramāṇā gamyatvadoṣajanyatvādyupādhipradarśanaparatvāt vijñānavādanirākaraṇapareṇāpi nānena sūtreṇa virodhaḥ| rupādirahitabrahmajagadupādānatvaprati pādakasamanvayasya nīlādyākāraṃ vijñānaṃ sādhayatā anumānena virodhasandehe -- 
svapnadhīsāmyato buddherbudhyā'rthasya sahekṣaṇāt|
tadbhedenānirupyatvāt jñānākāro'rtha iṣyatām||
vimatā dhīḥ, na jñānavyatiriktālambanā, dhītvāt, svapnadhīvat| vipakṣe ca jñānābhāne'pyarthabhānaprasaṅgo bādhakaḥ| nahi bhinnayoraśvamahiṣayoḥ sahopalambhaniyamo'sti| tasmānna jñānātiriktaṃ saditi prāpte parihātasūtraṃ `nābhāva upalabdhe' rityādi|
bādhena sopādhikatānumāne upāyābhāvena sahopalambhaḥ|
sārūpyato buddhitadarthabhedasthūlārthabhaṅgo bhavato'pi tulyaḥ||
sūtrārthastu -- nābhāvaḥ jñānātiriktasyārthasya nāsatvam, kintu vyavahāradaśābādhyārthakriyākāritva rūpaṃ sattvameva| upalabdheḥ jñānātirekeṇa pramāṇairupalabdheḥ| svapnavaidharmyoktiḥ bādhyatvādyupādhipradarśanāya| tena bādhāt sopādhikatvācca pūrvānumānaṃ duṣṭamityarthaḥ| tasmānnaivamapi virodhaśaṅkā| taduktaṃ tasmānna jñānākāro'rthaḥ, kintu bāhyaḥṣa sa cārthakriyākāritvasattvopeto'pi advaitaśrutivaśād brahmaṇi kalpito na paramārthasanniti siddhāntasya sugatamatādbheda iti| uktaṃ cātmatattvaviveke -- 
`na grāhyabhedamavadhūya dhiyo'sti vṛtti
stadvādhane balini vedanaye jayaśrīḥ|
no cedanindyamidamīdṛśameva viśvaṃ
tathyaṃ tathāgatamatasya tu ko'vakāśaḥ||'iti
dharmigrāhakamānabādhaśca prāgeva parihṛta iti śivam|| iti viśvamithyātvasyāgamādibādhoddhāraḥ||
nanu sattvasādhakānāṃ mithyātvasādhakānumānebhyaḥ prābalyam| mithyātva sādhakapratijñādyupanītapakṣādīnāṃ mithyātvābodhane sarvamithyātvāsiddhiḥ, tadbodhane parasparavyāhatirāśrayāsiddhyādikaṃ ceti cenna| mithyātvasādhakapratijñādyupanītapakṣādīnāṃ mithyātvabodhane'pi vyāhatyabhāvāt| pratijñādibhisteṣāṃ trikālābādhyatvarūpasattvāpratipādanāt| nanu sādhakatvānyathānupapattyā paramārthasattvamāyāti| paramārthasata eva sādhakatvāt| sādhakatāyāḥ prāksattvaghaṭitatvāt| na tu dhīmātraviṣayatvam, aparokṣadhīviṣayatvam, sattvena tādṛśadhīviṣayatvaṃ vā sādhakatāprayojakam| tucche nityātīndriye cātivyāptyavyāptibhyām| tattvena jñānamapi na tatra prayojakam| vahnitvenājñāte'pi vahnau dāhakatvadarśanād| vahnitvena jñāte'pi guñjāpuñje tadadarśanācca| nāpi tricaturakakṣyāsvabādhitāsattvapratītistantram| ātmano gauratvenānityatvasya nabhaso nailyena sparśavattvasya cāpatteḥ| gauro'haṃ; `nīlaṃ nabhaḥ' ityādipratītāvapi tricaturakakṣyāsvabādhād, yauktikabādhasya tvanmate prakṛte'pi bhāvāditi cenna| yādṛśyā buddhyā tava nabhonailyādidhīvyāvṛttayā ghaṭādau sattvasiddhiḥ tādṛgbuddhiviṣayatvasyaiva sādhakatve tantratvāt| ata eva lokaprasiddhistantramitīṣṭa siddhyuktamapyuktābhiprāyeṇa samyageva| evaṃ tricaturakakṣyāsvabādhitā vādiprativādiprāśnikādīnāṃ sattavabuddhistantramityupapannameva| guñjāpuñjasya vahnitve, ātmano gauratve, nabhaso nīlatve ca tādṛgbuddhiviṣayatvasya tavāpyasampratipatteḥ| anyathā teṣāmapi tatra sattvasiddhiprasaṅgāt|
atha yādṛśyā śabde klṛptadoṣarahitayā buddhyā tava brahmaṇi sattvasiddhiḥ tādṛśyā pratyakṣe klṛptadoṣarahitayā mama jagati sattvasiddhirastu, sādhakatulyatvāditi cenna| brahmasattvabuddhivad jagatsattvabuddherabādhitatvābhāvāt| trikālābādhyatvarūpasya sattvasya pratyakṣāviṣayatāyā uktatvācca| na ca buddhiviṣayatvasya tantratve vahnitvenājñātasya vahneradāhakatvaprasaṅgaḥ, amṛtatvena jñātasya ca viṣasya sañjīvakatvaprasaṅga iti vācyam| vahnau tādṛgbuddhiviṣayatvasyeśvarādisādhāraṇasya sattvāt| viṣe sañjīvakatvaprasaṅgasya nabhonailyāditulyatvāt| vastutastu jñātājñātasādhāraṇaṃ vyāvahārikaṃ sattvameva sādhakatve tantram| tacca brahmajñānetarabādhyatvameva| tacca na mithyātvaghaṭitam| atyantābādhye brahmajñānabādhye ca tulyatvāt| ata eva nedaṃ paramārthasattvavyāpyam| evaṃ ca paramārthasattvasya sādhakatāyāmatantratvena tadabhāve'pi na sādhakatānupapattiḥ|
etena -- vyāvahārikatvaṃ brahmajñānabādhyatvaṃ vā ? vyāvahārikaviṣayatve sati sattvaṃ vā ? sattvena vyavahāramātraṃ vā ? nādyaḥ| mithyātvasiddheḥ prāk tadasiddhyā anyonyāśrayāt| nāpi dvitīyaḥ| tasyāsmākaṃ mithyātvavirodhitveneṣṭatvāt| na tṛtīyaḥ| sattvābhāve sādhakatvānupapatteriti -- nirastam| uktanirukteraduṣṭatvāt| na ca hetvādīnāṃ vyāvahārikasattve sādhyasyāpi vyāvahārikasattvameva syādanumitiviṣayasādhyasya parāmarśaviṣayahetunā samānasattākatvaniyamāditi vācyam| dṛśyatvavanmithyātvasyāpi vyāvahārikatvena samānasattākatvasyeṣṭatvāt| samānasattākatvaniyamāsiddheśca| dhūlīpaṭale dhūmabhramādapi vahnyanumitipramādarśanād| gandhavyāpyapṛthivītvapramāto'pi gandhaprāgabhāvāvacchinne ghaṭe pakṣe bādhāsphūrtidaśāyāmanumitibhramadarśanācca| mithyātvasya mithyātve'pi tattvāvedaka śrutivedyatvopapattiḥ| sattvena sata iva mithyātvena mithyābhūtasyāpi pramāṇagamyatvāvirodhād| ekāṃśe tattvāvedakatvābhāve'pi aparāṃśe tattvāvedakatvopapatteḥ|
nanu vyāvahārikatvaṃ sādhakatāyāmatantram| ajñānādisādhake paramārthasati sākṣiṇi tadabhāvāditi cenna| brahmajñānetarābādhyatvasyātyantābādhye'pi sattvasyoktatvāt| traividyavibhāge pāramārthikavyāvṛttavyāvahārikatvaniruktāvapi janakatāyāṃ tatsādhāraṇye'pyadoṣāt| vastutastu -- sākṣyapyajñānopahita evājñānādisādhakaḥ| sa ca vyāvahārika eva| anupahitena paramārthasadākāreṇa tasyāsādhakatvāt| evaṃ ca vyāvahārikasattvameva sarvatra sādhakatāyāṃ prayojakamiti sthitam| yathā cājñānopahitasya sākṣitve'pi nātmāśrayādidoṣaḥ tathoktaṃ dṛśyatvahetūpapādane prāk| agre ca vakṣyate| yatra ca yatsādhakaṃ vyāvahārikam tatra tad vyāvahārikam| yatra tu sādhakaṃ prātītikaṃ, tatra phalamapi tathaiva, na tu vyāvahārikamiti sarvavidhipratiṣedhādivyavahārāsaṅkaraḥ| ata eva lokasyāpi vyatikrame vicārasya yādṛcchikavāṅmātratāpattirityudayanoktamapi nirastam, vyāvahārikasattvena lokamaryādā natikramāt| bhaṭṭācāryavacanāni viruddhatvena bhāsamānāni sattvatraividhyanirūpaṇāyāmavirodhena vyākhyāsyante| tasmāt pakṣādisarvamithyātvasādhane'pi na vyāhatiḥ|| ityadvaitasiddhau asataḥ sādhakatvopapattiḥ|
