nanu sattvāpekṣayā tucchavilakṣaṇatvādergauravataratvena sādhakatve kathaṃ tantratvamiti cenna| trikālabādhaviraharūpasya sattvasya laghutvābhāvāt| jātyādirūpasya tasya mithyātvāvirodhitvād| ubhayasiddhe sadvivikte sādhakatvadarśanena pāramārthikasattvasya sādhakatvāprayojakatvācca| tathā hi -- pratibimbe bimbasādhakatvaṃ tāvadasti| tasya bimbātmanā sattve'pi pratibimbākāreṇāsattvāt paramārthasattvaṃ na sādhakatve prayojakam| evaṃ svāpnārthasyāsato'pi bhāviśubhāśubhasūcakatvam| yadyapi tatratyadarśanasyaiva sūcakatvam| `puruṣaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ kṛṣṇadantaṃ paśyati' ityādiśrutibalāt| tathāpi darśanamātrasyātiprasaktatvena viṣayo'pyavaśyamapekṣaṇīya eva| evaṃ sphaṭikalauhityasya upādhisannidhānasādhakatvaṃ ca| na ca lauhityaṃ sphaṭike na mithyā, kintu dharmamātrapratibimba iti na pṛthagudāharaṇamiti vācyam| dharmibhūtamukhādinairapekṣyeṇa taddharmabhūtarūpādipratibimbādarśanāt| pratibimbasyā vyāpyavṛttitvaniyamena lauhityasya sphaṭike vyāpyavṛttitvapratītyayogācca| lauhitye sphaṭikasya tvārope tasya pratibimbatvam, sphaṭike lauhityārope tu tasya mithyātvamiti vivekaḥ| "sphaṭikamaṇerivopadhānanimitto lohitimā" iti lohitimano mithyātvaṃ darśitaṃ pratibimbasatyatvavādibhiḥ pañcapādikākṛdbhiḥ| evaṃ rekhātādātmyenāropitānāṃ varṇānāmartha sādhakatvam| na ca -- rekhāsmāritā varṇā evārthasādhakā iti -- vācyam| āśaiśavamayaṃ kakāro'yaṃ gakāra ityanubhavād, abhedenaiva smaraṇād| viveke satyapi dṛḍhatarasaṃskāravaśāt nāropanivṛttiḥ| ata eva kakāraṃ paṭhati likhati ceti sārvalaukiko vyavahāraḥ| varṇāropitadīrghahrasvatvādīdāṃ ca nago nāga ityādāvarthaviśeṣapratyāyakatvam| na ca varṇeṣvanāropitadhvanisāhityaṃ tadabhivyaktatvarūpaṃ vā dairdhyaṃ pratyāyakam, evaṃ hrasvatvādikamapīti vācyam| dhvanīnāmasphuraṇe'pi dīrgho varṇa ityādipratyayāt|
nanu āropitena varṇadairghyādinā kathaṃ tāttvikārthasiddhiḥ ? na hyāropitena dhūmena tāttvikavahnisiddhiriti cenna| sādhakatāvacchedakarūpavattvameva sādhakatāyāḥ prayojakam, na tvāropitatvamanāropitatvaṃ vā| dhūmābhāsasya tvasādhakatvam sādhakatāvacchedakarūpavyāptyabhāvāt nāsattvād| anābhāsatvagrahaśca tatra bahulordhvatādigrahaṇavadvyāptigrahaṇārthamevāpekṣitaḥ| taduktaṃ vācaspatimiśraiḥ -- `yathā satyatvaviśeṣe'pi cakṣuṣā rūpameva jñāpyate na rasaḥ tathaivāsattvāviśeṣe'pi varṇadairghyādinā satyaṃ jñāpyate, na tu dhūmābāsādinā' iti| dṛṣṭaṃ hi māyākalpitahastyādeḥ rajjusarpādeśca bhayādihetutvaṃ, savitṛsuṣirasya ca maraṇasūcakatvaṃ, śaṅkāviṣasya ca maraṇahetutvam| `nanu tatra śaṅkaiva bhayamutpādya dhātuvyākulatāmutpādayatīti saiva maraṇahetuḥ, na tu śaṅkitaṃ viṣamapi| evaṃ savitṛsuṣiramāyākalpitagajādīnāmapi jñānameva tattadarthakriyākāri, na tvartho'pi| tathā ca sarvatrodāhṛtasthaleṣu jñānameva hetuḥ| tacca svarūpataḥ satyameva| anvayavyatirekāvapi jñānasyaiva kāraṇatāṃ grāhayataḥ| na hi sannihitaṃ sarpamajānāno bibheti| na ca arthānavacchinnasya jñānasya hetutve'tiprasaṅgādarthāvacchinnameva jñānaṃ hetuḥ| tathā cārtho'pi hetureveti vācyam, arthāvacchinnasya jñānasya hetutve'pi avacchedakasyārthasya tāṭasthyenāhetutvopapatteḥ ghaṭāvacchinnasya tadatyantābhāvatad‌dhvaṃsāderghaṭadeśakālabhinnadeśakālāditve'pyavacchedakasya ghaṭasya tadabhāvavat, ghaṭecchābrahmajñānayorghaṭajñānavedāntasādhyatve'pi ghaṭabrahmaṇoḥ tadabhāvavat, ghaṭaprāgabhāvasya ghaṭaṃ prati janakatve'pi ghaṭasyājanakatvavat, viśeṣādarśanasya bhramaṃ prati janakatve'pi viśeṣadarśanasya tadabhāvavat, vihitākaraṇasya pratyavāyajanakatve'pi vihitakaraṇasya tadabhāvavat, svarga kāmanāyāḥ yāgajanakatve'pi svargasya tadajanakatvavat, atītādismṛtyāderduḥ svādi janakatve'pyatītādestadajanakatvavat, asadviṣayakaparokṣajñānasya tad‌vyavahārahetutve'pya satastadabhāvavat, cikīrṣita ghaṭabuddherghaṭahetutve'pi ghaṭasya tadahetutvavat, brahma jñānasya tadajñānanivartakatve'pyudāsīnasvabhāvasya brahmaṇastadabhāvavat, brahmājñānasya jagatpariṇāmikāraṇatve'pi brahmaṇastadabhāvavacca| na ca -- tathāpi mithyārthe jñānavyāvarta katā'stītyasato'pi hetutvamiti -- vācyam| na hi vyāvṛttidhīhetutvaṃ vyāvartakatvam| kintu vyāvṛttidhīhetudhīviṣayatvameva| satyapi daṇḍe tadajñāne vyāvṛttyajñānāt| athāvacchedakasya mithyātve avacchinnasyāpi tanniyamaḥ| na| tucchajñāne tucchavailakṣaṇye ca tucchatvasya, prātibhāsikādvailakṣaṇye prātibhāsikatvasya, pañcamaprakārāyāmātmasvarūpabhūtāyāṃ vā anirvacanīyājñānasya nivṛttau caturthaprakarānirvacanīyatvasya, pāramārthikātmasvarūpe tadbhinne vā anṛtadvaitasyābhāve'nṛtatvasya cādarśanāt tatrāvacchedakānāmasadādīnāṃ tāṭasthye'trāpi tathāstviti -- cet, atrocyate| yaduktaṃ tāṭasthyalakṣaṇamupalakṣaṇatvameva sarvatrāvacchedasyeti, tanna| viśeṣaṇatve sambhavatyupalakṣaṇatvāyogāt| viśeṣaṇabādhapūrvakatvādupalakṣaṇatvakalpanāyāḥ| anyathā, `daṇḍī praiṣānanvāha ', `lohitoṣṇīṣā ṛtvijaḥ pracaranti' ityādāvapi vede daṇḍalauhityāderupalakṣaṇatvāt tadabhāve'pi anuṣṭhānaprasaṅgaḥ| `sarvādīni sarvanāmāni' ityatra sarvaśabdasya sarvanāmasaṃñjñā na syāt| `janmādyasya yataḥ' ityatra janmano brahmalakṣaṇatvaṃ na syāt, viśeṣaṇārthatvena tadaguṇasaṃvijñānabahuvrīhisambhave'pyupalakṣaṇārthatvenātad guṇasaṃvijñānabrīhisvīkāraprasaṅgāt| evaṃ `asipāṇayaḥ praveśyantām' ityādilaukika prayoge'pi| pratibimbādijñānānāṃ janakatve ca viśeṣaṇatayā pratibimbādīnāmapi janakatve bādhābhāvāt nopalakṣaṇatvapakṣo yujyate| udāhṛtasthaleṣu sarvatra bādhakamastyeveti viśeṣaḥ| tathā hi -- prathame ghaṭadeśakālau gṛhītvā tadbhinnadeśakālatvaṃ tadatyantābhāvādau grāhyam| ghaṭasyāpi tatsambandhe tad‌deśakālabhinnadeśakālatvameva vyāhataṃ syāt| dvitīye tviṣṭāpattiḥ| kvacid ghaṭajñānasya ghaṭecchājanakatvavad ghaṭaṃ pratyapi janakatvād| brahmaṇo vedāntasādhyatve tu nityatvavirodhaḥ| tṛtīye prāgabhāvavad ghaṭasya svajanakatve pratiyogiprāgabhāvayoḥ samānakālīnatvāpattiḥ| svāvadhikapūrvatvaghaṭitajanakatvasya svasmin vyāhatatvaṃ ca| caturthe pañcame ca pratiyogitadabhāvayoḥ sahāvṛttyā bhramapratyavāyayoranupapattiprasaṅgaḥ| ṣaṣṭhe kāmanāvat kāmanāviṣayasya yāgajanakatve tasya prāk‌sattayā tatkāmanaiva vyāhanyeta, siddhe icchāvirahāt| saptame atītasya janakatve kāryāvyavahitapūrvakāle svasvavyāpārānyatarasattvāpattiḥ| aṣṭame asato janakatve niḥsvarūpatvavyāghātaḥ| navame cikīrṣitaghaṭajñānavat svasya janakatve pūrvavad vyāghātaḥ| daśame udāsīnasya brahmaṇo na nivartakatvam| svarūpataḥ upahitasyaiva vṛttiviṣayatvena tasyāviṣayatvād| upahitasya ca nivartakatvamastyeva| ekādaśe brahmājñānasya pariṇāmikāraṇatve'pi na brahmaṇo jagatkāraṇatvam, kārye jaḍatvopalambhāt| evaṃvidhabādhakabalena tatropalakṣaṇatvasvīkārāt| na ca prakṛte bādhakamasti| avyavahitadeśakālādivṛttitvasya prātibhāsikasādhāraṇatvāt| idānīmatra sarpa ityādipratītyaviśeṣāt| na hi kvacid bādhakabalena mukhyaparityāgaḥ kṛta iti sarvatra tathaiva bhaviṣyati| utkarṣādyanuvidhānācca| tathā hi -- svapne jāgare cotkṛṣṭakaladhautadarśanād utkṛṣṭaṃ sukham utkṛṣṭasarpādidarśanāccotkṛṣṭaṃ bhayādi dṛśyate| viṣayasyākāraṇatve tadutkarṣānuvidhānaṃ kārye na syāt| na hyakāraṇotkarṣaḥ kāryamanuvidhatte iti nyāyāt| na ca jñānaprakarṣādeva tatprakarṣaḥ, jñāne'pi viṣayagatapnakarṣaṃ vihāyānyasya prakarṣasyābhāvāt|
atha jñānagatā jātireva prakarṣaḥ, na cākṣuṣatvādinā saṅkaraprasaṅgād| viṣayaprakarṣeṇaivopapattau cākṣuṣatvādivyāpyanānājātyaṅgīkāre gauravānmānābhāvācca| kiṃ ca jñānasya bhayādijanakatve sarpādyavacchinnatvameva kāraṇatāvacchedakamāstheyam| jñānatvena janakatve atiprasaṅgāt| tathā ca mithyātvāvacchinnatvākāreṇa jñānasya mithyātvād bhramasthale jñānamātrasya janakatve'pi mithyābhūtasya janakatvamāgatameva| janakatāvacchedakarūpeṇa ca mithyātve rūpāntareṇa ca mithyātve rūpāntareṇa sattvamapyasattvāt nātiricyate; anupayogāt| taduktaṃ khaṇḍanakṛdbhiḥ -- `anyadā sattvaṃ tu pāṭaccaraluṇṭhitaveśmani yāmikajāgaraṇa vṛttāntamanusarati'iti| svarūpeṇāpi tu bhramajñānasya mithyātvamastyeva| svarūpato bādhābhāve viṣayato'pyabādhaprasaṅgāt| na ca guṇajanyatvamupādhiḥ, tasyāpyāpādyatvena vahnyanumāne vahnisāmagryā iva sādhanavyāpakatvenānupādhitvād| viṣaya iva mithyātvaprayojakadoṣādisamavahitasāmagryā jñāne'pi aviśeṣācca| tucchajñānatadvailakṣaṇyādau ca tucchatvādarśanamabādhakam| avacchedyāvacchedakayoḥ sarvatra sārūpyaniyamānabhyupagamāt| prakṛte cāvacchedaka ivāvacchedye'pi mithyātvaprayojakarūpatulyatvena sārūpyopapatteḥ| sarvasādhāraṇaṃ caikaṃ kāraṇatvamabhyupagamyaitadavocāma| vastutastu -- daṇḍatantvādisādhāraṇamekaṃ kāraṇatvaṃ nāstyeva| yatra tava satvamavacchedakaṃ (tatra na) mama tucchavilakṣaṇatvādikam, kiṃ tu kāryatāvacchedakaṃ ghaṭatvapaṭatvādi, kāraṇatāvacchedakaṃ ca daṇḍatantvādi| tadbhedācca kāraṇatvaṃ bhinnam| yathā gogavayasādṛśyamanyad bhrātṛbhaginyādisādṛśyamanyat| tatra naikamavacchedakam, kintu gavayatvabhaginītvādikameva| tadvadatrāpi daṇḍatvādikameva sattvāsattvodāsīnamavacchedakaṃ vācyam| tathā ca janakatvānusāreṇa na sattvāsattvasiddhiḥ| taduktaṃ khaṇḍanakṛdbhiḥ -- 
`pūrvasambandhaniyame hetutve tulya eva nau|
hetutattvabahirbhūtasattvāsattvakathā vṛthā||' iti
antarbhāvitasattvaṃ cet kāraṇaṃ tadasattataḥ|
nāntarbhāvitasattvaṃ cet kāraṇaṃ tadasattataḥ|| 'iti ca|
na caivam -- antarbhāvitasattvaṃ cedadhiṣṭhānamasattataḥ|
nāntarbhāvitasattvaṃ cedadhiṣṭhānamasattataḥ||' iti
iti tavāpi samānamiti -- vācyam| mamādhiṣṭhāne svarūpata eva sattāṅgīkāraḥ| tava tu kāraṇe svarūpātiriktattāṅgīkāra iti viśeṣāt| yattu artho na jñānasya janakatāyāmavacchedako'pi| mānābhāvāt| na cātiprasaṅgaḥ| viṣayāvacchedamanapekṣyaiva sarpajñānasyāsarpajñānādvyāvṛttisiddheḥ| tathā hi -- sarpajñānasyāsarpajñānādvyāvṛttirvyāvarta kādhīnā| na ca viṣayastatsambandho vā vyāvartakaḥ| svarūpātiriktadviṣṭhasambandhābhāvāt| asambaddhasya cāvyāvartakatvāt| atha sambandhāntaramantareṇa viśiṣṭavyavahārajananayogyaṃ jñānasvarūpameva vā jñānamātraniṣṭhaḥ kaściddharmo vā sambandhaḥ tarhi viṣayamanantarbhāvyaiva jñānāttadgatadharmādvā viśeṣasiddhirityāyātam| kiṃ ca sarpajñānamasarpajñānāddharmyantara sambandhamanapekṣya vilakṣaṇam, tajjanakavilakṣaṇajanyatvād, yavāṅkurāt kalamāṅkuravat, tajjanyavilakṣaṇajanakatvādvā, yavabījātkalamabījavat| na ca vilakṣaṇaviṣayasambandhenaiva hetvorupapattāvaprayojakatvam| tathātve hi yavabījatadaṅkuravilakṣaṇajanyajanake kalamāṅkuratadvīje'pi yavāṅkuratadvījāṅkurābhyāṃ kalamāṅkura tadvījatvarūpasvābhāvikavailakṣaṇyaṃ vinā kadācidupalakṣaṇībhūtacaitrādisambandhitvamātreṇa vilakṣaṇe syātām| sākṣātkāro'pi parokṣajñānādanyasambandhitāmātreṇa vilakṣaṇaḥ syāt| evaṃ ca yathā pratiyoginamanantarbhāvyaiva ghaṭasyābhāvo'bhāvāntarād, yathā ca viṣayamanantarbhāvyaiva śiloddharaṇakṛtirmāṣoddharaṇakṛtitaḥ, yathā cātītādijñānamasadviṣayakaparokṣajñānavyavahārau ca jñānāntarāditaḥ, yathā ca ghaṭasmṛtirghaṭānubhavād, yathā ca ghaṭasya parokṣajñānaṃ tadaparokṣajñānād vilakṣaṇam, anyathā tattatkāryasaṅkaraḥ syād, evaṃ sarpajñānamapi rajjau sarpajñānasya bhramatvenādhikajanyatve'pi sarpajñānatvena taddhetujanyatvāt svata eva vā asarpajñānādvilakṣaṇamiti na ko'pi doṣaḥ| na cābhāvādāvapi pratiyogyāderavacchedakatvam| dhvaṃsādeḥ kṛteratītādijñānasya ca sattāsamaye pratiyogiviṣayayorasattvād iti| tanna| sarpajñānatvāvacchinnasyāsarpajñānād vyāvṛttau prayojakaṃ na tattatsvarūpameva| sarvajñānasādhāraṇyābhāvāt| kintvanugato dharmaḥ kaścit| so'pi sarpajñānamātre na jātirūpaḥ, pratyakṣatvānumānatvādinā saṅkaraprasaṅgāt, kintūpādhirūpaḥ| sa ca svarūpasambandhenādhyāsikasambandhena vā sambandhibhūtaviṣayādanyo na bhavati,mānābhāvāt| ata eva dharmyantarasambandhamanapekṣya vilakṣaṇamutyuktānumānaṃ bādhitaṃ draṣṭavyaṃ vyabhicāri ca| tathā hi -- ghaṭasaṃyogaḥ, paṭasaṃyogānna jātyā bhidyate, tadavṛttijātyanadhikaraṇatvāt, kintu ghaṭarūpopādhinaiveti dharmyantarasambandhamapekṣyaiva vilakṣaṇe ghaṭasaṃyogatvāvacchinne sādhyābhāvavati uktahetusattvād vyabhicāraḥ, aprayojakatvam| na ca upalakṣaṇībhūtacaitrasambandhenāpi kalamāṅkurādervyāvṛttatāpattiḥ, vipakṣabādhāyāmiṣṭāpatteḥ| na hi jātervyāvartakatve upādhiravyāvartako bhavati| evaṃ śiloddharaṇamāṣoddharaṇakṛtyoḥ parasparaṃ jātyā vyāvṛttāvapi viṣayarūpopādhinā'pi vyāvṛttiraviruddhā| śiloddharaṇo ca jātiviśeṣaviśiṣṭāyāḥ kṛterjanakatvena tadrahitāyā māṣoddharaṇakṛtestadaniṣpattiradoṣaḥ| vyāvṛtteranyato'pi siddhisambhave kāryakāraṇabhāvādinirvāhāya jātiviśeṣasyāpi kalpanāt| atītāsaddhiṣayakajñānavyavahārādau cātītāsatoreva vyāvartakatvam| na hi vyāvṛttidhījanakatvaṃ tat, yena sattvābhāve prāksattvaśarīratayā na syāt| kiṃ tu vyāvṛttidhījanaka dhīviṣayatvamityuktam| taccātītādau sulabhameva| ata evābhāvādinidarśanamapi nirastam| uktarūpavyāvartakatvasyātyantāsatyapi sambhavena kadācit sati sambhavasya kaimutikanyāyasiddha| nanu viṣayasya vyāvartakatve'pi sarvatra viśeṣaṇatvāsambhavād upalakṣaṇatvameva vācyam| upalakṣaṇena copalakṣyagatasvasambandhavyatiriktaḥ kaściddharma evopasthāpyate, kākeneva gṛhasambandhinā tadgatasaṃsthānaviśeṣaḥ| tathā ca sa eva vyāvartaka iti viṣayasambandhamanapekṣya svagatenaiva dharmeṇa jñānasya vyāvṛttisiddhiriti cenna| viṣayasya viśeṣaṇatvavadupa lakṣaṇatvasyāpyanamyupagamāt| yena hi svoparāgādviśeṣye vyāvṛttibuddhirjanyate tadviśeṣaṇaṃ vyāvṛttibuddhikāle viśeṣyoparañjakamityarthaḥ, yathā gotvādi| yena ca svoparāgamudāsīnaṃ kurvatā viśeṣyagatavyāvartakadharmopasthāpanena vyāvṛttibuddhirjanyate tadupalakṣaṇam, yathā kākādi| yattu -- viśeṣye noparañjakam, na vā dharmāntaropasthāpakam, atha ca vyāvartakaṃ tadupādhiḥ| yathā paṅkajaśabdaprayoge padmatvam, yathā vodbhidādiśabdaprayoge yāgatvāvāntarajātiviśeṣaḥ| atra hi padmatvayāgatvāvāntaradharmau paṅkajanikartari phalodbhedanakartari ca na dharmāntaramupasthāpayataḥ, apratīteḥ| na vā svoparaktāṃ buddhiṃ janayataḥ,samudāye śaktyantarānabhyupagamād| atha ca kumudajyotiṣṭomādibhyo vyāvartakāvityupādhī eva| idaṃ ca prābhākarāṇāṃ bhāṭṭānāṃ ca saṃmatamudāharaṇayugalam| tārkikāṇāṃ tvākāśaśabdaprayoge śabdāśrayatvamudāharaṇam| ata evāvidyādikaṃ sākṣitvādāvupādhiriti siddhānto vedāntinām| ato yatra viṣayasya viśeṣaṇatvaṃ na sambhavati, tatkālāsattvāt, tatropādhitvābhyumagamānnopalakṣaṇatvanibandhana doṣāvakāśaḥ| sandehe tu viśeṣaṇatvamevābhyarhi tatvādupeyate| tasmādviṣaya eva sarvatra jñāne vyāvartakaḥ| ekaviṣayakasmṛtyanubhavayoḥ parokṣāparokṣayośca viṣayamanapekṣya jātyā parasparavyāvṛttidarśanāt| sarvatra viṣayanirapekṣā jātireva vyāvartiketi na yuktam bhinnaviṣayake samānajātīye tadasambhavāt| na ca tatrāpi cātirasti kṣīrādimādhuryavaditi vācyam| cākṣuṣatvādinā saṅkarasyoktatvāt| na ca tava mate tattad‌vṛttestattadākāratvena caitanyasya tatpratibimbitatvena tadabhivyaktatvena vā mama tu tattajjñānasya tattadīyasvabhāvatvena tattadvyavahārajananaśaktatvena vā svata eva vailakṣaṇyamiti vācyam| viṣayasyaivākārasamarpakatvena svabhāvavyavahārayoḥ paricāyakatvena ca tannairapekṣyeṇa vyāvartakatāyā vaktumaśakyatvād| asmābhiśca tucche janakatvasyānuktatvāt| viśeṣaṇatvopādhitvayoḥ sambhave ca nopalakṣaṇatvamityuktam| na ca `kathamasataḥ sajjāyeta' iti śrutyā `nāsato'dṛṣṭatvāt' iti sūtreṇa `śaśaviṣāṇādibhyaḥ sadutpattyadarśanād' -- ityādibhāṣyeṇa ca virodhaḥ| teṣāṃ tucche janakatvaniṣedhaparatvād| asmābhiśca tucche janakatvasyānuktatvāt| tasmāt sadviviktatvaṃ sādhanamiti siddham|| ityadvaitasiddhau asataḥ sādhakatvābhāve bādhakam||
nanu mithyātvānumānamaprayojakam, satyatve'pi dṛśyatvopapatteriti cenna| dṛgdṛśyasambandhānupapatteḥ| na hi jñānaṃ jñeyāsambaddhameva prakāśakam, atiprasaṅgāt| nāpi sambaddham, ātmasvarūpasya tadaguṇasya vā jñānasya jñeyena saṃyogasamavāyayorabhāvāt| anyasya cānādhyāsikasya sambandhasyābhāvāt|naca viṣayaviṣayibhāvaḥ saḥ| tasya viṣayatvaviṣayitvarūpasya ekaikamātraniṣṭhatvena dviṣṭhasambandhātmakatvāsambhavād| durnirūpatvācca| tathā hi viṣayatvaṃ kiṃ jñānajanyaphalādhāratvam ? kiṃ vā jñānajanyahānādibuddhigocaratvam ? uta jñānakarmatvam ? jñānākārārpakatvaṃ vā ?dṛśyamānatve sati tattvaṃ va ? jñānajanyavyavahārayogyatvaṃ vā ? yatsannikṛṣṭakaraṇena yajjñānamutpādyate tattvaṃ vā ? yasyāṃ saṃvidi yo'rtho'vabhāsate sa tasyā viṣayaḥ, tathā ca saṃvidi bhāsamānatvamiti vā ? sambandhāntaramantarā jñānāvacchedakatvaṃ vā ? ādye phalaṃ na tāvat jñātatā, anaṅgīkārād, atītādāvabhāvācca| nāpi hānādiḥ| gaganādau tadabhāvāt| kaladhautamalāderapi tajjñānaviṣayatvaprasaṅgācca| nāpyabhijñābhilapane, tayorjñeyāvṛttitvāt|naca viṣayaviṣayibhāvena te tatra sya iti vācyam| tasyaiva vicāryamāṇatvāt| ata eva na dvitīyo'pi| na tṛtīyaḥ| īśvarajñānasyātītādijñānasya ca karmakārakājanyatvena nirviṣayatvaprasaṅgāt|nacaturthaḥ| jñānatadākārayorabhedena sarveṣāṃ jñānahetunāṃ viṣayatvāpātād| anumityādiviṣaye tadabhāvāpātācca| na pañcamaḥ| dṛśyamānatvasya viṣayatvaghaṭitatvenātmāśrayāt| na ṣaṣṭhaḥ| yogyātāyāṃ yogyatāntarābhāvāt|naca yogyatā yogyatāṃ vinaiva yogyā, yathā dṛśyatvaṃ dṛśyatvāntaraṃ vinaiva dṛśyamiti vācyam| avacchedakarūpāpiracaye yogyatāyā eva grahītumaśakyatvāt|naca jñānaviṣayatvaṃ tadavacchedakam, ātmāśrayāt| na saptamaḥ| nityeśvarajñānasya nirviṣayatvaprasaṅgāt| nāṣṭamaḥ, saṃviditi na tāvadadhikaraṇasaptamī| jñānasya jñeyānadhikaraṇatvāt| nāpi viṣayasaptamī, tasyaiva nirūpyamāṇatvāt| saṃvido viṣayatvaṃ saṃvedyasya ca viṣayitvamiti vaiparītyāpātācca| nāpi sati saptamī| bhāsamānatvasya viṣayatāghaṭitatvenā'tmāśrayāt| nāpi navamaḥ| matsamavetaṃ rūpajñānamityatra rūpajñānasamavāyasya sambandhāntaraṃ vinaiva rūpajñānāvacchedakasya `idaṃ rūpam' iti jñāne'pi viṣayatvāpātāt| nanu jñānaviṣaya ityabhiyuktaprayoga eva jñānaviṣayayoḥ sambandhaḥ| yathā abhiyuktasya mantra iti prayogaviṣayatvameva mantralakṣaṇam|nacānyonyāśrayaḥ, pūrvapūrvaprayogamapekṣyottarottaraprayogāditi -- cenna| etāvatā hi jñeyatvamātraṃ sāmānyataḥ syāt, na tvetajjñānaviṣayatvam|nacāsmin sādau pūrvaprayogamapekṣya uttarottaraprayogo vaktuṃ śakyate| tasyānādimātraviśrāntatvāt| kiṃ ca prayogo'pi svaviṣaye sambandha ityātmāśrayo'pi |
nanu yajjñānaṃ yadabhilapanarūpavyavahārakāraṇaṃ sa tasya viṣayaḥ| karaṇapāṭavādyabhāvena vyavahārānudaye'pi sahakārivirahaprayuktakāryābhāvavattvarūpaṃ kāraṇatvamastyeva|naca nirvikalpakaviṣaye avyāptiḥ| tasyānaṅgīkārāt|naca yattadbhyāmananugamo doṣaḥ| kasya ko viṣaya iti ananugatasyaiva praśnaviṣayatvena tasyādoṣatvāt|naca ghaṭajñānānantaraṃ pramādādyatra paṭa iti vyavahārastatra ghaṭajñānasya paṭābhilapanarūpavyavahārajanakatvena paṭaviṣayatvāpattiḥ| samānaviṣayābhilāpaṃ pratyeva jñānasya janakatayā ghaṭajñānasya bhinnaviṣayatayā tatrājanakatvāditi cenna| abhilapanarūpavyavahārajananayogyatvaṃ na prātisvikarūpeṇa nirṇeyam| avacchedakatvasya phalanirṇeyatvāt| pratisvaṃ ca phalādarśanād| ajanitaphale prātisvikayogyatāyāṃ mānābhāvāt, kintu tatra tatrānugatatattadvṛttiviṣayatvena, tathā ca ātmāśrayaḥ| ata eva jñānakarmatvaṃ viṣayatvam| karmatvaṃ ca na kārakaviśeṣaḥ, yenātītādau tadabhāvo bhavet| kintu kriyādhīnavyavahārayogyatvarūpātiśayavattvam| anyathā ghaṭaṃ karotītyādāvasiddhaṃ ghaṭādi na janakaṃ, siddhaṃ ca na kṛtikarmeti dvitīyāvibhaktiranarthikā syāditi nirastam| vyavahārayogyatvaṃ na vyavahārarūpaphalopahitatvam| kutracit pratiruddhe vyavahāre avyāpteḥ| nāpi tatsvarūpayogyatvam| viṣayatvādanyasya tasyāsambhavāditi pūrvoktadoṣāt| naca avacchedakād bhinnaṃ sahakārivirahaprayuktakāryābhāvavattvaṃ taditi vācyam| anugatāvacchedakadharmaṃ vinā tasyāpi grahītumaśakyatvāt| ghaṭaṃ karotītyatra siddhasyaiva kapālādeḥ kṛtikarmatā| (vyāpāryatayā'siddhasyaiva kṛtikarmatāṅgīkārāt| ata eva niṣpādanāvācidhātusamabhivyāhṛtakarmapade śakyāvayave nirūḍhalakṣaṇāmāhurasatkāryavādinaḥ| satkāryavādināṃ tu pūrve sato'pyabhivyañjanīyatayā na kārakatvakṛtikarmatvayoranupapattiḥ| etena -- `yasyāṃ saṃvidi' ityādi pūrvokte'pi na doṣaḥ| saṃviditi sati saptamī| bhāsamānatvaṃ ca vyavahārayogyatvam| tacca sati kāraṇāntare vyavahārāvaśyambhāva ityetadapi nirastam|
nanu yaḥ sambandhāntaramanapekṣya yajjñānāvacchedako yajjñānānavacchinnasvabhāvaśca sa tasya viṣayaḥ| yadyapyātmā svaviṣayajñānasamavāyavān, tathāpi na tasya jñānāvacchede samavāyāpekṣā| jñānāsamavāyino'pi ghaṭādestadavacchedakatvadarśanāt| yadyapi ca rūpajñānaṃ matsamavetaṃ, dhvastamiṣṭamityādau rūpajñānāviṣayā apyātmasamavāyecchādhvaṃsādayaḥ sambandhāntaramanapekṣya jñānāvacchedakāḥ, tathāpi samaveteṣyamāṇapratiyogyātmakarūpajñānāvacchinnasvabhāvā eva, sambandhecchādīnāṃ sambandhīṣyamāṇādyavacchinnasvabhāvatvāditi nātivyāptiḥ| jñānaviṣayastu na jñānāvacchinnasvabhāvaḥ| jñānasya ghaṭādyavacchinnasvabhāvatvavad ghaṭāderjñānāvacchinna svabhāvatvādarśanāt| yadyapi svagrāhakajñānaviṣayībhūtaṃ jñānaviṣayakānumityanuvyavasāyādikaṃ (jñānaṃ)jñānāvacchinnasvabhāvam, tathāpi svayaṃ yajjñānaṃ prati viṣayastadavacchinnasvābhāvaṃ neti nāvyāptiriti cenna| matsamavetaṃ rūpajñānamityākārakajñānasyātma samavāyaviṣa katvābhāvaprasaṅgād| ātmasamavāyasya sambandhatvena sambandhibhūtasvajñānāvacchinnatvād| ghaṭasya jñānamiti pratītyā ghaṭāvacchinnasvabhāvatvaṃ yathā jñānasya tathā jñāto ghaṭa iti pratītyā ghaṭasyāpi jñānāvacchinnasvabhāvatvenāsambhavācca|
atha yajjñānaṃ yadīyasvabhāvaṃ sa tasya viṣayaḥ| matsamavetaṃ rūpajñānamityatra tu samavāya eva rūpajñānāvacchinnasvabhāvo na tu rūpajñānaṃ tadavacchinnasvabhāvam| idaṃ ca jñānasyaiva viṣayatvamuktam, na tvicchādisādhāraṇamiti nāvyāptiriti cenna| yadīyasvābhāvamiti taddhitasya yadviṣayakatvārthakatve ātmāśrayād| arthāntarasya ca nirūpayitumaśakyatvād| rūpajñānābhāvābhāvasya rūpajñānarūpatvena rūpajñānasyāpyabhāvīyatayā tadviṣayatvāpatteḥ| nanu jñānajanakakaraṇasannikarṣāśrayatvaṃ tadviṣayatvam|naca rūpajñānakaraṇamanassannikarṣāśrayasyātmanastadviṣayatvāpattiḥ| karaṇapadenāsādhāraṇajñānakaraṇasyaiva vivakṣitatvāt| sannikarṣapadenāpyasādhāraṇajñānajanakasannikarṣasyaivoktatvāditi cenna| cakṣurmanassaṃyogasyāpi cākṣuṣajñānāsādhāraṇakāraṇatvena manaso'pi cākṣuṣajñānaviṣayatvāpatteḥ| parokṣaviṣaye avyāpteśca|naca tatra liṅgajñānaṃ karaṇam| tatra ca liṅginaḥ tadvyāptatvaṃ sambandho'stīti vācyam| liṅgasyāpi svajñānasambandhitvenānumitiviṣayatvāpatteḥ|nacānumitau tadvyāptatārūpasambandha eva viṣayatāniyāmakaḥ| vyāpakatāvacchedakavyāpaka sambandhādīnāmaviṣayatvāpatteḥ|naca jñānakaraṇasannikarṣasamānādhikaraṇo jñānāvacchedakatva sākṣādvyāpyadharmo viṣayatvam, idaṃ ca nityaparokṣasādhāraṇamiti vācyam| vastutvādikameva viṣayatvamityāpatteḥ| jñānāvacchedakatvasya eva viṣayatvamiti vācyam, asiddheḥ| tathā hi -- svarūpasambandha ityasya svarūpaṃ sambandha ityarthatve saṃyogādāvativyāptiḥ|naca tadubhayānyatvaṃ viśeṣaṇam| himavadvindhyayorapi svarūpasambandhāpatteḥ| sambandhāntaramantareṇa viśiṣṭapratītijananayogyatvaṃ svarūpasambandhaṃ iti cenna| ātmānaṃ jānāmītyatrāvyāpteḥ| tatra sambandhāntarasya samavāyasyaiva sattvād| atīndriyābhāvādāvavyāpteśca| nahi tasya viśiṣṭapratītijananayogyatve mānamasti| anyathā tena viśiṣṭapratyayajananāpatteḥ| kiṃ ca viśiṣṭapratītijananayogyatvaṃ dharmo vā sambandhaḥ ? tādṛśaṃ svarūpadvayameva vā ? ādye svarūpasya sambandhatvavyāghātaḥ| pratītighaṭitasyāsya cākṣuṣādijñānāgocaratvaprasaṅgaśca| na dvitīyaḥ, ananugamāt| kiṃ caivamabhāvabhramānupapattiḥ| tatrāpi viśiṣṭapratītisambhave svarūpasambandhasya sattvāt|naca pramātvaghaṭitaṃ tallakṣaṇam| vāstavasambandhasattve pramātvasyāpyāpādyatvāt| anyathā tatra tasyāpramātve sambandhābhāvaḥ, tasmiṃśca tasyāpramātvamityanyonyāśrayāt|
nanu sambandhāntaramantareṇa viśiṣṭapratītijananayogyatāvacchedakāvacchinnasvarūpasya sambandhatvaṃ saṃyogatvāvacchinnasya daṇḍītyādau sambandhatvavad, viśiṣṭabuddhiścāvacchedakā viṣayiṇyevāvacchedyaviṣayā| ato na svarūpasambandhagocaraviśiṣṭabuddheścākṣuṣatvavirodhaḥ|naca tarhyabhāvapramābhramayoḥ svarūpadvayamātraviṣayatvāviśeṣāt pramābhramavyavasthānupapattiriti vācyam| ghaṭābhāvavati ghaṭābhāvajñānatvena tadbhinnajñānatvenaca vyavasthopapatteḥ| nanu atiriktāviṣayatve tasyaivānupapattiḥ na hi bhavadrītyā tasyobhayātmakatvena tadubhayasattvena vyadhikaraṇaprakāratva rūpabhramatvasyaivābhāve bhramatadanyatvābhyāṃ vyavasthā sambhavatīti cenna| ghaṭābhāvābhāvasya ghaṭatvena tadvati ghaṭābhāvajñānasya vyadhikaraṇaprakārakatvasambhavāt| kiṃ ca bhramasya vastugatyā yad ghaṭavat sa viṣayaḥ| na tu pramāyā ityatiriktaviṣayatvamastyeva|nacātīndriyābhāve avyāptiḥ| atyantābhāve pratiyogideśānyadeśatvaṃ, prāgabhāvādau pratiyogideśatve sati pratiyogikālānyakālatvam, anyonyābhāve pratiyogitāvacchedakadeśānyadeśatvaṃ viśiṣṭapratyaya jananayogyatāvacchedakam| tadavacchinnatvaṃ ca viśiṣṭapratītyajanake'pyatīndriyābhāve sulabham| na hyaraṇyastho daṇḍo na ghaṭanananayogyatāvacchedakāvacchinna iti cet, nityasyātīndriyasyākāśā tyantābhāvāderviśiṣṭapratītijananayogyatāvacchedakāvacchinnatve avaśyaṃ viśiṣṭapratyayajanakatva prasaṅgāt| nityasya svarūpayogyasya sahakārisamavadhānaniyamāt|
kiṃ ca viśiṣṭasya pratyaya ityatra svarūpasambandhasya ṣaṣṭhyarthatve ātmāśrayaḥ| sambandhamātrasya tadarthatve ātmatvādiviśiṣṭātmasambandhisamūhālambanaviṣaye ghaṭapaṭādāvati vyāptiḥ| tayorapi viśiṣṭasambandhyaviśiṣṭaviṣayajñānajanakatvāt| jñānasyābhāvaḥ jñāto'bhāva iti pratītyorvailakṣaṇyaṃ na syāt| jñānābhāvayerubhayorevobhayatra svarūpasambandhatve viṣayakṛtaviśeṣābhāvāt| ata eva viśiṣṭapratītijananayogyatvaṃ jñānajñeyādisthale atiriktameva sambandha iti nirastam| atīndriye nityābhāve'vyāpteḥ| na hi tatra viśiṣṭapratītijananayogyatā, phalopadhānāpatteḥ| pratītighaṭitasya cākṣuṣādipratītāvaviṣayatvaprasaṅgācca| tasmātsatyatve sambandhānupapatterādhyasika eva dṛgdṛśyayoḥ sambandha iti| ityadvaitasiddhau prapañcasatyatve dṛgdṛśyasambandhabhaṅgaḥ|
syādetat -- sarvasyāpi dṛśyasya brahmātmakadṛgadhyastatve'pi kasyacit kadācit kañcit prati prakāśāya tvayā'pi tattatsannikṛṣṭendriyajanyatattadākāravṛttidvāraka evānāvṛtadṛksambandhaḥ svīkṛtaḥ, tathā ca satyatve'pi taddvāraka eva sambandho'stu, kimādhyāsikasambandhadurvyasanena ? na hi bhavatāṃ vijñānavādināmiva tattajjñāne tattadarthādhyāsasvīkāraḥ, śuddhadṛśaḥ svato bhedābhāvād upādhiviśiṣṭāyā bhede'pi ghaṭādivattasyā api mithyātvenādhiṣṭhānatvāyogāditi, -- cenna, prakāśasya sākṣāt svasaṃsṛṣṭaprakāśakatvaniyamenacaitanyasya paramparāsambandhena viṣayaprakāśakatvāyogāt| na hi pradīpaḥ paramparāsambaddhaṃ prakāśayati, ato viṣayādhiṣṭhānacaitanyamanāvṛtameva prakāśakam, āvaraṇabhaṅgaśca vṛttyā, ato vṛtteḥ pūrvamādhyāsikasambandhe vidyamāne'pi dṛśyā'pratītirupapannā| ata eva -- vṛttipratibimbita caitanyasya ghaṭaprakāśakatve ādhyāsikasambandhasyātantratāpātaḥ, ghaṭābhivyaktacetanyasya ghaṭaprakāśakatve āvaśyakena vṛttipratibimbitacaitanyenaiva ghaṭaprakāśopapattau tadadhiṣṭhānacaitanyābhivyaktikalpanāyā yuktatvāt|naca -- śuddhacaitanyasya caramasākṣātkārātpūrvaṃ nābhivyaktiḥ, abhivyaktasya ca ghaṭādyavacchinnacaitanyasya na tadadhiṣṭhānatvam, ātmāśrayāditi -- vācyam, caramasākṣātkārāt pūrvamapi śuddhacaitanyasyāvidyāvaśādadhiṣṭhānabhūtasya mūlājñānanivṛttilakṣaṇābhivyaktyabhāve'pi tadavasthāviśeṣādinivṛttilakṣaṇābhivyaktyā viṣayaprakāśakatvopapatteḥ|naca -- ghaṭaprakāśikāyāḥ dṛśo mithyātvenādhiṣṭhānatvaṃ satyatve doṣājanyatvena pramātvāt satyaṃ svaviṣayaṃ prati nādhiṣṭhānatvamityubhayataḥ pāśārajjuriti -- vācyam yato doṣājanyatvaṃ na pramātvaprayojakam, caitanyasya sarvatra doṣājanyatvāt, kintu doṣājanyavṛttyavacchinnatvam, prakṛte ca tadabhāvāt na viṣayasya satyatvam, ato mithyābhūtaviṣayaṃ pratyādhiṣṭhānatvaṃ satyāyā dṛśo yuktam|
nanu tāttvikasambandhāsambhave ādhyāsikasambandhakalpanam ? sa eva tu kutaḥ ? klṛptasaṃyogabādhe guṇaguṇinoḥ samavāyavattadubhayabādhe tṛtīyasya sambhavāt|naca tatra mānābhāvaḥ, samavāyavadanumanādhyakṣayoḥ sattvāt| tathā hi -- parasparāsaṃyuktāsamaveta viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyakaviśiṣṭadhīrviśeṣaṇaviśeṣasambalandhaviṣayā, viśiṣṭadhītvād daṇḍīti viśiṣṭa dhīvad;uktā janyapramā, viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyasambandhanimittikā, abādhitajanyaviśiṣṭadhītvāt, saṃmatavat; vimatā dhīḥ,abādhitaviśeṣaṇaviśeṣyasambandhaviṣayā, abādhitajanya viśiṣṭadhītvāddaṇḍīti viśiṣṭadhīvat| gomāṃścaitra ityādibuddherapi pakṣakukṣinikṣepa eveti na tatra vyabhicāraśaṅkā| tathā ca saṃyogasamavāyātiriktasambandhasiddhiriti -- cenna, prathame dvitīye cārthāntaram, ādhyāsikasambandhasyaiva viṣayatvena nimittatvenacopapatteḥ| dvitīye parokṣadhīṣu vyabhicāraśca| tṛtīye'pi brahmajñānaparyantābādhitatvena siddhasādhanameva| sarvathā abādhitadhīviṣayatve sādhye sādhyavaikalyam|naca -- tāttvikasambandhabādhe ādhyāsikasambandhasiddhiḥ, tathā ca saṃyogasamavāyātiriktatāttvikasambandhabādhaparyantaṃ nādhyāsikasambandhasambhāvanā, tathā ca kathamarthāntarasiddhasādhanasādhyavaikalyānīti vācyam, tāttvikasambandhasya vyāpakānupalabdhyā bādhāt| tathāhi -- tāttvikasambandhasya vyāpako deśakālaviprakarṣābhāvaḥ| sa cātītādiviṣayakajñānādīnāṃ nāstyeveti kathaṃ tāttvikasteṣāṃ sambandhaḥ ?naca -- samavāyavat sambandhyabhāvaviprakarṣādyaviruddhatvenaiva tatsiddhiriti vācyam, samavāyasyāpi deśakālaviprakṛṣṭayoḥ sambaddhavyavahārāprayojakatvāt| na hi sambandhyabhāve'pi san samavāyo'dya vinaṣṭaṃ ghaṭaṃ śvastanena rūpeṇa viśinaṣṭi|nacādhyāsikatve sambandhasya sādhye dharmigrāhakamānabādhaḥ, viśiṣṭabuddhitvena prathamaṃ tāttvikātātvika sādhāraṇasambandhasyaiva siddheḥ|
kiṃ ca sambandhagrāhaka eva tāttvikasambandhavyāpakānupalabdhirūpabādhasahakṛtaḥ ādhyāsikasambandhe paryavasyati| ato na dharmigrāhakabādhaśaṅkāpi|nacaivaṃ -- yutasiddhayoreva saṃyogarūpasambandhadarśanādayutasiddhirapi samavāyasya bādhikā syāditi vācyam, yutasiddhayorapi kvacitsambandhādarśanena yutasiddhatvasya sambandhāprayojakatvād, yasmin satyavaśyaṃ sambandhaḥ, sa eva sambandhasya prayojaka iti samavyāptatvābhāvena yutasiddhyanupalabdherabādhakatvād, yatra sambandhastatrāvaśyaṃ yutasiddhiriti viṣamavyāptikalpane'pi mānābhāvād, anukūlatarkādarśanāt, deśakālīviprakarṣābhāvavatāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ sambandhadarśanena viprakarṣe tadadarśanenaca samavyāptatayā prayojakasya deśakālaviprakarṣābhāvasyānupalabdheḥ sambandhabādhakatva syāvaśyamaṅgīkaraṇīyatvāt| na hi prayojakābhāve prayojyasambhavaḥ|
nanvevaṃ -- dhvaṃsādaretītādinā, mithyātvalakṣaṇāntargatasyātyantābhāvasya pratiyoginā,śakteḥ śakyena ajñānasyājñeyena,itchāyā iṣyamāṇena, vyavahārasya vyavahartavyena, vākyasyārthena vṛttirūpajñānasya jñeyena, sambandho neti tvadvākyoktasambandhābhāvasya jñānenāsambandhāt svanyāyasvakriyāsvavacanavirodhāḥ syuḥ, na hi jñāne jñeyamiva pratiyogyādika mabhāvādāvadhyastamiti cenna, yadyapyuktanyāyasāmyena dhvaṃsādīnāṃ svapratiyogyādibhistāttvikaḥ sambandho nāstyeva, adhyāso'pi na jñānajñeyanyāyena, ubhayorapi mithyātvāt| tathāpi pratīyamānaṃ pratiyogyanuyogibhāvādikaṃ sarvathā na nirākurmaḥ, kintu tāttvikādhyāsābhyāṃ bhinnameva jñeyakukṣinikṣiptatvāt mithyābhūtamaṅgīkurmaḥ| sa ca saṃyogādivadatirikto vā svarūpaṃ vā parāṅgīkṛtapadārthāntargato vā tadatirikto vetyasyāṃ kākadantaparīkṣāyāṃ na no nirbandhaḥ|naca mithyātvasiddheḥ prāk tadasiddhyā anyonyāśrayaḥ, dṛgdṛśyasambandhānupapattyā jñeyamātrasyādhyāsikatve siddhe tanmadhyapatitasya pratiyogyabhāvādisambandhasyāpi mithyātvaṃ, na tu pratiyogyabhāvādisambandhamithyātvasiddhyanantaraṃ dṛśyamithyātvasiddhiriti vyavahāropayuktasambandhasāmānyasyāpratikṣepāt na svavacanādivirodhaḥ| taduktaṃ khaṇḍanakṛdbhiḥ -- 
bādhe'dṛḍhe'nyasāmyāt kiṃ ? dṛḍhe tadapi bādhyatām|
kva mamatvaṃ mumukṣūṇāmanirvacanavādinām '||iti|
nacādṛḍhatvaṃ bādhasya vyāpakānupalabdhirūpatarkasyoktatvāt, svakriyādi virodharūpapratikūlatarkasya parihṛtatvācca| ata eva na jātivādisāmyam, tena hi niyamasāpekṣānityatvasādhakakṛtakatvādau niyamānapekṣeṇa darśanamātreṇa rūpavattvādikamāpādyate, na tvasmābhistathā niyamanirapekṣeṇa sāhacaryamātreṇa kiñcidāpādyate|nacaivaṃ jñānajñeyayorapi pratiyogyabhāvādisamakakṣya eva sambandho'stviti vācyam, parasparādhyāsātmakasambandhāsambhavenaiva sambandhāntarakalpanāt, tatsambhave tasyaiva sambandhatvāt|naca ajñānaviṣayasya brahmaṇo viṣayiṇyajñāne'nadhyāsena viṣayasya viṣayiṇyadhyāsaniyamo na siddha iti vācyam, evaṃ niyamānabhyupagamāt, kiṃ tu jñānājñānayoradhyāsa eva viṣayeṇa sambandhaḥ| sa ca jñāne jñeyasyājñeye cājñānasyādhyāsād upapadyate| ata evādhyāsikasambandhavyatirekapradarśane ajñānasyājñeyenetyanudāharaṇam|
nanu -- śravaṇādīnāṃ caramasākṣātkārāntānāṃ svaviṣayeṇa brahmaṇā sambandhānupapattiḥ, na hi śravaṇādau sākṣātkāre vā brahmādhyastamiti cenna, sāśrātkāro hi vṛttirvā ? tadabhivyaktacaitanyaṃ bā ? ādye tasyāḥ brahmaṇyadhyasyatvenājñānājñeyayoriva sambandhopapatteḥ| ata eva śravaṇādināpi mānasakriyārūpeṇa na sambandhānupapattiḥ, dvitīye tu abhedena tatsambandhānupayogāt tatsambandhānupapattirna doṣāya| ata eva caramasākṣātkārasya brahmaṇyadhyastatvād yadi tadviṣayatvaṃ, tadā ghaṭasākṣātkārasyāpi brahmaṇyadhyastatvāt tadviṣayatvāpattiriti nirastam, ghaṭasākṣātkārasya ghaṭābhivyaktacaitanyarūpatve
vṛttirūpatve tasyāḥ brahmaṇyadhyāse'pi nādhiṣṭhānabhūtasya brahmaṇo viṣayatvam, brahmaviṣayatāprayojakasyādhyāsaviśeṣasya tatrābhāvāt, tasya ca phalabalakalpyatvāt, na hi caramavṛttau brahmākāratāvadatrā'pi sā'nubhūyate, iccheṣyamāṇayostu jñānadvāraka eva sambandha iti na pṛthaksambandhāpekṣā|naca jñāne sannikarṣādhīnasyeva smṛtāvanubhavādhīnasyevecchāyāṃ jñānādhīnasya viṣayasambandhasyānubhavāt sannikarṣādibhyo bhinna iva jñānād bhinna eva sambandho vaktavya iti vācyam, sambandhānubhavasya jñānadvārakasambandhenāpyupapatteratiriktasambandhakalpane mānābhāvāt, jñānādhīnasambandhāntarasyānanubhavāt| jñāne tvindriyasannikarṣādinā na sambandhānubhavopapattiḥ, indriyasannikarṣādīnāmatīndriyatvena teṣāmanumityādinopasthitiṃ vinaiva ghaṭajñānamityādi sambandhānubhavāt| smṛtau tu anubhavādhīnasambandhasya śaṅkaiva nāsti, anubhavasya tadānīmasattvād, ubhayorapi jñānatvena tulyavadeva sambandhasambhavācca|naca samūhālambanajanyaikaviṣayecchāyāmubhaya viṣayatvāpattiḥ, janakajñānasyobhayaviṣayatvāditi vācyam, atiriktasambandhapakṣe'pi tulyatvāt| athaikaviṣayāvacchedenaiva jñānasya janakatvāt nobhayaviṣayatvaṃ, samaṃ mamā'pi, janakajñāne janakatāvacchedakaviṣayatvasyaiva sambandhatvāt|naca nityeśvarecchāyā viṣayatvasambandhānupapattiḥ, tasyā asmābhiranaṅgīkārāt, tārkikāṇāmapi tatsādhakamānabalena vilakṣaṇasambandhakalpane'pi janyajñānajanyecchayoruktaprakāreṇaiva viṣayatābhyupagamāt|naca -- putrādidhījanyasukhādeḥ putrādi viṣayatvāpattiḥṣa icchānyāyāditi vācyam, vaiṣamyāt| jñānasya samānatve'pi icchādāveva saviṣayatvapratītiḥ, na tu sukhādau| vastusvābhāvyāt tvayāpyasyaivārthasya vaktavyatvāt| anyathā sphaṭike japākusumasannidhānāllauhityavalloṣṭhe'pyāpadyeta|
atha dharme tātparyasyānaghyāsāttātparyasambandho na syāt, na| tātparyaṃ hi tatpratītyud‌deśyakatvam, pratīteśca jñeyāntareṇeva dharmeṇā'pi sambandho'dhyasya eva, pratītidvārā ca dharmatātparyayoḥ sambandha ityanupapattyabhāvāt|naca -- jñānasya prakāśatvena pradīpasāmye'pi āntaratvena tadvailakṣaṇyamaṅgīkartavyam, ata icchādivadviprakṛṣṭenāpi sambandhaḥsyād, anyathā pradīpavadevādhyāsikasambandho'pi na syāt, parokṣavṛttau viprakṛṣṭasambandhadarśanācceti vācyam, deśakālaviprakarṣābhāvasya sambandhasāmānyaprayojakatve sambhavatyāntarapratiyogikasambandhabhinnasambandha evāsya prayojakatvamiti kalpanābījābhāvāt| icchāyāstu neṣyamāṇena sākṣātsambandhaḥ, kiṃ tu jñānadvārakaḥ paramparāsambandha evetyuktam| parokṣasthale tu yadyapyadhiṣṭhānacaitanyena sākṣādeva sambandhaḥ, tathāpi viṣayākāravṛttyā sākṣātsambandhābhāvād vṛttyavacchinnacaitanyena viṣayasya paramparāsambandha eva| nanu -- tavāpi mate jñeyasya na svajñāne'dhyastatvaniyamaḥ, anadhyastasya tucchasya pañcamaprakāratvapakṣe avidyānivṛtterbhāvādvaitapakṣe abhāvasya cā'dṛgrūpatve'pi svajñāne'nadhyāsād, aparokṣaikarase nrahyaṇyadhyastasya vyāvahārikasyātītādernityātīndriyasya ca parokṣānubhavarūpe svajñāne'nadhyāsāt, smaryamāṇasya ca smṛtirūpe svajñāne'dhyāsāt, prātibhāsikasya ca prātibhāsike svajñāne'nadhyāsāt, tvanmate bhramarūpajñānasyāpi kalpitatvāditi cet, maivam, tucchasyājñeyatvena jñāne adhyāsābhāvād, jñeyasya hi jñāne'dhyāsaḥ, tucchasya tu na jñeyatetyagre vakṣyate| pañcamaprakārāvidyānivṛtterapi pratiyogyadhikaraṇe dhvaṃsasyāpi tatra vṛtteravaśyambhāvād adhyāsa eva sambandhaḥ| vastutastvavidyānivṛtteḥ pañcamaprakāratvaṃ ca bhāvādvaitaṃ cānabhyupagamaparāhatam| yathā cāvidyānivṛtterbrahmarūpatvaṃ sarvādvaitaṃ ca tathopariṣṭādvakṣyate| aparokṣaikarase brahmaṇyadhyastasyātītāderanumityādirūpajñāne anadhyāse'pi yasmiṃścaitanye tadadhyastaṃ tadeva caitanyamanumityādirūpavṛttyavacchinnamiti nādhyāsānupapattiḥ| atiprasaṅgaparihārārthaṃ caitanyasya viṣayasambandhe vṛttyuparāgāpekṣāyāmapi nādhiṣṭhānatvena tadapekṣā| evameva nityaparokṣasthale smṛtisthale'pi prātibhāsikasya prātibhāsikyāṃ vṛttāvanadhyāse'pyadhiṣṭhānaviṣayakavṛttyabhivyaktacaitanya evādhyāsa iti na kāpyanupapattiḥ|
naca -- rūpyādikamidamaṃśāvacchinnacaitanye'dhyastam, bhāsate na avidyāvṛttipratibimbita caitanyeneti viṣayiṇi jñāne viṣayasyādhyāsaḥ kathamiti -- vācyam; ekāvacchinna evāparāvacchedena nirapekṣopādherivātra bhedakatvābhāvāt| ata eva abhiyuktaiḥ phalaikyādaikyaṃ jñānasyocyate|naca -- rūpyādeḥ svajñāne'dhyastatve rūpyajñānasyājñāne bhramotpattistañjñānena tannivṛttiriti ca syād, adhiṣṭhānājñānajñānābhyāmadhyāsasya janmanivṛttyorniyatatvād, jñānaṃ rajatamiti pratītiprasaṅgācceti vācyam; rajatākāravṛttyavacchinnacaitanyasya rajatabhramādhiṣṭhānatvānabhyupagamād, idamaṃśāvacchinnacaitanyameva tu rajatabhramādhiṣṭhānam| tacca daivādrajatākāravṛttyavacchinnacaitanyamapi, naitāvatā bhramādhiṣṭhānatve tadapekṣā| tasya ca bhramavirodhiśuktitvādyākāreṇājñānaṃ bhramakāraṇam| tenākāreṇa ca jñānaṃ bhramanivartakam| ata eva na jñānaṃ rajatamiti bhramākārāpattiḥ, vṛttyavacchinnasyaiva jñānatvāttasya cādhiṣṭhānatvābhāvāt| adhiṣṭhānatādātmyenacāropyapratītiriti idaṃ rajatamityeva bhramākāraḥ|
nanu -- ghaṭādeḥ svasannikṛṣṭendriyajanyasvajñānāt pūrvaṃ sattvena tatrādhyāso na yuktaḥ|naca yā ghaṭendriyasannikarṣajā vṛttistayā ghaṭo na prakāśyaḥ, yenaca prakāśyo ghaṭādhiṣṭhānacaitanyena na tatsannikarṣajanamiti vācyam, vṛttyatiriktajñāne mānābhāvāt| ajñānanivṛtterapi tata eva bhāvāditi cenna, vṛttyudayāt prāgajñātārthasiddhyarthaṃ vṛttyatiriktajñānasyāvaśyamabhyupeyatvāt| anyathā tasya sādhakābhāvena śaśaśrṛṅgatulyatayā sannikarṣatajjanyajñānahetutvena prāk sattvakalpanā niṣprāmāṇikī syāt| tasmādyādṛśasya ghaṭāderindriyasannikarṣāśrayatvena jñānakāraṇatvam, tādṛśasya sādhakaṃ kiñcinmānamavaśyamabhyupeyam, anyathā'nvayavyatirekayoragraheṇa kāryakāraṇabhāvāgrahāt sarvamānameyādivyavasthocchidyeta| tacca mānaṃ na vṛttirūpam| tadānīṃ vṛttikāraṇāpravṛtteriti tadvilakṣaṇaṃ nityaṃ svaprakāśamekameva lāghavād, vṛttigatotpattivināśajaḍatvādibhistadasaṃsparśāt| tadeva ca nānāvidhopādhi sambandhānnānāvidhavyavahārabhāg bhavati nabha iva ghaṭamaṇikamallikādyupādhibhedena; taccājñānasādhakatvātsvarūpato nājñānanivartakaṃ, vṛttyuparaktaṃ tvajñānanivartakamiti na vṛtteranupayogaḥ| tathā ca sarvājñānasādhake sākṣicaitanye tasmin ghaṭāderadhyāsa iti na kā'pyanupapattiḥ ? taduktaṃ sureśvarācāryaiḥ -- 
sarvatīrthadṛśāṃ siddhiḥ svābhipretasya vastunaḥ|
yadabhyupagamādeva tatsiddhirvāryate kutaḥ||iti|
sarvatīrthadṛśāṃ tāvatsāmānyaṃ mānalakṣaṇam|
ajñātārthāvagamanaṃ tvadukte tanna yujyate||
svataḥ siddho'thavāsiddho dehādiste bhavan bhavet|
pramāṇānāṃ pramāṇatvaṃ nobhayatrāpi labhyate|
pramāṇānyantareṇāpi dehādiścet prasidhyati|
vada pramāṇaiḥ konvartho na hi siddhasya sādhanam||
svato'siddhe'prameye tu nāsato vyañjikā pramā|
nābhivyanakti savitā śaśaśrṛṅgaṃ sphurannapi||iti|
naca `ghaṭo'yamityasāvuktirābhāsasya prasādataḥ|
vijñāto ghaṭa ityuktirbrahmānubhavato bhavet||'
iti vadatā vṛttipratibimbitasya ghaṭānadhiṣṭhānacaitanyasya ghaṭānubhavatvoktivirodha iti vācyam| vṛttipratibimbitacaitanyasya ghaṭādhiṣṭhānacaitanyena saha bhedābhāvāt, caitanyasyaikatvāt| yathā caikasyaiva caitanyasya sarvabhāvakatvaṃ tathā vistareṇopapāditaṃ `nābhāva upalabdherityasminnadhikaraṇe' bhāṣyakṛdbhiḥ|
nanu -- dṛśyatvānyathānupapattyā mithyātvamityarthāpattirvivakṣitā ? kiṃ vā satyatve dṛśyatvaṃ na syādityanukūlatarkamātram ? nādyaḥ, tatsāmagryabhāvāt| tathā hi -- ākṣepyasyopapādakatvam, pramāṇāviruddhatvam, ākṣepakasyānupapadyamānatvaṃ, pramitatvaṃ cetyarthāpattisāmagrī| prakṛte cākṣepyaṃ sambandhino mithyātvaṃ mithyātvaṃ nākṣepakasya sambandhasyopapādakam, pratyuta pratikūlameva|nacādhyastatvarūpasambandhasya na tatpratikūlatvam, tasyādyāpyasiddheranākṣepakatvāt| pratyakṣādiviruddhaṃ cedamākṣepyam| nāpyekasya dṛśyatvasyopapattaye pramitānekasya tyāgo yuktaḥ| ākṣepakaṃ ca na dṛgadhyastatvam, tasyaiva phalata ākṣepyatvāt| nāpi dṛgviṣayatvarūpo dṛgsambandha, tavāsiddheḥ| na dṛgadhīnasiddhikatvam, dṛgviṣayatvātiriktasya tasyāsiddheḥ| nāntyaḥ, sattve'pyuktarītyā sambandhāntareṇaiva dṛśyatvasyopapannatayā anupapatterevābhāvāditi cenna, anukūlatarkasyaiva prakrāntatvenārthāpattirvetyādivikalpānavakāśād, ubhayathāpyadoṣācca| tathā hi -- satyatve dṛgdṛśyasambandhānupapattiḥ| mithyātvaṃ ca tadupapādakam|naca tatsambandhapratikūlam, mithyātve'pi śuktirūpyasyedamaṃśe'dhyastatvarūpasambandhadarśanena sambandhasāmānye pratikūlatvābhāvāt| ākṣepako'pi dṛgviṣayatvarūpo dṛksambandha eva aghyāsarūpasya dṛgviṣayatvasya mamā'pi sampratipatteḥ tāttvikasyaiva tasya niṣedhāt|nacādhyastatvasyādyāpyasiddhiḥ dṛksambandhasāmānyasyākṣepakasya prasaktaviśeṣa niṣedhe'pyadhyastatvarūpaviśeṣaparyavasānenāsiddhyabhāvāt| na hi adhyastasambandhatvenākṣepakatā kiṃ tu sambandhatvena| sa cādhyastatvasambandhasambhāvanayāpyabādhita eveti|naca -- ghaṭasya jñānamiti dhīsiddhasambandhasāmānyasyādhyastatvaṃ na viśeṣaḥ, na hi rūpyasya śuktiriti pratītirastīti vācyam, rūpyasya śuktiriti pratītyabhāve'pi rūpyasya śuktiradhiṣṭhānimiti pratītyā adhyastatvasya sambandhaviśeṣatvasiddheḥ, caitrasya maitra iti pratītyabhāve'pi caitrasya pitā maitra iti pratītivat| ākṣepyamapyatra pramāṇāviruddhameva, adhyakṣādivirodhasya prāgeva parihṛtatvāt| ākṣepake ca pramitatvamanapekṣitameva, apramitenāpi pratibimbena bimbākṣepadarśanāt| tarkaparatāyāmapi nāprayojanakatā, satyatve sambandhānupapatterbhavaduktanyāyakhaṇḍanena prathamata evopapāditatvāt| dṛśyatvābhāvasyāpādakamatra sattvamanirvācyatvābhāve vā, trikālābādhyatvaṃ vā, ubhayathā'pi na doṣaḥ|nacānirvācyatvābhāvasya tucche parokṣadhīvedyatayā dṛśye'pi sattvena vyabhicāraḥ, kāraṇāsāmarthyena tatra tadākāravṛttisamullāse'pi dṛksambandharūpasya dṛśyatvasya tucchatvavirodhinastatrābhāvāt,tucchākāratāyā vṛttigatatve'pi vṛttisambandhasya tucchagatatvābhāvopapatteḥ| nāpi -- yathā sato brahmaṇaḥ svavyavahṛtyā sambandhaḥ, tathā ghaṭāderapi sata eva svajñānena sambandho'stviti vācyam, dṛṣṭānte brahmaṇyadhyāsasyaiva vyavahṛtisambandhatvāt| tathā ca ubhayasambandhisattve viṣayaviṣayibhāvānupapattiḥ nāprayojakatvādinā paribhūyate|
etena -- adhyāsikaḥ sambandho nāma adhyastasambandho vā ? adhyastatvameva vā ? ādye sambandhasya mithyātve'pi sambandhino dṛśyasya dṛśa iva mithyātvānupapattiḥ, dvitīye jñānasyāpyadhyastatvena tatra adhyāsānupapattiḥ svasvajñānaparamparāyāmadhyāsasvīkāre anavasthā ceti nirastam, jñānaṃ hi vṛttyavacchinnaṃ caitanyam, tatrāvacchedikāyā vṛtterjaḍāyā adhyastatve'pyavacchedyasya caitanyasya prakāśarūpasya anadhyastatvena tatra dṛśyasyādhyāsād dṛśyamithyātve'pyanavasthāvirahasyopapatteḥ| ata eva -- śābdavṛttiviṣayo brahma na vṛttau kalpitamavidyāviṣayo brahmāvidyāyāṃ na kalpitaṃ yathā tathā dṛśyaṃ na dṛśikalpitam, tathā ca dṛgdṛśyādestāttvika eva sambandhaḥ, sāmānyasambandhenaivātiprasaṅge niraste viśeṣajijñāsā viśeṣoktiśca viśeṣajijñāsādivadanarthikaiveti nirastam, vṛttyavidyayoḥ brahmaṇo'nadhyāse'pi tayoreva brahmaṇyadhyāsāt sambandhopapatteḥ, atastatra tāttvikasambandhābhāvāt, kathaṃ tad‌dṛṣṭāntena dṛgdṛśyayorapi tāttvikasambandha ityucyate ? tathā ca prasiddhaviśeṣe bādhite sāmānyasyaiva bādhaśaṅkayā atiprasaṅge prāpte viśeṣajijñāsāyā viśeṣokteśca sāphalyāt, na te nirarthike| etena sambandhasya prāmāṇikatve yathākathañcana lakṣaṇaṃ bhaviṣyati| tathā hi -- saṃyogasamavāyāntarbhāve tallakṣaṇameva lakṣaṇaṃ bhaviṣyati, tadanantarbhāve tu tadubhayabhinnasambandhatvameva lakṣaṇamastviti nirastam, uktayuktyā prāmāṇikasambandhasya saṃyogasamavāyāntarbhāvasya ca dūṣitatvāt| tadubhayabahirbhūtasambandhatvaṃ tu vayamapi na nirakurmaḥ, kiṃ tu tasya prāmāṇikatvam| kiṃ ca dṛkdṛśyayoḥ na tāttvikasambandhaḥ, sambandhibhinnatve anavasthānāt|naca dṛśyatvāntarahīnasya dṛśyatvāderiva sambandhasyāpi svanirvāhakatvaṃ kvacid bhaviṣyatīti vācyam, dṛśyatvamapi dṛksambandha eva| tasya ca svanirvāhakatvaṃ na māyikatvaṃ vineti nāsmākaṃ pratikūlamabhyadhāyi devānāṃ priyeṇa, abhinnatve sambandhatvāyogāt|nacaivamādhyāsikasambandhatve'pyetaddoṣaprasaṅgaḥ, tasya māyikatvena māyāyāścāghaṭitaghaṭanāpaṭīyastvena sarvānupapatterbhūṣaṇatvāt|naca -- atiprasaṅganirākaraṇārthaṃ dṛgdṛśyayoḥ sambandhanirvacanaṃ prakṛtam, na tu viṣayatvanirvacanam, ato viṣayatvakhaṇḍanamanuktopālambhanamiti vācyam; viṣayatvakhaṇḍanena nirucyamānaprakṛtasambandhasyaiva khaṇḍanāt|naca -- viṣayitvāniruktāvapi viṣayiṇaḥ satyatvavad viṣayatvāniruktāvapi viṣayaḥ satyaḥ syāditi vācyam, viṣayitvāniruktāvapi viṣayādhyāsenaiva tadupapattyā viṣayiṇaḥ satyatvaṃ yuktam, viṣayatvāniruktau tu viṣayasya satyatvaṃ na yuktam, viṣayiṇo'nadhyastatvena viṣayādhyāsamantareṇānya syopapādakasyābhāvat| yatra tu viṣayiṇa evādhyāsaḥ, tatra viṣayaḥ satya eva, yathājñānaviṣayo brahma|nacobhayādhyāsaḥ,śūnyavādaprasaṅgāt| anyatarādhyāse ca vinigamakamanuvṛttatva vyāvṛttatvaprakāśatvajaḍatvādikameva| tasmādviṣayiṇo nityadṛśo'nadhyāsād viṣayasyai vātrādhyāsaḥ|naca -- `pramāṇajñānaṃ svaviṣayāvaraṇe' tyādyuktvā dṛgviṣayatvarūpadṛśyatvasya hetūkaraṇenaca tvayā'pi viṣayatvaṃ virvācyameveti vācyam tattvato'nirvācyatve'pi'pyadhyastatvena ghaṭādisamakakṣanirdācyatvasya sambhavāt|
nanu -- kathaṃ pramāṇajñānaviṣayo'dhyasta iti -- cet (na), prapañcaviṣayakajñāne tattvāvedakatvalakṣaṇaprāmāṇyābhāvāditi gṛhāṇa| ata eva -- 
yādṛśaṃ viṣayatvaṃ te vṛttiṃ prati cidātmanaḥ|
tādṛśaṃ viṣayatvaṃ me dṛśyasyāpi dṛśaṃ pratīti
nirastam, cidātmano'nadhyāse'pi vṛttestatrādhyastatvena tad‌dṛṣṭāntena prakṛte'nadhyāsasya vaktumaśakyatvāt|
syādetat -- mityātvaṃ nirvacatā tatsādhanaṃ dṛśyatvādikaṃ nirvaktavyameva, na hi ghaṭapaṭādyasaṅkīrṇākārajñānaṃ vinā tadvilakṣaṇavyavahāraḥ, athāniruktāsaṅkīrṇākārajñānamātreṇa tadupapattiḥ tarhi tulyaṃ mamā'pi| iyāṃstu viśeṣaḥ, yattva sa ākāraḥ sadvilakṣaṇaḥ, mama tu tvanmatasiddhaprātibhāsikavailakṣaṇyasādhakamānasiddhasattākaḥ, na hi lakṣaṇoktyanuktibhyāṃ sadasadvailakṣaṇyarūpānirvacanīyatvahānilābhau, brahmaṇyapi śrautasyāpi jagatkāraṇatvādilakṣaṇasya kāraṇatvakhaṇḍanarītyā asambhavāt, tvayaiva -- 
`kīdṛktatpratyagiti cettādṛgīdṛgiti dvayam|
yatra na prasaratyetatpratyagityavadhāraye' ti
brahmaṇo'pi durnirūpatvokteśca, prapañce'pi tvaduktānirvācyatvasamakakṣalakṣaṇasambhavācca, `yatkaṭhinaṃ sāpṛthivī' tyādiśrutyā pṛthivyādīnāmapi lakṣaṇatvokteśca| tasmādanirvācyatvaṃ na sattvavirodhi| sattve'pyadbhutatvādevānirvācyatvopapatteḥ|naca nirvācyatvamapi sattvaprayojakam, na hi śuktirūpyasyāpītarabhedasādhakaṃ rūpyatvaṃ prātītikajātirūpatayā suvacamapi satyam| kiṃ ca brahmaṇa ānandatvajñānatvasatyatvasvaprakāśatvādi khaṇḍanoktarītyā durvacamiti brahmatatvato'nānandādyātmakaṃ syāt| tasmādikṣukṣīrādimādhuryavadani rvācyamapi viṣayatvaṃ sadeveti| atrocyate -- dṛśyatvāderanirvacanīyatvaṃ kiṃ sattvena, uta svarūpeṇa| nādyaḥ, sattvenānirvacanīyatve'pi tattadābhāsalakṣaṇānāliṅgitatvamātreṇa hetutvopapatteḥ tannirvacanānapekṣaṇāt| na dvitīyaḥ tāttvikātāttvikasādhāraṇena dṛksambandhitvādinā rūpeṇa dṛgviṣayatvasya nirvaktuṃ śakyatvāt| lakṣaṇoktyanuktyorna sadasadvailakṣaṇyarūpā nirvācyatvahānilābhakaratvamiti yadavocaḥ, tadapi na, pūrvoktavyāpakānupalabdhisahitāyā lakṣaṇānirukteḥ uktarūpānirvacanīyatvaprayojakatvāt|
yattvānandatvādinā dharmeṇa kīdṛgityādinā svarūpeṇa ca durnirūpatvād brahmaṇo'pyanirvacanīyatvaprasaṅga iti, tanna, ānandatvādidharmavattayā durnirūpatve'pi duḥkhapratyanīkatvādyupalakṣitasvarūpasya sattvena nirvaktuṃ śakyatvāt|nacaivaṃ prapañce sattvaṃ śakyanirvacanam, bādhakasadbhāvāt| ata eva -- kaṭhinasparśavattvādinā pṛthivītvādīnāṃ nirvacanamastyeva, sattve'pyadbhutatvādinānirvācyatvopapattiriti nirastam, na hi niruktivirahamātreṇānirvācyatvaṃ brūmaḥ, kintu sattvādinā niruktiviraheṇa| sa ca prapañce bādhakādastyeva|naca -- jñāne viṣayasyādhyastatvena tadajñānajanyaṃ tajjñānanivartyaṃ cādhyāsaṃ prati viṣayatvaṃ tadanuviddhatayā pratītyabhāvaśca na sambhavātīti vācyam, caitanyamātrājñānajanyatvāt, tajjñānanivartyatvācca ghaṭādiprapañcasyetyuktatvāt| saditi pratīyamānādhiṣṭhānacaitanyānuviddhatayā pratīyamānatvamapyastyeva| tasmātsattve dṛgdṛśyasambandhānupapattirdṛḍhaiva|| ityadvaitasiddhau prapañcamithyātvānukūlatarkanirūpaṇam||
iti śrīmadhūsūdana sarasvatīviracitāyāṃ advaitasiddhau prathamaparicchede prathamo bhāgaḥ
<1.2>
nanu -- viśvasyādhyāsikatve prātibhāsikasthala iva viṣayendriyasannikarṣādhīnāyāḥ pratikarmavyavasthāyāḥ anupapattiriti cenna| vṛttiḥ pūrvameva ghaṭādīnāṃ caitanye'dhyāsena prātibhāsikasthalāpekṣayā vailakṣaṇyāt| tathā hi -- antaḥkaraṇaṃ cakṣurvattejovayavi| taccendriyadvāreṇa tatsaṃyuktaṃ viṣayaṃ vyāpya tadākāraṃ bhavati| yathā nadyādyudakaṃ praṇālyā niḥsṛtya kedārādyākāraṃ bhavati| saiva vṛttirityucyate| tatra jīvacaitanyamavidyopādhikaṃ sat sarvagataṃ antaḥkaraṇopādhikaṃ sat paricchinnamiti matadvayam| tatrā'dye viṣayaprakāśakaṃ jīvacaitanyam| dvitīye brahmacaitanyam| ādye pakṣe'pi jīvacaitanyamavidyānāvṛtam, āvṛtaṃ ca| tatrā'dye vṛttirjīvacaitanyasya viṣayoparāgārthā| dvitīye tvāvaraṇābhibhavārthā| paricchinnatvapakṣe tu jīvacaitanyasya viṣayaprakāśakatadadhiṣṭhānacaitanyābhedābhivyaktyarthā| anāvṛtatvapakṣe hyanāvṛtaṃ sarvagatamapi jīvacaitanyaṃ tattadākāravṛttyaivoparajyate, na tu viṣayaiḥ, asaṅgatvāt, yathā gotvaṃ sarvagatamapi sāsnādimadvyaktyā'bhivyajyate, na tu kesarādimadvyaktyā| yathā vā pradīpaprabhā ākāśagandharasādivyāpinyapi tānaprakāśayantī rūpasaṃsargitayā rūpameva prakāśayati tadvat| kevalāgnyadāhyasyāpi ayaḥpiṇḍādisamārūḍhāgnidāhyatvavacca kevalacaitanyāprakāśyasyāpi ghaṭādestattadākāravṛttyupārūḍhacaitanyaprakāśyatvaṃ yuktam| evañcānāvṛtatvapakṣe tattadākāravṛttidvārā caitanyasya tattaduparāge tattadarthaprakāśaḥ| āvṛtatvapakṣe tattadākāravṛttyā tattadviṣayāvacchinnacaitanyāvaraṇābhibhavena tattadarthaprakāśaḥ| antaḥkaraṇāvacchinnacaitanyarūpatve jīvasyāvacchedakāntaḥkaraṇatattadviṣayākāravṛttyā tattadviṣayāvacchinnacaitanyābhivyaktau tattatprakāśaḥ|
yadyapi prakāśakamadhiṣṭhanacaitanyaṃ sarvagataṃ jīvacaitanyaṃ cāntaḥkaraṇāvacchinnam, tathāpi caitanyābhedenābhivyaktatvād vyavasthopapattiḥ|
nanu -- iyaṃ pratikarmavyavasthā nopapadyate| tathā hi -- svasannikṛṣṭendriyajanyasvajñānāt pūrvaṃ ghaṭādeḥ sattve pratītimātraśarīratvavyāptakālpanikatvāyogaḥ| na ca kālpanikatvaviśeṣaḥ prātibhāsikatvādireva tadvyāptaḥ, gauravāt| na ca pratītimātraśarīratvābhāve'pi jñānanivartyatvādinaiva kalpitatvaṃ bhaviṣyati| pratītimātraśarīratvābhāvena jñānavirtyatvābhāvasyāpyāpādyatvāt| pratīterviśvasatyatvena vā mithyātve'pi svapnādivadindriyasannikarṣanirapekṣatayaivopapatteḥ| vyāvahārikatvasyāpi bhrāntidairdhyamātreṇopapatteśceti cet, maivam| pratītimātraśarīratvasya kalpitatvaṃ na vyāpyam| dṛgdṛsyasambandhānupapattyādisahakṛtoktānumānāt prapañce kalpitatve siddhe pratyabhijñābalācca sthāyitve tatraiva vyabhicārāt| na ca śuktirūpyādipratyabhijñāsāmyaṃ prakṛtapratyabhijñāyā iti -- vācyam, pratītyaviśeṣe'pi vaṇigvīthīsthaśuktirūpyayoḥ parīkṣitatvāparīkṣitatvābhyāṃ sthāyitvāsthāyitvarūpaviśeṣasambhavāt| tathāpi parokṣavṛtterivāparokṣavṛtterapi prakāśakatvamastu, kiṃ taduparaktacaitanyeneti cenna| parokṣasthale'pi parokṣavṛtyuparaktacaitanyasyaiva prakāśakatvāt| atha tatrāpyaparokṣaikarasacaitanyoparāge viṣayāparokṣyaprasaṅgaḥ, na| viṣayacaitanyābhivyaktāveva viṣayasyāparokṣyam| na ca parokṣasthale tadasti| viṣayendriyasannikarṣābhāvena viṣayaparyantaṃ vṛtteragamanād| antareva tatra dhīsamullāsāt| aparokṣasthale tu pramātṛcaitanyābhedābhivyaktādhiṣṭhānacaitanyoparāgo viṣaye'sti| tatra viṣayasya karmakārakatvāt| na ca vṛttigataviśeṣādāparokṣyam| tatra hi viśeṣo viṣayakṛtaścedomiti brūmaḥ| jātikṛtastu viśeṣo na sambhavati| so'yamiti pratyabhijñāyāṃ parokṣatvāparokṣatvayoḥ saṅkaraprasaṅgād, avyāpyavṛttitvāt, pramātvādināṃ saṅkaraprasaṅgācca|
kiṃ ca vṛtterjaḍatvādeva na prakāśakatvam| na ca -- vṛttāvantaḥkaraṇāvṛttyapi svaprakāśakatvaṃ jñānatvavaditi vācyam| svaprakāśātmasambandhenaiva tasyāḥ prakāśatvopapattau tatsvaprakāśatve mānābhāvāt| kiṃ ca ghaṭaṃ jānāmītyanubhūyamānasakarmakavṛttyanyā saṃvid ghaṭaprakāśarūpā ghaṭaḥ prakāśata ityākārakānubhavasiddhaiva| na ca karoti yatate calati gacchatītyādāvekārthatve'pi sakarmakākarmakatvasvabhāvatvadarśanād atrāpyekārthatve'pi tathā syāditi vācyam| tatrāpyekārthatvābhāvāt| anukūlayatno hi kṛñ‌dhātvarthaḥ| yatyarthastu yatnamātram| evaṃ gamyartha uttarasaṃyogaphalakaḥ spandaḥ| calatyarthastu spandamātram| tathā caikārthakatve kutrāpi na sakarmakatvākarmakatvavyavasthā| na ca -- tvanmate pariṇaterakarmakatvāt pariṇativiśeṣabhūtāyā vṛtteḥ kathaṃ sakarmakatvamiti vācyam| ekasya hi sakarmakatvākarmatve ekarūpeṇa viruddhe, na tu rūpāntareṇāpi, mānābhāvāt| yathā sthiterakarmikāyā api agamanatvena rūpeṇa sakarmakatvam, tathā pariṇatitvena rūpeṇākarmikāyā api vṛtteḥ jñānatvena sakarmakatvaṃ bhaviṣyatītyadoṣaḥ| nanu tarhyatītaḥ prakāśate iti dhīrna syāt, iṣṭāpatteḥ| tatrāpi vṛttipratibimbatacaitanyasattvena prakāśata ityādiprayogasambhavācca| nanu yathā ajñānavirodhivṛttāvanubhavatvaṃ nāsti, kiṃtu anyatra, tathā dveṣavirodhivṛtteranyatrecchātvamityapi syāditi cenna| bādhakasattvāsattvābhyāṃ viśeṣāt| atreva tatra sakarmakākarmakavilakṣaṇakriyānanubhavācca| yathā ca vṛttyatiriktabhānasiddhistathā svayaṃjyotiṣṭvaprastāve vistareṇa vakṣyāmaḥ|
nanu -- astu caitanyasya viṣayaprakāśakatvam, tathāpyantaḥkaraṇasya dehānnirgatiḥ na kalpyā| parokṣavailakṣaṇyāya viṣayasyābhivyaktāparokṣaciduparāga eva vaktavyaḥ| ciduparāgādau cāparokṣavṛttestadākāratvameva tantram| tasya ca tatsaṃśleṣaṃ vināpi parokṣavṛtteriva tatsannikṛṣṭakaraṇajanyatvenaivopapattiḥ, na tu prabhāyā iva vṛttestadāvaraṇanivartakatvādau tatsaṃśleṣastantram| netrānnirgacchad‌dhruvādyākāravṛttyaiva svasaṃśliṣṭanetrasthakajjalāderdhruvanetramadhyavartinaḥ
paramāṇvādeścāparokṣatvāpātāditi -- cet,na| viṣayeṣvabhivyaktaciduparāge na tadākāratvamātraṃ tantram, parokṣasthale'pi prasaṅgāt, kiṃtu tatsaṃśleṣaḥ| prabhāyā viṣayasannikṛṣṭatejastvenāvaraṇābhibhāvakatvadarśanāt| taijasasya manaso'pyajñānarūpāvaraṇābhibhavāya tatsaṃśleṣa āvaśyakaḥ| dhruvādidehamadhyavartiparamāṇvādāvatiprasaṅgastu tadākāratvaprayojakasāmagrīvirahādeva pariharaṇīyaḥ| anyathendriyasannikarṣādervidyamānatvāt paramāṇvādyākaratāyā durnivāratvāpatteḥ| tasmāt prabhāvadaviśeṣānvayavyatirekābhyāṃ yat klṛptaṃ sannikṛṣṭatejastvenāvaraṇābhibhāvakatvaṃ, tasya tadākāratvarūpaviśeṣāpekṣāyāmapi na tyāgaḥ| na hi pṛthivītvagandhatvādinā kāryakāraṇabhāve āvaśyake anityaguṇatvadravyatvādinā tattyāgaḥ| ata eva -- taditarahetusākalye sati ghaṭacakṣuḥsannikarṣasyaiva ghaṭānubhavajanakatvam, na tu ghaṭamanaḥsannikarṣasya, tadvilambena tadvilambābhāvāditi nirastam| āvaraṇabhaṅge sannikṛṣṭatejaḥkāraṇatvāvadhāraṇena tasyāpyāvaśyakatvāt| na ca spārśanapratyakṣe cakṣurādivanniyatagolakadvārābhāvenāntaḥkaraṇanirgatyayogādāvaraṇābhibhavānupapattiriti vācyam| sarvatra tattadindriyādhiṣṭhānasyaiva dvāratvasambhavāt| na ca antaḥkaraṇavṛttitvāviśeṣādicchādveṣādirūpavṛttayo'pi dehānnirgatya viṣayasaṃsṛṣṭā bhavantīti kathaṃ na svīkriyata iti vācyam| āvaraṇābhibhāvakatejastvasya tatpramāpakasya jñānavat tatrābhāvāt|
nanu ghaṭaprakāśakaṃ caitanyamupadeśasāhasryanusāreṇa ghaṭākāradhīsthā cidvā? parāgarthaprameyeṣvityādivārttikoktarītyā dhīpratibimbacaitanyābhedābhivyaktaviṣayādhiṣṭhānacaitanyaṃ vā? nādyaḥ| ādhyāsikasambandhasyātantratāpātāt| na dvitīyaḥ| āvaśyakena viṣayasaṃśliṣṭavṛttipratibimbitacaitanyenaiva tadajñānanivṛttivat tatprakāśasyāpyupapattau kiṃ viṣayādhiṣṭhānacaitanyābhivyaktikalpaneneti cenna| prakāśakaṃ tāvad adhiṣṭhānacaitanyam| taccādhyāsena viṣayaiḥ saha sākṣātsambaddham| prakāśasya ca svayaṃ bhāsamānasya svasambaddhasarvabhāsakatvamapi klṛptameva| etadanabhyupagame kalpanāntare gauravāpatteḥ| taccānabhivyaktaṃ nirvikalpakarūpamācchāditadīpavanna prakāśakamiti tadabhivyaktirapekṣitā| tacca parokṣasthale vṛttyavacchedenaivābhivyajyate| aparokṣasthale tu vṛttisamparkādāpannajāḍyābhibhave viṣayo'bhivyajyate, vṛtterviṣayaparyantatvāt| na ca parokṣasthale'pyevaṃ prasaṅgaḥ| dvārābhāvenāntaḥkaraṇanirgatyabhāvāt| nanu -- vṛttestadākāratvaṃ na tāvattadviṣayatvam| tvayaiva nirāsāt| nāpi tasmin caitanyoparāgayogyatāpādakatvaṃ, tadajñānābhibhāvakatvaṃ vā| ubhayorapi tadākāratvaprayojyatvena tattvāyogāt| nāpi ghaṭādivat pṛthubudhnodarādyākāratvaṃ, sākāravādāpātāt| saṃsthānahīnajātiguṇādivṛtternirākāratvaprasaṅgācca| ghaṭapaṭāviti samūhālasbane viruddhanānākāratvāpatteśceti cenna| astītyāditadviṣayakavyavahārapratibandhakājñānanivartanayogyatvasya, tatsannikṛṣṭakaraṇajanyatvasya vā tadākāratvarūpatvāt| tadubhayaṃ ca svakāraṇādhīnasvabhāvaviśeṣāt| na cātmāśrayaḥ| nivṛttijananasvarūpayogyatayā phalopadhānasya sādhyatvena svānapekṣaṇāt|
nanu -- dṛśi viṣayādhyāsasvīkarturjīvacaitanyaṃ vā viṣayadṛk? brahmacaitanyaṃ vā? nādyaḥ, jīve avacchinnacitsvarūpe kalpite adhyāsāyogāt| na ca viṣayadṛk jīvacaitanyameva, adhyāsastu brahmacaitanya iti vācyam| dṛśyayorevādhyāsikasambandhāpatteḥ, adhyastādhiṣṭhānayorubhayorapi dṛgbhinnatvāt| ata eva na dvitīyo'pi| saviśeṣabrahmaṇo'pi kalpitatvena tatrādhyāsāyogācca| na ca śuddhacaitanyamekameva| tadevādhiṣṭhānam| tatrāvacchedakamavidyādikaṃ nādhiṣṭhānakoṭau praviśati| tadeva ca jīvaśabdena brahmaśabdena ca vyapadiśyate, upādhiviśeṣāt| tathā ca jīvacaitanyasya dṛktve'pi dṛśyādhyāso nānupapanna iti vācyam| śuddhacaitanyasya āsaṃsāramāvṛtatvena jagadāndhyaprasaṅgāditi cenna| mūlāvidyānivṛttyabhāvena sarvata āvaraṇābhibhavābhāve'pi ghaṭādyavacchedenāvaraṇābhivād āndhyavirahopapatteḥ| nanu tarhīdānīmapi brahmasphuraṇe caramavṛttivaiyarthyam| adhikabhāne'pi tasya sphuraṇāt| na hyakhaṇḍārthavedāntajanyāyāṃ vṛttau bhāvo abhāvo vā viśeṣaṇamupalakṣaṇaṃ vā prakāraḥ prakāśata iti cenna| upādhyaviṣayakabrahmasphuraṇasya caramapravṛttiprayuktatvena tasyāḥ sāphalyāt| prakārāsphuraṇaṃ tu tasyāḥ bhūṣaṇameva| idānīntanasphuraṇasya saprakārakatvenopādhiviṣayatvāt| `ekadhaivānudraṣṭavyam' ityādiśrutibalāt svasamānaviṣayajñānādeva cājñānanivṛtterakhaṇḍacinmātrajñānasyaiva mokṣahetutvāvadhāraṇāt| na ca antaḥkaraṇāvacchinnacaitanyasya jīvatve suṣuptidaśāyāṃ tadabhāvena kṛtahānyādyāpattiriti vācyam| tadā'pyasya kāraṇātmanā'vasthānāt| sthūlasūkṣmasādhāraṇasyāntaḥkaraṇasyopādhitvāt| `tadapīteḥ saṃsāravyapadeśāt' ityasmin sūtre cāyamarthaḥ spaṣṭataraḥ| na ca vṛttyuparaktatvaṃcaitanyasya na tatpratibimbitatvam| darpaṇe mukhasyevānud‌bhūtarūpe'ntaḥkaraṇe śabdānyapratibimbanopādhitāyā acākṣuṣacaitanyasya pratibimbanāyāścāyogāditi vācyam| ud‌bhūtarūpavattvaṃ na pratibimbanopādhitāprayojakam| asvacche'pi loṣṭādau pratibimbāpatteḥ|
kintu svacchatvam| tacca prakāśasvabhāvatvena manasastatpariṇāmabhūtāyā vṛtteścāstyeva, triguṇātmakasyāpyajñānasya svacchasattvātmakatāyā api sattvena tatrāpi pratibimbanopādhitāyāḥ sattvāt| nāpi cākṣuṣatvaṃ pratibimbitatvaprayojakam| acākṣuṣasyāpyākāśādeḥ pratibimbitatvadarśanāt|
nanu -- cākṣuṣavṛttyupārūḍhacitaḥ kathaṃ rūpamātraprakāśakatvam? na ca prabhāvanniyamaḥ, vaiṣamyāt| tathā hi -- prabhāyāṃ tamovirodhitvaṃ rūpaṃ pratīva gandhādīn pratyapi samam| na hi sā gandhadeśasthaṃ tamo na nivartayati| na ca ajñānavirodhitvalakṣaṇaṃ prakāśakatvaṃ rūpaṃ pratyeva, na tu rasādīn pratīti vācyam| ajñānanivartakatvasya vṛttibhinne'naṅgīkārāt| prabhāyā rūpagrāhakacakṣuḥsahakāritvavat gandhādigrāhighrāṇādisahakāritvābhāve'pi cito grāhakāntarāsadṛkāritvena tadvatsahakārivilambena vilambasya vaktumaśakyatvāt| tathā ca citaḥ sarvagatatvena sarvasambandhādrūpādivad gurutvāderapyāśrayadvārā sākṣādvā sambandhitvāt prakāśāpattiḥ, vṛttyuparaktacitsambandhasyaiva prakāśakatvāt| `asaṅgo hyayaṃ puruṣaḥ' iti śrutistu (īśvarasya) tatkṛtalepābhāvaparā, na tu sambandhaniṣedhikā| `sa yattatra yatkiñcitpaśyatyananvāgatastena bhavati' iti pūrvavākyāt, `yathākāśasthito nityaṃ vāyuḥ sarvatrago mahān' ityādismṛteśceti cenna| prabhāyā rūparasādideśagatatamonāśakatvaṃ tatsambandhādyujyate, caitanyasya tu svabhāvato'sambaddhatvāt tadākāravṛttyā tadekasambandhasyopādānāt kathamanyāvabhāsakatvaprasaṅgaḥ? svabhāvato hyasaṅgatve `asaṅgo hyayaṃ puruṣaḥ' iti śrutiḥ pramāṇam| na caiṣā lepābhāvaparā| akartṛtvapratipādanāya sambandhābhāvaparatvāt| yathā caitattathā vyaktamākare|
evaṃ smṛtirapyetat śrutyanurodhena neyā| ataḥ sarvaiḥ saha sambandhābhāvāt| na sarvāvabhāsaḥ, kiṃtu yadākārā vṛttistasyaiva| ata eva `idaṃ rajatam' iti bhrame idamākāravṛttyavacchinnacaitanyena rajatabhānānupapatteḥ rajatākārā'pyavidyāvṛttirabhyupeyate| svataścidvimbāgrāhake caitanyasya tadākāratvāyogāt| svataścidvimbagrāhake tvantaḥkaraṇavṛttyādau na vṛttyapekṣeti nānavasthā| na ca āśrayasambandhāviśeṣe'pi rūpākārā vṛttirna gandhādyākāreti kuta iti vācyam| yathā tava cākṣuṣajñāne āśrayasambandhāviśeṣe'pi na gandho viṣayaḥ| tathā'smākamapi cakṣurdvārakavṛttau na gandhādyākāratvam| indriyaviṣayasambandhānāṃ svabhāvasya niyāmakasya samānatvāt| nanu ādhyāsikasambandho vṛtteḥ pūrvamapyastyeva| anyastūparāgo na dṛśyatve tantramiti kiṃ tadarthayā vṛttyeti cenna| jīvacaitanyasyādhiṣṭhānacaitanyasya vā'bhedābhivyaktyarthatvād vṛtteḥ| anyathā mayedaṃ viditamiti sambandhāvabhāso na syāt|
nanu jīvacaitanyasyāsaṅgatve brahmacaitanyaṃ sutirāmasaṅgam| tathā ca māyopādhikaviṣayoparāgatvāt svataḥ sārvajñyaṃ na syāt| na ca brahma sarvopādānatvādupādhiṃ vinaiva svasvarūpavatsvābhinnaṃ jagadavabhāsayatīti vācyam| upādānatvaṃ na tāvadviśiṣṭaniṣṭhaṃ pariṇāmitvam| ādhyāsikasambandhasyātantratāpatteḥ| anādyavidyādikaṃ prati tadabhāvācca| nāpi śuddhaniṣṭhamadhiṣṭhānatvam| śuddhasya sarvajñatvasarvaśaktitvāderabhāvāditi cenna| brahmaṇo'saṅgatve'pi sarveṣāṃ tatrādhyāsena māyopādhiṃ vinaiva tasya sarvaprakāśakatayā sārvajñyopapatteḥ| na ca śuddhaniṣṭhamadhiṣṭhānatvaṃ nopādānatvam sārvajñyābhāvādityuktamiti vācyam| avidyākalpitānāṃ sarvajñatvādīnāṃ śuddhe sattvāt| anyathā teṣāṃ taṭasthalakṣaṇatvamapi na syāt| nanu āvaraṇābhibhavārthatvapakṣo na yuktaḥ| vivartādhiṣṭhānasya cinmātrasyājñānādisākṣitvena sadā prakāśanāt| anyasyājñānakalpitasyāvaraṇasyābhāvāditi cenna| ajñānādisākṣitvena svaprakāśe'pyaśanāyādyatītatvādinā prakāśābhāvādāvaraṇasyāvaśyatvāt| nanu ajñānasya nayanapaṭalavat puṅgatatve caitrasyājñānanāśe'pi maitrasya tadanāśād aprakāśo yuktaḥ| viṣayagatatve tu caitrārjitayā vṛttyā ajñāne dīpena tamasīva nāśite maitrasyāpi prakāśaḥ syāditi cenna| caitrāvaraṇaśakterevājñānagatāyāścaitrārjitavṛttyā nāśitatvena sa paśyati, na maitraḥ tatpratiyogikāvaraṇaśakteranāśād| āvaraṇaśaktīnāṃ draṣṭṛviṣayabhedābhyāṃ bhinnatvāt| tamastu na tathetyekānītapradīpenāpyanyāt prati prakāśo yujyate| etena -- ekājñānapakṣe śuktijñānena tadajñānanivṛttau sadya eva mokṣāpātaḥ| anivṛttau rūpyādeḥ savilāsāvidyānivṛttirūpabādhāyoga iti nirastam| āvaraṇaśaktināśe'pi mūlājñānanāśābhāvena sadyo mokṣābhāvasya rūpyādau savilāsaśaktimadavidyānivṛttirūpabādhasya copapatteḥ|
nanu ekājñānapakṣe rūpyādeḥ śuktijñānena svakāraṇe pravilayamātraṃ kriyate| mud‌garaprahāreṇeva ghaṭasya| na tvajñānaṃ nivartyata iti te mataṃ na yuktam| yato jñānamajñānasyaiva nivartakamiti vyāptibalāt jñānasyājñānanivṛttidvāraivānyavirodhitvenājñānamanivartya rūpyādinivartakatvāyogāt| śuktijñānenājñānānivṛttāvabhivyaktacaitanyasambandhābhāvena bhrāntāviva bādhe'pi śukteraprakāśāpatteśceti cenna| yato jñānamajñānanivartakamiti vyāpterucchedaviṣayatvāt| svakāraṇe sūkṣmarūpeṇāvasthāne tadanaṅgīkārāt| śuktijñānasya cānavacchinnacaitanyāvaraṇarūpamūlājñānānivartakatve'pi avacchinnacaitanyāvaraṇarūpatūlājñānanivartakatvenābhivyaktacaitanyasambandhāt bādhadaśāyāṃ rūpyanivṛttiśuktiprakāśayorapyupapatteḥ| na ca upādeyabhūtayā vṛttyopādānabhūtāvidyābhibhavo na ghaṭate| upādeyenopādānābhibhavādarśanāditi vācyam| vṛścikādinā gomayāderupādānasyāpyabhibhavadarśanāt| ārambhavādānabhyupagamācca na gomayāvayavānāmupādānatvaśaṅkā|
nanu cakṣurādijanyaśuktyādivṛtteḥ saprakārikāyāḥ niṣprakārakaśuddhacaitanyāviṣayatayā tadāvaraṇarūpamūlājñānābhibhavābhāve'pyavacchinnaviṣayayā tayā avacchinnacaitanyāvaraṇarūpatūlājñānābhibhavo yujyata iti te matamayuktam| avacchinne avidyākalpite aprasaktaprakāśe mūlāvidyāyā iva tadāvaraṇaśakterayogāt| tvayānabhyupagatatvācca| jaḍaviśiṣṭātmānaṃ prati tadabhyugame ca viśeṣaṇānāvarakaviśiṣṭāvarakaśaktyabhibhavasya viśeṣyāvarakaśaktyabhibhavaṃ vinā'yogena śuktyākāravṛttyaiva śuddhātmaprakāśāpātāditi cenna| anavabodhāt| na hyavidyākalpite'vacchinne asmābhiravidyā vā tacchaktirvābhyupeyate| kiṃ tu caitanyamātra na hyavidyākalpite'vacchinne asmābhiravidyā vā tatchaktirvābhyupeyate| kiṃ tu caitanyamātra eva| tasmiṃstu sarvaṃ jaḍamadhyastamastītyekāśrayāśritatvasambandhāt jaḍāvacchinnacaitanyamāvṛtamiti vyapadeśaḥ| ghaṭādyākāravṛttyā tu tadadhiṣṭhānacaitanyābhivyaktau tadavacchedenaiva tanniṣṭhāvaraṇābhibhavo jāyata iti na śuddhātmaprakāśāpattiḥ| taduktaṃ saṃkṣepaśārīrake -- 
āśrayatvaviṣayatvabhāginī nirvibhāgacitireva kevalā|
pūrvasiddhatamaso hi paścimo nāśrayo bhavati nāpi gocaraḥ||
bahu nigadya kimatra vadāmyahaṃ śṛṇuta saṅagrahamadvayaśāsane|
sakalavāṅmanasātigatā citiḥ sakalavāṅmanasavyavahārabhāk||
iti ca| tasmādavidyāyāṃ satyāmapi śaktyabhibhavādvā, tūlājñānanāśādvā, avasthāviśeṣapracyavādvā, ekadeśanāśādvā, bhīrubhaṭavadapasaraṇādvā, kaṭavat saṃveṣṭanādvā, āvaraṇabhaṅgānirmokṣabādhānāmupapattiḥ|
nanu avasthāviśeṣāṇāmajñānābhinnatve ekājñānapakṣakṣatiḥ| ajñānabhinnatve ca sākṣāt jñānena nivṛttiḥ bhramādyupādānatvaṃ ca na syāt| teṣāmiva rūpyasyaivopādānanāśaṃ vinā nāśaprasaṅgaśca| śuktyajñānaṃ naṣṭamityanubhavavirodhaśceti cenna| yato'vasthā tāvadavasthāvato'bhinnaiva| ajñānaikyaṃ tu sarvāvasthānusyūtaikākāramādāya| evaṃ cājñānāvasthāyā ajñānatvena na jñānasākṣānnivartyatvādyanupapattiḥ| yattvavasthāviśeṣāṇāmiva rūpyasyaivopādānanivṛttiṃ vinā nivṛttyāpādanaṃ tadayuktam| ajñāna eva jñānasya sākṣādvirodhāvadhāraṇenājñānāvasthāyāstadabhinnāyā jñānasākṣānnivartyatvārhatvāt| na tu rūpyādīnām, anīdṛktvāt| anekājñānapakṣe tu śaṅkāpi nodeti| nanvasminpakṣe ekayā vṛttyā sarvatadajñānasya nivṛttiḥ? uta ekatadajñānasya? ādye punaḥ śukteḥ kadā'pyaprakāśo na syāt| antye vṛttikāle'pi prakāśo na syāt| ekasyāvaraṇasya nivṛttāvapyāvaraṇāntarānivṛtteriti cenna| ekayā vṛttyā ekājñānanāśe'pi tayaivāvaraṇāntarāṇāṃ pratiruddhatvād| yāvat sā tiṣṭhati tāvatprakāśaḥ| tasyāmapagatāyāṃ punaraprakāśaścopapadyate| ajñānasya jñānaprāgabhāvasthānīyatvāt| yathā tava ekaṃ jñānamekameva prāgabhāvaṃ nāśayati, tannāśarūpeṇodayāt prāgabhāvāntaranibandhanamajñātatvādivyavahāraṃ ca pratibaghnāti tathā mamāpyekaṃ jñānamekamevājñānaṃ nivartayati, ajñānāntaranibandhanaṃ ca prayojanaṃ pratibaghnātī ti kimanupapannam? atra ca pratibandhapadena kāryānutpattiprayojakatvaṃ kāraṇābhāvapratibandhakasādhāraṇamabhihitam| evamavasthāviśeṣapakṣe'pi prakāśāprakāśāvupapādanīyau| evamamūrtasyājñānasya yadyapi daṇḍādinā gavādīnāmivāpasāraṇaṃ karādinā kaṭādīnāmiva saṃveṣṭanaṃ ca na sambhavati, tathāpi kāryākṣamatvasāmyenāpasāraṇasaṃveṣṭanapakṣau yojanīyau| yathā hi uttejakābhāvasahakṛtasya maṇeḥ pratibandhakatāyāmuttejakasattve pratibandhakakāryākṣamatvaṃ, tathā vṛttyabhāvasahakṛtasyājñānasya pratibandhakatāyāṃ vṛttau satyāṃ tatkāryānudaya iti draṣṭavyam|
nanu caitanyasya niravayavatvāt tasyaikadeśena prakāśo na yujyate| athākāśa iva tattadarthāvacchinnatvamekadeśaśabdārthaḥ, tarhi nāgantukapadārthāvacchinnaṃ caitanyamanādyajñānasya viṣayaḥ| nirviṣayasyāvaraṇasyāyogāt| prāganavacchinnāvaraṇamevedānīmavacchinnāvaraṇaṃ jātamityapi na| avacchinnacaitanyajñānenaivānavacchinnāvaraṇanāśāpatteḥ| etena vyaktitaḥ pūrvaṃ jātiriva viṣayātpūrvamajñānamastīti nirastamiti cenna| anādyajñānaviṣaye anādicaitanye tattadāgantukapadārthāvacchedābhyupagamād| `āśrayatvaviṣayatvabhāginī nirvibhāgacitireva kevalā' ityuktatvāt| yadavacchinnagocarā ca vṛttistadavacchedenaivāvaraṇāpasāraṇāt nānavacchinnacaitanyāvaraṇabhaṅgaprasaṅgaḥ| ata eva vṛttiviṣayāvacchinnacaitanyāt prāgajñānamastītyabhiprāyeṇa viṣayātprāgajñānamastīti sādhūktam| tasmādadhiṣṭhānacaitanyaṃ svādhyastaṃ bhāsayatīti siddham|
tadayamatra niṣkarṣaḥ -- yadyapi viṣayaprakāśakaṃ viṣayādhiṣṭhānabhūtaṃ prameyacaitanyam| antaḥkaraṇāvacchinnacaitanyaṃ tu tasya pramātṛ| antaḥkaraṇavṛttyavacchinnacaitanyaṃ tu pramāṇam| tathāpi yadīyāntaḥkaraṇavṛttyā viṣayaparyantaṃ cakṣurādidvārā nissṛtayā yatprakāśakaṃ caitanyaṃ yatpramātṛcaitanyābhedenābhivyajyate tameva sa eva jānāti nānyaṃ nānyo vā| ata evaikavṛttyupārūḍhatvalakṣaṇaikalolībhāvāpannaṃ pramātṛpramāṇaprameyacaitanyaṃ bhavati| tatastadavacchedenājñānanivṛttyā bhāsamānaṃ prameyacaitanyamaparokṣaṃ phalamityucyate| tat svayaṃ bhāsamānaṃ sat svādhyastaṃ ghaṭādyapi bhāsayatīti tat phalavyāpyamityupeyate| yanniṣṭhā ca yadākārā vṛttirbhavati tanniṣṭhaṃ tadākāramajñānaṃ sā nāśayatīti niyamāt pramātṛprameyobhayavyāpinyaparokṣavṛttiḥ svāvacchedenāvaraṇamapasārayati| prakāśasya svāvacchedenāvaraṇāpasārakatvadarśanāt| ataḥ pramātravacchinnasyāsattvāvaraṇasya prameyāvacchinnasyābhānāvaraṇasya cāpasāraṇād ghaṭo'yaṃ me sphuratītyādyaparokṣavyavahāraḥ| parokṣasthale tu indriyasannikarṣalakṣaṇadvārābhāvādantaḥ -- karaṇanissaraṇābhāvena viṣayaparyantaṃ vṛtteragamanādviṣayāvacchinnaprameyacaitanyena saha pramātṛcaitanyasyaikavṛttyupārūḍhatvābhāvenāparokṣatayā'bhivyaktyabhāve'pi pramātṛpramāṇacaitanyayorekalolī bhāvāpattyā pramātravacchinnamasatatvāvaraṇamātraṃ nivartate| tāvanmātrasya vṛttyavacchinnatvāt| idameva suṣuptivyāvṛttiśabdena vivaraṇācāryairvyākhyātam| viṣayāvacchinnābhānāvaraṇatatkāryasadbhāve'pi pramātravacchinnāsattvāvaraṇanivṛttyā anumānādau vyavahāropapattiḥ| ata eva jānāmyahaṃ parvate vahnisstīti, sa tu kīdṛśa iti me na bhātītyādivyavahāraḥ| trayāṇāmekalolībhāve aparokṣatvam, dvayorekalolībhāve tu parokṣatvamiti na saṅkaraḥ| vṛtteśca viṣayeṇa samaṃ sākṣādevāparokṣasthale sambandhaḥ | parokṣasthale tvanumiteranumeyena tadvyāpyajñānajanyatvam| śābdyāḥ saṃsargeṇa saha tadāśrayavācakapadajanyatvam| smṛteḥ smartavyena saha tadviṣayānubhavajanyatvam| evamanyatrāpi paramparāsambandha eveti parokṣāparokṣavibhāgaḥ| vistareṇa vyutpāditā'smābhiriyaṃ prakriyā siddhāntabindau| tasmādviṣayasya mithyātve'pi pratikarmavyavasthopapatteriti dik|
ityadvaitasiddhau pratikarmavyavasthopapattiḥ||
nanu -- mithyātvānumānaṃ pratikūlatarkaparāhatam| tathā hi -- viśvaṃ yadi kalpitaṃ syāt, sādhiṣṭhānaṃ syāt| na caivam, sāmānyato jñātatve satyajñātaviśeṣavattvasyādhiṣṭhānatvaprayojakasya nirviśeṣe nissāmānye ca brahmaṇyasambhavāditi cenna| svarūpeṇa jñātatve sati viśeṣeṇājñātatvasyādhiṣṭhānatvaprayojakatvenājñātaviśeṣavattvasyāprayojakatvāt| tathā ca nissāmānye nirviśeṣe ca brahmaṇi svaprakāśatvena jñānāt paripūrṇatvānandatvādinā cājñānādadhiṣṭhānatvamupapannam| vastutastu -- kalpitasāmānyaviśeṣavattvaṃ brahmaṇyapi sulabhaveva| akalpitasāmānyaviśeṣavattvaṃ cāprasiddham| na ca tatkalpane anyonyāśrayaḥ| kalpitasāmānyaviśeṣāṇāṃ pravāhānāditvāt| sattvānandatvādīnāmeva kalpitavyaktibhedena sāmānyatvāt| paripūrṇānandatvādīnāṃ ca viśeṣatvāt| ata eva sāmānyākārajñānaṃ vinā saṃskāranudbodhāt kathamadhyāsa iti na vācyam| sadātmanā svarūpajñānasyaiva sāmānyajñānatvāt| na hyadhyasanīyaṃ sadātmanā na bhāti| etāvāneva viśeṣaḥ -- yadadhiṣṭhānaṃ svata eva tadātmanā bhāti| adhyasanīyaṃ tu tatsambandhāt| nanu -- adhiṣṭhānatirodhānaṃ vinā bhramāsambhavaḥ| prakāśarūpatirodhāne tu tadadhyastāvidyādeḥ prakāśānupapattiriti cet, na| ekasyaivānandādyātmanā tirohitasya sadātmanā prakāśasambhavāt| taduktaṃ vārtikakārapādaiḥ -- 
yatprasādādavidyādi sidhyatīva divāniśam|
tamapyapahnute'vidyā nājñānasyāsti duṣkaram|| iti|
na ca bādhakāle'pi sadviśeṣājñānamastīti -- vācyam| paripūrṇānandatvādeḥ sata eva viśeṣatvena tadā tadajñānābhāvāt| dharmatvamātrasyaiva kalpitatvāt| yadvā, bhramavirodhijñānābhāva eva tantram| na tu viśeṣājñānam| viśvopādānagocarājñānasya śravaṇādijanyamātmamātraviṣayakaṃ vṛttirūpaṃ jñānaṃ virodhi, na tu cidrūpaṃ svataḥsiddhaṃ jñānam| bhramavirodhinaśca vṛttirūpasya jñānasyedānīmabhāvo'styeva| nanu -- ātmānātmanordṛgdṛśyatvātmānātmatvādinā bhedajñānāt kathamadhyastādhiṣṭhānabhāva iti cenna| idamanidaṃ na bhavatīti purovarttyapurovartinorbhedagrahe'pīdaṃ rajatamityadhyāsavat san ghaṭa ityādyadhyāso bhaviṣyati| na hi rūpāntareṇa bhedagraho rūpāntareṇādhyāsavirodhī| san ghaṭa ityādipratyaye ca sadrūpasyātmano ghaṭādyanuviddhatayā bhānānna tasya ghaṭādyadhyāsādhiṣṭhānatānupapattiḥ| sadrūpeṇa ca sarvajñānaviṣayatopapatterna rūpādihīnasyāpyātmanaḥ kālasyeva cākṣuṣatvādyanupapattiḥ|
nanu -- viśvaṃ yadi kalpitaṃ syāttadā sapradhānaṃ syāt| na caivaṃ, tasmāt na kalpitamiti cenna| atrāpi pradhānasya sajātīyasya sattvāt| pūrvaprapañcasajātīyasyaivottaraprapañcasyādhyasanāt| adhyāso hi svakāraṇatayā saṃskāramapekṣate| na tu saṃskāraviṣayasya satyatām, anupayogāt| na ca -- pramājanya eva saṃskāro bhramahetuḥ| ato viṣayasatyatvamāvaśyakamiti vācyam| mānābhāvād, viparīte lāghavācca| ata eva -- adhyastasajātīyaṃ pūrvamadhyastāpekṣayā'dhikasattākamapekṣaṇīyamityapi nirastam| satyatāvadadhikasattāyā apyanupayogāt| pūrvaṃ tajjñānamātramapekṣyate, taccāstyeva| nanu -- evamadhiṣṭhānasyāpi jñānamātrameva hetuḥ, na tu taditi na sadadhiṣṭhānāpekṣā syāditi śūnyavādāpattiriti cenna| adhiṣṭhānasya jñānadvārā bhramāhetutve'pyajñānadvārā bhramahetutvena sattvaniyamāt| bhramopādānājñānaviṣayo hyadhiṣṭhānamityucyate| tacca satyameva| asatyasya sarvasyāpyajñānakalpitatvenājñānaviṣayatvāt| tadasatyatve tajjñānasya bhramābādhakatvaprasaṅgāt| jagati bhramabādhavyavasthā ca na syāt| bādhena hi kiñcidviruddhaṃ tattvamupadarśayatā āropitamatattvaṃ bādhanīyam| ubhayādhyāse tu kiṃ kena bādhyeta? ata eva bhagavatā bhāṣyakāreṇa -- "satyānṛte mithunīkṛtya" ityuktam|
nanu etatprapañcāsādhyārthakriyākāriṇaḥ prapañcāntarasyābhāvena svocitārthakriyākāriṇo'sya na mithyātvamiti cenna, svāpnamāyādau vyabhicārāt| svocitārthakriyākāritvasya pāramārthikasattvāprayojakatvāt| nāpi śrutyādisiddhotpattyādimattvaṃ sattve tantram| svapnaprapañce vyabhicārāt| tasyāpi "na tatra rathā na rathayogā na panthāno bhavantyatha rathān rathayogān pathassṛjate" ityādiśrutyotpattyādipratipādanāt| na ca kalpādyabhramāyogaḥ| kalpāntarīyasaṃskārasya tatra hetutvāt| na ca janmāntarīyasaṃskārasya kāryajanakatve'tiprasaṅgaḥ| adṛṣṭādivaśena kvacidudbodhe'pyanyatrānudbodhopapatteḥ| kāryonneyadharmāṇāṃ yathākāryamunnayanāt| anyathā jātasya stanyapānādau pravṛttirna syāt| nanu -- caitreṇa maitre saṃskārādhyāse'pi maitrasya bhramādarśanāt jagadbhramahetusaṃskārasya sattvaṃ durvāram| na ca svenādhyastātsaṃskārādbhramaḥ| bhramāt pūrvaṃ svasya kāryānumeyasaṃskārādhyāsaniyamābhāvāditi cenna| śuktirūpyasya kuṇḍalājanakatvavaccaitrādhyastasaṃskārasya maitrabhramājanakatve'pi vaṇigvīthīstharūpyasya kuṇḍalajanakatvavat svenādhyastasya saṃskārasya viyadādyadhyāsajanakatvopapatteḥ| tatpratītyabhāve'pi tadadhyāsasya pūrvaṃ sattvāt kṛtsnasyāpi vyāvahārikapadārthasyājñātasattvābhyupagamāt|
nanu -- prātibhāsikarūpye traikālikaniṣedhasya tvanmate vyāvahārikarūpyaviṣayatvavad vyāhārikaprapañce'pi `neha nānā' iti traikālikaniṣedhasya pāramārthikaprapañcāntaraviṣayatā'vaśyaṃ vācyeti cenna| bhramabādhavaiyadhikaraṇyāpātenāsya pakṣasyānaṅgīkāraparāhatatvāt| aṅgīkāre'pi vyāvahārikaniṣedhe pāramārthikaviṣayatvaṃ na sambhavati| apratītasya niṣedhāyogāt| pratītyā sahādhyāsātiriktasambandhābhāvena pāramārthike pratītatvābhāvāt| nanu -- pradhānādhiṣṭhānayoḥ sādṛśyābhāvātkathamadhyāsaḥ? atha nirguṇayorapi guṇayoḥ sādṛśyavadatrāpi kiñcitsādṛśyaṃ bhaviṣyatīti, tanna| nirdharmake brahmaṇi tasyāpyadhyāsādhīnatvenānyonyāśrayāt| yadyapi sādṛśyaṃ sopādhikādhyāse na kāraṇaṃ, vyabhicārāt| tathāpi nirupādhikādhyāse'nvayavyatirekābhyāṃ tasyāvaśyamapekṣaṇīyatvāt| sopādhike'pi `raktaḥ sphaṭikaḥ' dravyatvādinā sādṛśyānapekṣatvād| antaḥkaraṇādhyāse tvavidyāsambandhitvasyaiva sādṛśyasya vidyamānatvāt| vastutastuna bhrame sādṛśyāpekṣāniyamaḥ| nirupādhike'pi `pītaḥ śaṅkhaḥ' ityādau vyabhicārāt| `raktaḥ sphaṭikaḥ' ityādāvapi dravyatvādinā sādṛśyamastītyapi na| pradhānamātravṛttitayā prāgavagatamadhyāsasamaye cādhiṣṭhānavṛttitayā gṛhītaṃ yat tadeva hi sādṛśyaṃ viparyayaprayojakamiti tvayāpi vācyam| na tu prāgeva pradhānādhiṣṭhānobhayavṛttitayā gṛhītam| tasya saṃśāyakatvāt| dravyatvādi ca lohitālohitavṛttitayā prāggṛhītamiti na viparyayaprayojakam| kiṃ ca sādṛśyaṃ na svato bhramakāraṇaṃ, mānābhāvāt| kiṃ tu saṃskārodbodhena sāmagrīsaṃpādakatayā| saṃskārodbodhaśca na sādṛśyaikaniyataḥ| adṛṣṭādinā'pi kiṃ tu saṃskārodbodhena sāmagrīsampādakatayā| saṃskārodbodhaśca na sādṛśyaikaniyataḥ| adṛṣṭādinā'pi tatsambhavāt| taduktam -- `sadṛśādṛṣṭacintādyāḥ smṛtibījasya bodhakāḥ' iti| cintādikaṃ ca praṇidhānasūtre vyākhyātam| tathā cānyataḥ saṃskārodvodhe sati sādṛśyamanupayogi| taduktaṃ vivaraṇe `nirupādhikabhramakāryadarśanameva guṇāvayavasāmānyābhāve'pi ketakīgandhasadṛśaḥ sarpagandha itivat sādṛśyāntaraṃ vā, śaṅkhapītimādāviva kāraṇāntaraṃ vā kalpayati' iti|
nanu -- doṣaṃ vinā bhramasvīkāre tadaprāmāṇyasya svatastvāpattiḥ, doṣajanyatvasvīkāre tu doṣasyāpyadhyasanīyatvenānavasthāpattiriti -- cenna| anādyavidyādhyāsasya doṣānapekṣatvāt| sādyadhyāsasya cāvidyādoṣajanyatvāt nāprāmāṇyasya svatastvam| nāpyanavasthā| anyathā tārkikāṇāmapyanādipramā guṇaṃ vinā'pīti prāmāṇyaparatastvaṃ bhajyeta| janyapramāmātrasya guṇajanyatvaṃ tu janyādhyāsamātrasya doṣajanyatvena samam| nanu -- lāghavena prathamopasthitatvena ca pravṛttimātraṃ prati saṃsargadhiya iva dhūmamātraṃ prati vahneriva cādhyāsamātraṃ prati doṣādīnāṃ janakatvādavidyādyāso'pi kathaṃ klṛptakāraṇena vinā bhavatu? anyathā saṃsargadhīrapi pravṛttiviśeṣe, vahnirapi dhūmaviśeṣe heturiti syāt| tathā cākhyātivādaścānumānamātrocchedaścāpadyeyātām| kiṃ ca avidyārūpaviṣayasyānāditve'pi tatpratīterdoṣājanyatve prāmāṇyāpātaḥ| aprāmāṇyaprayojakasya doṣajanyasyābhāvāt|
atha bhedavadavidyākhyadoṣasya svaparanirvāhakatvam, evamapi bhedo bhinna itivad `ajñānamajñātam' iti vyavahāro bhavatu| pratītimātraśarīrasya svaviṣayadhīhetutvaṃ kuta? svasya svasmāt pūrvavṛttitvāsambhavāditi -- cenna| adhyāsatvasya laghutve'pi prathamopasthitatve'pi na doṣajanyatāyāṃ tantratvam| doṣasyāpi dṛśyatvenādhyasanīyatayā'navasthāpatteḥ| yathā nityajñānavādināṃ jñānatvasya na śarīrajanyatāvacchedakatvaṃ, na vā guṇajanyatvasya prāmāṇyaprayojakatvaṃ, bādhakabalāt, tadvat janyādhyāsaṃ pratyevaṃ doṣādīnāṃ kāraṇatvam| guṇājanyatve'pyabādhitaviṣayathā nityajñānaprāmāṇyavat doṣājanyatve'pi bādhitaviṣayatayā'nādyadhyāsasyāpyaprāmāṇyopapattiḥ| bādhitaviṣayatve'pi na doṣajanyatvamavacchedakam| doṣajanyatve'pyavacchedakāntarānveṣaṇe'navasthāpātāt| bādhitaviṣayatvasya doṣājanyavṛttitve'pi doṣajanyatvasya tadvyāpyatvopapatteḥ| ata eva śabarasvāminā `yasya duṣṭaṃ karaṇaṃ, yatra ca mithyeti pratyayaḥ sa evāsamīcīno nānyaḥ' iti vadatā duṣṭakaraṇajanyatvamantareṇāpi arthānyathātvamaprāmāṇyaprayojakamuktam| avidyādhyāsarūpasya sākṣicaitanyasyāvidyājanyatvānabhyupagamāt na pratītimātraśarīratvavyāghātaḥ| `ahamajñaḥ' ityādyabhilāpakāraṇībhūtavṛttirūpādhyāsaṃ prati tvavidyāyāḥ kāraṇatvamastyeva, ghaṭādīnāmiva svapratyakṣaṃ prati| vahniviśiṣṭadhiyostu bādhakābhāvāt sāmānyenaiva dhūmapravṛttī prati hetuteti na pūrvoktadoṣāpātaḥ| nanu -- avidyādhyāsasyānāditvena doṣādyanapekṣāvadadhiṣṭhānānapekṣāpi syāditi cenna| janakatvenādhiṣṭhānānapekṣāyāmapyāśrayatvena tadapekṣāniyamāt| paramamahattvāderāśrayāpekṣāvad adhyāsasya sādhiṣṭhānakatvaniyamenātrāpi paratantratvasya samatvāt| bhāsyasyāvidyādhyāsasya bhāsakatayā'pyadhiṣṭhānāpekṣaṇācca| avidyāvacchinnasya caitanyasyāvidyādisakaladvaitadraṣṭatvāt tasyaiva cāntaḥkaraṇāvacchedena pramātṛtvād bhramaprayamayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyopapatterbhramasya samānādhikaraṇapramānivartyatvamupapadyate|
nanu dehendriyādikaṃ vinā kathamantaḥkaraṇādhyāsaḥ? kā'trānupapattiḥ? adhiṣṭhānāparokṣatvaṃ hi aparokṣabhrame kāraṇam| tad yatrādhiṣṭhānaṃ svato nāparokṣaṃ, yathā śuktyādyavacchinnacaitanyaṃ, tatra tadaparokṣatārthaṃ dehendriyādyapekṣā| prakṛte cāvidyāvacchinnaṃ caitanyamadhiṣṭhānam| tatra caitanyasya svaprakāśatvenāvidyāyāśca tadadhyastatvena tenaiva sākṣiṇā aparokṣatvāt kutra dehendriyādyapekṣā?
