athaivaṃ pralaye dehendriyādyabhāve'pyajñānasadbhāvenāntaḥkaraṇādhyāsaprasaṅgaḥ| na, tadā dehendriyādisarjanavilambahetunaiva tadvilambasambhavāt| anyathā tadā dehendriyādikamapi kuto notpadyeta? na ca -- doṣādīnāmadhyastatvena tadabhāvasya tāttvikatvād atāttvikena tāttvikakāryapratibandhasyāyuktatvāt, bauddhena duṣṭatayā kalpitasya vedajanyajñānasyeva kalpitadoṣajanyasya dvaitavijñānasya prāmāṇyāpāta iti vācyam| bauddhakalpitasya prātibhāsikadoṣasya vyāvahārikavedāpekṣayā nyūnasattākatvena tadaprāmāṇyāprayojakatve'pyavidyākhyadoṣadvaitaprapañcayoḥ samasattākatvena kāryakāraṇabhāvaniyamena ca kāraṇībhūtāvidyākhyadoṣābhāve kāryabhūtadvaitaprapañcatadvijñānayorabhāvaniyamena nāvidyāmithyātvena dvaitajñānasatyatāpātaḥ| kāraṇamithyātve kāryamithyātvasyāvaśyakatvāt| brahmajñānetarābādhyatvarūpavyāvahārikatvasya bādhyābādhyasādhāraṇasya mithyātvasiddhyanapekṣatvāt na sattvavibhāgāsiddhiḥ| nanu doṣādīnāṃ rūpyādibhramahetūnāṃ pāramārthikasattvamautsargikaprāmāṇyena siddhamiti paramārthasatāmeva teṣāṃ hetutvamiti cenna| vyāvahārikaprāmāṇyasya sākṣiṇā grahaṇe'pi trikālābādhyatvarūpatāttvikaprāmāṇyaṃ na kenāpi gṛhyata iti pratyakṣabādhoddhāre prāgevābhihitatvāt| na ca -- rūpyādyadhyāse doṣādīnāmadhiṣṭhāna -- samasattākatvaṃ dṛṣṭamiti ihāpi tatheti vācyam| sādharmyasamajātyuttaratvāt| vastutastu sarvatra caitantasyaivādhiṣṭhānatvena kutrāpi doṣādīnāmadhiṣṭhānasamasattākatvābhāvāt| na ca -- bādhakaṃ jñānaṃ satyameva vaktavyam, anyathā bādhaparamparayā anavasthāpatteriti vācyam| vedāntavākyajanyacaramacittavṛtteḥ katakarajonyāyena svaparabādhakatayā'navasthāyā abhāvāt| dṛśyatvamātreṇa yugapatkṛtsnabādhasambhavāt| na hi guhāyāṃ na śabda iti śabdaḥ svaṃ na niṣedhati| anyathā svasya svenāniṣedhe tavāpyanavasthāpattiḥ| śabdamātraniṣedhānubhavavirodhaśca| yadyapi bādhakajñānaṃ vṛttyuparaktacaitanyarūpaṃ svataḥ satyameva| tathāpi tadavacchedikāyā vṛtterdṛśyatvena mithyātvād bādhopapattiḥ|
nanu bandhasyātyantābhāvapratiyogitvarūpamithyātve tadabhāvārthaṃ yatno na syāt| atyantābhāvasyāsādhyatvāt, ata eva na tatpratītyabhāvārthamapi yatnaḥ| tasyā api mithyātvād; anyathā mokṣe'pi bandhapratītyā taddaśāyāmapi prātibhāsikabandhāpātāt| atha pāramārthikatvākāreṇa mithyātvaṃ, svarūpeṇa tu nivṛttireva, na| tasyāḥ svarūpābādhenāpyupapatteriti cenna| satyasya brahmaṇo nivṛttyadarśanena svarūpato mithyātvābhāve nivṛttyayogāt mithyātvaṃ nivṛttyanukūlameva| na ca tadarthaṃ pravṛttyanupapattiḥ, adhiṣṭhānasākṣātkārānantaraṃ tathaiva| tataḥ pūrvaṃ tu kaṇṭhagatavismṛtacāmīkaraprāptaya iva bhramabādhakajñānotpattaye pravṛttyupapatteḥ| atyantābhāvādhikaraṇe ca pratiyogivattannivṛttirapyupapāditaiva| na ca -- traikālikaniṣedhapratiyogini tucche nivṛttirna dṛṣṭeti kathaṃ tādṛśi prapañce sā syāditi vācyam| yathākathaṃcit sajātīye'darśanasyāprayojakatvāt| anyathā anutpanne nivṛttirna dṛṣṭeti prāgabhāvo'pi na nivarteta| tasmāt svabhāvaviśeṣa eva tucchanityavilakṣaṇo nivṛttiprayojaka iti vācyam| sā ca nivṛttiradhikaraṇasvarūpeti pakṣe ghaṭanāśārthaṃ mudgarapātādāviva mananādau pravṛttirūhanīyā| atirikteti pakṣe tvanirvacanīyā, pañcamaprakārā caramavṛttirūpā vā sā, sarvathā janyaiveti na kā'pyanupapattiḥ|
nanu bandhasya brahmaṇyadhyastatve tannididhyāsanasādhyatatsākṣātkāranivartyatvaṃ śravaṇādiniyamādṛṣṭasāpekṣabrahmajñānanivartyatvaṃ ca na syāt| na hi devatānididhyāsanasādhyatatsākṣātkāranivartyaṃ duritaṃ tatrādhyastam| na vā dūragamanādiniyamādṛṣṭasāpekṣasetudarśanavivartyaṃ duritaṃ tatrādhyastamiti cenna| ātmādhyastagauratvādeḥ, śuktyādyadhyastarūpyādeśca tattatsākṣātkāranivartyatvadarśanena prapañcasyāpi brahmaṇyadhyastatayā tatsākṣātkāranivartyatvasyāvaśyakatvāt| na hi śuktyādyadhyastaṃ rūpyādi śuktyādijñānaṃ vinā nivartate| devatādarśanādinā tu prāyaścittasamakakṣyeṇa duritasya kāraṇātmanā'vasthānamātraṃ kriyate, natu śuktijñānena rūpyasveva nivṛttiḥ| adhiṣṭhānājñānarūpopādānakasyāropitasya tannivṛttiṃ vinā nivṛttyayogāt| ajñānanivṛttiścādhiṣṭhānajñānādevetyuktaṃ prāk| śravaṇādiniyamādṛṣṭaṃ ca na muktiṃ prati kāraṇaṃ kintu brahmāparokṣyaṃ prati|
nanu -- avaghātasādhyavaituṣyānyāpūrvasyeva śravaṇādisādhyāparokṣyānyamuktereva tatsādhyatvam| anyathā śravaṇaniyamādṛṣṭasādhye sākṣātkāre śravaṇanirapekṣasyopāyāntarasyāprasaktyā tatprasaktyadhīnaniyamavidhyayogāt| na ca -- parokṣajñānaṃ śravaṇād, aparokṣaṃ tu niyamādṛṣṭāditi yuktam| vaṇādividhau parokṣajñānapravāharūpanididhyāsanasādhyāparokṣasyaiva dṛśinoddeśāt| tvanmate parokṣajñāne kāmanāyā ayogena tasyoddeśyatvāyogācceti| cenna| tatra kratvarthasya niyamāpūrvasya paramāpūrvasādhakatve'pi puruṣārthahiraṇyadhāraṇādiniyamādṛṣṭasya tadabhāvavat śravaṇādisādhyasākṣātkārānyaphalābhāve'pi tenaiva phalavattvopapatteḥ| `sarvāpekṣā ca yajñādiśruteraśvat' iti nyāyāt, `sarvaṃ karmākhilaṃ pārtha! jñāne parisamāpyate' iti smṛteśca| atra sarvākhilapadābhyāṃ karmaśabdavācyāpūrvamātrasya jñāne samāptirdarśitā| mekṣasyāvidyānivṛttirūpasya jñānātiriktā sādhyatvaniyamācca| jñāne samāptirdarśitā| mokṣasyāvidyānivṛttirūpasya jñānātiriktāsādhyatvaniyamācca| jñāne tvasaṃbhāvanādinivṛttyā pratibandhakaduritanivṛttyā ca dṛṣṭādṛṣṭāṃśopayogaḥ| sāmānyapuraskāreṇa ca prasaktasya sādhanāntarasya nivṛttiḥ sarvatra niyamavidheḥ phalam| viśeṣarūpeṇa tvapūrvavidhitvameva| yathā hi `vrīhīnavahanti' ityādāvapūrvasādhanībhūtavrīhivaituṣye (viśiṣya) avaghātātiriktasādhanāntarāprasaktāvapi vrīhivaituṣyamātre prasaktasya nakhavidalanādernivṛttiḥ, viśiṣya kāryakāraṇabhāvabodhanāt, tathā nirviśeṣabrahmātmābhedasākṣātkārapratibandhanivṛttau śravaṇādyatiriktasādhanāntarāprasaktāvapyātmajñānamātrapratibandhavṛttau sāṅkhyādiśāstrasyāpi prasakteḥ, tannivṛttirviśiṣya vedāntavākyavicāravidhānāditi paramagambhīro'yaṃ granthārthaḥ|
nanu -- yadi viśvaṃ kalpitaṃ syāt, tadā `janmādyasya yataḥ' iti sūtre, `yato vā imāni' ityādiśrutau ca janmādyuktiḥ, `īkṣaternāśabdam' iti sūtre, `tadaikṣata' ityādiśrutau ca īśvarasyekṣāpūrvakakartṛtvoktiḥ, `lokavattu līlākaivalyam' iti sūtre, `āptakāmasya kāspṛhā'tyādiśrutau ca prayojanābhāve'pi līlayā sṛṣṭyādyuktiḥ, `vaiṣamyanairghṛṇye na sāpekṣatvāt' iti sūtre `puṇyena puṇyaṃ lokaṃ nyati' ityādiśrutau ca karmasāpekṣatvenāvaiṣamyoktiḥ, `tejo'tastathā hyāha' iti sūtre, `vāyoragniḥ' ityādiśrūtau ca teja ādervāyvādijanyatvoktiḥ, `viparyayeṇa tu kramo'ta upapadyate ca' iti sūtre, `pṛthivyapsu pralīyate' ityādismṛtau ca pṛthivyādīnāmabādhau layoktirityādyayuktaṃ syāt| na hi kalpite tattadvirodhaśaṅkā tannirākaraṇaṃ ca yuktamiti cenna| prapañcasya kalpitasyāpi vyāvahārikasattvābhyupagamena taddaśāyāṃ virodhaśaṅkātatparihārayorucitatvāt| indrajālādāvadhyaste'pyaindrajālikāderīkṣāpūrvaka sraṣṭratvāderdarśanācca| yathā ca kalpitasyāpi janmādyupapattistathā'nirvacanīyavāde vakṣyate| svapne'pi sṛṣṭyādeḥ śrutyā pratipādanācca| adhyastasyāpi sarpasya bhayakampādijanakatvavat vāyvādīnāṃ tejaādijanakatvamapyupapannam| `tadabhidhyānādeva tu talliṅgātsaḥ' iti sūtre ca tattadbhāvāpannasya brahmaṇa eva kāraṇatvābhidhānāt| abādau pṛthivyādilayoktirapi tattadbhāvāpannacaitanye vyākhyeyeti nādhiṣṭhānātirikte layoktiḥ| vaiṣamyanairghṛṇyaprayojanādiśaṅkhāparihārādikaṃ tūpāsanāvasthāyām| bhoktāpatteravibhāgaścetsyāllokavat' iti āpātataḥ pariṇāmavādābhyupagamena| `tadananyatvamārambhaṇaśabdādibhyaḥ' iti tu vivartavāde paramasiddhāntadaśāyāṃ na śaṅkā na cottaram| māyāvina iveśvarasya svapratibimbabhūtajīvabhramayitṛtvena sarvavirodhanirāsopatteḥ|
nanu -- īśvarasyāpi saparikarasya jīvenādhyastatvāt kathaṃ bhramayitṛtvam? na avidyopahitacita evānāderīśvaratvenāntaḥkaraṇopahitajīvakalpitatvāyogāt| jīvakalpitatvapakṣe'pi tādṛgdharmaviśiṣṭatayaiva kalpanena tasya bhramayitṛtvādyupapatteḥ| `parikalpito'pi maraṇāya bhavedurago yathā na tu nabho malinam' iti nyāyāt| nanu na jīvānāṃ vāyvādibhyo'gnyādyutpattiriti bhramo'sti, yaḥ svāpnabhrama iva śruterālambanaṃ syāt| na ca bhrāntiṃ vinā kalpitamasti| na caitadvākyajabhrāntikalpitameva etadvākyālambanam| vedasya bhramajanakatvaprasaṅgāt| anuvāde tu na doṣaḥ| na ceśvara eva tatkalpakaḥ| tasya bhrāntatvaprasaṅgāt| tadabhyupagame'pi na nistāraḥ| bhrānterdehendriyādikāryatvāt| teṣāṃ ca pṛthivyādikāryatvāt| pṛthivyādyutpatteḥ prāk bhrāntyayogāditi cenna| bhrāntimātre dehendriyādyapekṣāyāḥ prāgeva nirāsāt| īśvarādhyastavāyvādihetukāgnyādyutpattyālambanatvena vedasya bhramājanakatvād| adhyastasya cādhyastatvena sphuraṇānna māyāvina iva īśvarasya bhrāntatvaprasaṅgaḥ| na cādhyastatve utpattyādyanupapattiḥ| anadhyastasya kvāpyutpattyādyadarśanenādhyastatvasyaiva tadupapādakatvāt| satkāryavādāsatkāryavādaniṣedhenānirvacanīyakāryavādamātre kāryakāraṇabhāvaparyavasānāt| tadevaṃ kṛtsnasya prapañcasyādvaite brahmaṇi kalpanopapatterna pratikūlatarkaparāhatiḥ|
ityadvaitasiddhau brahmaṇi pratikalatarkanirākaraṇam||
etadanumānam `ekamevādvitīyam' ityādiśrutirapyanugṛhṇāti| nanu śrutyāsvasvarūpasvaprāmāṇyasvayogyatādermithyātvābodhanena pratyakṣādisiddhatatsattvopajīvanena ca brahmetarasakalamithyātvāsiddhiḥ, `sannipātalakṣaṇo vidhiranimittaṃ tadvighātasye'ti nyāyena pratyakṣādisiddhaghaṭādimithyātvāsiddhiśca, yogyatādimithyātvabodhane ca śrutyarthasyātāttvikatvāpattiḥ, śabdabodhyasya śabdatatprāmāṇyayogyatādinā samasattākatvaniyamāt| na ca sadarthe svāpnadevatāvākye vyabhicāraḥ āptatvāpauruṣeyatvāyogena tasya śabdatvena prāmāṇyāyogāt, kiṃ tūpaśrutivattādṛśaśabdajñānaṃ liṅgatvena pramāṇamiti cenna nirdoṣaśabdatvena tasya śabdavidhayaiva prāmāṇyasambhād, āptatvāpauruṣeyatvayordoṣābhāva evopakṣayād vyāptyādyupasthitikalpane gauravād, vaktuḥ ktalpitatve'pi tad‌gatadoṣasyārthasaṃvādena kalpayitumaśakyatvācca| tathā ca śabdasamasattākatvasya vyabhicārād yogyatādisamasattākatvaniyamasiddheraprayojakatvācca, parokṣatvānityatvādyupādhisambhvācca śrutyā yogyatādisakalamithyātvabodhane'pi tadarthasya na mithyātvam, mithyātvaprayojakarūpābhāvāt| mahābhāṣyoktanyāyodāharaṇamapi na yuktam, viṣayavaiṣamyāt| tathā hi -- `śatāni sahasrāṇī'tyatra sarvanāmasthānasaṃjñakaśisannipātena vihito num `ṣṇāntāṣaḍi'ti ṣaṭsaṃjñādvārā `ṣaḍbhyo lugi'ti śiśvarūpasarvanāmasthānasya pañcetyādāviva luṅ‌nimittaṃ na bhavati, tatsannipātenaiva vihitatvāt, tatsadbhāvaniyamenaiva vihitatvādityarthaḥ| aluptasyaiva sarvanāmasthānasya numnimittatvāt, `na lumatāṅgasye'ti lumatā lupte'ṅgakāryaniṣedhāt| tathā cāluptapratyayatvena yatra nimittatā tatra sannipātalakṣaṇanyāyāvatāraḥ| yatra tu `pratyayalope pratyayalakṣaṇami'ti nyāyena lupte'pi pratyaye kāryaṃ bhavati tatrāluptatvaviśeṣaṇanairapekṣyeṇa pratyayatvamātreṇaiva nimittatvāt na sannipātalakṣaṇanyāyāvatāraḥ pratyayasadbhāvasya tatrānupajīvyatvāt| evaṃ sthite yadyamithyābhūtatvena pratyakṣādernimittatā syāt tadā pratyayasyāluptatvena nimittatāyāmiva bhavedetannyāyāvatāraḥ| pratyakṣādestu svarūpeṇaiva nimittatā svapnādyarthasyāpyarthakriyākāritvadarśanena prāgevopapāditā| ato yad bādhyate tāttvikatvaṃ tannopajīvyam, yaccopajīvyamarthakriyāsāmarthyalakṣaṇavyāvahārikaprāmāṇyaṃ tacca na bādhyata iti kiṃ kena saṅgatam? taduktaṃ ṭīkākṛdbhiḥ -- `utpādakāpratidvandvitvā'diti| ata eva jyotiṣṭomādividherupajīvyāgnividyāvadviṣayatveneva dvaitaniṣedhasyāpi svopajīvyayogyatā dītaraviṣayatvena saṅkocasya vā sṛṣṭyādiśruteriva kalpitaviṣayatvasya vopapattau na tāttvikasarvamithyātvakalpanaṃ yuktamiti apāstam, dṛṣṭānte agnividyāderiva dārṣṭāntike yogyatādestāttvikasyānupajīvyatvāt| na hi yogyatā tāttvikayogyatātvena nimittam kiṃ tu yogyatātvenaiva| sakaladvaitābhāvasyādhikaraṇasvarūpatvena tadadhikaraṇasya ca brahmaṇaḥ| `satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma', `tatsatyaṃ sa ātme'tyādiśrutyā satyatvapratipādanāt, na sṛṣṭyādiśruteriva kalpitaviṣayatvopapattiḥ| tasmādyogyatādeḥ mithyātvepi vedānta bodhyaṃ satyameveti sthitam| yathācāvidyātatkāryasya svarūpato niṣedhe'pi tucchavailakṣaṇyaṃ, pāramārthikatvākāreṇa niṣedhe vā pāramārthikatvadharmaśūnyasyāpi brahmaṇaḥ svarūpeṇa sattvaṃ, tathopapāditamadhastāt| nanu -- tattvamasyādivākyena pratyakṣādyavirodhāya tattvaṃpadalakṣitayoraikyamiva mithyātvaśrutyāpi tadavirodhāya pratyakṣādisiddhādanyasyaiva mithyātvaṃ bodhyam, anyathā pratyakṣādyanugrahāya vyāvahārikamapi sattvaṃ na kalpyeta `neha nāne'tyādiniṣedhenātyantāsattvabodhanād iti cenna, viśiṣṭayoraikya viśeṣaṇayorapyaikyāpātena sarvatra viśiṣṭābhedaparavākyasya lakṣitaviśeṣyaikyaparatvaniyamena `tattvamasī'tyatrāpi tathābhyupagamāt| taduktam -- 
aviruddhaviśeṣaṇadvayaprabhavatve'pi viśiṣṭayordvayoḥ|
ghaṭate na yadaikatā tadā natarāṃ tadviparītarūpayoḥ|| iti|
mithyātvabodhakaśrutau tu nāsti pratyakṣādivirodhaḥ, tāttvikatvāṃśasyānupajīvyatvād, vyāvahārikasattvasya copajīvyatvānnātyantāsattvakalpanamityasyāpyuktaprāyatvāt| nanu -- śrutestātparyaṃ caitanyamātre vā? dvitīyābhāvaviśiṣṭe vā? tadupalakṣite vā? nādyaḥ viśvamithyātvāsiddheriṣṭāpatteḥ, tasya svaprakāśatayā nityasiddhatvena śrutivaiyarthyācca| na dvitīyaḥ, akhaṇḍārthatvahānāt| ata eva na tṛtīyaḥ kākavaditivad dvitīyābhāvavadityanenāpi saprakārakajñānajananenākhaṇḍārthatvāyogāt, cinmātrasya nityasiddhatvena tadanyasya ca mumukṣvajñeyatvena kākena saṃsthānaviśeṣasyeva dvitīyābhāvenopalakṣyasyānyasyābhāvāt tasyopalakṣaṇatvāyogācceti cenna, kākasya saṃsthānaviśeṣa iva dvitīyābhāvasya svarūpamevopalakṣṇatvāyogācceti cenna, kākasya saṃsthānaviśeṣa iva dvitīyābhāvasya svarūpamevopalakṣyamityupalakṣyābhāvanibandhanopalakṣaṇatvānupapatterabhāvāt| upalakṣaṇatve hi upalakṣyasattvamātraṃ tantram, na tu tasya svarūpātiriktatvamapi, gauravād, upalakṣyatāvacchedakarūpābhāve'pi svatovyāvṛttajātivadupalakṣyatvasambhavāt| ata eva na saprakārakatvāpattiḥ, kākavadityatrāpyupalakṣaṇasyāprakāratvāt, kiṃ tu svarūpātiriktadharmasya tatropalakṣaṇatvena saprakāratvam, iha tu tanneti vaiṣamyam| na copalakṣaṇavaiyarthyam, anarthanivṛttihetutvena dvitīyābhāvadvārakasvarūpajñānasyoddeśyatvāt, tasya prāgasiddhatvāt| na ca mithyātvāsiddhyeṣṭāpattiḥ, avāntaratātparyasya tatrāpi sattvāt, tad‌dvāraiva svarūpacaitanye mahātātparyāt|
ata eva -- śrutibodhyasya viśeṣaṇasyopalakṣaṇasya vā dvitīyābhāvasya sattve advaitahāniḥ, asatve cādaṇḍe daṇḍīti vākyavat kākahīne kākavaditi vākyavaccādvaitavākyasyātattvāvedakatvāpattiriti nirastam| ādye dvitīyābhāvasattvena dvitīyābhāvāsiddhyāpādanasyānucitatvād, abhāvasyādhikaraṇātirekānabhyupagamācca| dvitīye tu sṛṣṭyādivākyavadupalakṣyasvarūpasatyatvamādāya tattvāvedakatvāt, mukhyatātparyaviṣayasyāsatyatāyāmevātattvāvedakatvābhyupagamāt| ata eva mahātātparyābhiprāyeṇa caitanyamātre tātparyamityādyapakṣe'pi na doṣaḥ, avāntaratātparyeṇa mithyātvasiddherapi svīkāreṇeṣṭāpatterapyasambhavāt|
nanu dvitīyābhāve mahātātparyābhāvaḥ kiṃ pramāṇāntaraprāptyā, yathā vāyukṣepiṣṭhatvādau? uta tadvirodhitvena yathātmavapotkhananādau? utoddeśyaviśeṣaṇatvādinā yathā grahaikatvādau? nādyaḥ, tvayaiva dvitīyābhāvasya pramāṇāntaraprāptyanabhyupagamāt| dvitīye'pi virodhimānaṃ na tāvatpratyakṣādi dvaitagrāhi tvanmate tasyaiva śrutibādhyatvāt nādvaitavākyāntaram tasyātmamātraparatve dvitīyābhāvavirodhitvāt, na hi viśeṣyaviṣayaṃ `agnihotraṃ juhotī'ti vākyaṃ viśiṣṭaviṣayeṇa `daghnā juhotī'ti vākyena virudhyate dvaitābhāvaparatve tvekaviṣayatvena sutarāmavirodhāt| nāpi tṛtīyaḥ, `grahaṃ saṃmārṣṭī'tyatra saṃmārjanasyevākhaṇḍārthapare vākye vidheyāntarasyābhāvena viśeṣyasya śāstragamyasya cinmātrasyāprāptatvenoddeśyatvāyogācca dvitīyābhāvasyoddeśyaviśeṣaṇatvānupapatteḥ, avivakṣāhetoranuvādyatvasyāpyabhāvācceti cenna, svayameva svabodhitamapi dvitīyābhāvaṃ dvitīyatvādeva niṣedhatīti svavirodhādeva śrutestatrātātparyāt| mānavirodhitvamātrasya tātparyābhāve prayojakatvāt svavirodhe'pi na kṣatiḥ|
nanu -- ekenaiva pramāṇenaikasya prāptiniṣedhāvanupapannau na, rūpabhedenāvirodhāt| dvitīyābhāvasvarūpaṃ hi śāstreṇa prāpyate| tasya ca prāpyatāvacchedakarūpaṃ dvitīyābhāvatvam tacca na niṣedhyatāvacchedakam kiṃ tu dvitīyatvameva niṣedhyamātrānugatam| tatra tadanabhyupagame tu na tasya niṣedhyatvam na vā tenātmanaḥ sadvitīyatvāpattiriti na ko'pi doṣaḥ| yatra tu prāpyatāvacchedakameva niṣedhyatāvacchedakaṃ tatra prāptiniṣedhaśāstrayoratulyaviṣayatve'pi viśeṣaśāstraviṣayaparityāgena sāmānyaśāstrapravṛttiḥ tulyaviṣayatvetvagatyā vikalpa iti na niṣedhasyāsaṅkocena pravṛttiḥ| yathā `na hiṃsyātsarvā bhūtānī'ti niṣedhaśāstrasya `agnīṣomīyaṃ paśumālabhete'tyādiprāptiśāstraviṣayetaraviṣayatvaṃ, `atirātre ṣoḍaśinaṃ gṛhṇāti' `nātirātre ṣoḍaśinaṃ gṛhṇātī' tyādiprāptinaṣedhaśāstrayostu vikalpenaikaviṣayatvam, ekasyaiva hiṃsātvasya ṣoḍaśigrahatvasya ca prāptiniṣedhayoravacchedakatvāt tatra niṣedhaśāstrasyāsaṃkucad‌vṛttitve prāptiśāstrasya sarvātmanā vaiyarthyāpattiḥ prakṛte ca dvitīyatvena rūpeṇa niṣedhasyaiva śāstrārthatvānna kasyāpi vaiyarthyaśaṅkā| ata eva dvitīṃyābhāvaniṣedhe punardvitīyonmajjanāpattiriti nirastam| upapāditametat mithyātvamithyātvasādhane| yathā pratiyogyabhāvayorniṣedhyatāvacchedakaikye naikaniṣedhe'parasattvāttiriti| na ca svenaiva niṣiddhasya dvitīyābhāvasya dvitīyasyaiva viśeṣaṇatvenopalakṣaṇatvena vā punarupādānaṃ na yuktamiti vācyam, abhāvabuddhau niṣiddhasyāpi pratiyoginaḥ `sā śukti' rityatra pratiṣiddhasyāpi pūrvapratītarajatasyopalakṣaṇatayopādānadarśanād, asaṅkīrṇajñānaprayojakatvasya prakṛte'pi tulyatvāt| tasmāt `ekamevādvitīya' mityādiśrutirviśvamithyātve pramāṇamiti siddham|
ityadvaitasiddhau sāmānyena mithyātvaśrutyupapattiḥ|
nanu -- āpātapratipanna eva na tāvat śrutyarthaḥ, `kaśchandasāṃ yogamāveda dhīra' iti śrutyā `bibhetyalpaśrutādveda' iti smṛtyā ca vedārthasyātigahanatokteḥ, mīmāṃsāvaiyarthyaprasaṅgācca, kintu mānāntareṇa pūrvottareṇa cāviruddha evārthaḥ, avirodhagrahaṇārthaṃ ca mīmāṃsāsāphalyam, ata eva `ājyaiḥ stuvate' `ākāśādeva samutpadyanta' ityādāvāpātapratītaghṛtagaganādiparityāgenājyākāśādipadānāṃ sāmaparamātmādyarthatvaṃ sthāpitaṃ pūrvottaramīmāṃsayościtrākāśādyadhikaraṇeṣu, anyathā tattatpūrvapakṣābhyupagamāpatteḥ, tathā coktaṃ vārtikakāraiḥ śāstraṃ śabdavijñānādasannikṛṣṭe'rthe vijñānamityatra -- 
asannikṛṣṭavācā ca dvayamatra jihāsitam|
tādrūpyeṇa paricchedastadviparyayato'pi ca||
viṣayāviṣayau jñātvā tenotsargāpavādayoḥ|
bādhābādhau vivektavyau na tu sāmānyadarśanāt||
anya evaikadeśena śāstrasyārthaḥ pratīyate|
anyastu paripūrṇena samastāṅgopasaṃhṛtau|| iti|
anyatrāpyuktam -- 
viruddhavatpratīyanta āgamā yatra ye mithaḥ|
tatra dṛṣṭānusāreṇa teṣāmarthā vivakṣitāḥ|| iti|
tathā ca pratyakṣādivirodhāt pūrvottaravirodhācca nādvaitaparatvamekamevetyādivākyānāmiti cenna| dvaitapratyakṣasya candraprādeśikatvapratyakṣavat sambhāvitāprāmāṇyatayā advaitaśrutivirodhitvābhāvāt| yathā ca śrutyā pratyakṣaṃ bādhyate, tathā prapañcitamadhastāt| kiṃ ca pratyakṣaṃ niyataviṣayam, śrutiḥ sarvaviṣayā, tathā ca yatra pratyakṣeṇa bhedo na gṛhītaḥ, tatraivābhedaśruteravakāśaḥ| nanu yayoraikyaṃ śrutyā bodhyate tayorbhedaḥ prasakto na vā? nāntyaḥ, aprasaktapratiṣedhāpātāt, nādyaḥ, prasañjakapramāṇavirodhenaikyasya bodhayitumaśakyatvāditi cenna, antyapakṣābhyupagame doṣābhāvāt| aprasaktapratiṣedha iti ca kimaprasiddhapratiyogitvam? kiṃ vā niṣprayojanatvamiti vivecanīyam| nādyaḥ, anyatra prasiddhasyaiva bhedasya bhedatvenopasthitasya parasparapratiyogyanuyogibhāvenānyatra niṣedhasambhavāt| na ca tatraiva prasiddhistantram, niṣedhapramāmātrocchedaprasaṅgāt| na dvitīyaḥ anarthanivṛttereva prayojanatvāt, `nāntarikṣe'gniścetavya' ityādau stutimātraprayojanenāpyaprasaktaniṣedhadarśanācca|
atha śrutyā yayorabhedo bodhyate tayorupasthitirasti? na vā? nāntyaḥ, anupasthitayorabhedabodhanāyogāt| ādye sā kiṃ śrutijanyā, pratyakṣādijanyā vā| nādyaḥ| śrutermānāntarāgocarābhedamātraparatvena ghaṭādyupasthitestajjanyatvābhāvena sarvādvaitāsiddheḥ, śrutisthakiñcanetyādipadānāmanuvādakatvābhyupagamāt| dvitīye tu tayorbhedo'pi pratyakṣādisiddha iti kvādvaitaśrutyavakāśaḥ? maivam, yat pratyakṣādinā gṛhyate, tadbhedo'pi tena gṛhyata eveti niyamābhāvāt| tathā hi -- na tāvatpadārthasvarūpajñānameva bhedajñānam, abhedabhramocchedaprasaṅgāt| svarūpabhedavādināmapi svarūpajñānād ghaṭatvādiprakārakāt bhedatvaprakārakaṃ bhedajñānaṃ vilakṣaṇameva, anyathā bhedāgrahanibandhanavyavahārānudayaprasaṅgāt| ata eva svarūpajñānottarakālamavaśyaṃ bhedajñānamityapi na, anavasthāprasaṅgācca| tathā hi -- `ghaṭapaṭau bhinnau jānāmī'ti ghaṭapaṭabhedadhīḥ svaprakāśā vā, anuvyavasāyasiddhā vā, sākṣisiddhā, vā, na svapratiyogikabhedaviṣayā, bhedadhiyaḥ pratiyogidhījanyatvaniyamena pratiyogidhīvyaktibhinnavyaktitvāvaśyakatvāt, svasyā eva svajanyatvānupapatteḥ| jñānāntareṇa ca tadbhedagrahe kvacid bhedadhīdhārāviśrāntiravaśyaṃ vācyā, anyathā suṣuptiviṣayāntarasañcārādikaṃ na syāt| ataḥ tatrāpi caramabhedadhīrevodāharaṇam| tathā ca bādhakatvābhimatā yā ghaṭapaṭabhedadhīḥ svabhedāviṣayā bhāsate tayā saha bādhyatvābhimatāyā aikyadhiya aikyaṃ bodhayitvā nirbādhā satī śrutiḥ sarvābhede paryavasyati| na hyabhede'pi bādhyabādhakabhāva svasyāpi svabādhakatāpatteḥ| taduktaṃ khaṇḍanakṛdbhiḥ|
sudūradhāvanaśrāntā bādhabuddhiparamparā|
nivṛttāvadvayāmnāyaiḥ pārṣṇigrāhairvijīyate|| iti|
na ca -- siddhānte ghaṭataddhībhedagrāhiṇā svaprakāśena sākṣiṇā svasminnitarabhedasyāpi grahaṇānnānavasthā, anyathā svasya ghaṭādibhyo'bhedasaṃśayaḥ syāditi vācyam, sākṣiṇaḥ svaprakāśatve'pi svaniṣṭhetarapratiyogikabhedagrahe itarapratiyogyupasthitisāpekṣatvāt| anyathā svasyāntaḥkaraṇādyabhedabhramo na syāt| svaprakāśena bhedāgrahe'pi mānāntareṇa bhedagrahāt na ghaṭādyabhedasaṃśayaḥ iti na kiñcidetat| syādetat -- `ghaṭapaṭau bhinnā'viti pratyakṣaṃ svasyādvaitajñānādinā bhedaṃ vinānupapattestamapyākṣipatīti sarvatra bhedasyāpratyakṣatve'pi nādvaitaśruteravakāśaḥ| atrocyate -- ākṣepo hi anumānamarthāpattirvā? tatra vivādādhyāsitā buddhiḥ sarvato bhinneti nānumānaṃ sambhavati, svato'pi bhedasādhane bādhād, dṛṣṭāntasya ca sādhyavikalatvāt| yataḥkutaścid bhedasādhane tvanumānāviṣaye labdhāvakāśā śrutirabhedaṃ bodhayiṣyati| na ca svavyatiriktāt sarvato bhinneti sādhyam, advaitavādinaṃ pratyaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt| etena -- sarvaṃ sarvasmādbhinnamiti vākyamapi nirastam, taduktam -- 
hetvādyabhāvasārvajñye sarvaṃ pakṣayatā''sthite|
kiñcittu tyajatā dattā saiva dvāradvayaśruteḥ|| iti|
nāpyarthāpattiḥ sarvabhedaviṣayā, svāviyatvāt| yayorhi bhedaṃ vinā yatrānupapattirgṛhītā, tayostatra bhedagrahe'pyanupapattāvanupapattyantarāgrahaṇāt| sarvatra tad‌grahaṇe tu dhārāviśrāntau caramadhīrudāharaṇam| tadukm -- 
ādyadhīvedyabhedīyāpyanyā'nupapannatā|
svajñānāpekṣaṇādante bādhate nādvayaśrutim|| iti|
nanu yāvadupapādakaṃ tatsarvamarthāpatteviṣayaḥ, na tu yatkiñcidupapādakam, tathā cārthāpatteritarasmād bhedābhāve tatraivābhedaśruterlabdhāvakāśatvād ghaṭapaṭabhedāsiddhyāpatterarthāpattibhedasyāpi ghaṭapaṭabhedopapādakatvenārthāpattiviṣayatvaṃ vācyam, anyathā dṛgdṛśyasambandhānupapattirjñānanivartyatvānupapaṃttiśca svamithyātvaviṣayā na syāt, `sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahme'ti śrutiḥ `neha nāne'ti brahmaṇi bhedamātraniṣedhānupapattiśca svābhedaviṣayā na syāt, tathā ca tatrāpi śrutyantaramarthāpattyantaraṃ vā vācyamiti tavāpyanavasthāpattiḥ iti| maivaṃ vocaḥ, vastuta upapādakatvaṃ nārthāpattiviṣayatve tantram, kitūpapādakatvena jñātatvam, anyathā arthāpattibhramānupapatteḥ| tathā ca yena rūpeṇopapādakatvaṃ gṛhītaṃ, tadrūpāvacchinnamupapādakamarthāpatterviṣayaḥ| tatra yadyarthāpattigatabhedasādhāraṇamupapādakatāvacchedakamekaṃ bhavet tadā so'pi bhāyādeva| na caivamasti tadanirūpaṇāt| tathā hi -- ghaṭapaṭabhinnatvamupapādyam, tadupapādakaṃ ca na sarvabhinnatvam, svato'pi bhedāpattyā tadasambhavāt| nāpi svātiriktasarvabhinnatvam; advaitavādinaṃ prati svātirekaviśeṣaṇāsiddheḥ, svatvānanugamācca| tathā ca tena tena rūpeṇa tattadbhinnatvameva upapādakamupeyam| ata upapādakatāvacchedakanānātvānna sarvamupapādakamarthāpatterviṣaya iti pṛthakpṛthaganupapattijñānāpekṣāyāṃ sarvatrānupapattijñāne anavasthānāt kvaciddhārāviśrāntau tatraiva labdhāvakāśā śrutiḥ sarvādvaite paryavasyatīti kimanupapannam? dṛṣṭānte ca sarvatra svasādhāraṇamupapādakatāvacchedakamekameveti tadavacchinnatayā svasyāpi bhānamiti vaiṣamyam| tathā hi -- dṛśyatvāvacchinnamithyātvaṃ vinā dṛksambandhānupapattigrahāttadavacchinnamithyātvamarthāpatterviṣaya iti svamithyātvamapi svaviṣayaḥ, evameva jñānanivartyatvānupapatterapi svaviṣayatvam, tatrāpi dṛśyatvāderekasyaivāvacchedakatvāt| evaṃ ca brahmaṇi sarvābhedabodhikāyāḥ śruterbhedamātraniṣedhānyathānupapatteśca svābhedaviṣayatvamaviruddham| na hi sarvabhede svabhedāpattiriva sarvābhede svābhede doṣāya| tasmādadvaitaśrutirbādhyabādhakayoraikyabodhanena nirābādhā sarvādvaitaṃ pratipādayati|
nanu -- śabdabuddhikarmaṇāṃ viramya vyāpārābhāvāt kathamādāvalpaviṣayā buddhiḥpaścād bahuviṣayā bhavatītyucyata iti cenna, śrutito drāgeva jātāyāḥ sarvaviṣayāyā advaitabuddheḥ prāmāṇyaṃ vyasthāpayantīnāmasmad‌buddhīnāmeva krameṇa jāyamānatvāt| ayogyatājñānaṃ ca na śābdabodhe pratibandhakam, na vā yogyatājñānaṃ hetuḥ, yena prathamaṃ sarvādvaitabuddhirna syāt| taduktam -- 
atyantāsatyapi jñānamarthe śabdaḥ karoti hi|
abādhāttu pramāmatra svataḥ prāmāṇyaniścalām|| iti|
vedāntakalpalatikāyāmasyārthasya prapañco draṣṭavyaḥ| etena -- caramajñānamithyātve'pi na tadviṣayasya mithyātvam, jñānamithyātvasya viṣayamithyātvāsādhakatvāt, advaitajñāne vyabhicārāditi nirastam, śrutyaiva dvaitamātraniṣedhyatvabodhānāt| advaitajñānaviṣaye ca mithyātvabodhakābhāvādeva satyam, na tu jñānamithyātvāditi na kiñcidetat| nanu dvaitajñānādvaitajñānayorabhede kathaṃ bādhyabādhakabhāvaḥ? na ca vyāvahārikabhedamātreṇa saḥ, dvaitajñānasyāpi bādhakatvāpatteḥ iti cenna, vyāvahārikabhedamātrasya bādhakatvāprayojakatvāt| yadvi parīkṣitapramāṇabhāvatvena balavat, tad‌ bādhakam, yattu sandigdhapramāṇabhāvatvena durbalaṃ tad‌ bādhyamiti vyavasthāyāṃ dvaitajñānasya durbalatvenābādhakatvasyādvaitajñānasya ca balavattvena bādhakatvasya śabdapratyakṣabalābalavicāre darśitatvāt| yattu -- `āpo vā idaṃ sarvaṃ bhūta' mityādiśrutiḥ `vimataṃ jalābhinnaṃ pratītatvāt jalavadi'tyanumānaṃ vā svabādhakasya jalābhedaṃ gṛhītvā nirbādhaṃ sat tvaduktanyāyena sarvasya jalābhedaṃ bodhayed -- iti, tanna, jalābhedabodhane'pi bādhyabādhakayoraikyābodhanād bādhakasya bādhakatvopapatteḥ, aikyajñānabhedajñānayorbādyabādhakabhāvasya jalābhedajñānenānapāyāt| bādhakābhedo hi bādhakatvābhāve prayojakaḥ, bādhakasya svabādhakatvādarśanāt| ato na bādhyabādhakaikyajñānasya jalābhedajñānasāmyam| etena -- pūrvaṃ sarvasmādbhinnamiti madvākyamadvaitavākyatajjñānatadviṣayāṇāṃ tebhyo bhedamādau gṛhītvā nirbādhaṃ satsarvabhede paryavasyatīti nirastam, bādhyabādhakayorabhede bādhakatvābhāvavat bhede'pi bādhakatvaṃ na syādityatra hetvabhāvāt pūrvoktadoṣācceti dik|
sarvāsattvaṃ sarvamithyātvānnātiricyate, ataḥ `sarvamasadi'ti pratyavasthānamanavakāśam| nanu śrutyā sarvasya mithyātvaṃ vā bodhyate, brahmābhinnatvaṃ vā| ādye `sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahme'ti sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ na syāt, satyānṛtayoraikyāyogāt| dvitīye `idaṃ rajataṃ' `gauro'ha' mityādibhramāṇāṃ pramātvaṃ syāt, ātmani dehādibhedasyānṛtādivyāvṛtteśca bodhakānāṃ vedāntānāṃ `nedaṃ rajata'mityādibādhakasya cāprāmāṇyaṃ syāt, ghaṭajñānenaiva tadabhinnabrahmatadabhedādeḥ sarvasyāpi vastuto jñānatvena sārvajñyam, vedāntānāṃ vaiyarthyam, sadyo mokṣaśca syāt, sukhaduḥkhabandhamokṣabhedā'bhedadūṣaṇabhūṣaṇajayaparājayabhrāntipramāderapi vastuto bhedābhāvena sarvasaṅkarāpattyā svakriyāsvanyāyasvavacanavirodhāśca syuriti cenna, ādye `mṛd ghaṭaḥ' `idaṃ rajata'mityādāviva upādānopādeyabhāvenāpi sāmānādhikaraṇyopapatteḥ| dvitīye vastuto bhedābhāve'pi āvidyakabhedamādāya sarvavyavasthopapatteḥ| na ca bhedasyāpyanāvidyakabrahmābhinnatvenāvidyakatvāyoga iti -- vācyam āvidyakatvasyāpyāvidyakasyaivāṅgīkāt, athāvidyakatvasyāpi brahmābhinnatvāt kathamāvidyakatvamiti cet, tasminnapi tasya kalpitatvāditi gṛhāṇa|
nanu -- muktāvāvidyakasyāpi bhedasyābhāvenānandasya duḥkhābhinnatvenāpuruṣārthatvāpātaḥ, tattadasādhāraṇasvabhāvasya tatra tatrābhāve'pi tattadabhede pāribhāṣiko'yamabhedo bhede paryavasyed, asādhāraṇarūpeṇa bhedamabhyupetya sadrūpeṇa bhedaniṣedhe'pi iṣṭāpattiraprasaktaniṣedhaśceti cenna, ekasyāmeva brahmavyaktau tattadasādhāraṇasvabhāvānāṃ kalpitatvenāsattvāt sarvakalpanāniṣedhakāle kalpitadharmāvacchinnabhedābhedādiprasaktareyogāt| ata eva nāprasaktapratiṣedha iṣṭāpattirvā, `sad dravyaṃ' `san guṇa' ityādipratītyā prasaktānāṃ tattaddharmāṇāṃ brahmaṇi pratiṣedhāt| ataḥ sarvadharmaśūnyāyā ekasyā eva sad‌vyakteścidānandarūpāyāḥ pratipādanānna pāribhāṣiko'yamabheda iti siddham| tadevaṃ `sarvaṃ brahmābhinnami'ti mate mithyābhūtasya brahmabhede'pi sanmātrameva brahmābhinnamiti mate vā na pratyakṣādivirodhaḥ nāpi pūrvottaravirodhaḥ||
ityadvaitaśruterbādhoddhāraḥ|
nanu -- yadyapi salila eko draṣṭā advaita ityatra salilaśabdasya tatsādṛśyasvacchatvamātraparatvāt tasya ca sarvamalāsaṃsargitvasvarūpasyādvaite'pyupapatteḥ, `sadeva somyedamagra āsī'dityatra cāgrapadasya `tadaikṣata nāmarūpe vyākaro'dityādeśca kālekṣaṇanāmarūpātmakaprapañcaprāpakasyāvidyakadvaitaviṣayakatvena vāstavādvaitavirodhitvābhāvaḥ tathāpi `sadeva sobhyedamagra āsī'dityanena idaṃ śabdoditasya viśvasya sadabhedena sattvamuktvā punaradvitīyapadena tanniṣedhe vyāghātaḥ| na hi `sadāsī'dityasyāsadāsīdityartha iti cenna, sadvyatirekeṇa nāsīdityarthasyaiva niṣedhārthatvāt| vivṛtaṃ caitad bhāṣyakārādibhirārambhaṇādhikaraṇe| na ca -- sad‌vyatirekeṇāsattvoktau sadātmanā satyatvamāgacchatīti vācyam| āgacchatu nāma| ko hi brahmābhinnasyāsattvasādhanāya pravṛttau yo bibhīyāt| advaitavākyasya ca ṣaḍ‌vidhatātparyaliṅgavattayā balavattvenāvidyakadvaitapratipādakatvaṃ sṛṣṭyādivākyānāmiti śravaṇasvarūpanirūpaṇe vedāntakalpalatikāyāmabhihitamasmābhiḥ, ihāpyabhidhāsyate ṣaḍ‌vidhatātparyaliṅgāni pradarśayadbhiḥ| ata ekavijñānena sarvavijñānapratijñayopakramāt `etadātmyamidaṃ sarvaṃ tatsatyaṃ sa ātmā tattvamasī'tyupasaṃhārācca advaitasyaiva mahāprākaraṇikatayā tadanusāreṇa tadvākyasthapadānāṃ vyākhyeyatvāvadhāraṇāt, nānārthapadānāmarthāntaropasthāpakatvasambhave'pi prakṛtavākyārthānanvayitayā tatparityāgena prakṛtavākyārthānukūlapadārthopasthitiparatvamevāstheyam| tatra na dvitīyamadvitīyamiti tatpuruṣābhyupagame na dvitīyam kiṃtu prathamaṃ tṛtīyaṃ vetyarthaḥ syāt, sa ca na sambhavati tayorapi kiñcidapekṣya dvitīyatvād| ato na vidyate dvitīyaṃ yatreti bahuvrīhirevādaraṇīyaḥ| na ca ekenaivādvitīyapadena bhedatrayaniṣedhasambhave ekāvadhāraṇapadayorvaiyarthyamiti vācyam| vijātīyaṃ kiñcidapekṣya dvitīyatvāvacchinnaniṣedhasyādvitīyaśabdārthatvāt| ayaṃ cātra saṅkoco balīvardapadasannidhānād gopada iva sajātīyasvagatabhedaniṣedhakaikāvadhāraṇapadasannidhiprayukta eva| taduktam -- 
vṛkṣasya svagato bhedaḥ patrapuṣpapalādibhiḥ|
vṛkṣāntarātsajātīyo vijātīyaḥ śilāditaḥ||
tathā sadvastuno bhedatrayaṃ prāptaṃ nivāryate|
ekāvadhāraṇadvaitapratiṣedhaistribhiḥ kramāt|| iti|
svagatabhedaḥ nānātvarūpajīveśvarabhedaḥ| sajātīyabhedo'tra dravyatvādinā sajātīyapṛthivyādibhedaḥ| vijātīyabhedo guṇādibhedaḥ| athavā jaḍabhedo vijātīyabhedaḥ| caitanyabhedaḥ sajātīyabhedaḥ| jñānānandādidharmabhedaḥ svagatabhedaḥ| yadi ca "asya gordvitīyo'nveṣṭavya ityukte gaureva dvitīyo'nviṣyate, nāśvo na gardabha" iti mahābhāṣyānusārāt samānajātīyadvitīyaparatvaṃ dvitīyaśabdasya tadā advitīyaśabdasya sajātīyabhedaniṣedhaparatvam, vijātīyasvagatabhedaniṣedhaparatvaṃ tu ekāvadhāraṇapadayoryatheṣṭaṃ vyākhyeyam| athavā advitīyapadenaiva bhedatrayaniṣedhaḥ, ekāvadhāraṇapade tu saṅkocaśaṅkāparihārāya|
yattu kenacit pralapitaṃ -- dvitīyaśabdaḥ sahāyavācī, `asidvitīyo'nusasārapāṇḍava'miti prayogāt, `asidvitīyaḥ asisahāyaḥ' iti mahāmāṣyokteśca, tathā cādvitīyamasahāyamityartho'stu, evamekaśabdasyāpi nānārthatvenāviruddhārthamādāyopapattau na mithyātvaparyavasāyitā''stheyā| tathā ca `eke mukhyānyakevalā' ityamaraḥ, `ekaśabdo'yamanyapradhānāsahāyasaṅkhayāprathamasamānavācī'ti `eko gotra' iti sūtre kaiyaṭaḥ| `ṣṇāntāṣaḍi'ti sūtre mahābhāṣyakāro'pi ekaśabdo'yaṃ bahvarthaḥ, asti saṅkhayārthaḥ, astyasahāyavācī, astyanyārthaḥ ityādi vyākhyātavān| tathā ca jīvādibhyo'nyatvaṃ prādhānyaṃ vā ekaśabdārtho'stu| evamanyānyapi śrutipadāni vyākhyeyāni iti| tat pūrvoktayuktibhirapāstam| vistareṇa ca vakṣyate tātparyanirūpaṇe| tadevaṃ sadrūpe brahmaṇi padatrayeṇa bhedatrayaniṣedhāt tadbhinnamithyātve paryavasitaṃ `ekamevādvitīya'ti vākyam| evamanyā api śrutayaḥ smṛtayaśca granthavistarabhayānnodāhṛtāḥ| svayameva sūribhirākāre draṣṭavyāḥ||
ityadvaitasiddhau sarvādvaitaśruteḥ advaitatātparyakatvanirṇayaḥ|
`tarati śokamātmavit', `tathā vidvān nāmarūpādvimuktaḥ', `bhidyate hṛdayagranthiśchidyante sarvasaṃśayāḥ| kṣīyante cāsya karmāṇi tasmin dṛṣṭe parāvare||' ityādiśrutismṛtibodhitajñānanivartyatvānyathānupapattirapi bandhamithyātve pramāṇam| satyatve brahmavadanivartyatvāpatteḥ| tathā hi -- śuktirūpyarajjusarpādau jñānanivartyatve na tāvat tattadrūpavattvaṃ jñānanivartyatāvacchedakam| ananugamāt| kintu sarvānugataṃ mithyātvamevājñānakalpitatvāparaparyāyamavacchedakam, evaṃ jñānasyāpi tannivartakatve na| śuktyādiviṣayatvamacchedakam| ananugamāt| kintu sarvānugatamadhiṣṭhānapramātvameva| tathā ca yatra jñānasyādhiṣṭhānapramātvena nivartakatā, tatra mithyātvenaiva nivartyateti niyamaḥ sidhyati| etādṛśaniyamānabhyupagame cānantaniyamakalpanāgauravarūpo bādhakastarkaḥ| tathā hi -- yanniṣṭhā yadākārā pramārūpā'ntaḥkaraṇavṛttirudeti, tanniṣṭhaṃ tadākāramajñānaṃ nāśayatīti niyamasya siddhatvāt| upādānanāśasya copādeyanivartakatvāt śuktyādijñānena tattadākārājñānanāśe tadupādeyānāṃ rajatādīnāṃ nivṛttiraucityāvarjitaiveti niyamāntarākalpanena lāghavamanukūlastarko'smatpakṣe| ajñānopādeyatvaṃ ca śuktirajatādīnāmanvayavyatirekasiddhamagre sthāsyati| evaṃ sthite kṛtsnasyāpi prapañcasyātmapramānivartyatve tadajñānakalpitatvameva tatrāvacchedakaṃ kalpyate| na tvananugatamākāśatvādi| na vā brahmabhinnatvaṃ sadbhinnatvaṃ vā sarvānugatamapi| tucche'tiprasakteḥ| tadvārakaviśeṣaṇaprakṣepe tu sadasadvilakṣaṇatvarūpamithyātvameva nivartyatāprayojakaṃ paryavasitam| anyathā niyamāntarakalpanāgauravāpatteḥ| tathā ca śuktyādijñanasya yena rūpeṇa nivartakatvaṃ, tena rūpeṇātmajñānasya nivartakatvam| rūpyādau yena rūpeṇa nivartyatvaṃ, prapañce tadrūpaṃ vinā'nupapadyamānaṃ svopapādakatayā tatra tat kalpayatīti siddhaṃ mithyātvam|
nanu bhavedetadevam, yadyātmajñānasya prapañce nivartye śuktyādijñānasādhāraṇamadhiṣṭhānapramātvamevāvacchedakamityatra kiñcinmānaṃ bhaved| rūpāntareṇāpi nivartakatvasambhavāt| śrutistu dvaitaprapañcasyādvitīyātmajñānaṃ nivartakamityetāvanmātre pramāṇam| na tvavacchedakaviśeṣe'pi| na ca jñānanivartyatāmātrānmithyātvasiddhiḥ, setudarśanādinivartyaduritādiṣu vyabhicārāt| tatra vihitakriyātvādinā nivartakatvānna vyabhicāra iti cet, prakṛte'pi rūpāntaraṃ nāvacchedakamiti kuto niraṇāyi? jñānasya hi svaprāgabhāvaṃ prati pratiyogitvena nivartakatā, pūrvajñānādikaṃ prati tu uttaravirodhiguṇatvena, saṃskāraṃ prati phalatvena, rāgādikaṃ prati viṣayadoṣadarśanatvena, viṣaṃ prati garuḍadhyānatvena, setvādidarśanasya duritaṃ prati vihitakriyātvena| evaṃ ca mithyātvaṃ vināpi jñānanivartyatvadarśanāt na tanmithyātvasya sādhakam| udāhṛteṣvapi satyatvāsaṃpratipattyā mithyātvamevāstīti cet, astu vā māstu| jñānanivartyatvamātraṃ tu na tasya sādhakamiti brūmaḥ| hetvantareṇa siddhau caitadupanyāso vyarthaḥ| śuktirūpyādau kathamiti cecchṛṇu| adhiṣṭhānajñānatvena tatra jñānasya nivartakatvāt| adhiṣṭhānajñānatvaṃ hi ajñānanāśakajñānatvaṃ vā, ajñānasamānaviṣayakapramātvaṃ veti tena rūpeṇa nivartakatve mānābhāvāt nātmājñānopādānakatvarūpamityātvasiddhiḥ prapañcasyeti prāptam| atrocyate| ātmajñānasyāpyadhiṣṭhānajñānatvenaiva prapañcaṃ prati nivartakatvam| prakārāntarāsambhavāt| tathā hi -- pratiyogitvaṃ tāvannāvacchedakam| prapañcasya bhāvarūpatvāt| jñānasya prāgabhāvanivṛttirūpatvena pratiyogitvena prāgabhāvanivartakatvāsiddheśca| nāpyuttaraguṇatvam| ākāśāderātmaviśeṣaguṇatvābhāvād| icchāderapi prapañcanivartakatvāpātācca| nāpi phalatvam| saṃskārasya smaraṇajanakatvavadākāśāderātmajñānajanakatvābhāvāt| saṃskārasya smṛtyanāśyatvenodāharaṇāsiddheśca| viṣayadoṣadarśasya tu rāgādinivartakatvaṃ rāgādikāraṇībhūtabalavadaniṣṭānanubandhīṣṭasādhanatvabhramarūpatatkāraṇanivartakatveneti na prakṛtodāharaṇādatiricyate| śuktirūpyatulyatvāt| garuḍadhyānaṃ tu na pratyudāharaṇam| dhyānasya rāgāderiva jñānatvānabhyupagamāt| jñānasyecchānadhīnatvena tadadhīnajñānāpekṣayā vailakṣaṇyāt| spaṣṭaṃ caitadākare| jñānatve'pi tasya setudarśanapakṣānnātirekaḥ, śāstravihitatvāviśeṣāt| kevalaṃ setvādidarśanavadvihitakriyātvamavaśiṣyate| tacca na sambhavati| jñānasya kartumakartumaśakyatvena vidheyatvāyogāt| vistareṇa ca jñāne vidhirākareṣu nirākṛtaḥ| nirākariṣyate cehāpi| setudarśane kathamiti cet? viśiṣṭākāreṇa viṣeyatvopapatteḥ| na hi setudarśanamātrasya duritanāśakatvam| tatratyamlecchānāmapi duritanāśaprasaṅgāt| kintu pararāṣṭrādupasthānādipūrvakavratakalāpaviśiṣṭasya| tathā ca chatrapādukādivarjanadoṣoddhoṣaṇadūradeśagāmitvabhikṣābhojitvādiniyamānāṃ kṛtisādhyatvāt tadviśiṣṭaṃ setudarśanamapi kṛtisādhyamiti viśiṣṭarūpeṇa vidhānopapattiḥ| ātmajñāne tu nāsti kiñcidviśeṣaṇamapi kṛtisādhyaṃ, yena tadviśiṣṭatvenāpi vidheyatvaṃ syāt| karmasamuccayasya nirākariṣyamāṇatvād| bandhasyājñānamātrahetukatvena jñānātiriktanivartakānapekṣaṇācca| bandhasyājñānahetukatvaṃ ca `māyāṃ tu prakṛtiṃ vidyāt' `ajñānenāvṛtaṃ jñānaṃ tena muhyanti jantavaḥ' ityādiśrutismṛtinyāyasiddham| vakṣyate cāgre| ajñānanivartakajñānasya cotpattimantareṇānyāpekṣā nāstīti śuktyādijñāne dṛṣṭam| tathā coktaṃ vārtikakṛdbhiḥ|
"tattvamasyādivākyotthasamyagdhījanmamātrataḥ|
avidyā saha kāryeṇa nāsīdasti bhaviṣyati"|| iti|
"pratyagyāthātmyadhīreva pratyagajñānahānikṛt|
sā cātmotpattito nānyad dhvāntadhvastāvapekṣate||"
iti ca| ata eva `yatra hi dvaitamiva bhavati taditara itaraṃ paśyati|' `yatra tvasya sarvamātmaivābhūttatkena kaṃ paśyet' ityādiśrutiḥ|
`yā niśā sarvabhūtānāṃ tasyāṃ jāgarti saṃyamī|
yasyāṃ jāgrati bhūtāni sā niśā paśyato munoḥ||'
ityādismṛtiścāvidyāvasthāyāṃ saṃsāropalambhaṃ vidyāvasthāyāṃ ca tadanupalambhaṃ darśayati| tasmādadhiṣṭhānapramātvenātmajñananivartyatvācchuktirūpyādiṣviva bandhe'pi mithyātvaṃ siddham|
yattvīśvarajñānena satyaṃ ghaṭādi nivartata iti pratyudāharaṇam| tanna| īśvarajñānasya tārkikamate'pi upādānagocarāparokṣajñānatvenaiva kāraṇatvād| abhāvasya ca nirupādānatvād, abhāvaṃ prati kāraṇatve mānābhāvāt| sopādānatve tu samavetatvena tasyāpi bhāvatvāpatteḥ| anyantābhāvādivacca tadajanyatve'pi dhvaṃsasya tadviṣayatvopapatteḥ| na ca tādṛgīśvarajñāne sampratipattirapyanyeṣāmiti na kā'pyanupapattiḥ| yathā ca śuktyādijñānasyarūpyādinivartakatvamaprāmāṇyajñānavirahamapekṣyaiva, evamātmajñānasyāpi śravaṇādinirvṛttāvasambhāvanādinivṛttirūpāprāmāṇyajñānavirahāpekṣatvamiti na kiñcidapyadhikaṃ kalpitam| ātmajñānasya sarvasukṛtasādhyatvaṃ śuktyādijñānāpekṣayā vilakṣaṇamiti tu dṛṣṭāntadārṣṭāntikayorvaidharmyamātrodbhāvanād vaidharmyasamā jātiḥ| ajñānasya ca samānādhikaraṇasamānākārajñānanivartyatvaṃ, jīvanmuktau ca prārabdhakarmapratibandhena bandhanāśavilamba ityādi sarvamupariṣṭādupapādayiṣyate| satyasya jñānanivartyatve tu āśrayaviṣayobhayasambandhitvādinā atiprasaṅgo vivaraṇakārairvarṇitaḥ| tasmādadhiṣṭhānajñānatvena jñānanivartyatvaṃ mithyātve pramāṇamiti siddham|
ityadvaitasiddhau jñānanivartyatvānyathānupapattiḥ|
śuktirūpyasvapnādivad dṛṣṭidṛṣṭyanyathānupapattyā'pi jagato mithyātvasiddhiḥ| atha keyaṃ dṛṣṭisṛṣṭiḥ? dṛṣṭireva sṛṣṭiriti vā? dṛṣṭivyatiriktasṛṣṭyabhāvo vā? dṛṣṭivyatirekeṇa sṛjyābhāvo vā? dṛṣṭisāmagrījanyatvaṃ vā? dṛṣṭisamānakālīnasṛṣṭirvā? dṛṣṭisattāsamānasattākasṛṣṭirvā? sadasadvilakṣaṇatvaṃ vā? trividhasattvabahirbhūtatve satyasadvilakṣaṇatvaṃ vā? ajñātasattvābhāvo vā? jñātaikasattvaṃ vā? ādye vṛttirūpā caitanyarūpā vā dṛṣṭirabhimatā? prathame caramavṛttiviṣayabrahmaṇo'pi dṛṣṭisṛṣṭyāpattiḥ| dvitīye sarvadā'pi sṛṣṭyāpattiḥ| na dvitīyaḥ| caitreṇa sṛṣṭo mayā dṛṣṭa iti vailakṣaṇyena vyavahārānupapatteḥ| na tṛtīyaḥ| `jñāto ghaṭo na jñānam' iti anubhavavirodhāt| na caturthaḥ| ekasāmagrīprasūtatvena ghaṭāderdṛṣṭyabhinnatvenānantaroktadoṣāt| na pañcamaḥ| śābdādijñānasamakālotpannaghaṭādau siddhasādhanāt| tadvadanyatrārthāntaratāpatteśca| na ṣaṣṭhaḥ| ubhayasattve'pyupapatteḥ siddhasādhanāt| na saptamaḥ| asyaiva mithyātvarūpatvena tatsādhanāyaiva tadupanyāsānupapatteḥ| nāṣṭamaḥ trividhasattvamadhye prātibhāsikasattvasyāpyantarbhāvena dṛṣṭisṛṣṭipakṣetadvati jagati tadbahirbhāvānupapatteḥ| na navamaḥ| tucchasādhāraṇyāt| na daśamaḥ| sukhādau siddhasādhanāt, tadvadanyatrārthāntarācceti -- cenna| doṣaprayuktatvanibandhanasya jñātaikasattvasyajñātasattvābhāvasya vā, pratipannopādhidṛṣṭijanyajñātaikasattvasya vā, draṣṭrantarāvedyatve sati jñātaikasattvasya vā vivakṣitatvāt| tathā ca na sukhādyaṃśe siddhasādhanam, tadvadanyatrārthāntaraṃ vā|
nanu -- 
jīva īśo viśuddhā cit tathā jīveśayorbhidā|
avidyā taccitoryogaḥ ṣaḍasmākamanādayaḥ||
iti prācāṃ vacanena, bauddhaṃ prati pratyabhijñānādinā viśvasya sthāyitvapratipādakena ca sūtrabhāṣyavārtikavivaraṇādigranthena virodha iti cenna| anādyatirikte sṛṣṭiviṣaya eva dṛṣṭisṛṣṭisvīkārāt, kāraṇātmanā sthāyitvasvīkārācca| tāvataiva bauddhābhimatakṣaṇikatvanirākaraṇopapatternākaravirodhaḥ| pratyutākareṣu bahuśo dṛṣṭisṛṣṭirupapāditaiva|
nanvevaṃ pratītimātraśarīratvena niyatakāraṇājanyatve śrutiṣu svargādyarthaṃ jyotiṣṭomādividherbrahmasākṣātkārārthaṃ śravaṇādividherākāśādervāyvādihetutvasya coktirayukteti cenna| svāpnakāryakāraṇabhāvabodhakavadupapatteḥ| na caivaṃ vedāntavākyasya tanmīmāṃsāyāśca svapnavākyatanmīmāṃsātulyatāpattiḥ| viṣayabādhābādhābhyāṃ viśeṣopapatteḥ| ata eva tṛptyarthaṃ bhojane parapratyayanārthaṃ śabdādau ca pravṛtterayogena svakriyāvyāghāta iti nirastam| svāpnavyavaharavadupapatteḥ|
athaivaṃ ghaṭādeḥ svajñānāt pūrvamasattvena pratikarmavyavasthānupapattiḥ| adhiṣṭhānasyāpi śuktidamaṃśasya rūpyādivad `idaṃ rajatam' iti jñānāt prāgasattvena saṃprayogādihetutraya janyatvarūpādhyāsataṭasthalakṣaṇasya satyasya vastuno mithyāvastu sambhedābhāva ityasya svarūpalakṣaṇasya cāyoga iti cenna| pratikarmavyavasthāyāḥ saṃprayogādihetutraya janyatvarūpādhyāsataṭasthalakṣaṇasya ca mandādhikāriviṣayatvāt| satyasya vastuno mithyāvastusambhedāvabhāsa iti svarūpalakṣaṇaṃ tu dṛṣṭisṛṣṭipakṣepyaviruddham| na hi idamaṃśā vacchinnaṃ caitanyaṃ na vastu, na vā mithyārūpyasya tena saha na sambhedāvabhāsaḥ| na ca -- `idaṃ rūpyam' iti jñānakāle śuktitvāderabhāvenādhyāsasya tadajñānakāryatvādiprakriyāvirodha iti -- vācyam| `idaṃ rūpyam' iti jñānakāle śuktitvasyābhāve'pi tadajñānasthityavirodhāt| na hi sattākāla iva sattāvirahakāle'pi ajñānaṃ virudhyate| na ca -- `idaṃ rūpyaṃ nedaṃ rūpyam' iti jñānayorbhinnaviṣayatvena bādhyabādhakabhāvānupapattiriti -- vācyam| bhinnaviṣayatve'pi viṣayayoḥ sārūpyāt svapnabādhyabādhakayoriva bādhyabādhakabhāvopapatteḥ| na ca -- rūpyādibādhasyāpi dṛṣṭisṛṣṭitve tena rūpyādermithyātvāsiddhiriti -- vācyam| bādhyānyūnasattākatvameva bādhakatve prayojakam, na tvadhikasattākatvamityasyopapāditatvena vyāvahārikeṇa vyāvahārikabādhavat prātibhāsikena prātibhāsikabādhāvirodhāt| na ca -- suṣuptipralayādau jīvabrahmavibhāgasyāpratītatvenāvidyamānatayā pratisuṣupti pratipralayaṃ ca muktasya punarāvṛttyāpattiriti -- vācyam, jīvabrahmavibhāgāderanāditvena dṛṣṭisṛṣṭitvānubhyupagamasyoktatvāt| na ca suptaṃ prati saṃskārāderapyabhāvena tasya punaḥ prabodhāyogaḥ, kāraṇātmanā saṃskārādeḥ sattvāt| na ca mokṣasya dṛganyatvena svāpnamokṣavad dṛṣṭisṛṣṭyāpattiḥ| mokṣasya brahmasvarūpatvena dṛgbhinnatvāsiddheḥ| na ca -- caitanyamātrarūpā dṛṣṭirna sṛṣṭiḥ kintu vṛttiviśiṣṭacaitanyarūpā vā, vṛttirūpā vā dṛṣṭiḥ sṛṣṭiriti vācyam| tathā ca tasyā api dṛṣṭyantaraṃ sṛṣṭirityanavastheti -- vācyam| caitanyamātrasya dṛṣṭitve yadyapi tatsamānasattākatayā ghaṭādeḥ sadātanatvāpattiḥ,tathāpi vṛttyupahitacaitanyameva dṛṣṭiśabdārthaḥ| vṛttāvapi vṛttireva svasvarūpā caitanyopādhiriti nānavasthā| ata eva -- doṣājñānadraṣṭṛdehendriyādīnāmabhāve na bhrama iti teṣāmapi dṛṣṭisṛṣṭitve anavastheti -- nirastam| svāpnabhramavad dehendriyādinairapekṣyeṇāpyupapatteḥ| anvayavyatirekānuvidhānaṃ ca tadvadeva| na ca -- dṛṣṭisṛṣṭerapi dṛṣṭisṛṣṭitvena ghaṭāderadṛṣṭisṛṣṭitvāpattiriti -- vācyam| jñānasya jñeyatve'pi viṣayasyājñeyatvābhāvavat dṛṣṭisṛṣṭerdṛṣṭisṛṣṭitve'pi ghaṭāderapi dṛṣṭisṛṣṭitvopapatteḥ|
nanu aikyapratyabhijñāvirodhaḥ| pūrvakālapratītasyedānīmabhāvāt| na caiṣā bhrāntiḥ| dīpādau pariṇāmabhedasyeveha bādhakasyābhāvāt| tadabhāve'pi bhrāntitve ghaṭāderapyekasmin kṣaṇe bhedasyātmano'pi pratikṣaṇaṃ bhedasya ca prasaṅga iti cenna| `neha nānā' ityādiśrutibhiḥ prapañcasya mithyātve'vadhṛte rajjusarpādivat pratibhāsamātraśarīratvameva pratibhāsakālātiriktakālasattve bādhakam| ato bhinnakālānāmātmabhinnānāṃ pratyabhijñā bhrāntiḥ| ātmanyekatvapratītirekakālāvacchedena ghaṭādau caikyapratyabhijñā na bhrāntiḥ| ekakālāvacchinnaghaṭādāvātmani cābhede bādhakābhāvāt| puruṣāntarapratītena sahaikakālāvacchedenāpi ghaṭādau pratyabhijñānaṃ bhrama eva| pratibhāsasya bhedāt| yathā ekasyāmeva rajjavāṃ mandāndhakārartinyāṃ daśānāṃ yugapat sarpabhrameṇa palāyamānānāṃ parasparasaṃvādenaika eva sarpaḥ sarvairanubhūyata iti pratyabhijñā bhramaḥ, anyabhramasiddhasyānyena jñānumaśakyatvāt|
nanu -- atra kathamabhedabhramaḥ? tatkāraṇasya sādṛśyādeḥ kasyāpyabhāvāditi cenna, svāpnābhedabhramavad dṛṣṭisṛṣṭisiddhasādṛśyādisambhavāt| na caivamabheda evotpadyatāmiti vācyam| iṣṭāpatteḥ| rajjusarpādivadutpannasyaiva grahaṇaniyamāt| na ca kvacidutpadyate kvacinnetyatra niyāmakābhāvaḥ| māyāyā vicitraśaktikatvābhyupagamāt| na ca `so'yaṃ devadattaḥ' iti dṛṣṭāntena tattvamasyādivākye jahadajahallakṣaṇayaikyaparatvoktayoga iti vācyam| yadyapi dharmavaddharmyabhedo'pi bādhita eveti jahadajahallakṣaṇāpi na yujyate| tathāpi yadā dharmābhedo bādhānna gṛhītaḥ, kintu dharmyabheda eva, tadā `so'yam' ityādau jahadajahallakṣaṇāsambhavena dṛṣṭāntatvopapattiḥ| na cābhedasyāpi dṛṣṭisṛṣṭitvena tajjñānasya bādhakatvāyogaḥ| ātmābhedasyātmarūpatvena dṛṣṭisṛṣṭitvābhāvāt| anyūnasattākatvamātreṇa bādhakatvopapatteśca| na ca sākṣātkārasyāpi dṛṣṭisṛṣṭitvena pramāṇajanyatvābhāvāt tattvajñānatvābhāvena tato muktirna syāditi -- vācyam| abādhitaviṣayatvenaiva tattvajñānatvopapatteḥ| tasya ca dṛṣṭisṛṣṭitve'pyakṣateḥ| na ca `dhruvā dyaurdhruvā pṛthivī dhruvāsaḥ parvatā ime| dhruvaṃ viśvamidaṃ jagat' ityādiśrutivirodhaḥ| anityatāvādibhirapi dhruvetyasyānyathānayane āvaśyake dṛṣṭisṛṣṭipratipādakaśrutyanurodhena ākalpaṃ santānāvicchedaparatvasyaiva yuktatvāt| anyathā `dhruvo rājā' ityādāvagateḥ| dṛṣṭisṛṣṭau ca `evamevāsmādātmanaḥ sarve prāṇāḥ sarve lokāḥ sarve vedāḥ sarvāṇi bhūtāni sarva eta ātmāno vyuccaranti' iti śrutiḥ suptotathitajīvāt prāmādisṛṣṭiṃ pratipādayantī pramāṇam| na ca suṣuptau prāṇādiprapañcasya sattvātkimarthaṃ punaḥ sṛṣṭiriti vācyam| `na tu tad‌dvitīyamasti tato'nyadvibhaktaṃ yatpaśyet' ityādinā suṣuptau sakalakāryaprapañcalayaśravaṇāt| na ca suṣuptau `hitā nāma nāḍya' iti nāḍīsattvapratipādakavākyavirodhaḥ| kena krameṇa suṣupto bhavatītyapekṣāyāṃ `hitā nāma nāḍyo hṛdayātpurītatamabhipratiṣṭhante tābhiḥ pratyavasṛpya purītati śete' ityādinā suṣuptyavyavahitakāle kramoktaye nāḍīsattvaṃ pratipādyate, na tu suṣuptikāle'pi| vākyāntaravirodhāt, prāk sattvamātreṇa ca kramābhidhānaparyāpteḥ|
nanu -- `yatraiṣa etatsupto'bhūt' iti yacchabdena suptādhāratvenoktasya brahmaṇa evāsmādātmana ityanena parāmarśāttatkartṛkaiva prāṇādisṛṣṭirna tu suptotthitajīvakartṛkā| anyathā'gryūrṇanābhyādestantuvisphuliṅgādijananoktiratrāpi vākye sarvalokasṛṣṭyuktiścālīkārthā syāt| na hi dṛṣṭisṛṣṭipakṣe agnyūrṇanābhyādestantvādijanaktavaṃ sarvalokasṛṣṭirvāstīti cet, na| yatretyasya kālaparatvena yacchabdena brahmaṇo nirdeśābhāvāt| na ca yatretyasya brahmarūpādhikaraṇaparatvaṃ kālaparatvaṃ vetyatra vinigamanāvirahaḥ| anantaravākye `kvaiṣa tadābhūt' ityatra kva tadeti padadvayopādānasyaiva vinigamakatvāt, yatretyanena deśanirdeśe kveti deśapraśnānupapatteḥ| kālānirdeśe ca tadeti pratinirdeśānupapatteḥ| bhāṣyakārādibhiśca sthūlādhikāriṇaṃ prati tathā vyākhyānāt| ūrṇanābhyādestantvādijanmoktistu laukikabhramasiddhakāryakāraṇabhāvaprasiddhimanurudhya| sarvalokādisṛṣṭiśca tattad‌dṛṣṭivyaktimabhipretya| yadā tat paśyati, tatsamakālaṃ tat sṛjatītyatra tātparyāt| na cāvidyāsahakṛtajīvakāraṇakatve jagadvaicitryānupapattiḥ| jagadupādānasyājñānasya vicitraśaktikatvāt| upapattyantaraṃ cātra siddhāntabindukalpalatikādāvasmābhirabhihitam| vāsiṣṭhavārtikāmṛtādāvākare ca spaṣṭamevoktam| yathā -- 
`avidyāyonayo bhāvāḥ sarve'mī budbudā iva|
kṣaṇamudbhūya gacchanti jñānaikajaladhau layam|'
ityādi| tasmāt brahmātiriktaṃ kṛtsnaṃ dvaitajātaṃ jñānajñeyarūpamāvidyakameveti prātitikasattvaṃ sarvasyeti siddham|
rajjusarpādivadviśvaṃ nājñātaṃ saditi sthitam|
prabuddhadṛṣṭisṛṣṭatvātsuṣuptau ca layaśruteḥ|| ityadvaitasiddhau dṛṣṭisṛṣṭyupapattiḥ|
sa ca draṣṭaika eva| tannānātve mānābhāvāt| nanu -- kathameka eva jīvaḥ? pratiśarīraṃ `ahaṃ sukhī, ahaṃ duḥkhī, ahaṃ saṃsārī, ahamasya svāmi' ityādyanubhavavirodhāditi cenna| avidyāvaśād brahmaivaikaṃ saṃsarati| sa eva jīvaḥ| tasyaiva pratiśarīramahamityādibuddhiḥ| svāpnaśarīre `ayaṃ sukhī, ayaṃ duḥkhī' ityeva yatra buddhirna tvahaṃ sukhītyādi, tattu nirjīvam| yatra tvahamityādi tat sajīvam| jāgraccharīrāntare ahamiti pratītyavacchedake sajīvatoktirna dvitīyena jīvena sajīvatvamityabhipretya| tatra mānābhāvāt| bandhamokṣādivyavasthānupapattistatra mānamiti cenna| bandhamokṣaguruśiṣyādivyavasthāyāḥ svapnavadyāvadavidyamupapatteḥ| na caivaṃ tasminnekasminneva jīve supte samastajagadapratītyāpātaḥ| samaṣṭyabhimānino mukhyajīvasyāsuptatvāt| tasmin layakāle prasupte jagadapratīteḥ| antaḥkaraṇāvacchinne jīvābhāse tu supte tameva prati jagadapratītiḥ| na tvanyānapi prati| tapupādhīnāmapralīnatvāt| saṃskārasya kāraṇātmanā sthiterna suptasya punarutthānānupapattirityuktam|
etena -- mama kalpakatve tava mokṣārthaṃ pravṛttyayogaḥ| tava kalpakatve tvatkalpitāsmadādibodhārthaṃ tava śabdaprayogādyanupapattiḥ| na ca svapnavat paryanuyogāyogaḥ| evamaparyanuyojyatve nirmaryādatayā kathānadhikāraprasaṅgāditi nirastam| caitramaitrādisarvābhimānino jīvasya kalpakatvena tava mametyādivikalpānupapatteḥ| nāpi svakriyādivirodhaḥ| svakriyāyāḥ kalpitatvādiniścayavirahakālīnatvena paryanuyogāyogāt| atha brahmaṇa eva jīvatvena tasyaiva bandhamokṣāviti tasya nityamuktatvādiśrutivirodhaḥ| na| mukteḥ svasvarūpatvena bandhasya cā'vidyakatvena tadavirodhaḥ| na hi mṛgatṛṣṇikākalpitodakena svabhāvaśuṣkā'pi marubhūmirārdrībhavati|
etena kalpitasya jīvasya kalpakaṃ prati pratyaktvāyogaḥ| tena kalpakena pratyaktvenājñānāt| anyasyānubhaviturabhāvāt| tathā'nubhavāpalāpe ekajīvādvaitaśrutyāderapyasiddhiriti nirastam| anekaśarīraikajīvavādasyāṅgīkārāt| na ca tarhi tameva prati pratyaktvaparāktvayorayogaḥ| maitraṃ prati tvamitidhīviṣayasya caitrasya tameva prati ahamitidhīviṣayatvāyogaśceti vācyam| bhinnabhinnāntaḥkaraṇābhedādhyāsena tattadantaḥkaraṇamādāya pratyaktvaparāktvāhamityādibuddhiviṣayatvavyavasthopapatteḥ| na ca caitrasukhaduḥkhādīnāṃ maitreṇānusandhānāpattiḥ| antaḥkaraṇāvacchinnenāvidyāvacchinnena vā? nādyaḥ| tatra parasparaṃ bhedāt| na dvitīyaḥ iṣṭāpatteḥ| ata eva caitrasya śuktisākṣātkāreṇa rajatabhramanivṛttāvanyeṣāmapi tannivṛttiḥ syāditi nirastam| antaḥkaraṇabhedena vyavasthopapatteḥ| nanu evaṃ muktāvapi caitrādyanyatamāntaḥkaraṇāvacchedena sākṣātkāre utpanne tadavacchedenaiva saṃsāranivṛttiḥ syāt, na tu taditarāntaḥkaraṇāvacchedenāpīti cenna| tatsākṣātkārasya savilāsamūlājñānanivṛttirūpatayā tatkāle'ntaḥkaraṇasyābhāvena vaiṣamyāt|
nanu śrutiṣu `avidyāyāmantare vartamānāḥ' ityādāvavidyā, `ramaṇīyacaraṇāḥ' ityādau karmabandhaḥ, `sati sampadya na viduḥ' ityādau suṣuptiḥ, `vedāntavijñānasuniścitārthāḥ' ityādau tattvajñānaṃ, `parāmṛtātparimucyanti sarve' ityādau muktiśca cetanadharmaḥ kathamanekeṣūcyata iti cenna| `anādimāyayā supto yadā jīvaḥ prabudhyate' ityādiśrutisthaikavacanaprāptaikatvavirodhenodāhṛtaśrutīnāmanekatvaparatvābhāvāt| sarvajanīnabhramasiddhatadanuvādenāvirodhāt| na codāhṛtaśrutivirodhena `iti sṛṣṭau viniścitāḥ' iti pūrveṇa, `sa pūjyaḥ sarvabhūtānām' ityuttareṇa ca virodhenedamekavacanaṃ `yadā nītiparo rājā,' `svargakāmo yajeta' ityādivannaikatvaparamityeva kiṃ na syāditi vācyam| pratyaktvaparāktvamahamityādivyavahāraprayojakāntaḥkaraṇābhedādhyāsabalād bahutvasya prāptatvena pūrvottaravākyodāhṛtaśrutyādīnāmatatparatvāt| na ca muktabahutvaṃ nānyataḥ prāptamiti vācyam| jīvabahutvasya prāptatvena muktyaṃśa evāprāptatvaparyavasānāt| na caikasyaiva jīvasya sarvakalpakatve jīvasya kāraṇatvaṃ niṣidhya īśvarakāraṇatvavidhāyakaiḥ śrutyādibhirvirodhaḥ| avidyāyāḥ cinmātrāśrayatvopapādane nirasiṣyamāṇatvāt| na cai evaṃ sarvajñatvasarvakartṛtvādibodhakaśrutīnāṃ nirviṣayatvam| śuddhacaitanye jñattvasyaivābhāvāt| īśvarasya ca jīvabhinnasyābhāvād, jīve sārvajñyasyānubhavabādhitatvāditi vācyam| samaṣṭyabhimānino jīvasya sarvajñatvasarvakartṛtvādisvīkārāt| na cānubhavavirodhaḥ antaḥkaraṇābhedādhyāsabalāttadananubhavatadviparītānubhavayorupapatteḥ| sarvābhimāninastu sārvajñyānubhavo'styeva| ata eva `tānyahaṃ veda sarvāṇi na tvaṃ vettha parantapa ityādyupapadyate| na ca `ācāryavān puruṣo veda' iti śruterupadeśaṃ vinā jīvasya tattvajñānamanupapannam| upadeṣṭavyādanyasya caitanyasyābhāvācca nopadeśo yujyata iti vācyam| svapna ivopadeṣṭuḥ kalpitasya sambhavāt|
nanu upadeṣṭṛtvaṃ na kalpitamātrasya| kintu tattvavittvena kalpitasya| tathā copadeśātprāk tattvajñāne tadaiva mokṣāpattiḥ| upadeśavaiyarthyaṃ ca| caivaṃ svapne'pi tulyam| tadā hi śabdaviśeṣavaktṛtvenaiva gurukalpanā, na tūpadeśasādhyajñānaviṣayaviśeṣavittveneti viśeṣāditi cenna| atrāpi tadvadeva vākyaviśeṣavaktṛtvenaiva tatkalpanasambhavāt| nanu tarhi `yadeva bhagavānveda tadeva me brūhi' ityādiśrutiḥ, `upadekṣyanti te jñānaṃ jñāninastattvadarśinaḥ' ityādismṛtiścāyuktā syāditi cenna| sāmānyato mokṣopayogijñānaviṣayavittvenājñātatattvavittvena tattvamasyādivākyavaktṛtvena vā kalpitasya upadeṣṭṛtvasambhavena udāhṛtavākyāvirodhāt| anyathā tavāpi mate tattvavittvena pramita evācāryatvenānusaraṇīya iti prathamata eva tattvajñāne tatkālamokṣāpattyupadeśavaiyarthyādikaṃ ca syāt| etena `svādhyāyapravacanābhyāṃ na pramaditavyam' ityādividhirapi bhāvitattvajñānikalpakacetanaṃ pratyeva| na ca tasya śiṣyaḥ svājñānakalpita iti jānatastanmokṣārthaṃ pravacane pravṛttiryuktā| na ca svapnavat kalpitatvājñānātpravṛttiḥ| tattvavidastadajñānānupapatteriti nirastam| svapnaguruvat kalpitatvena guroraparyanuyojyatvāt| na ca tattvajñānahetutvena vedasya mīmāṃsyatvavad gurorapi paryanuyojyatvamiti vācyam| tarkeṇa veda iva tattadrūpakalpanayā gurāvapi tatparihārāt| na ca kathāsvapi saduttarāparisphūrtāvahaṃ tvalkalpito na paryanuyojya ityuttaraṃ syāditi vācyam| kathāyāḥ kalpitatvāniścayakālīnatvena samayabandhaviśeṣanibandhanatvena ca tādṛguttarānavakāśāt| tasmācchiṣyavat gurorapi kalpitatvāt svapnavatsarvavyavasthopapattiḥ|
atha kalpako na niścitādvaitaḥ| śāstrapraṇayanavaiyarthyāt| nāpyaniścitādvaitaḥ| śāstrasya pramāmūlakatvābhāvaprasaṅgāditi cenna| pramāmūlakatvābhāve'pyabādhitaviṣayatvena śāstraprāmāṇyopapatterantyapakṣābhyupagamāt| na cāmukaḥ sa ityaniścaye bahvāyāsasādhyamokṣārthaṃ pravṛttyayogaḥ| pratiśarīramahamahamikayā `baddho'ham' iti niścayasya svānubhavasākṣikatvena pravṛttisambhavāt, ekenaiva jīvena caitrametrādiśarīrāṇāṃ sajīvatvasambhavasya prāgevoktatvāt| kiṃ ca caitramaitrādiṣu `ko'sau' iti praśnasya kiṃ kenacit kroḍīkṛtaṃ caitanyaṃ viṣayaḥ| kiṃ vā nirastasamastabhedam| nādyaḥ| tasya kalpitatvenākalpakatvāt| na dvitīyaḥ| tasyaikatvena tadaniścayāsiddheḥ| śuddhacita ekatvena vastuto'saṃsāritve'pi āvaraṇavikṣepaśaktidvayaśālisvāśritāvidyāvaśāt saṃsāritvakalpakatvamokṣārthaṃ yatamānatvādyupapattiḥ|
nanvanādau saṃsāre kasyacittatvajñānaṃ muktiścābhūnna vā? ādye idānīṃ saṃsāropalabdhirna syāt| jīvasyaikatvād| antye saṃpradāyāsambhavena tattvajñānāsambhava iti cenna| na hyasāmpradāyikatvamutpattivirodhi| apūrvajātīyānutpattiprasaṅgāt| kintu kāraṇāsattvam| tannedānīm upadeṣṭrādikāraṇasya kalpanāsudṛḍhasya sattvāt| jīvaikyasya pramāṇāsiddhatvena saṃsāropalambha evātaḥ pūrvaṃ tattvajñānānutpattau pramāṇam| na ca tattvavittvena śrutyādisiddhānāṃ śukavāmadevādīnāṃ muktirmā bhūt, mama tu bhaviṣyatīti kathaṃ śraddadhyāditi vācyam| śāstraprāmāṇyadārḍhyāditi gṛhāṇa| anyatā teṣāṃ mahānubhāvānāṃ muktatve'pi mama bhaviṣyati na veti śaṅkāpiśācyā pravṛttipratibandhāpatteḥ| nanu tarhi śrutiprāmāṇyabalādeva tatsiddho jīvabhedaḥ, pūrvamapi keṣāṃcinmokṣaścābhyupeyatām| śrūyate hi `tadyo yo devānāṃ pratyabudhyata sa eva tadabhavattatharṣīṇāṃ tathā manuṣyāṇām,' `ajo hyoko juṣamāṇo'nuśete jahātyenāṃ bhuktabhogāmajo'nyaḥ', `nityo nityānāṃ cetanaścetanānām' ityādi| smaryate ca -- 
bahavo jñānatapasā pūtā madbhāvamāśritāḥ|
idaṃ jñānamupāśritya mama sādharmyamāgatāḥ||
ityādīti cenna| uktavākyānāṃ sārvalaukikabhramasiddhabhedānuvādakatvena tatparatvābhāvāt| jīvaikyabodhakavākyānāṃ ca mānāntarāprāptasvārthaparatvāt| svapnanyāyena bhedasya kalpitatvopapatteśca| jñānastutiparāṇi vākyāni nātmabhedaṃ pramātuṃ śaknuvanti| tātparyavadvākyāvirodhenātātparyavadvākyānāṃ guṇavādatvopapatteḥ|
"atītānāgatāścaiva yāvantaḥ sahitāḥ kṣaṇaḥ|
tato'pyanantaguṇitā jīvānāṃ rāśayaḥ pṛthak||"
ityādismṛtirapi jīvopādhibhedānuvādakatayā vyākhyeyā| tasmādavidyopādhiko jīva eka eveti siddham| ityadvaitasiddhau ekajīvājñānakalpitatvopapattiḥ||
atha -- keyamavidyā? na tāvad anādibhāvarūpatve sati jñānanivartyā seti| sādiśuktyādyavacchinnacaitanyāvārakājñāne'vyāpteḥ| tasyānāditvābhāvāt| abhāvopādānājñāne ca bhāvatvābhāvāttatrāvyāptiḥ| abhāvasya bhāvopādānakatve asatyasyāpi satyopādānakatvaṃ syād| ajñānānupādānakatve tasya jñānānnivṛttirna syāditi| atra brūmaḥ -- rūpyopādānājñānamapyanādicaitanyāśritatvādanādyeva| udīcyaṃ śuktyādikaṃ tu tadavacchedakamiti na tatrāvyāptiḥ| bhāvatvaṃ cātrābhāvavilakṣaṇatvamātraṃ vivakṣitam| ata āropitābhāvopādānājñāne'pyabhāvavilakṣaṇatvasvīkārānnāvyāptiḥ| na ca sajātīyopādānakatvaniyamaḥ, anyathā asatyasyāpi satyamupādānaṃ syāditi vācyam| sarvathā sājātye sarvathā vaijātye vopādānopādeyabhāvādarśanena tathā sājātyasya vaijātyasya vā āpādayitumaśakyatvāt| na hi kāryākārakāraṇākārato'pyabhede kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ| satyasya tvasatyopādānatve satyasya nivṛttyasambhavena tadupādeyasyāsatyasyāpi nivṛttirna syād| upādānanivṛttimantareṇopādeyānivṛtteḥ| ato na satyamasatyasyopādānam| satyasyāpariṇāmitvācca| vivartādhiṣṭhānatvaṃ tvabhyupeyata eva| na ca brahmājñāne brahmaṇo vṛttyavyāpyatvapakṣevyāptiḥ| tasya jñānānivartyatvāditi vācyam| svarūpasadupādhimattadviṣayakajñānavivartyasya tanmate'pi bhāvāt| upapāditaṃ caitad dṛśyatvahetūpapādane|
atha -- aupādhikabhramopādānājñāne, brahmasākṣātkārānantaravidyamānajīvanmuktājñāne ca jñānanivartyatvābhāvādavyāptiḥ| tayorjñānanivartyatve upādhikālajīvanmuktikālayoreva jñānaprāgabhāvavattannivṛttyāpattiriti cenna| upādhiprārabdhakarmaṇoḥ pratibandhakayorabhāvavilambena nivṛttivilambe'pi tayorjñānanivartyatvānapāyāt| na hi kvacidavilbena janakasya kvacit pratibandhena vilambe janakatā'paiti| na ca tarhi jñāte'pi tatrājñāta iti vyavahārāpattiḥ| tādṛgvyavahāre āvaraṇaśaktimadajñānasya kāraṇatvena tadāvaraṇaśaktyabhāvādeva īdṛgvyavahārānāpatteḥ| yathā caitattathopapādayiṣyate| na cāvidyācaitanyasambandhe'tivyāptiḥ| sākṣājjñānanivartyatvasya vivakṣitatvāt, tasyāpyavidyātmakatvādvā| na ca viśeṣaṇāntaravaiyarthyam| anādipadasyottarajñānanivartye pūrvajñāne, bhāvapadasya jñānaprāgabhāve jñānajanyakāryaprāgabhāve cātivyāptivārakatvena sārthakatvāt| jñānatvena sākṣājjñānanivartyatvaṃ tu bhavati lakṣamāntaram| nanu -- asambhavaḥ kalpitatvena doṣajanyadhīmātraśarīrasyājñānasya jñānanivartyasyābhāvavilakṣaṇasyi ca rūpyavadanāditvāyogāditi cenna| kalpitatvamātraṃ hi na doṣajanyadhīmātraśarīratve sāditve vā tantram| kintu pratibhāsakalpakasamānakālīnakalpakavattvaṃ, sādikalpakavattvaṃ, vidyā'nivṛttyaprayuktanivṛttipratiyogitvaṃ, prāgabhāvapratiyogitvaṃ vā tantram| na ca tat prakṛte'sti| jñānanivartyatvasamānādhikaraṇābhāvavilakṣaṇatvenāvidyāyāḥ sāditvasādhane `ajāmekām' `anādimāyayā' ityādiśāstravirodhaḥ| anāditvasādhakena jñānanivartyatve sati bhāvavilakṣaṇatvena satpratipakṣaśca, bhāvatvasyopādhitvaṃ ca|
na ca abhāvavilakṣaṇāvidyādau bhāvavilakṣaṇatvamasambhavi, parasparavirodhāditi vācyam| bhāvatvābhāvatvayorbādhakasattvena tṛtīyaprakāratvasiddhau parasparavirahavyāpakatvarūpavirodhāsiddheḥ| parasparavirahavyāpyatvarūpastu virodho naikaviraheṇāparamākṣipati| na hi gotvaviraho'śvatvamākṣipatītyuktam| na cātmavadanāderabhāvavilakṣaṇasyānivartyatvam| ātmatvasyaivopādhitvāt| na cātyantābhāvānyonyābhāvayoḥ sādhyāvyāptiḥ| adhikaraṇātiriktasyānivartyasyātyantābhāvāderanabhyupagamāt| na ca tucche sādhyāvyāptiḥ| abhāvavilakṣaṇatvarūpasādhanāvacchinnasādhyavyāpakatvopapatteḥ| kiṃ ca sāditvamanāditvaṃ vā na nivartyatvānivartyatvayoḥ prayojakam| dhvaṃsaprāgabhāvayostadabhāvāt| nāpi bhāvatvaviśeṣitaṃ tat tathā| abhāve tadasattvena bhinnabhinnaprayojakakalpanāpatteḥ| bhāvanivṛttyanivṛttyoreva tayoḥ prayojakatve ca bhāvavilakṣaṇāvidyādau tābhyāṃ tayoranāpādanāt| tasmānnāśasāmagrīsannipātāsannipātāveva nivartyatvānivartyatvayoḥ prayojakāviti mantavyam| tau ca phalabalakalpyāviti na ko'pi doṣaḥ| api ca yadyavidyāderabhāvavilakṣaṇatvasamānādhikaraṇānāditvenā'tmavadanivartyatvaṃ sādhyate| tarhi bhāvavilakṣaṇatvena prāgabhāvavannivartyatvameva kiṃ na sādhyate? na ca dhvaṃsātyantānyonyābhāveṣu vyabhicāraḥ| adhikaraṇātireke teṣāmapi nivartyatvābhyupagamāt| na ca -- ajñānasya yāvatsvaviṣayadhīrūpasākṣisattvamanuvṛttiniyamena nivṛttyayoga iti vācyam| duḥkhaśuktirūpyādeḥ svabhāsake sākṣiṇi satyeva nivṛttyabhyupagamena sākṣibhāsyānāṃ yāvatsākṣisattvamavasthānaniyamānabhyupagamāt| kiṃ ca kevalacinmātraṃ na sākṣi, kintvavidyāvṛttyupahitam| tathā cāsthirāvidyāvṛttyupahitasya sākṣiṇo'pyasthiratvena tatsattvaparyantamavasthāne'pyavidyādernivṛttirupapadyate| na ca vṛttyanupadhānadaśāyāmavidyādeḥ śuktirūpyavadasattvāpattiḥ| sādipadārthaṃ evaitādṛṅniyamād| dhārāvāhikāvidyāvṛttiparamparāyā atisūkṣmāyā abhyupagamādveti śivam|
 yadvā bhramopādānatvamajñānalakṣaṇam| idaṃ ca lakṣaṇaṃ viśvabhramopādānamāyādhiṣṭhānaṃ brahmeti pakṣe| na tu brahmamātropādānatvapakṣe, brahmasahitāvidyopādānatvapakṣe vā| ato brahmaṇi nātivyāptiḥ| itaratra tu pakṣe pariṇāmitvenācetanatvena vā bhramopādānaṃ viśeṣaṇīyamiti na doṣaḥ| na cābhāvāropanivartakapramānivartye'vyāptiḥ| tasyāpi bhramopādānatvāt| nanu -- bhrame bhāvavilakṣaṇājñānopādānakatvaṃ na ghaṭate| bhramasya bhāvavilakṣaṇatve upādeyatvāyogād| bhāvatve ca bhāvopādānakatvaniyamāditi cenna| ajñānasya bhramasya ca bhāvavilakṣaṇatve'pyupādānopādeyabhāvopapatteḥ| na hi bhāvatvamupādānatve upādeyatve vā prayojakam| ātmani tadadarśanāt, kintvanvayikāraṇatvamupādānatve tantram| sāditvamupādeyatve| tadubhayaṃ ca na bhāvatvaniyatam| ata upādānopādeyabhāvo'pi na bhāvatvaniyataḥ| na caivaṃ dhvaṃsasyāpyupādeyatvāpattiḥ, iṣṭāpatteḥ| na caivaṃ jñānaprāgabhāvasyaiva bhramopādānatvamastu, kimabhāvavilakṣaṇājñānopādānakalpaneneti vācyam| prāgabhāvasya pratiyogimātrajanakatvaniyamena bhramaṃ prati janakatvasyāpyasiddheḥ, tadviśeṣarūpopādānatvasyaiva dūranirastatvāt| ataḥ sadvilakṣamayorajñānabhramayoryukta upādānopādeyabhāvaḥ| bhramasya ca sadvilakṣaṇatvamuktam| vakṣyate ca| na caivamajñānānuviddhatayā bhramasya pratītyāpattiḥ, mṛdanuviddhatayā ghaṭasyeveti vācyam| yad yadupādānakaṃ, tat tadanuviddhatayaiva pratīyata iti vyāptyasiddheḥ| na hi ghaṭopādānakaṃ rūpaṃ ghaṭa iti pratīyate| prakṛtidvyaṇukādyanuviddhatayā pratīteḥ parairapyanabhyupagamāt| kenaciddharmeṇa tadanuvedhastu prakṛte'pīṣṭa eva| na ca yāvanti jñānāni tāvantyajñānānīti pakṣe bhramāpūrvakapramānivartye'jñāne avyāptiḥ| bhramopādānatāyogyatvasya vivakṣitatvāt| sahakārivaikalyāt kāryānudaye'pi yogyatānapāyāt| atha yogyatāvacchedakarūpāparicaye kathaṃ tadgrahaṇam? prathamalakṣaṇasyaiva yogyatāvacchedakatvāt| ekamevājñānamiti pakṣe tu tatra bhramopādānatvamakṣatameva| na caivaṃ śuktijñānenaivājñānanāśe mokṣāpattiḥ| tasyāvasthāviśeṣanāśakatvāṅgīkārāt| vyutpāditaṃ caitadasmābhiḥ siddhāntabindau| jñānatvena rūpeṇa sākṣājjñānanivartyatvaṃ vā tallakṣaṇamiti ca prāguktameva| tasmānnāvidyālakṣaṇāsambhava iti sarvamavadātam|
|| ityadvaitasiddhāvavidyālakṣaṇopapattiḥ||
tatra cājñāne sāmanyataḥ `ahamajño māmanyaṃ ca na jānāti' iti pratyakṣaṃ, `tvaduktamarthaṃ na jānāmi' iti viśeṣataḥ pratyakṣam, `etāvantaṃ kālaṃ sukhamahamasvāpyasaṃ na kiñcidavediṣam' iti parāmarśasiddhaṃ sauṣuptapratyakṣaṃ ca pramāṇam| na ca -- ahamarthasyājñānānāśrayatvena kathamayaṃ pratyayo bhāvarūpājñānapakṣe upapadyata iti -- vācyam| ajñānāśrayībhūtacaitanye antaḥkaraṇatādātmyādhyāsena ekāśrayatvasaṃbandhenopapatteḥ| ata eva -- jaḍe āvaraṇakṛtyābhāvāt `ghaṭaṃ na jānāmi' ityādipratīterjñānābhāvaviṣayatve prakṛte'pi tathā'stviti -- nirastam| tattadavacchinnacaitanyasyaivājñānāśrayatvena tatrāpi tad‌vyavahāropapatteḥ| na ca -- sākṣivedye sukhaduḥkhājñānādau prātibhāsike ca bhāvarūpājñānābhāvena tatra na jānāmīti pratītiḥ kathamupapadyata iti -- vācyam| svasminvidyamāne sākṣivedye sukhādau svabhramasiddhe rūpyādau ca `na jānāmi' iti vyavahārāsambhavāt, parasukhādau `na jānāmi' iti vyavahārasya parokṣajñānanivartyena pramātṛgatājñānenaivopapatteḥ| ata eva parokṣajñānena pramātṛgatajñāne nāśite'pi viṣayagatājñānasattvena `na jānāmi' iti vyavahārāpattiriti -- nirastam| pramātṛgatājñānakāryasya `na jānāmi' iti vyavahārasya viṣayagatājñānenāpādayitumaśakyatvāt|
nanu -- bhāvarūpājñānaviṣayatvenābhimatasya `ahamajñaḥ' iti pratyapasya `mayi jñānaṃ nāsti' iti jñānābhāvaviṣayāt pratyayāt `aghaṭaṃ bhūtalam' iti pratyayasya `ghaṭo nāsti' iti pratyayādiva viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvavyatyāsaṃ vinā icchādveṣābhāvajñānayoriva viṣayabhedāpratītiriti -- cet, satyam| dharmipratiyogijñānājñānājñānābhyāṃ jñānasāmānyābhāvajñānasya vyāhatatvena `mayi jñānaṃ nāsti' ityasyāpi bhāvarūpājñānaviṣayatvena viṣayabhedāpratīteryuktatvāt| tathā hi -- `mayi jñānaṃ nāsti' iti pratītiḥ `vāyau rūpaṃ nāsti' iti pratītivadyāvadviśeṣābhāvānyasāmānyābhāvaviṣayā, sāmānyāvacchinnapratiyogitākayāvadviśeṣābhāvaviṣayā vā abhyupeyā| tathā ca tatkāraṇībhūtadharmipratiyogijñānājñānābhyāṃ kathaṃ na vyāghātaḥ? yatkiñcidviśeṣābhāvasya sāmānyāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvābhāvād| abhāvajñāne pratiyogyaṃśe prakārībhūtadharmasyaiva pratiyogitāvacchedakatvāt| anyathā sāmānyābhāvasiddharna syāt| yāvadviśeṣābhāvānyasāmānyābhāvānabhyupagame'pyayaṃ doṣaḥ| yatkiñcidviśeṣābhāvasya sāmānyāvacchinnapratiyogitākatve ghaṭavatyapi bhūtale `nirghaṭaṃ bhūtalam' iti pratītiḥ syāt| `vāyau rūpaṃ nāsti' `puro deśe rajataṃ nāsti' ityādyāptavākyajanyapratītyanantaramapi tattatsaṃśayanivṛttirna syāt| ekaviśeṣābhāvabodhane'pi viśeṣāntaramādāya saṃśayopapatteḥ|
atha -- abhāvabodhe prakārībhūtadharmasyāvacchedakatvaṃ pūrvānupasthitamapi saṃsargamaryādayā śabdābodhe anyatra ca bhāsate| na hvavacchedakatvasya svarūpasaṃbandhaviśeṣasya grahe anyā sāmagrī klṛptā| tathā ca tattadviśeṣābhāvānāṃ tattadviśeṣāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvāt, sāmānyāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvaṃ yāvadviśeṣābhāvakūṭe vā vyāsajyavṛttitadatiriktasāmānyābhāve vā pratyekaviśrāntamiti tādṛgabhāvapratīteyoryāvadviśeṣapratītivirodhitvāt kuto viśeṣasaṃśayādiriti cet, satyam| prakṛte'pi jñānatvasāmānyāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvapratītiryāvajjñānaviśeṣavirodhinīti kathaṃ tattatkāraṇatvābhimatajñānaviśeṣe sati sā na vyāhanyate? tathā ca klṛptābhāvapratītivailakṣaṇye'vaśyakalpye lāghavādviṣayasyaivābhāvavailakṣaṇyaṃ kalpayitumucitam| viṣayāvailakṣaṇye pratītivailakṣaṇyāyogāt| viṣayajñānamanubhūya ca puruṣastannivṛttyarthaṃ vicāre pravartata iti sarvānubhavasiddham| yadyapi jñānaviśeṣābhāvo `na jānāmī'ti pratīterviṣayaḥ, tadā jñāte'pi tathā pratītyāpātaḥ| tadvicārārthaṃ ca pravṛttiḥ syāt| sāmānyābhāve ca bādhakamuktameva| tasmādabhāvavilakṣaṇamevājñānaṃ `mayi jñānaṃ nāstyahamajñaḥ' ityādi -- dhīviṣaya iti siddham|
nanu abhāvavilakṣaṇamapyajñānaṃ `na jānāmi' iti jñānavirodhitvenaiva bhāsate| mohādipade'pi pralayādipadavattadanullekhamātram| uktaṃ ca vivaraṇe -- `ajñānamiti dvayasāpekṣajñānaparyudāsenābhidhānād' iti| anyathā jñānasyājñānavirodhitvamaprāmāṇikaṃ syāt| tathā ca virodhanirūpakajñānasya jñānājñānābhyāṃ tavāpi kathaṃ na vyāghātaḥ? evaṃ nirviṣayājñānāpratīterviṣayajñānājñānayorapi vyāghāta āpadanīyaḥ| tathā ca
yatrobhayoḥ samo doṣaḥ parihāro'pi vā samaḥ|
naikaḥ paryanuyoktavyastādṛgarthavicāraṇe||
iti nyāyena ubhayapariharaṇīyasya vyāghātasya jñānābhāvapakṣa evāpādanamanucitamiti -- cenna| pramāṇavṛttinivartyasyāpi bhāvarūpājñānasya sākṣivedyasya virodhanirūpakajñānatadvyāvartakaviṣayagrāhakeṇa sākṣiṇā tatsādhakena tadanāśādvyāhatyanupapatteḥ| ajñānagrahe viṣayagocarapramāpekṣāyāṃ vyāhatiḥ syādeva, sā ca nāsti| taduktaṃ vivaraṇe -- `sarvaṃ vastu jñātatayā'jñātatayā vā sākṣicaitanyasya viṣaya eva'iti| na caivaṃ jñānābhāvapakṣe'pi viṣayādijñānaṃ sākṣirūpam| `na jānāmi' iti dhīstu pramāṇavṛttyabhāvaviṣayeti na vyāhatiriti -- vācyam| bhāvarūpājñānasya sākṣāt sākṣivedyatvena tadavacchedakaviṣayādestad‌dvārā sākṣivedyatvasambhave'pi abhāvasyānupalabdhigamyatvena sākṣāt sākṣivedyatvābhāvāt na tad‌dvārā tadavacchedakaviṣayādeḥ sākṣivedyatvamiti vaiṣamyāt| yadyapi jñānaṃ sākṣivedyaṃ, tad‌dvārā tadavacchedako viṣayaśca sākṣivedyaḥ, tathāpi jñānābhāvo na sākṣivedyaḥ, tasyānupalabdhatvāt| utpannaṃ ca jñānaṃ sākṣāt sākṣivedyam| tasmiṃścotpanne tadviṣayo'pi sphuratīti kuto jñānābhāvo'pi? ajñānaviśeṣaṇatayā tu anutpannamapi jñānaṃ sākṣivedyamiti na doṣasāmyam| na ca -- avacchedakasya viṣayādeḥ prāgajñāne kathaṃ tadviśiṣṭājñānajñānam? viśeṣaṇajñānādhīnatvādviśiṣṭajñānasyeti -- vācyam| viśeṣaṇajñānasya viśiṣṭajñānajanakatve mānābhāvāt| pratiyogitvābhāvatvayoḥ pūrvānupasthitayorapi tārkikairabhāvabodhe prakārībhūya bhānābhyupagamāt| tathāpi -- viśeṣaṇatāvacchedakaprakārakajñānaṃ vinā kathaṃ viśiṣṭavaiśiṣṭabuddhiriti -- cenna| viśiṣṭavaiśiṣṭyabuddhitvena viśeṣaṇatāvacchedakaprakārakajñānatvena ca kāryakāraṇabhāve mānābhāvāt| pratyakṣatvādirūpeṇa pṛthak pṛthak klṛptakāryakāraṇabhāvenaivopapatteḥ viśiṣṭavaiśiṣṭyabuddhitvasyārthasamājasiddhatvāt| iha ca sāmagrītulyatvena `viśeṣye viśeṣaṇaṃ tatra ca viśeṣaṇāntaram' iti nyāyena viśiṣṭavaiśiṣṭyajñānasambhavāt| anyathā tārkikāṇāmapīśvarasya bhrāntijñatvaṃ na syāt| bhramaviṣayasya svātantryeṇa grahe bhrāntatvāpattyā bhramāvacchedakatayaiva tad‌grahaṇaṃ vācyam| tathā ca kva prāktadavacchedakagrahaniyamaḥ? grahaṇasāmagrītulyatvaṃ ca prakṛte'pi samam|
nanu -- śravaṇādisādhyamokṣahetubrahmajñānaprāgabhāvasya sattvena tajjñānaṃ tvayāpi vācyam| tathā ca tatrāpi vyāhatistulyeti -- cenna| śravaṇādisādhyamokṣahetubrahmajñānarūpasya pratiyogino jñānājñānābhyāṃ vyāhatyabhāvāt| na hi śravaṇādisādhyatvamokṣahetutvādiprakārakaṃ brahmajñānajñānaṃ brahmajñānamapi sat śravaṇādisādhyam, mokṣaheturvā| yena tasmin sati tādṛgjñānaprāgabhāvo vyāhanyeta|
nanvevaṃ -- `na jānāmi' iti dhiyo jñānābhāvaviṣayatve'pi na pratiyogijñānādinā vyāhatiḥ| sāmānyato viṣayapratiyogijñāne'pi viśeṣatastadabhāvasambhavāt| anyathā prāgabhāvadhīrna syāt| tatpratiyogiviśeṣasya sāmānyadharmaṃ vinā viśeṣato jñātumaśakyatvāditi -- cenna| viśeṣajñānābhāve hi viśeṣajñānatvāvacchinnaṃ pratiyogīti tasya jñāne sa viśeṣo'pi jñāta eveti viśeṣajñānābhāvavyāghātāt| yatkiñcidviśeṣābhāvaśca na sāmānyāvacchinnapratiyogitāka ityuktam| pratiyogitāvacchedakaprakārakajñānābhāvena prāgabhāvapratītirasiddhaiva| nanu -- pratiyogitāvacchedakaprakārakajñānaṃ nābhāvajñāne kāraṇam| kiṃtvabhāvajñāne bhāsamānapratiyogivṛttidharmaprakārakaṃ jñānam| sāmānyalakṣaṇapratyāsattyabhyupagame tu pratiyogiviṣayatvamapi tasyādhikam| itarathā tu tadeva iṣṭavṛttisāmānyadharmaprakārakajñānamivāsiddhavyaktiviṣayecchākṛtyoḥ| na ca -- pratiyogitānavacchedakadharmeṇa kathaṃ pratiyogitā gṛhyatāmiti -- vācyam| viśeṣāvacchinnāyā vyāpteriva sāmānyena grahaṇasambhavāt| tathā hi -- `idamabhidheyavat, prameyāt' ityanumāne `yatra prameyaṃ tatrābhidheyam' iti vyāptigrahaṇasamaye vṛttimatprameyatvāvacchedenaiva sāmānādhikaraṇyarūpavyāptisattve'pi tasyāḥ prameyatvarūpeṇaiva grahaṇaṃ, na tu vṛttimatprameyatvena, gauravāt| vṛttimattvaviśeṣaṇasya vyabhicārāvārakatvena vaiyarthyācca| avṛttiṣu sādhyasāmānādhikaraṇyarūpavyāptyabhāvavat sādhyābhāvasāmānādhikaraṇyarūpavyabhicārasyāpyabhāvāt| vyarthaviśeṣaṇatvarahitatve sati vyabhicārivyāvṛttatvamātreṇaiva vyāpyatāvacchedakatvasambhavācca| tathā ca yathā vṛttimatprameyagatāpi vyāptiḥ prameyatvenaiva gṛhyate, tathā tattannīlādivyaktigatā pratiyogitā nīlatvādirūpeṇa gṛhyata iti na kācidanupapattiḥ| evaṃ ca `ihedānīṃ' ghaṭo nāsti' iti pratītiriva ghaṭopādānagatatatprāgabhāvaviṣayā `mayi jñānaṃ nāsti' iti pratītirapi pramātṛgatatatprāgabhāvaviṣaye'ti na kāpyanupapattiriti -- cenna| abhāvajñāne pratiyogyaṃśe bhāsamānasya dharmasyaiva pratiyogitāvacchedakatayā yatkiñcidviśeṣābhāvasya sāmānyāvacchinnapratiyogitākatve ghaṭavatyapi bhūtale `nirghaṭa bhūtalam' iti, ghaṭajñānavatyapi svasmin `mayi ghaṭajñānaṃ nāsti' iti ca pratīterāpatteḥ pūrvoktadoṣāt| yatkiñcid‌ghaṭajñanaṃ ghaṭābhāvajñāne pratibandhakamiti tu jñānajñāne'pi tulyam| udāhṛtavyāptigrahaṇe tu bādhakābhāvāt sāmānyāvacchede'pi na doṣaḥ|
athaivaṃ prāgabhāvapratītireva na syāt| na syādeva| `ghaṭo bhaviṣyati' iti pratīteḥ dhātvarthabhaviṣyattāviṣayatvena prāgabhāvāviṣayatvāt| anyathā dināntarotpatsyamānaghaṭe etaddinavṛttiprāgabhāvapratiyogitvena `adya ghaṭo bhaviṣyati' iti dhīprasaṅgaḥ| bhaviṣyattvaṃ ca pratiyogitad‌dhvaṃsānādhārakālasambandhitvam| dhvaṃsatvaṃ ca prāgabhāvānaṅgīkartṛmate kādācitkābhāvatvameva| tadaṅgīkartṛmate'pi pratiyogyajanakakādācitkābhāvatvam| janakatvaṃ ca svarūpasambandhaviśeṣo na prāgabhāvaghaṭitaḥ| prāgabhāvasyājanakatvāpatteḥ| anyathātmāśrayāt| ataḥ prāgabhāvamaṅgīkurvato'pi tatpratyakṣatvaṃ durlabham| tamanaṅgīkurvatastu na kā'pi hāniḥ| `ihedānīṃ ghaṭo nāsti' iti pratītistu sāmānyadharmāvacchinnapratiyogitākatatkālāvacchinnayāvadviśeṣabhāvaviṣayā, samayaviśeṣasyāpyabhāvavācchedakatvāt| anyathā `ādyakṣaṇe ghaṭo nīrūpaḥ' ityādipratītirna syāt|
atha -- asminpakṣe sāmānyābhāvo na sidhyediti -- cet, prāgabhāvābhyupagame'pi tulyametat| sāmānyābhāvaprāgabhāvayoḥ sundopasundayoriva parasparaparāhatatvāt| tathā hi -- prāgabhāvasiddhau viśeṣābhāvasyāpi sāmānyāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvāt na tāvanmātrapramāṇakasāmānyābhāvasiddhiḥ| sāmānyābhāvasiddhau ca viśeṣābhāvasya sāmānyāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvābhāvāt kādācitkābhāvasya ca sāmānyābhāvatvāyogāt na sāmānyadharmāvacchinnapratiyogitākaviśeṣapratītimātraśaraṇaprāgabhāvasiddhiriti na tadubhayamapi vipaścitāṃ cetasi camatkāramāvahati| nanu -- yāvadviśeṣābhāvaniścaye'pi `rūpaṃ vāyuvṛtti na vā', `vāyū rūpavānna vā' iti rūpābhāvasandehāt niścite ca saṃśayāyogādyāvadviśeṣābhāvānyasāmānyābhāvasiddhiḥ| ata etāvantyeva rūpāṇīti niścayadaśāyāmetādṛśasaṃśayasyānanubhūyamānatvena tadaniścayadaśāyāmevaitādṛśaḥ saṃśayo vācya| tathā ca `rūpatvaṃ pārthivāpyataijasarūpannitayātiriktavṛtti bhaviṣyati' ityadhikasaṃbhāvanayā aniściteṣveva saṃśayaḥ| uktasambhāvanāvirahasahakṛtaniścayasyaiva pratibandhakatvāditi -- cenna| evaṃ pratibandhakakalpane mānābhāvād| uktasambhavanāvirahadaśāyā mapyetādṛśasaṃśayadarśanācca|
nanu -- yathā yāvadviśeṣābhāvebhyo'tiriktaḥ sāmānyābhāvo rūpasya saṃśayakoṭiḥ, tathā rūpasāmānyamapi yāvadviśeṣebhyo'tiriktaṃ saṃśayakoṭirnābhyupagantuṃ śakyate| tathā ca kathaṃ rūpasya saṃśayakoṭitvam? sarvarūpābhāvaniścayāt| yadi tu nīlapītādyabhāvatvena niścaye'pi rūpābhāvatvenāniścayādrūpasaṃśaya iti brūṣe, tadā kiṃ sāmānyābhāvena? rūpatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvatvena saṃśayasambhavāt| dharmikalpanāto dharmakalpanāyā laghutvena yāvadviśeṣābhāvānāmeva rūpatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvakalpanād| ato na yatkiñcidabhāvamādāya `ghaṭo nīrūpaḥ' iti pratitiprasaṅga iti -- cenna| yāvadviśeṣābhāveṣu yadrūpatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvaṃ, tat pratyekaviśrāntaṃ, vyāsajyavṛtti vā? ādye yatkiñcidabhāvamādāya `ghaṭo nīrūpaḥ' iti pratītiprasaṅgaḥ| dvitīye, tattadrūpatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvasyāvyāsajyavṛttisvabhāvatvena tadvyatiriktaṃ rūpatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvaṃ vyāsajyavṛtti kalpanīyam| tadvaraṃ rūpatvāvacchinnapratiyogitāka eka evābhāvaḥ kalpyate| mamaiko'bhāvaḥ rūpatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvaṃ ceti vastudvayaṃ kalpyam| tava tu rūpatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvaṃ tasya ca vyāsajyavṛttitvena bahuṣvabhāveṣu pratyekaṃ saṃbandhā iti bahu kalpyam| `dharmikalpanāto dharmakalpanāyā laghutvam' iti nyāyastu kalpanīyādhikyāpekṣaḥ| kiṃ ca ghaṭadvaye yāvadviśeṣābhāvasattve'pi rūpasāmānyābhāvabuddhyanudayād aikādhikaraṇyāvacchedenāpyabhāvā viśeṣaṇīyāḥ| tathā cātigauravam| api ca vyāsajyavṛttidharmagrahe yāvadāśrayagrahastadbhedagrahaśca hetuḥ| agṛhīteṣvabhinnatayā vā gṛhīteṣu vastrādiṣu dvitvādibuddhyanudayāt| tathā ca yāvadabhāvatadbhedāgrahe prathamata eva nīrūpa iti dhīrna syāt| vyāsajyavṛttisāmānyapratiyogitākatvasyāgrahaṇāt| ataḥ sāmānyābhāvasya prāmāṇikatvāt kathaṃ tatparāhatiriti -- cet, atra brūmaḥ -- evaṃ tarhi sāmānyaprakāreṇa viśeṣābhāvāpratīterjñānaviśeṣaprāgabhāvo na jānāmīti dhiyo jñānatvāvacchinnapratiyogitāko na viṣaya iti siddhaṃ naḥ samīhitam| na hi prāgābhāvo'pi kaścitsāmānyābhāvo'sti| yena tatpratiyogitā sāmānyadharmeṇāvacchidyeta, viśeṣābhāvapratiyogitā tu tattad‌ghaṭatvādinā viśeṣeṇāvacchidyate| na ca tena tena rūpeṇa bhaviṣyaddhaṭādi jñātuṃ śakyam| tajjanmāntaraṃ tu tattadrūpeṇa tajjñānasambhave'pi na prāgabhāvadhīḥ pratyakṣā syāt| tadānīṃ prāgabhāvāsattvāt| pratyakṣasya viṣayajanyatvāt| sāmānyaprakārakajñānaṃ ca na viśeṣābhāvajñāne heturityuktam| pratiyogitāvacchedakaprakārakapratiyogijñānasyābhāvatvaprakārakābhāvajñāne hetutvāt| tasyānumānagamyatve'pi `na jānāmi' iti dhiyo'parokṣāyastadviṣayatvāyogāt| avyabhicāriliṅgādyabhāvāttadanumānamapi dūranirastameva| nanu -- `idaṃ mā bhūt' itīcchāviṣayatayā tatsiddhiḥ| na, prāgabhāvasya svarūpato'sādhyatvena pratiyogijanakavighaṭanena tatsaṃbandhasyevātyantābhāvasambandhasyāpi sādhyatvāttenaivānyathāsiddheḥ|
atha -- utpannasya dvitīyakṣaṇe punarutpattyabhāvāttatpūrvakṣaṇe sāmāgryabhāvo vācyaḥ| sa ca prāgabhāvābhāvādeva| anyahetūnāṃ sattvāditi -- cenna| sāmayikātyantābhāvenaivānyathāsiddheḥ| utpannasyaiva svotpattivirodhitvācca| api ca sāmagrī kāryasattve prayojikā, na tu tasyā'dyakālasambandharūpotpattāvapi| ādyakālasambandho hi svasamānakālīnapadārthadhvaṃsānādhārakālādhāratvam| tatra sāmagrī kāryasya kālādhāratvāṃśamātre prayojikā, na tu viśeṣaṇāṃśe'pi| tasya tādṛkpadārthadhvaṃsasāmagrīvirahādeva siddheḥ| pākajarūpādibhedo'pyagnisaṃyogabhedāt pūrvarūpādidhvaṃsabhedādvā, na tu prāgabhāvabhedāt| pratiyogibhedaṃ vinā prāgabhāvabhedāyogācca| nāpyupādānatvavyavasthā tatra mānam| tantutvādinaiva tatsiddheḥ| anyathā prāgabhāvasya sambandhiviśeṣo'pi kutaḥ siddhyet? na ca tadatyantābhāvavataḥ kathaṃ tadupādānatvam? sambandhāntareṇa tvayā'pyabhyupagamātsamayāvacchedatadanavacchedābhyāṃ vailakṣaṇyābhyupagamāccetyalamativistareṇa|
evaṃ sāmānyābhāvo'pi gauravaparāhata eva| tathā hi -- sāmānyāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvaṃ, abhāvaḥ, tasya ca tattadadhikaraṇasambandhā iti trayaṃ vā kalpyatām? klṛptattadadhikaraṇasambandhānāmekādhikaraṇavṛttitvāvacchedena siddhānāmabhāvānāṃ sāmānyāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvaṃ, tasya ca vyāsajyavṛttitvamiti dvayaṃ vā kalpyatām| tatrottaraḥ pakṣa eva prekṣāvadbhyo rocate| ādyakṣaṇe `ghaṭo nīrūpaḥ' iti pratīteḥ sarvasiddhatvād| yāvadaśrayatadbhedagrahasya dvitvādigrahe hetutve'pi uktapratiyogitāgrahe hetutvānabhyupagamāt| kāryonneyadharmāṇāṃ yathākāryamunnayanāt| na caivaṃ -- atilāghavāt klṛptānāmadhikaraṇānāmevābhāvadhīhetutvamastu, kiṃ viśeṣābhāvairapīti -- vācyam| asmākamiṣṭāpatteḥ| ghaṭābhāvo netyādāvatiriktābhāvasya tvayā'pyanabhyupagamena bhāvasyāpyabhāvatvaprakārakapramāhetutvasyobhayavādisiddhatvāt|
yadapi kaścidāha -- pratiyogitāvacchedakabhedasyābhāvabhedaniyāmakatvādviśeṣābhāvānyasāmānyābhāvasiddhiḥ| anyathā abhāvabhedāsiddheḥ| pratiyogibhedasyābhāvabhedakatve ekaghaṭapratiyogikasya prāgabhāvādicatuṣṭayasyābhedaprasaṅgad| avacchedakabhedāttu tadbheda na ko'pi doṣaḥ| kvacittādātmyasya, kvacitsaṃsargasya, kvacit pūrvāparakālīnatattad‌ghaṭatvādeśca bhedāt -- iti| tanna| saṃsargapratiyogiviśeṣaṇasādhāraṇasyaikasyāvacchedakatvasya durvacatvāt| tādātmyādeśca pratiyogitāvacchedakatve mānābhāvāt| bhedasiddhistu bhāvavadabhāvasyāpi viruddhadharmādhyāsādeva| avacchedakabhedasyābhāvabhedaniyāmakatvaṃ liṅgavidhayā tajjñāpakatvameva vācyam, na tu tajjanakatvam| tacca na| vipakṣabādhakatarkābhāvena sāmānādhikaramyābhāvena ca vyāpterevāsiddheḥ| ata eva -- taditaradharmāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvaṃ tadavacchinnapratiyogikatākānyatvavyāpyamityapi -- nirastam| evaṃ cāvṛttīnāṃ gaganādīnāṃ samaniyatānāṃ cā'nyeṣāṃ dharmāṇāmeka evātyantābhāvaḥ, yugapadvinaṣṭānāmutpannānāṃ vā samānadeśānāmasati bādhake eka eva dhvaṃsaḥ prāgabhāvo vā, vyadhikaraṇadharmāvacchinnapratiyogitāko'pi cedabhāvaḥ prāmāṇikaḥ tadā tasyaikasyaiva pratiyogitāḥ savairaiva vyadhikaraṇaiḥ sarvaiśca samānādhikaraṇaiḥ saṃbandhairevāvacchidyantām| ākāśābhāva eva vā tathā''stām| ekenaivopapattāvabhāvabhedakalpane mānābhāvāt| na ca -- evameka eva jagatītale bhavatvabhāvaḥ| sa eva tattadavacchedakadeśakālādibhedena tattadvyavahārabhedaṃ janayiṣyatīti kimadhikakalpanayeti -- vācyam| upapadyate cedastu| prakṛte tu na bādhakaṃ kiñcit| ata eva vaiśeṣikāṇāṃ svābhyupagatakālapadārthasyaiva sarvavyavahārahetutvopapattau na padārthāntarasiddhirityadvaitavādino vadanti| tadevaṃ `ahamajñaḥ' iti jñānasyābhāvajñānasāmagrīvilakṣaṇasāmagrījanyatvādabhāvavilakṣaṇaviṣayatvaṃ siddham|
