yattu śabdajajñānasvarūpameva śravaṇaṃ vidheyamiti, tanna, āpātadarśanasya tadvinā jāyamānatvāt| na ca tadbhinne prativākyamasya vākyasyāyamartha iti siddhārthopadeśenānekaśākhāsthavākyārthajñānam, vedāntarūpavākyārthavicārasya sāmānyataḥ sādhanatvena prāptatvāt| na ca yathā ghaṭādāvitarabhede siddhe'pi pṛthivīirebhyo bhidyata ityatra na siddhasādhanam, yathā ca sthānaprakaraṇādhikaraṇoktarītyā rājasūyāntargateṣṭipaśusomeṣu prātisvikarūpeṇa codakata itikartavyatāprāptāvapi rājasūyarūpe tadaprāptiḥ, tathā vedāntavicāratvopahite brahmajñānahetutāyā aprāpterapūrvavidhiteti vācyam, sāmānyataḥ prāptasādhanasādhyabhāvamanādṛtya viśeṣopādhinā āprāptasādhanatvaprāptaye apūrvavidhyaṅgīkāre jyotiṣṭomādivākyavicāre'pi tena rūpeṇa tadvākyārthajñānaṃ pratyaprāptasādhanatāsiddhaye apūrvavidhyantarakalpanāpatteḥ|
etena nididhyāsanasyāvyaktasvabhāvabrahmasākṣātkārahetutā nānyataḥ prāpteti sa tāvadapūrvavidhiḥ, tathā ajñātādau nididhyāsanāsambhavena śravaṇamananayornididhyāsane liṅgādeva prāptāvapi niyataviṣayakanididhyāsanaviṣayaguṇānyaguṇeṣu liṅgena tayoraprāpteḥ tadvidhirapyapūrvavidhireva, evaṃ ca śravaṇamananayornididhyāsitavyaguṇāṃśe dṛṣṭadvāreṇa, anyatra tvadṛṣṭadvāreṇa nididhyāsanāṅgateti -- nirastam| śravaṇādīnāṃ nirguṇaviṣayatayā saguṇaviṣayatvābhāvāt, viparītāṅgāṅgibhāvasya sthāpitatvācca, sūkṣmārthagocaranididhyāsanasya tādṛśārthaviṣayakasākṣātkārahetutāyā dṛṣṭatvenāpūrvatvābhāvācca|| 7 ||
nanu atra śrūyamāṇaliṅādeḥ kā gatiriti cet, śilātalaprayuktakṣurataikṣṇyavat kuṇṭhībhāvāt `jartilayavāgvā vā juhuyā'dityādāvivārthavādatvameva| na ca `patnyavekṣitamājyaṃ bhavati', `viduṣā karma kartavyaṃ tasmāt paśyeta nityaśaḥ|' ityādinā jñānasyāpi vidhānaṃ dṛṣṭamiti vācyam, tatra taddhetvindriyasaṃyogādervidheyatvād ananyagatyā lakṣaṇāyā iṣṭatvāt| na ca `na vidhau paraḥ śabdārtha' ityanena virodhaḥ `gobhiḥ śrīṇīta matsara'mityādau vidhivākye'pi lakṣaṇādarśanena tasyautsargikatvād, arthavādānurodhenavidhau na paraḥ śabdārtha ityarthakatvācca| na ca sākṣātkṛtisādhyatvasya indriyasamprayoge'pyabhāvād indriyaniṣṭhakriyādvārā paramparayā kṛtisādhyatvasya jñāne'pi sambhavād, anicchato durgandhādijñānavad aniṣṭhasamprayogasyāpi darśanācca jñānasāmyaṃ saṃyogasyeti vācyam, svargādāviva svecchādhīnakṛtasādhyatvasya vidheyatāprayojakasya jñāne abhāvāt, samprayogasya tu tadvaiparītyena viśeṣāt| na ca `bhakṣehī'ti bhakṣamantreṣu tṛpteranuniṣpāditatayā'nanuṣṭheyatvena tṛptiprakāśabhāgasya tatra vibhajya viniyogābhāve'pi avekṣaṇasyānuṣṭheyatvāt tatprakāśakabhāgasya avekṣaṇo viniyoga iti tṛtīyādhyāyoktivirodha iti vācyam, indriyasamprayogarūpāvekṣaṇasya tṛptyādivadanuniṣpāditvābhāvenānuṣṭheyatayā tatprakāśakabhāgasya tasminvibhajya viniyoga ityevamparatvāt| yacca ekaśrotragatānekagakāreṣu bubhutsāprayatnābhyāmabhīṣṭagakārajñānadarśanena jñānasyāpi vidheyatvamiti, tanna, anabhimataviṣayavaimukhyasyaiva bubhutsāprayatnasādhyatayā jñānaṃ prati tayorajanakatvāt| na ca -- "yadeva vidyayā karotī"ti vidyāyā aṅgatvaśravaṇād avihitasya cāṅgatvānupapattyā vidyāyā vidheyatvamiti vācyam, tasya vākyasyod‌gīthopāsanāprakaraṇasthatayā tatratyavidyāpadasya upāsanāparatayā vihitatvenāṅgatvāvirodhāt, `tannirdhāraṇāniyamastad‌dṛṣṭe'rityatra vidyāyā aṅgāvabaddhāyā aṅgatvanirāse'pi na vidheyatvahāniḥ|
nanu yathā durgandhādijñānasyecchāviṣayatvābhāve'pi brahmajñānasya tadviṣayatvam, yathā ca jñānāntarasya niyamādṛṣṭāsādhyatve'pi brahmajñānasya tatsādhyatvam, yathā parvatādijñānasya nayanonmīlane sati prayatnāntaranirapekṣatve'pi dhruvārundhatyādijñāne tatsāpekṣatvam, tathā'tisūkṣmabrahmajñānasya icchāprayatnasādhyatvasambhava iti cenna, prāmāṇikadṛṣṭavijātīyakiñciddharmadarśanenāprāmāṇikavaijātyasya kalpayitumaśakyatvāt, prayatnānvayavyatirekayośca jñānasādhanajanane upakṣīṇatayā dhruvārundhātyādinidarśanānyathāsiddhatayā tannyāyena brahmajñāne kṛtisādhyatvasya vaktumaśakyatvācca| na ca śāstrārthajñānasya niyamena puṃtantratvadarśanād brahmaṇaśca śāstrārthatvāt tajjñānasya puṃtantratvamiti vācyam, tatrāpi jñānasādhanasyaiva puṃtantratvāt| yacca praśastāpraśastajñānayorvidhiniṣedhadarśanam, tadapi jñānakāraṇaviṣayakameva| yacca icchādinā utpattinirodhayoranubhavaḥ, so'pi karaṇavyāpāraviṣayaka eva| na ca evaṃ nāmādiṣu brahmopāsanāyāṃ tvayā vidheyatvenāṅgīkṛtāyāṃ puṃtantratvaṃ na sidhyet, tatrāpi manodhāraṇādereva puṃtantratvasya vaktuṃ śakyatvāditi vācyam, jñānavidhyuktānupapattestatrābhāvena nirodhādīnāmanyaparatvasya vaktumaśakyatvāt| na ca uktajñānarūpaśravaṇādiṣvatiprayatnasādhyatvasyānubhavasiddhatvād vidheyatvamiti vācyam, anubhavānyathāsiddheruktatvāt| na ca tvayāpi nididhyāsanasya parokṣajñānasantānarūpasya vidheyatvaṃ vadatā santānināṃ jñānānāmapi vidheyatvamaṅgīkartavyameva tadavidhāne santatividhānānupapatteriti vācyam, āvṛttiguṇayogasyaiva vidheyatvād upaniṣadamāvartayedityatreva | na ca śravaṇādeḥ jñānānātmakatve śravaṇaṃ yāvadajñānamityajñānavirodhitvasmṛteḥ śravaṇenājñānaṃ nivṛttamityanubhavasya cānupapattiriti vācyam, āvṛttiguṇayogavākyārthapramityupayuktatātparyanirṇayadvārā upakramādiliṅgavicārātmakaśravaṇāderajñānādivirodhitayā smṛtyanubhavayorupapatteḥ| `vijñāya prajñāṃ kurvīte'tyādau jñānavidhāvuktānupapattyā prajñāśabdasya vicāralākṣaṇikatvopapatteḥ| na ca tvayā vidheyatvenāṅgīkṛtasya dhyānasya jñānabhinnatve tasya svaviṣaye vyavahārajanakatvaṃ na syāditi vācyam, tadanyajñānenaiva tatkāraṇatayā''vaśyakena tadviṣaye vyavahāropapatteḥ pramāṇavato'sya gauravasyādoṣatvāt| ata eva dhyānaviṣaye jānāmītyanubhavasmaraṇe tadbhinnajñānaprayuktatayā vyākhyāte| tasyābhidhyānāditi śrutau dhyānasya māyākhyāvidyānivartakatve'pi taduktiḥ dhyānānantarabhāvisākṣātkāradvārābhiprāyeti na tayā virodhaḥ| yattūpāsītetyupakramya `ya evaṃ vede'tyupasamhāreṇa tvayāpi vidheyatvenāṅgīkṛtāyāmupāsanāyāṃ vididhātuprayogeṇa upāsanāyāḥ jñānatvāvagamād anyatrāpi jñānatvaṃ na vidheyatvavirodhīti, tanna, uktānupapattyā videstatrāmukhyatvenaiva jñānatvāgamakatvād dhātvanekārthatvena vā| na ca mānatantratāmātreṇa kathamapuṃtantratā pratigrahītrāditantrasyāpi dānādestaddarśanāditi vācyam; na hyanyatantratāmātreṇāvidheyatvaṃ brūmaḥ kintu pumicchābhiryatkāraṇe sati anyathā kartumaśakyatvam| jñānasya puruṣatantratābhaṅgaḥ|
kiṃ ca vidhīyamānaṃ jñānaṃ kiṃ śābdam ? kiṃ vā tadabhyāsaḥ ? yadvā tattvamanapekṣyāropitaviṣayatayā dhyānam ? kiṃ vāparokṣyaphalakaṃ jñānāntaram ? nādyaḥ, karmakāṇḍa iva gṛhītasaṅgateḥ svataḥ siddhatvena kṛtyasādhyatayā vidhyaviṣayatvāt| na ca karmakāṇḍe'pyāpātajanyasya svataḥ siddhatve'pi vimarśajanyasya kṛtisādhyatvam, anyathā adhyayanavidheruttarakratuvidhervā tadākṣepakatā na syāditi vācyam, tajjanakavicāra eva vidheyatāprayojakakṛtisādhyatvaparyavasānena tādṛśajñāne'pyasambhavāt| na dvitīyaḥ, sa kiṃ mokṣāya vidhīyate ? sākṣātkārāya vā ? nādyaḥ, tasya naiyogikatvena svargādivadanityatvāpātāt| na ca bandhadhvaṃsasya naiyogikatve'pi dhvaṃsatvāt śrutyādibalācca nityatvopapattiriti vācyam, na hi mannaya iva tvannaye'pi bandhadhvaṃsamātraṃ muktiḥ kintu lokāntaraprāptiḥ, tasyāmuktadoṣatādavasthyāpattiḥ| na cānāvṛttiśrutyā nityatvam, `tadyathehakarmacito lokaḥ kṣīyate evamevāmutra puṇyacito lokaḥ kṣīyate' iti śrutyā naiyogikasyānityatvāvagamāt| na caitacchrativirodhena tasyānityatvābodhakatvam, `yatkṛtakaṃ tadanityam' iti yuktyupodvalitatayā prābalyāt| na dvitīyaḥ, tat kiṃ dṛṣṭaphalakam ? adṛṣṭaphalakaṃ vā ? nādyaḥ, parokṣajñānābhyāsena tattvasākṣātkārādarśanāt| na dvitīyaḥ, pramāṇāyattatvāttasya| ata eva na tṛtīyaḥ, dhyānasya jñānarūpatvābhāvācca| na caturthaḥ śabdasyāpi prāmāṇyābhāvāpātād brahmaṇi pramāṇībhūtajñānakartavyatāyāmeva prāmāṇyāt| na ca mahātātparyamādāya brahmaṇyapi prāmāṇyam, tādṛśajñānasyāpi pramāṇāyattatayā vidhiphalatvābhāvāt| tasmāt patnyavekṣitamājyamityādāvapi jñānasyāvidhānād dhyānasyāpi vijñānavyatirekasādhanāt sūkṣmaśāstrārthabuddhau kṛtyanvayavyatirekayostatsādhanaviṣayatayānyathāsiddhatvācca nātmajñānaṃ vidhātuṃ śakyate| tadevaṃ śravaṇasya jñānarūpatve vidheyatvānupapattiḥ| tasmāt jñānavijātīyaṃ śravaṇam aparokṣajñānajanakaśabdetikartavyatārūpavicārātmakaṃ manananididhyāsanāṅgakaṃ prameyāvagamaṃ pratyaṅgatayā pradhānabhūtamaparokṣajñānaphalakatayā vidhīyata iti siddham|| ityadvaitasiddhau jñānavidhibhaṅgaḥ|| 8 ||
nanu kathamaparokṣajñānajanakatvaṃ śabdasya ? mānābhāvāditi cenna, "taddhāsya vijajñau tamasaḥ pāraṃ darśayatī"tyādeḥ "vedāntavijñānasuniścitārthā" ityādeśca mānatvāt| pūrvavākye tajjanakāparokṣajñānasyopadeśamātrasādhyatvokteḥ, dvitīyaśrutau śābdajñānasya vipadena viśeṣaviṣayatvasya lābhāt supadenāparokṣatvokteḥ| na ca vijajñāvitītyādeḥ parokṣajñānenāpi caritārthatā, "tamasaḥ pāraṃ darśayatī"tyuttaravākyasvarasena aparokṣajñānaparatvasiddheḥ| na ca grāmamārgopadeṣṭari grāmaṃ darśayatītivat paramparayā prayojakatayopacāraḥ sākṣātsādhanatve bādhakābhāvena tasyātrānyāyyatvād, upadeśātiriktakāraṇasya nāradasanatkumārākhyāyikāyāmapratīteśca| na ca manasaivānudraṣṭavyamityādiśrutivirodhaḥṣa tasyāścittaikāgryaparatvāt| na ca supadasyāprāmāṇyaśaṅkāvirahaparatvena dvitīyavākyena tenāparokṣarūpatāprāptiḥ, anyathā vedāntabodhyasya vicārakartavyatādeścāparokṣatvāpātāditi vācyam, niścitapadenaivāprāmāṇyaśaṅkāvirahāderlabdhatayā supadasyātatparatvāt| nāpi vedāntabodhyasya brahmātiriktasyāpyevamāparokṣyāpattiḥ, arthapadasya mukhyatastātparyaviṣayaparatvād, vedāntabodhyatāyā brahmamātraparyavasannatvācca|
evamanumānamapyatra mānam -- `aparokṣatvam, tattvamasyādivākyajanyajñānavṛtti, aparokṣajñānaniṣṭhātyantābhāvāpratiyogitvāt, jñānatvavat'| na ca karmakāṇḍajanyajñānavṛttītyevamapi sādhyeteti vācyam, vipakṣabādhakasattvāsattvābhyāṃ viśeṣāt| tathā hi tattvamasyādivākyasyāparokṣajñānājanakatve aparokṣabhramanivṛttirna syāt| na ca manasaivāparokṣajñānam, manasaḥ kutrāpyasādhāraṇyena pramākaraṇatvābhāvād, ātmanaḥ svaprakāśatvāt sukhādīnāṃ sākṣivedyatvāt| na ca śabde aparokṣajñānajanakatvavad anyatrāklṛptameva manasi tatkalpanīyam| evaṃ hi sarvāṃśasyaiva manasi kalpyatvena viśeṣāt| na caivaṃ jyotiṣṭomādiviṣayakakarmakāṇḍajanyajñāne kalpakamasti| tatra hi kalpanīyamanuṣṭhānāya vā ? phalāya vā ? nādyaḥ, parokṣajñānādeva tatsambhavāt| tata evānuṣṭhānāt phalasiddherna dvitīyo'pi|
na ca vimataḥ śabdo nāparokṣadhīhetuḥ śabdatvāditi pratisādhanam, `daśamastvamasī'tyādāveva vyabhicārāt| na ca tatrāpīndriyameva karaṇaṃ śabdastatsahakārīti vācyam, kvacit bahulatame tamasi locanahīnasyāpi tadvākyādaparokṣabhramanivartakasya daśamo'smītyaparokṣajñānasya darśanāt| yatrāpīndriyasadbhāvaḥ, tatrāpi tadaprayojakameva| na ca dharmavāṃstvamasi parvato'gnimānityādau viśeṣyāparokṣatve'pi viśeṣaṇapārokṣyavad atrāpi daśamatve pārokṣyamastviti vācyam, atra parokṣatve aparokṣabhramānivṛttiprasaṅgāt|
nanu evamapi śabdasyāparokṣajñānajanakatvaṃ kiṃ svābhāvikam ? utāparokṣaviṣayanimittakam ? nādyaḥ, atiprasaṅgāt| na dvitīyaḥ, jīvāḥ paramātmano na bhidyante ātmatvādityādinā jāyamānānumiteḥ śravaṇāt prāgāpatato vedāntajanyāyā bhāṣāprabandhajanyāyā anadhītavedāntajanyāyā aikyapratīteścāparokṣyāpātāt śravaṇaniyamāderaniyamāt| kiñcārthasyāparokṣyaṃ na tāvadaparokṣabuddhirūpatvam, brahmaṇyasya sattve'pi daśamatvādāvabhāvāt, caitrāparokṣajñāne maitrasya śabdādinā āparokṣyādarśanācca| nāpyaparokṣavyavahāraviṣayatvam, vyavahārāparokṣyasya tādṛgarthabhedaviṣayakatvarūpatve anyonyāśrayāt, aparokṣo'yamityevaṃrūpatve ajñānāvṛte'pi tadabhāvāt, tvayāpi na prakāśata ityādivyavahārārthamevāvaraṇakalpanād, uktavyavahārayogyatvarūpatve vyavahitaghaṭe śabdādaparokṣajñānaprasaṅgād, aparokṣajñānajanyatvarūpatve ca vakṣyamāṇapakṣāntarbhāvāt| tasmādarthasyāparokṣadhīviṣayatvamevāparokṣatvaṃ vācyam| tatra caitajjñānaviṣayatvena taduktāvanyonyāśrayaḥ, jñānāntarābhiprāye tu keṣāñcidaparokṣe svargādāvasmākaṃ śabdādaparokṣadhīprasaṅgāt| ekapumabhiprāye tu pūrvāparokṣe śabdādinā idānīmaparokṣadhīprasaṅgāt| ekakālābhiprāye pratyakṣāgnau liṅgācchabdādvā āparokṣyaṃ syāditi cenna, yaṃ śābdabodhamādāya yasya bodhyatvam, tatsākṣātkārārthaṃ tadabhinnārthāvagāhitvanimittakamityuktadoṣānavakāśāt|
na ca evaṃ pratyakṣāntarbhāvaḥ śabdasya syāditi vācyam, bodhyabhinnārthakaśabdātiriktatve sati pratyakṣapramākaraṇatvasya pratyakṣasyāntarbhāve tantratvāt| nanu -- "manasaivānudraṣṭavya"mityāderiva manaḥkaraṇatāpratipādakasya prakṛte abhāvāt anaupadeśikaṃ śabdasya sākṣātkārakaraṇatvamiti cenna, "taṃ tvaupaniṣadaṃ puruṣaṃ pṛcchāmī"tyādau tatra sādhuriti tadanyāsādhutve sati tatsādhutvarūpasādhvarthavihitataddhitaśrutyā eva mānatvāt| nanu manasaḥ karaṇatve'pi aupaniṣadatvasya nididhyāsanāpekṣitatayā anyathāsiddhiḥ, na, "yanmanasā na manuta" iti manasaḥ karaṇatvaniṣedhāt| na ca "yato vāco nivartanta" iti śabdasyāpi karaṇatvānupapattiḥ, aupaniṣadatvaśrutyanusāreṇa tasyāḥ śaktyā abodhakatvaparatvāt| taduktaṃ -- `cakitamabhidhatte śrutirapī'ti| na ca manasaivānudraṣṭavyamiti tṛtīyāśrutyanusāreṇa na manuta ityasyaivāpakvamanoviṣayatayā'nyathānayanasāmyamiti vācyam, evaṃ sāmye'pi manasaḥ karaṇatve hyadhikakalpanā| śabdasya karaṇatve tvalpakalpaneti viśeṣāt| tasmāttatvamasyādivākyasyāparokṣajñānajanakatvādavidyānivṛttyātmakamokṣasādhanabrahmasākṣātkārāya mananādyaṅgakaṃ śravaṇamaṅgi niyamavidhiviṣaya iti siddham|| ityadvaitasiddhau śabdādaparokṣopapattiḥ||
viśveśvarākhyasya guroḥ prasādādadvaitasiddhirmadhusūdanasya|
abhūdabhūmiḥ khalu dūṣaṇānāṃ guṇairameyairavagumphitaśrīḥ||
sasambhramamapekṣayā paraguṇonnatirduḥsahā
nitāntamanapekṣayā nijapumarthahāniḥ parā|
ataḥ sumatayo yathānayamupekṣya durmatsaraṃ
prayojanavaśānugāḥ kuruta matkṛtau satkṛtim||
iti śrīmatparamahaṃsaparivrājakācāryaśrīviśveśvarasarasvatīśrīcaraṇaśiṣya śrīmadhusūdanasarasvatīviracitāyāmadvaitasiddhau śravaṇādinirūpaṇaṃ nāma tṛtīyaḥ paricchedaḥ|| 9 ||
<4>
nanu muktistāvadavidyānivṛttirna sambhavati| tathā hi -- sā kimātmarūpā ? tadbhinnā vā ? nādyaḥ, asādhyatvāpatteḥ dvitīye'pi kiṃ satī ? mithyā vā ? ādye advaitahāniḥ, dvitīye avidyātatkāryānyataratvāpattirivi cenna, caramavṛttyupalakṣitasyātmano'jñānahānirūpatvāt| tathā copalakṣaṇasādhyatayaiva mukterapi sādhyatā| na copalakṣaṇanivṛttyā mukterapi nivṛtteḥ, pāke nivṛtte'pi pācakānivṛttidarśanāt| taduktam -- 
"nivṛttirātmā mohasya jñātatvenopalakṣitaḥ|
upalakṣaṇanāśe'pi syānmuktiḥ pācakādivat||" iti |
na ca vṛttyupalakṣitasya paścādiva pūrvamapi sattvena mohakāle'pi taddhānyāpattiḥ, pūrvamasiddhasyopalakṣaṇatvāyogāt, nahi pākasaṃbandhāt pūrvaṃ pācako bhavati tathā vyavahniyate vā| yattu pākakartṛtvameva pācakatvam, tadā apacati tatprayogo bhūtapūrvanyāyenaupacārikaḥ| yadi tu pākakartṛtāvacchedakāvacchinnatvaṃ tatkartṛtvātyantābhāvānadhikaraṇatvaṃ vā, tad‌dvayamapi paścādasti| na caivaṃ muktāvātmātiriktaṃ yogyatvādikamasti, cinmātraṃ tu prāgapyasti ityasādhyatāpattiḥ, kākopalakṣitatvavad vṛttyupalakṣitatvasyādhikatve saviśeṣatāpattiḥ iti, tanna, upalakṣyasvarūpasyāsādhyatve'pi upalakṣaṇagatasādhyatvopapatteḥ, ghaṭākāśe utpattivat| yadvā avidyānivṛttistadvirodhivṛttireva yāvatkāryotpattivirodhikāryameva dhvaṃsa ityaṅgīkārāt| na ca vṛttau naṣṭāyāṃ virodhinaḥ kāryāntarasyānudayāt tadāpi dhvaṃsasattvena sa na dhvaṃsa iti vācyam, yāvadvirodhikāryodayameva tathātvād, yāvadvibhāgaṃ tasya dhvaṃsarūpatve'pi vibhāgadhvaṃsasyādhikaraṇarūpatāvaccaramavṛttiparyantaṃ virodhikāryarūpatve'pi dhvaṃsasya caramavṛttidhvaṃsasyādhikaraṇarūpataiva|
nanu iyaṃ prakriyā kimanyatra ? ihaiva vā ? nādyaḥ bimbapratibimbaikyājñānanivṛttirapi jñātatadaikyarūpeti tadaikyadhīkāle sopādhikatadbhedabhramopādānājñānānuvṛttyayogāt| nāntyaḥ, niyāmakābhāvāt| na ceha nivṛtterjñātādhiṣṭhānātireke viśvamithyātvaśrutiparyālocanayā nivṛtterapi nivṛttyāpattirniyāmikā, tasyā jñānādviśvanivṛttiparatvena svatātparyaviṣayanivṛttītaramithyātvaparatvāditi cenna, na tāvadādye doṣaḥ, sopādhikabhrame upādhivirahakālīnasyaiva tasya tathātvāt| nāpi dvitīyaḥ, neti netītiśruteḥ svārasyenātmātiriktasarvanivṛttāveva tātparyāt| na ca vṛttyupalakṣita ātmā jīvanmuktāvapyastīti tadāpi mokṣāpattiḥ, muktimātrāpādanasyeṣṭatvāt, paramamukteścaramasākṣātkāropalakṣitātmasvarūpatvena tadāpādakābhāvāt| na ca camarasākṣātkāranivṛtterātmatve'sādhyatvāpattiḥ, avidyānivṛtterasādhyatve'pravṛttyāpattivad atra tadabhāvāt| na ca jīvanmuktiprayojakavṛttyapekṣayā paramamuktiprayojakavṛttau ānandābhivyaktigataviśeṣābhāve caramakṣaṇena caramaśvāsena vā upalakṣita ātmā muktiriti kiṃ na syāditi vācyam, prārabdhakarmaprayuktavikṣepāvikṣepābhyāmabhivyaktiviśeṣasyāṅgīkārāt|
etena vedāntaśravaṇādisādhyaḥ pumartho vācyaḥ, na ca sa tvanmate vaktuṃ, śakyaḥ, muktyanusyūtasukhajñaptirūpasyātmanaḥ puruṣārthatve'pyasādhyatvāt, vṛtteḥ sādhyatve'pi svato'pumarthatvāt| tasmādātmavyatirikta eva vṛttisādhya āvaraṇanivṛttirūpaḥ ānandaprakāśaḥ pumartho vācyaḥ| tathā ca kathamātmaiva nivṛttiriti apāstam, prāptaprāptirūpatayā phalasyānandaprakāśasya svarūpato'sādhyatve'pi tattirodhāyakājñānanivartakavṛtteḥ sādhyatvamātreṇa sādhyatvopapatteḥ, kaṇṭhagatacāmīkarādau tathā darśanāt| tasmādajñānahānirātmasvarūpaṃ tadākārā vṛttirveti siddham| ye tu pañcamaprakārādipakṣāḥ, te tu mandabuddhivyutpādanārthā iti na tatsamarthanamarthayāmaḥ|| ityadvaitasiddhau avidyānivṛttinirūpaṇam|| 1 ||
avidyānivartakaṃ ca yadyapi na svaprakāśabrahmarūpajñānamātram, tasya tatsādhakatvāt, tathāpi śravaṇādisādhyāparokṣavṛttisamārūḍhaṃ tadeva| ata evaitadapāstam -- kiṃ svaprakāśacidavidyānivartikā ? tadākārā aparokṣavṛttirvā ? nādyaḥ, tasyā idānīmapi sattvāt| na dvitīyaḥ, asatyātsatyasiddherayogād ajñāne na jānāmīti jñaptirūpacidvirodhasyānubhavenājñāptirūpavṛttivirodhasyāsaṃbhavāt, citā prakāśamānasukhādāvajñānādarśanācca| kiñca icchānivartyadveṣavajjātinibandhanavṛttinivartyasyājñānasyāviśeṣeṇa sattvāpattiḥ iti vṛttyupārūḍhacito vā citpratibimbadhāriṇyā vṛttervā nivartakatvāt| na cāsatyāyāḥ satyotpādakatvavirodhaḥ, abhāvasya bhāvajanakatvavadasya saṃbhavāt, prātibhāsikasya vyāvahārikasukhajanakatvadarśanācca| nāpi na jānāmīti jñāptirūpacidvirodhitvānubhavavirodhaḥ cidasaṃsṛṣṭavṛttervirodhitvasyānaṅgīkārāt| yattūktaṃ dveṣavat satyatvamiti tanna, adhiṣṭhānatattvasākṣātkāratvanibandhananivartakatvasya śuktyādijñānavadatrāpi saṃbhavena tannivartyarūpyavat satyatvānāpatteḥ| yattūktaṃ caramavṛtterghaṭādivṛttyā cidviṣayatve aviśeṣaḥ iti, tanna, avacchinnānavacchinnaviṣayatayā viśeṣāt| yattu svanivartakatve sthitivirodhaḥ svopādānanivartakatvaṃ tvadṛṣṭacaram iti, tanna, anyatrādṛṣṭasyāpi pramāṇabalādatraiva kalpanāt| tathā hi -- `māyāṃ tu prakṛtiṃ vidyā'dityavagatamāyopādānakatvasyāpyātmatattvasākṣātkārasya `tarati śokamātmavit so'vidyāgranthiṃ vikiratīha somye'tyādinā tannivartakatvasya ca pramitatvāt| vṛttipratibimbatacito nivartakatve tu noktavacasaḥ śaṅkāpi| taduktam -- 
"tṛṇāderbhāsikāpyeṣā sūryadīptistṛṇaṃ dahet|
sūryakāntamupāruhya tannyāyaṃ citi yojayet||"
na ca aparokṣavṛttau satyāṃ cidapratibimbanibandhananivṛttivilambādarśanāt na viśiṣṭe nivartakateti vācyam, śuddhajaḍasya śuddhacitaśca jaḍatayā tadbhāsakatayā cājñānānivartakatayā viśiṣṭe nivartakatāyā āvaśyakatvāt| yattu nivartakaṃ jñānamapi na śuddhaviṣayakam, tasyādṛśyatvāt, nāpi viśiṣṭaviṣayakam, tasyādhyastatvena bhramatvāpatteḥ iti, tanna, upahitasya viṣayatve'pi upādheraviṣayatvenābhramatvāt| yattu antyasya jñānasya kiṃ nivartakam ? svayam ? anyadvā ? nādyaḥ, anyanirapekṣapratiyogino dhvaṃsajanakatve kṣaṇikatvāpatteḥ, dagdhadārudahanasyāpi īśvarecchādinaiva nāśāt| katakarajastu na paṅkaṃ nāśayati, nāpi svam, viśleṣamātradarśanāt| nāntyaḥ| śuddhātmanaḥ kiñcidapi pratyahetutvāt, tadanyasya ca nivṛttatvād iti, tanna, tantunāśasya paṭanāśaprayojakatvadarśanena svopādānāvidyānāśasyaiva tannāśakatvāt| na ca avidyānāśe tadupalakṣitātmā vā ? vṛttirvā ? pūrvatra śuddhasyāhetutvam, dvitīye pratiyogimātrasyaiva nāśakatāparyavasānamiti vācyam, ubhayathāpyadoṣād, vṛttimādāyaiva brahmaṇa upahitatvena śuddhatvābhāvāt| pratiyoginaḥ pratiyogitvena nāśakatāyāmitarānapekṣatve hi kṣaṇikatvam, rūpāntarāvacchedena yat kāraṇaṃ tasyāpekṣaṇe na taditi upādānanāśatvāvacchinnasya svasyaiva kṣaṇikatvānāpādakaṃ nāśakatvaṃ syāt| dvitīyakāraṇanirapekṣapratiyoginaḥ pratiyogitvopādānanāśatvarūpadvayāvacchinnasya nāśakatvopapatteḥ| vastutastu avidyānivṛttevṛttirūpatayā na nivartakakhaṇḍanāvakāśaḥ, vṛttinivṛtterātmarūpatayā na tajjanakakhaṇḍanāvakāśo'pīti sarvamavadātam|| ityadvaitasiddhau avidyānivartakanirūpaṇam|| 2 ||
nanu tvanmate muktau na duḥkhocchedamātram, kintu niratiśayānandasphuraṇamapi| taduktam -- 
`tasmādavidyāstamayo nityānandapratītitaḥ|
niḥśeṣaduḥkhocchedācca puruṣārthaḥ paro mataḥ||' iti |
tatra na sukhātmatā tāvatpuruṣārthaḥ, sukhī syāmitivat sukhaṃ syāmitīcchāyā adarśanāt, pumarthatāyā icchāniyamyatvāt| anyathā bauddhamatasiddhātmanāśādirapi pumarthaḥ syāt| ata eva nāparakīyaṃ sukhaṃ pumarthaḥ, tathecchāvirahāt, gauravācca| sukhasādhane parakīye'pi svakīye pumarthatvasyāparakīye'pyasvakīye apumarthatvasya ca darśanena iṣṭatvāviśeṣāt| sukhe'pi tatkalpanācca| duḥkhatatsādhanayoḥ svakīyatayaivāpumarthatvasya darśanena sukhāderapi tathaiva puruṣārthatvācceti cenna| sukhādau hi pumarthatā nāparakīyatvaprayuktā, nāpi svakīyatvaprayuktā, gauravāt| kintu sākṣātkriyamāṇatayā, sambandhasya cānityatvasādhanapāratantryāderivāvarjanīya -- sannidhikatvāt| na ca īśvarasyāpyasmadādisukhaṃ pumarthaḥ syāditi vācyam, heyatayā ajñātatve satītyasyāpi tatra prayojakatvādīśvarādinā cātmādisukhasya heyatvenaiva jñānāt| svarūpasukhe ceṣṭāpatteḥ| na ca gauravam, svasaṃbandhitvena pumarthatāvādino'pi nilīnasukhe puruṣārthatānivṛttyarthaṃ tathāvaśyaṃ varṇanīyatvāt|
yattu sākṣātkāre'pi svakīyatayā puruṣārthatāpakṣe muktasya sukhasākṣātkārarūpatayā taṃ pratyapi tasyāpuruṣārthatāpattiḥ, svetarāsambandhitvena svasya puruṣārthatve muktasvarūpeṇa sukhena saṃsārītarāsaṃbandhena saṃsāriṇaḥ puruṣārthaprasaṅgaḥ iti, tanna| sākṣātkriyamāṇatvenaiva hi pumarthatā| muktasukhasākṣātkārasya taṃ prati pumarthatve'pi na saṃsāriṇastathā, taṃ pratyabhāsamānatvād, bhāne vā'saṃsāritveneṣṭāpattiḥ| yattu pratyakprakāśamānatvena sukhaṃ pumarthaḥ iti, padapyetena vyākhyātam| pratyak‌prakāśamānatvena sākṣātkriyamāṇatāyā evokteḥ| yattu sukhatvena prakāśamānaṃ sukhaṃ pumarthaḥ, na ca tatparamate mokṣe'sti iti, tanna, sākṣātkriyamāṇatvenaivātiprasaṅgānirāse adhikoktergauravakaratvāt||
nanu kasyāyaṃ mokṣaḥ pumarthaḥ ? kimahamarthasya ? āhosviccinmātrasya ? nādyaḥ, tvanmate'hamarthasya muktyananvayāt| nāntyaḥ, `ahaṃ muktaḥ syā'mitivaccinmātraṃ syāditīcchāyā ananubhavāditi cenna ahamarthagataṃ cidaṃśaṃ muktikālānvayinaṃ prati pumarthasya mokṣe saṃbhava ityuktaprāyatvāt| na ca sukhasya duḥkhābhāvamātratve vaiśeṣikamokṣavadapumarthatā, atireke sadvitīyatvamiti vācyam, duḥkhābhāvātireke'pyātmānatirekāt| na ca ātmanaḥ sukhamātratve prakāśamātratve vā sukhaprakāśābhāvenāpumarthatvam, ubhayātmakatve cākhaṇḍārthatvahāniriti vācyam, sukhaprakāśayorekātmarūpatayā ubhayatvasyaivābhāvāt| na cārthabhedābhāve sukhaprakāśa iti sahaprayogāyogaḥ, avidyākalpitaduḥkhajaḍātmakatvarūpavyāvartyabhedena tadupapatteḥ| yattu duḥkhābhāvasya sukhasya ca tattvato duḥkhādbhede apasiddhāntaḥ, abhede tvapumarthatā iti, tanna, duḥkhasya kalpitatvena tadbhedasya tatsamānayogakṣematayā tāttvikatvābhāvenāpasiddhāntābhāvāt| yattu svaprakāśasya sukhasya svataḥ sphuraṇe'pi duḥkhābhāvasyāsphuraṇādapumarthatā iti, tanna, duḥkhābhāvasyātmānatirekeṇātmābhinne sukhe sphurati tasyāpi sphuraṇāt, tattvenāsphuraṇasyāprayojakatāyā uktatvāt| tasmātsvaprakāśacidabhinnaṃ sukhaṃ pumarthaḥ|| iti cinmātrasya mokṣabhāgitvopapattiḥ|| ityadvaitasiddhau mukterānandarūpatvena puruṣārthatvam|| 3 ||
tacca jīvanmuktānāṃ svānubhavasiddham| jīvanmuktaśca tattvajñānena nivṛttāvidyo'pyanuvṛttadehādipratibhāsaḥ| na ca tattvajñānādavidyānāśe sadyaḥśarīrapātāpattiḥ, nivṛttasarpabhramasyāpi saṃskārād bhayakampānuvṛttivat, daṇḍasaṃyoganāśe'pi cakrabhramaṇavacca saṃskārānuvṛtteravidyānivṛttāvapi tatkāryānuvṛttisaṃbhavāt| na ca kriyājñānayoreva saṃskāra, nānyasyeti vācyam, niḥsāritapuṣpāyāṃ sampuṭikāyāṃ puṣpavāsanādarśanāt, vimato nāśaḥ saṃskāravyāptaḥ, saṃskāranāśānyatve sati nāśatvāt, jñānanāśavat ityanumānācca, saṃskāraḥ kāryo'pi dhvaṃsa iva nirupādānakaḥ avidyeva ca śuddhātmāśrita iti nāvidyāsāpekṣaḥ| na ca bhāvakāryasyādhyastasya saṃskāradehāditaddhetuprārabdhakarmādeḥ sthityarthaḥ tadupādānājñānānuvṛttyāpāta iti vācyam, vinaśyadavasthasya samavāyikāraṇaṃ vinā sthitidarśanāt| na ca kṣaṇamātrasthitāvapi kathaṃ bahukṣaṇasthitiriti vācyam, satyupapādake kṣaṇagaṇakalpanāyā aprayojakatvāt| tatra kṣaṇamātraṃ sthitiḥ, samasamayasyājanakatvāt, atra tu pratibandhakābhāvasahakṛtahetostāvatkālamabhāvāt| ata eva pūrvajñānānivṛttasyādhyastasya tadanadhikaviṣayeṇa kathaṃ nivṛttiriti nirastam, pratibandhakābhāvasahakārāsakārābhyāṃ viśeṣāt| jīvanmuktidaśāyāmānandasphūrtyāpādanamiṣṭameva, tattve jñāte dvicandrādivaddoṣādbādhitānuvṛttisaṃbhavācca| na ca tatrevātra jñānānivartyadoṣābhāvena vaiṣamyam, yāvatpratibandhakasattvaṃ jñānānivartyasya doṣasyātrāpi saṃbhavāt, sarvajñānānivartyasya tasya kutrāpyasaṃpratipatteḥ| taduktaṃ -- `na hi jātyaiva kaścid doṣo'stī'ti|
yadvā avidyāleśānuvṛttyā dehādyanuvṛttiḥ| nanu leśo nāvayavaḥ, ajñānasya niravayavatvād, ata evāvidyā dagdhapaṭanyāyena tāvattiṣṭhatītyapi nirastam, niravayave etannyāyāsaṃbhavāditi cenna, ākārasyaiva leśaśabdārthatvād, `indro māyābhiḥ pururūpa īyata' ityādiśrutyā avidyāyā anekākāratvāvagamāt| ākārinivṛttāvapyākārasyānuvṛttirvyaktinivṛttāvapi jāteriva| nanu ko'yamākāro nāma jātirvā ? śaktyādirūpo dharmo vā ? suvarṇakuṇḍalādivadavasthāviśeṣo vā ? nādyau, tayordehādibhramopādānatve avidyātvāpātāt, anupādānatve ca upādānāntarābhāvena dehādibhramotpattyayogāt ātmānyatvena jñānanivartyatvena cāvidyātatkāryānyataratvāvaśyaṃbhāvenājñāne nivatte sthityayogācca| ata eva na tṛtīyaḥ| avasthāvantaṃ vinā avasthāyāḥ sthityayogāditi cenna, anekaśaktimadavidyāyāḥ prapañce pāramārthikatvādibhramahetuśakteḥ prapañce arthakriyāsamarthatvasampādakaśakteśca prārabdhakarmasamakālīnatattvasākṣātkāreṇa nivṛttāvapi aparokṣapratibhāsayogyārthabhāsajanikāyāḥ śakteranuvṛtteḥ tadvatī avidyāpi tiṣṭhatyeveti noktadoṣāvakāśaḥ| na cāvidyāyāṃ satyāṃ kathaṃ mukta iti vyapadeśaḥ ? śaktināśamātrāt| ata eva samaye sarvaśaktimadajñānanāśaḥ tajjātīyenāpratiruddhena pratyayena| tathā ca śrutiḥ -- `tasyābhidhyānādyojanāttattvabhāvād bhūyaścānte viśvamāyānivṛtti'riti| na ca bhūya ityasya yojanādityanenānvayāt na leśānuvṛttāvasyāḥ śrutermānateti vācyam, viśeṣaṇānvayāpekṣayā viśeṣyānvayasyābhyarhitatvāt tattvabhāvādityanena vyavadhānād anta iti padavaiyarthyācca viparītayojanasyāsaṅgateḥ| na ca leśasthitau karmānuvṛttiḥ tadanuvṛttau ca jñānapratibandhena leśasthitirityanyonyāśraya iti vācyam, na tāvat jñaptau `bhūyaścānte viśvamāyānivṛtti' rityādiśrutereva leśānuvṛtteravagatatvāt, nāpi sthitau ekakālīnatvena doṣābhāvāt|
yadvā ajñānasya sūkṣmāvasthā leśaḥ| yathā `tasmāt phale pravṛttasya yāgādeḥ śaktimātrakam| utpattāvapi paśvāderapūrvaṃ na tataḥ pṛthak||' iti vārtikena yāge gate'pi yāgasūkṣmāvasthārūpamapūrvaṃ yāge sādhanatānirvāhakamaṅgīkriyate, tathā ajñāne gate'pi tatsūkṣmāvasthārūpo leśo dehādipratītyanukūlaḥ svīkriyate, svargajanakatāgrāhaśruterivātrāpi jīvanmuktiśrutestādṛgarthasvīkārāt| tasmādavidyāleśānuvṛttyā jīvanmuktirupapannatarā|| ityadvaitasiddhau jīvanmuktyupapattiḥ|| 4 ||
yattu paramate'parokṣajñānino'pi svayogyaparamānandahetuparamakāṣṭhāpannabhaktyabhāve tatsādhyasya mocakasya īśvaraprasādasyābhāvena prārabdhakarmaṇā saṃsārānuvṛttau jīvanmuktiḥ tadbhāve tu prasādasyāpi bhāvena niḥśśeṣaduḥkhanivṛttiviśiṣṭasvatonīcoccabhāvāpannasvarūpānandāvirbhāvarūpā muktiriti tanna, `tāvadevāsya cira'mityādiśrutyā asyotpannatattvasākṣātkārasya prārabdhakarmakṣayamātramapekṣaṇīyaṃ kaivalyasaṃpattyarthamiti pratipādanena īśvaraprasādāpekṣāyā vaktumaśakyatvāt| smṛtipurāṇādīnāṃ śrutivirodhena stutiparatvāt| `yamevaiva vṛṇute tena labhyaḥ tasmaiṣa ātmā vivṛṇute tanūṃ svā'miti bhaktijanyeśvaraprasādasyāpi tatsākṣātkārasvarūpa evopayogasya bodhitatvena smṛtyādīnāmapi tadanusāritvāt vaiparītyena sādhyasādhanabhāve mānābhāvāt| na ca prārabdhakarmakṣaye prasādaviniyogaḥ, prasādanirapekṣabhogādeva tatsiddheḥ| nāpi muktāvuccanīcabhāvaḥ, tasya dvitīyasāpekṣatvena tadāsaṃbhavāt| `paramaṃ sāmyamupaitī'ti sāmyaśruteśca| sātiśayatve mukteḥ svargādivadanityatvaṃ syāt| adhikadarśane duḥkhadveṣādikaṃ ca syāt|
nanu muktau atāratamyaṃ kiṃ bhedābhāvāt, uta satyapi bhede tatsāmyāt| nādyaḥ, śrutyā bhedasiddheḥ| nāntyaḥ, sāmyaṃ kiṃ jīveśvarayoḥ, uta jīvānāmeva| nādyaḥ, tayorvibhutvāṇutvaśeṣaśeṣibhāvasvātantryapāratantryādinā tāratamyāt| anekeśvarāpattyā jagatpravṛttyayogāt| tad‌dvayatāratamyapratipādakasmṛtibhiḥ `jagad‌vyāpāravarja'mityādisūtrairutkṛṣṭatvanikṛṣṭatvagrāhakānumānairvirodhācca| nāntyaḥ, jīvān prati śeṣiṇo lakṣmītattvāt tān prati niyāmakād viṣvaksenāditaścetarajīvānāṃ nikṛṣṭatvāt| `saiṣānandasye'tyāditaittarīyādiśrutibhiḥ|
muktānāmapi siddhānāṃ nārāyaṇaparāyaṇaḥ|
sudurlabhaḥ praśāntātmā koṭiṣvapi mahāmune||
ityādismṛtibhiḥ `vṛddhihrāsabhāktvamantarbhāvādubhayasāmañjasyādevami'tyādi sūtrairuktaśrutitarkānugṛhītairanumānairvirodhācceti cenna, bhedābhāvena tāratamyāsiddheḥ| yathā ca śrutyāderna bhedaparatvaṃ tathā prāgeva gatam| yattu dvitīyapakṣamāśaṅkya dūṣaṇaṃ, tadakāṇḍatāṇḍavam, bhedasattve abhedātmakaparamasāmyābhāvāt tatsattve bhedasyaivābhāvāt|
kiṃ ca tāratamyābhidhānaṃ paramamuktau ? uta brahmalokādivāsarūpāparamamuktau vā ? nādyaḥ, `evaṃ muktiphalāniyamastadavasthāvadhṛtestadavastāvadhṛteriti tṛtīyāntyādhikaraṇe `aihikamapyaprastutapratibandhe taddarśanā'dityetatsūtroktajñānagataihikatvāmuṣmikatvarūpaviśeṣavanmuktāvapi tāratamyamāśaṅkya niṣedhāt| dvitīye tviṣṭāpattiḥ|
yattu muktajīvabhogaḥ īśvarabhogānnikṛṣṭaḥ jīvabhogatvāt saṃsāribhogavat| evaṃ jīvajñānādikamapi pakṣīkṛtya prayoga ūhanīyaḥ| īśvarānandaḥ, jīvānandādutkṛṣṭaḥ tanniyāmakānandatvāt yadevaṃ tadevaṃ yathā sevakānandāt sevyānandaḥ| īśvaraḥ, jīvasvabhāvānandādita utkṛṣṭasvabhāvānandādimān tatprepsutve sati tatra śaktatvād, yo yatprepsutve sati yatra śaktaḥ sa tadvān yathā saṃmataḥ ityādīni tāratamyasādhakāni iti, tanna; ādye muktasya brahmarūpatayā upādhikṛtajīvatvābhāvenāśrayāsiddheḥ, īśvaratvābhāvena sādhyāprasiddheḥ, svarūpāsiddheśca| dvitīyādyanumāne jīveśvaravibhāgakāle tāratamyasādhanaṃ cetsiddhasādhanaṃ, tadbhinne kāle cet, pūrvadoṣānativṛttiḥ| `saiṣānandasye'tyādiśrutibhiḥ mānuṣānandamārabhya brahmānandaparyanteṣu uttarottaraśataguṇatvarūpatāratamyamupādhitāratamyena vadantībhirnirupādhike svarūpānande tāratamyasya vaktumaśakyatvāt| etena prakṛtā bandhanivṛttiḥ, svasajātīyabandhanivṛttyāśrayapratiyogikatāratamyavanniṣṭhā bandhanivṛttitvāt nigaḍabandhanivṛttivaditi nirastam, tāratamyasya guṇagatajātitvena bandhanivṛttyāśrayātmani vaktumaśakyatvāt| ata eva nivṛttigatatāratamyasādhanamapi apāstam, nivṛtterniratiśayatvādānandasya svarūpatayā ubhayavādisiddhatvena guṇatvābhāvena tatrāpi tasya vaktumaśakyatvāt| etena svarūpasukhānāṃ pratyekamekatvenāṇutvena ca saṃkhyāparimāṇakṛtavaiṣamyābhāve'pi jalasudhāpānajanyasukhayoriva madhuramadhurataratvādivacca svarūpakṛtavaiṣamyaṃ muktāviti nirastam, vaiṣayikasukhe sādhanatāratamyaprayuktatāratamye'pi svarūpānande tadabhāvācca|
na ca sālokyādimuktiḥ sāyujyādimuktibhyo'pakṛṣṭeti prasiddhivirodhaḥ, parāparamuktirūpatayā tadupapatteḥ| sāyujyādimuktāvutkṛṣṭatvavyapadeśo'pakṛṣṭatvābhāvamātreṇa| nanu sāyujyaṃ naikyam| `candramasaḥ sāyujyaṃ salokatāmāpnoti' ityādi śrutyā satyapi bhede sāyujyokteḥ `sayujaḥ paramātmānaṃ praviśya ca bahirgatāḥ|' ityādau sayujāṃ praveśamātrokteśca, sayujo bhāvaḥ sāyujyamiti yujaśabdena saṃbandhasyaivokteḥ `sālokyamapi sāmīpyaṃ sārūpyaṃ yoga eva ca'|| iti smṛtau sāyujye saṃbandhavācakayogaśabdaprayogācca| tasmātsāyujyaṃ nāma kṣīranīravad anyadehāviṣṭagrahadevatādivacca saṃśleṣamātraṃ, na tvaikyamiti cet, na, vyāpakeneśvareṇa saṃśleṣasya nityasiddhatvenāpumarthatvāt| na caitallokasthitasya jīvasya lokāntarasthitālokikaśarīrāvacchinneneśvareṇa saṃśleṣaḥ sādhyaḥ, `atra brahma samaśnuta' iti śruteḥ| utkramaṇagamanādisādhyabrahmalokāvāptivadupādhyavacchinnajīvasyānavacchinnabrahmābhedarūpaparamamukteḥ| pāralaukikaphalatvābhāvāt, `brahmavidāpnoti paraṃ' ityādau avāpteḥ brahmarūpatvāt sāyujyasyāpi tadrūpatayā aṅgīkaraṇīyatvācca| `candramasaḥ sāyujyami'tyādau ekopādhyavacchinnasyopādhyantarāvacchinnena aikyānupapattivadatrānupapatterabhāvāt, prasiddhārthasvīkāre bādhakasyoktatvācca, sāyujyaśabdastāvadvibhaktatvābhāvābhiprāyakaḥ|
yacca uttarottaraṃ śataguṇānandaprakāśakavākyeṣu prativākyaṃ muktāvakāmahataśabdaprayogāt mānuṣānandavad akāmahatamuktānande'pi tāratamyam iti, tanna, `etasyaivānandasyānyāni bhūtāni mātrāmupajīvantī'ti sarveṣāṃ laukikānandānāṃ paramānandāntarbhāvābhidhānopapatteḥ, na tu tasya tasyākāmahatasya tāvānevānanda iti, yena tatrāpi tāratamyaṃ kalpyeta| tathā ca `so'śnute sarvān kāmān' `kāmasya yatrāptāḥ kāmāstatra māmamṛtaṃ kṛdhī'tyādiśruteḥ `sa hi mukto'kāmahata' ityādibrahmāṇḍokteśca na virodhaḥ| nanu ekasyaiva śrotriyasya sarvatra parāmarśe mānuṣānandādibhyaḥ sāvadhāraṇaśataguṇitamanuṣyagandharvādyanekānandā ekasmin viruddhāḥ, bhinnāścedakāmahatatvāderekarūpatayā śrutasyāvyavasthāpakatvādānandavyavasthā'yogaḥ| ata vyavasthārthaṃ etat padākāma etaditarapadakāmaśca lakṣyate, tadā'śrutakalpanā bādhaśca| nahīndrādipade vā rājapade vā niṣkāmasya bhikṣukasya indrādyānandānubhavo'sti, tasmācchratyā pūrvavākyenāmuktānāṃ tāratamyamuktvā, `śrotriyasya ce'tyādinā `yaśca śrotriya' ityādinā cottaravākyena muktasya taducyata iti cenna, sarveṣu vākyeṣu akāmahatasya muktasyaikatve'pi tadānande sarvānandānāmantarbhāvāt sa eva tasmin tasminnānande vaktavye parāmṛśyate, tattadindrādisāmyena tasya sarvatrābhidhānopapatteḥ `adhikaṃ praviṣṭaṃ na tu taddhāni'riti nyāyāt| sāmye hi tatsajātīyadharmavattvaṃ tantram, na tu taditaradharmānadhikaraṇatvamapi, gauravāt|
yacca muktasukhaṃ parasparatāratamyavat parasparatāratamyavatsādhanakatvāt saṃmatavaditi, tanna, asiddheḥ| nanu muktiḥ prayāgamaraṇabhagavad‌dveṣādisādhyeti mate jñānakarmasamuccayasādhyeti mate ca prayāgamaraṇādīnāṃ varṇāśramakarmaṇāṃ ca viṣamatvāt nāsiddhiḥ| jñānaikasādhyeti mate'pi `kastaṃ madāmadaṃ devaṃ madanyo jñātumarhati' `sarve guṇā brahmaṇaiva hyupāsyā nānyairdevaiḥ kimu sarvairmanuṣyai'rityādiśrutyā `brahmādi tadveda na caiva samyaganye kuto devamunīndramartyāḥ|' ityādismṛtibhiḥ devādimanuṣyādisthamuktihetubrahmajñānagatasya bahubahutaraśākhāśravaṇasādhyatvasya bahubahutaraguṇaviṣayatvaṃ vinā'yogenārthāpattyā ca tatsiddhiriti cenna, kevalakarmapakṣe samuccayapakṣe vā karmasādhyatvena mukteranityatvāpatteḥ, `nānyaḥ panthāḥ' ityādiśrutivirodhācca, tṛtīyapakṣe tūdāhṛtaśrutīnāṃ brahmavidyādurlabhatvapratipādanaparatvena taduktasādhanasādhyatvāpratipādakatvāt| na cārthāpattyā tatsiddhiḥ, brahmasākṣātkārasya nirguṇaviṣayatayā guṇaviṣayatvāyogāt| yattu -- 
yasya āśiṣa āśāste na sa bhṛtyaḥ sa vai vaṇik|
sa vai bhṛtyaḥ sa vai svāmī guṇalubdhau na kāmukau||
mumukṣoramumukṣustu varaścaikāntabhaktimān|
ityādismṛtyā mumukṣubhaktāpekṣayā amumukṣorbhaktasyādhikyokteḥ, tadādhikyasya lokarītisiddhatvācca `bhaktiḥ siddhergarīyasī'tismṛtyā alpabhaktisādhyamuktyapekṣayā adhikamuktihetubhakterapi ādhikyasyokteśca iti, tanna, `yasta āśiṣaḥ' ityādinā phalamanicchato guṇalobhena yā bhaktistasyāstu garīyastvaṃ yat pratipāditaṃ, tattattvasākṣātkāre tvarāsampādakaṃ na tu muktitāratamyākṣepakam| `bhaktiḥ siddhe'rityādinā pratipāditaṃ garīyastvamapi tajjanakatvamātreṇa putrātpituriva| yattu -- 
anye tvevamajānantaḥ śrutvānyebhya upāsate|
te'pi cātitarantyeva mṛtyuṃ śrutiparāyaṇāḥ||'
ityatrāpiśabdena,
striyo vaiśyāstathā śūdrāste'pi yānti parāṃ gatim|
kiṃ punarbrahmaṇāḥ puṇyā bhaktā rājarpayastathā||'
ityatra kaimutyena ca sādhanatāratamyena sādhye tatpratītiḥ iti, tanna, na hi taraṇe'pītyasyānvayaḥ, kiṃtu adhikāriṇi| tathā ca vilambitataraṇarūpaphalasambandhamātraparyavasānāt kaimutyasyāpi tvarāphalalābhamātreṇopapatteḥ sādhanamātratāratamyasya phalatāratamyāprayojakatvācca| na hi daṇḍatāratamyena ghaṭatāratamyaṃ kvacidapi dṛśyate| yattu -- 
sādhanasyottamatvena sādhyamuttamamāpnuyuḥ|
brahmādayaḥ krameṇaiva yathānandaśrutau śrutāḥ||
iti brāhme' `adhikaṃ tava vijñānamadhikā ca gatistave'ti sākṣānmokṣadharme ca sādhanatāratamyena sādhye taduktiḥ iti, tanna, sādhanottamatvena sādhyottamatvasyāparamamuktiviṣayatvāt, vijñānagatādhikyokterapi sākṣātkāraprayojakasaguṇaviṣayakajñānaparatvācca, ata eva daharādividyānāmadhikālpaguṇaviṣayakatvena sādhanatāratamyaṃ yatparābhimataṃ tadapyevamiti na kaściddoṣaḥ|
tasmātsvarūpānandasya svaprakāśātmarūpiṇaḥ|
prāptirmuktirna tatrāsti tāratamyaṃ kathaṃñcana|| 1 ||
|| ityadvaitasiddhau muktau tāratamyabhaṅgaḥ||
yo lakṣmyā nikhilānupekṣya vibudhāneko vṛtaḥ svecchayā
yaḥ sarvān smṛtamātra eva satataṃ sarvātmanā rakṣati|
yaścakreṇa nikṛtya nakramakaronmuktaṃ mahākuñjaraṃ
dveṣeṇāpi dadāti yo nijapadaṃ tasmai namo viṣṇave|| 2 ||
śrīmādhavasarasvatyo jayanti yamināṃ varāḥ|
vayaṃ yeṣāṃ prasādena śāstrārthe pariniṣṭhatāḥ|| 3 ||
sahajasaralāṃ premṇā dīrghāṃ samastaviśodhinīṃ
sakṛdapi kṛpādṛṣṭiṃ santo diśantu bhavadvidhāḥ|
kathamapi satī pūtā sadyastayā viṣayīkṛtā
mama kṛtiriyaṃ hitvā doṣānbhavatvatisad‌guṇā|| 4 ||
gurūṇāṃ māhātmyānnijavividhavidyāparicayāt
śruteryanme samyaṅmananapariniṣpannamabhavat|
parabrahmānandasphuraṇamakhilānarthaśamanaṃ
tadetasmin granthe nikhilamatiyatnena nihitam|| 5 ||
iha kumatiratattve tattvavādī varākaḥ
pralapati yadakāṇḍe khaṇḍanābhāsamuccaiḥ|
prativacanamamuṣmai tasya ko vaktuvidvān
na hi rutamanurauti grāmasiṃhasya siṃhaḥ|| 6 ||
kutarkagaralākulaṃ bhiṣajituṃ mano durdhiyāṃ
mayāyamudito mudā viṣavighātimantro mahān|
anena sakalāpadāṃ vighaṭanena yanme'bhavat paraṃ
sukṛtamarpitaṃ tadakhileśvare śrīpatau|| 7 ||
granthasyaitasya yaḥ kartā stūyatāṃ vā sa nindyatām|
mayi nāstyeva kartṛtvamananyānubhavātmani|| 8 ||
śrīvyāsaśaṅkarasureśvarapadmapādān
vedāntaśāstrasunibandhakṛtasthathānyān|
vidyāpradāniha yatipravarāndayālūn sarvān
gurūn satatameva namāmi bhaktyā|| 9 ||
siddhīnāmiṣṭanaiṣkarmyabrahmagānāmiyaṃ cirāt|
advaitasiddhiradhunā caturthī samajāyata|| 10 ||
iti śrīmatparamahaṃsaparivrājakācāryaśrīviśveśvarasarasvatī śrīcaraṇaśiṣyaśrīmadusūdanasarasvatīviracitāyāmadvaitasiddhau muktinirūpaṇaṃ nāma caturthaḥ paricchedaḥ||
